Frame-wise affine transforms absorb global motion before canonical Gaussian construction, reducing the gap between the canonical representation and target frames. Since the proposed method only modifies the canonical construction stage, it can be integrated into existing canonical-Gaussian-based methods with negligible additional parameter cost. Experiments show that our method improves reconstruction quality especially for sequences with large camera motion.

</details>


### [71] [Open Vocabulary Word Recognition From Transcribed Bangla Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01134)
*Faias Satter,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对手写孟加拉语单词识别难题，采用三种目标检测模型（SSD+MobileNetV2、Faster R-CNN+InceptionResNetV2及二者集成）并结合改进的非极大值抑制，在自建9841张手写孟加拉语单词图像数据集上进行训练测试，集成模型取得最优F1-score 92.61%、单词级识别率96.12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语OCR系统在软件行业中缺乏可靠方案，手写文本场景尤为困难。单词识别是OCR流程中继单词分割之后的关键第二阶段，若该阶段失败，整体OCR性能将严重受损。因此研究旨在利用深度学习实现手写孟加拉语单词图像的识别。

Method: 使用三种目标检测模型进行训练与测试：SSD搭配MobileNetV2、Faster R-CNN搭配InceptionResNetV2，以及二者的集成模型；引入改进的非极大值抑制（NMS）以提升模型效果；构建包含9841张、来自不同书写者、风格多样的手写孟加拉语单词图像的自定义数据集。

Result: 三种模型均在测试集上评估，集成模型表现最佳，F1-score达92.61%；在单词级别上，集成模型能够在某种程度上正确识别96.12%的单词。

Conclusion: 集成目标检测模型结合改进NMS在手写孟加拉语单词识别中效果显著。未来可通过引入后处理阶段纠正系统产生的错误，进一步提升系统性能。

Abstract: An optical character recognition (OCR) can scan a paper and extract text using technology, making people's jobs easier. While various OCR systems are available in the software industry, finding a reliable equivalent solution for Bangla takes much work. When it comes to handwritten texts, the situation is much more unusual. Recognizing words from word images is the most critical stage in any OCR process. It is the second stage after segmenting words from text pictures. If this stage fails, the overall performance of the OCR will be poor, regardless of how well the other phases perform. This study aims to recognize words using deep learning in a handwritten Bangla word image. Three object detection models, SSD with MobileNetV2, Faster R-CNN with InceptionResNetV2, and an ensemble model of these two, have been used to train and test handwritten word images. A modified Non-Maximum Suppression has been introduced to enhance the effectiveness of the models' results. A customized dataset of 9841 handwritten Bangla word images has been compiled, featuring diverse handwriting styles from various individuals. All three models' performances have been checked against the test dataset, and the ensemble model has been the most impressive, with an F1-score of 92.61%. Also, at the word level, the ensemble model correctly recognizes 96.12% of the words to some extent. The system can be further improved by introducing a post-processing phase to correct errors generated by the system.

</details>


### [72] [The RSNA Intracranial Aneurysm (RSNA-ICA) Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01135)
*Maria Correia de Verdier,Rachit Saluja,Jason Sho,Maryam Vabarizad,Rennie Yung-Chieh Chen,Uyen N. T. Nguyen,Mona Alrehaili,Layal Aweidah,Deniz Bulja,Wesley C. Chan,Hernan Chaves,Madhavi Duvvuri,Huseyin Ekin Ergin,Undrakh-Erdene Erdenebold,Ekim Gumeler,Mohamed Sobhi Jabal,Chin-Chi Kuo,Fatima Mubarak,Sevde Nur Emir,Scott Riley K. Ong,Johanna Ortiz,Almudena Pérez-Lara,Andreas M. Rauschecker,Shayan Sirat Maheen Anwar,Charit Tippareddy,Tam Tran,Sorawis Visrutaratna,John Mongan,Adam E. Flanders,Robyn Ball,Greg Zaharchuk,Peter D. Chang,Felipe Kitamura,Errol Colak,Luciano Prevedello,Tyler Richards,Data Contributor Group,Dataset Annotator Group,Evan Calabrese,Jeffrey D. Rudie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSNA-ICA 是一个大型公开的颅内动脉瘤专家标注数据集（7202 个 CTA/MRA/MRI 序列，来自 12 国 21 家机构 4278 名患者），用于支持 2025 RSNA 颅内动脉瘤检测挑战赛及 AI 算法开发评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅内动脉瘤破裂致死致残率高，但动脉瘤检测（尤其小病灶及常规非血管造影影像）仍具挑战，亟需大规模高质量数据支持 AI 算法的开发与评估。

Method: RSNA 联合 ASNR、SNIS、ESNR 构建 RSNA-ICA 数据集，纳入 7202 个 CTA/MRA/MRI 序列（来自 4278 名成人患者、12 国 21 家机构），由专家标注动脉瘤有无及位置，178 个序列另含三维分割标注，并用于挑战赛算法开发与评测。

Result: 数据集包含 2566 个 CTA、2166 个 MRA、2470 个 MRI 序列，覆盖有/无囊状动脉瘤患者，具备显著的地域与影像多样性；其中 5041 个序列通过 MIRA 平台公开，其余用于挑战赛公开与私有测试集。

Conclusion: RSNA-ICA 面向研究社区免费开放（非商业用途），为推进血管造影及常规神经影像中的 AI 动脉瘤检测提供了全面资源。该工作属于医学影像数据集与算法评测范畴。

Abstract: Intracranial aneurysm rupture is associated with substantial morbidity and mortality, yet aneurysm detection remains challenging, particularly for small lesions and on routine non-angiographic imaging examinations. To support the development and evaluation of artificial intelligence (AI) algorithms for intracranial aneurysm detection and localization, the Radiological Society of North America (RSNA), in collaboration with the American Society of Neuroradiology (ASNR), the Society of Neurointerventional Surgery (SNIS), and the European Society of Neuroradiology (ESNR), curated the RSNA Intracranial Aneurysm (RSNA-ICA) Dataset. Developed for the 2025 RSNA Intracranial Aneurysm Detection Challenge, RSNA-ICA is a large, publicly available, expert-annotated dataset comprising 7202 CTA, MRA, and MRI series from 4278 adult patients collected across 21 institutions in 12 countries spanning five continents. The dataset includes 2566 CTA, 2166 MRA, and 2470 MRI series from patients with and without intracranial saccular aneurysms, providing substantial geographic and imaging diversity. Expert annotations indicate both aneurysm presence and location, and 178 series additionally include three-dimensional segmentations of challenge-defined vascular locations. RSNA-ICA was used to develop and evaluate algorithms in the 2025 RSNA Intracranial Aneurysm Detection Challenge. Of the 7202 image series, 5041 are publicly available through MIRA (https://mira.rsna.org/dataset/7), while the remainder were used for challenge public and private test sets. The dataset is freely available to the research community for noncommercial use and provides a comprehensive resource for advancing AI-based aneurysm detection across both angiographic and routine neuroimaging examinations.

</details>


### [73] [OptimusMesh: Compact Autoregressive Mesh Generation from Point Clouds via Sparse Latent Pivots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01148)
*Mazhar Iqbal,Naoya Chiba,Xuanmeng Sha,Tomohiro Mashita,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OptimusMesh 提出用稀疏潜在枢轴（sparse latent pivots）压缩点云条件，实现从点云直接生成紧凑三角网格，将条件集减少 128 倍、条件序列缩短 16.1 倍，并在保持几何保真度的同时大幅减少面数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从点云直接生成紧凑且几何保真的 3D 网格仍是基础性难题。点云无序稀疏，网格结构不规则且拓扑多变，现有方法多依赖隐式表示加表面提取，易产生稠密或过度平滑的网格，且需要昂贵的后处理与简化。因此作者希望实现从点云直接、紧凑地生成三角网格。

Method: 提出 OptimusMesh 框架，核心是稀疏潜在枢轴条件机制：将 2048 个带方向输入点压缩为仅 16 个稀疏潜在枢轴，使几何条件集减少 128 倍。这些枢轴作为紧凑结构表示，被共享于两阶段自回归框架中：第一阶段生成网格顶点，第二阶段基于生成的顶点与同一组枢轴预测三角面。

Result: 与所评估的近期点云条件自回归方法相比（其使用 257 个解码器条件 token），OptimusMesh 仅用 16 个，条件序列缩短 16.1 倍。实验显示其在对比的近期自回归方法中输出最紧凑，面数减少 25.7%–94.1%，同时保持有竞争力的几何保真度与分布质量。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏潜在枢轴压缩条件并采用两阶段自回归生成，OptimusMesh 能够直接从点云生成紧凑三角网格，在显著减少面数与条件长度的同时维持较好的几何质量，为点云到网格的直接生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Generating compact and geometrically faithful 3D meshes directly from point clouds remains a fundamental challenge. Point clouds are unordered and sparse, whereas meshes exhibit irregular structure and varying topology. As a result, many existing approaches rely on implicit representations followed by surface extraction or reconstruction. Although effective, these pipelines can produce dense or over-smoothed meshes, often requiring computationally expensive post-processing and simplification. We present OptimusMesh, a framework for direct compact triangle mesh generation from point clouds using sparse latent pivot conditioning. Our key idea is to compress $2{,}048$ oriented input points into only $16$ sparse latent pivots, reducing the geometric conditioning set by $128\times$. These pivots provide a compact structural representation shared across a two-stage autoregressive framework that first generates mesh vertices and then predicts triangular faces conditioned on the generated vertices and the same pivots. Compared with the evaluated recent point-cloud-conditioned autoregressive methods, which use $257$ decoder-conditioning tokens, OptimusMesh uses only $16$, yielding a $16.1\times$ shorter conditioning sequence. Experiments show that OptimusMesh produces the most compact outputs among the compared recent autoregressive methods, using $25.7\%$--$94.1\%$ fewer faces while maintaining competitive geometric fidelity and distributional quality.

</details>


### [74] [PhysicsLENS: Diagnosing Physical Property Blindness in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01162)
*Isaiah Milkey,Som Sagar,Aditya Taparia,Xinyuan Liu,Jiqing Wen,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PhysicsLENS，一个面向机器人场景的物理属性合理性评估数据集与基准，通过配对场景揭示视频世界模型常忽视指令中的物理属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的视频世界模型可为机器人学习、规划与评估提供可扩展的预测环境，但生成的机器人视频常违反物理规律，以物理上不合理的行为完成任务，降低其可靠性。现有视频生成基准忽略了隐藏在视觉之下的物理属性（如重量、粘度、摩擦），因此只评估物理的外观保真度，而非物理本质的正确性。

Method: 提出 PhysicsLENS：一个评估机器人相关物理属性合理性的数据集与基准。采用配对场景设计，保持相同条件帧与任务，仅在场景描述中改变底层物理属性。场景取自公开机器人视频，并在七个物理领域（碰撞、重力、动量、摩擦、形变、流体、因果）上标注。对四个视频生成模型进行评测，收集超过 400 个人工标注标签。

Result: 结果显示，看似合理的视频往往忽略所声明的物理属性（47 例中 34 例）；且在描述中明确声明该属性仅轻微降低合理性，且效果不显著。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型倾向于生成视觉上逼真但物理上不可靠的内容，忽视指令中的物理属性，说明现有模型作为机器人预测环境仍存在明显局限，PhysicsLENS 为更严格地评估物理本质正确性提供了基准。

Abstract: Reliable video world models could provide scalable predictive environments for robot learning, planning, and evaluation. However, generated robot videos can violate physical principles and complete tasks through physically implausible behavior, limiting their reliability for robot learning and planning. Current video-generation benchmarks exclude physics that are inherently hidden by visuals (e.g., weight, viscosity, friction). Due to this, video models are evaluated on the fidelity of physics, not the underlying accuracy of physics. We introduce PhysicsLENS, a dataset and benchmark for evaluating plausibility of physical properties grounded in robotics. PhysicsLENS uses matched scenario pairs that hold the same conditioning frame and task, while varying underlying physics in the scene description. Scenarios are curated from public robot video sources and annotated across seven physical domains: collision, gravity, momentum, friction, deformation, fluid, and causality. We evaluate across four video generation models, producing over 400 human-annotated labels. Results show that plausible-looking videos often ignore the stated property (34 of 47), and that stating the property lowers plausibility only slightly and not significantly.

</details>


### [75] [CineMR: Tool-Integrated Vision-Language Reasoning for Quantitative Cardiac MRI Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01166)
*Kunyang Li,Hai Nguyen,Joshua Lowe,Chenguang Zhao,Peace C. Madueme,Mehdi Hedjazi Moghari,Mubarak Shah,Pegah Khosravi,Yuzhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineMR 是一个工具增强的多模态大模型，通过调用心脏图像分析工具并整合其输出进行交错推理，实现心脏磁共振电影序列的定量评估，在自建多队列基准上显著超越现有医学 VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振电影成像（cine CMR）是评估心脏形态与心室功能的无创金标准，其判读需要结合定性视觉评估与心室容积、射血分数、心肌质量、壁厚及节段性室壁运动等定量测量。然而，现有医学视觉语言模型（VLM）无法在缺乏分析工具的情况下，从多维电影图像中可靠地得出定量测量结果，这限制了它们在临床定量 CMR 评估中的可用性。

Method: 作者提出 CineMR，一种工具增强的 VLM，能够调用心脏图像分析工具并将其输出整合到交错式推理过程中以完成定量 CMR 评估。同时构建了覆盖定量指标提取、多分类诊断与鉴别诊断的多队列视觉问答基准，并配套分割、时相选择、容积测定、形态测量和节段性室壁运动分析等工具。训练采用两阶段策略：先在工具交互轨迹上进行监督微调（SFT），随后使用带条件工具使用奖励的组相对策略优化（GRPO）。

Result: 在多队列 cine CMR 基准上，CineMR 达到 35.9% pass@1 与 58.9% pass@4，而基线 Qwen3-VL-8B 仅 1.5% pass@1，LLaVA-Med v1.5 与 MedGemma-4B 分别为 0.0% 和 7.0% pass@1。GRPO 后正确工具调用率达 99.8%，SFT 后为 78.9%。实时工具输出使心室测量精度比模型直接预测提升 20.4%–23.7%；移除所有工具后 pass@1 从 35.9% 降至 27.9%。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了可靠工具使用对于定量 cine CMR 推理的重要性，表明 CineMR 是辅助心脏图像评估的一种有前景的方法。代码、基准资源与模型权重已开源。

Abstract: Cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR), including cine imaging, is a reference standard for the noninvasive assessment of cardiac morphology and ventricular function. Cine CMR interpretation integrates qualitative visual assessment with quantitative measurements of ventricular volumes, ejection fraction, myocardial mass, wall thickness, and regional wall motion. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) cannot reliably derive quantitative measurements from multidimensional cine images without analysis tools. We present CineMR, a tool-augmented VLM that invokes cardiac image-analysis tools and integrates their outputs into interleaved reasoning for quantitative CMR assessment. We also construct a multi-cohort visual question answering benchmark covering quantitative metric extraction, multiclass diagnosis, and differential diagnosis, together with tools for segmentation, phase selection, volumetry, morphometry, and regional wall motion analysis. CineMR is trained with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on tool-interaction traces followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with conditional tool-use rewards. On the multi-cohort cine CMR benchmark, CineMR achieves 35.9% pass@1 and 58.9% pass@4, compared with 1.5% pass@1 for the Qwen3-VL-8B backbone and 0.0% and 7.0% pass@1 for LLaVA-Med v1.5 and MedGemma-4B, respectively. Correct tool invocation reaches 99.8% after GRPO, up from 78.9% after SFT. Live tool outputs improve ventricular measurement accuracy by 20.4--23.7% over direct model predictions, and removing all tools reduces pass@1 from 35.9% to 27.9%. These results highlight the importance of reliable tool use for quantitative cine CMR reasoning and support CineMR as a promising approach for assistive cardiac image assessment. Code, benchmark resources, and model weights are available at https://github.com/AI-MIND-Lab/CineMR.

</details>


### [76] [Skeleton-and-Strategy Prompting: Training-Free Negation Understanding for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01180)
*Yuliang Cai,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的Skeleton-and-Strategy Prompting（SSP），通过抽象问题骨架、检索同类否定问题并合成一句话答题策略，提升VLM的否定理解能力，在多个否定VQA基准上达到SOTA且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在多种VQA任务上表现优异，但在涉及否定从句的问题上持续失败，容易给出错误答案，说明其否定理解能力存在明显短板。

Method: SSP是一种免训练、基于上下文学习的方法，不更新任何参数。给定一个否定问题，先将问题结构抽象为骨架，从轻量问题池中检索少量同骨架问题，再提示VLM分析这些问题的共同否定模式并合成一句答题策略；最后将骨架与策略前置到测试样本中以引导模型作答。

Result: 在多个否定VQA基准上实验表明，SSP在聚焦否定理解的VQA任务上取得了最先进（SOTA）性能，同时保持较低的计算开销。

Conclusion: 通过骨架抽象与策略提示的免训练上下文学习方式，能够有效提升VLM对否定的理解，且方法高效、易于落地，为改善VLM语言推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Despite the strong performance of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on a wide range of visual question answering (VQA) tasks, these models consistently struggle to understand negation and produce incorrect answers when questions involve negated clauses. To address this limitation, we propose Skeleton-and-Strategy Prompting (\textbf{SSP}), a training-free, in-context learning method that improves VLM negation understanding capabilities without any parameter updates. Given a negation question, our method first abstracts the underlying question structure into a skeleton, retrieves a small set of same-skeleton questions from a lightweight question pool, then prompts the VLM to analyze their shared negation pattern and synthesize a single-sentence answering strategy. The skeleton and strategy are prepended to the test sample to guide the model correctly tackle the negation problems. Experiments on multiple negation VQA benchmarks show that SSP achieves state-of-the-art performance on negation-focused VQA tasks while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [77] [Color Independent Word Segmentation From Transcribed Bangla Passages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01191)
*Faias Satter,Noor Masrur,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对手写孟加拉文文本图像，提出一种可在任意智能手机拍摄图像上运行的单词分割方法，构建了包含阴影等干扰的自定义数据集。对7374个单词生成7278个边界框，召回率90.60%、精确率91.80%、F1值91.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR系统可扫描纸张并提取文字，但孟加拉文缺乏可靠的开源OCR方案，手写场景更为稀缺。单词分割是OCR的首要基础步骤，若该阶段失败，后续环节表现再好整体性能也会很差。因此本文聚焦手写孟加拉文文本图像的单词分割问题。

Method: 方法基于自适应阈值化与膨胀滤波进行图像预处理与单词分割，可适用于任意颜色和类型的纸张与墨水。针对智能手机拍摄图像可能存在的阴影干扰，专门构建了涵盖各类潜在障碍的自定义数据集。

Result: 在7374个单词上共生成7278个边界框，取得召回率90.60%、精确率91.80%、F1值91.20%的效果。

Conclusion: 该系统可通过以下方式进一步改进：对包含多个单词的边界框进行嵌套操作，或将自适应阈值化与膨胀滤波器的尺寸调整到更精细的水平。

Abstract: An optical character recognition(OCR) system can scan paper and extract text, making people's jobs easier. While numerous OCR systems are accessible in the software sector, finding a dependable equivalent solution for Bangla is tough. When it comes to handwritten texts, the case is even more rare. The first fundamental step to any OCR is to segment words from text images. If this stage fails, the total OCR's performance will be poor no matter how promising the later stages perform. This research aims to segment words in a handwritten Bangla text image. This research can be implemented on any smartphone-captured image, irrespective of the color and type of paper and ink. Furthermore, as smartphone-captured images can create shadow interferences, the custom dataset built for this research is created in such a way that every possible obstacle that can be faced is included. For 7374 words, a total of 7278 bounding boxes are generated, which have recall of 90.60 %, precision of 91.80 %, and F1-score of 91.20 %. The system can be further improved with nested operations on bounding boxes containing several words or by adjusting the adaptive thresholding and dilation filter sizes to a more precise level.

</details>


### [78] [FlashBack: Knowing When to Remember in Streaming Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01192)
*Yi Chen,MingMing Yu,Rui-Qi Wang,Boran Wang,Xiaohang Cao,Chu Tang,Jingmin Chen,Jie Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashBack：一种免训练的流式视觉语言模型选择性多级记忆框架，通过判断查询是否需要历史证据来决定走原生轨迹还是召回轨迹，在OVO-Bench与StreamingBench上提升了长时程与记忆任务表现且基本保持实时感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视觉语言模型需在有界算力下处理持续增长的视频流，实时感知与长期记忆之间存在持续张力。检索历史信息虽可缓解，但历史召回并非总是有益：无关历史会引入噪声、干扰实时感知。因此有效的流式记忆不仅要解决"记住什么"，还要解决"何时、如何访问"。

Method: 提出FlashBack——面向流式VLM的免训练、选择性、多级记忆框架。检索历史前，先利用冻结流式VLM的语义理解能力判断当前查询是否需要历史证据；据此决定推理保持Native轨迹，还是进入隔离的Recall轨迹。Recall轨迹通过查询本地的Side-KV通路，将近期上下文与检索到的长期记忆结合，在不修改持久Native状态的前提下保持局部时间连续性。

Result: 在StreamingVLM与Mage-VL-4B上实例化，并在OVO-Bench和StreamingBench上评估。结果显示，在若干长时程与记忆依赖任务上有提升，同时基本保持实时感知能力；尽管无需额外训练，性能与强训练型流式方法相比仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 选择性、多级且免训练的记忆访问机制能在有限算力下缓解实时感知与长期记忆的冲突，为流式视觉语言模型提供高效的历史召回方案。代码将随后公布。

Abstract: Streaming vision-language models must process continuously growing video streams under a bounded compute budget, creating a persistent tension between real-time perception and long-term memory. Retrieving historical information provides a natural remedy, yet historical recall is not uniformly beneficial: unnecessary history may introduce irrelevant context into current reasoning and interfere with native real-time perception. Effective streaming memory should therefore address not only what to remember, but also when and how to access it. To this end, we introduce FlashBack, a training-free framework for selective, multi-level memory in streaming vision-language models. Before retrieving history, FlashBack draws on the semantic understanding of the frozen streaming VLM to infer whether a query calls for historical evidence. This assessment determines whether inference remains on the Native trajectory or invokes an isolated Recall trajectory. The Recall trajectory combines recent context with retrieved long-term memory through a query-local Side-KV pathway, preserving local temporal continuity without modifying the persistent Native state. We instantiate FlashBack on StreamingVLM and Mage-VL-4B and evaluate it on OVO-Bench and StreamingBench. The results show improvements on several long-horizon and memory-dependent tasks while largely preserving real-time perception, with performance competitive with strong training-based streaming methods despite requiring no additional training. Our code will be announced later.

</details>


### [79] [iSEE: Object Permanence Through Self-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01201)
*Pramish Paudel,Ajad Chhatkuli,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iSEE是一个无标签的自监督框架，通过对象证据建模、外观-位置分离和重现在学习，实现遮挡下的物体恒存性，在跟踪和定位上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频表示需要物体恒存性（在遮挡期间保持物体身份和位置），但现有方法存在局限：基于框的跟踪器依赖标签，而自监督slot attention方法在遮挡下会丢失slot，无法实现恒存性。核心难点在于需检测遮挡、重现时重新识别、保持隐藏位置连续，且仅能利用重现作为学习信号。

Method: 提出iSEE框架，包含三个组件：(i) 对象证据建模：通过比较slot当前注意力与其历史，检测物体何时被遮挡；(ii) 外观-位置分离：使用两个slot流，外观流保持用于重新识别，位置流持续更新；(iii) 从重现中学习恒存性：用walker跟踪隐藏物体位置，仅以物体重现位置为训练信号。

Result: 在LA-CATER static数据集上，iSEE在86%的遮挡后将重现物体返回其原slot，而SlotContrast仅32%；在隐藏状态下定位误差在4.1 mAP以内，接近有标签训练的SoTA RAM。位置流可作为世界模型的action用于下游规划。

Conclusion: iSEE在无任何标签的条件下实现了遮挡下的物体恒存性，包括遮挡检测、重识别和隐藏位置连续跟踪，并支持下游规划任务，证明了自监督方法在此问题上的可行性。

Abstract: Object permanence, keeping track of an object's identity and position while it is occluded, is central to video representations that track, predict and plan. Trackers that achieve it learn from boxes, track identities and visibility labels. On the other hand, self-supervised object-centric methods discover objects without labels: through slot attention, it represents a video as slots that bind to objects and follow them across frames. However, these slots are lost under occlusion, making the desired permanence impossible. Reasoning permanence is a hard problem because it requires to detect when an object becomes occluded, re-identify when object reappears, and keep the object's hidden position continuous, using reapperance as the only learning cue. To address this, we propose iSEE, a novel framework that offers all three aforementioned requirements, without any labels whatsoever. We built iSEE using the following three proposed components: (i) Object evidence modelling: a slot's attention, compared with its own past, reveals when its object is hidden. (ii) Appearance-position separation: two slot streams let the appearance be held for re-identification while the position keeps changing. (iii) Permanence from reappearance: a walker follows the hidden object's position, trained only on where the object reappears. On LA-CATER static, iSEE returns a reappearing object to its own slot after 86% of occlusions, against 32% for SlotContrast, and localises it while hidden within 4.1 mAP of the label-trained SoTA RAM. The two streams also allow downstream planning, with the position stream as the action of a world model. Project page: https://insait-institute.github.io/iSEE/

</details>


### [80] [Semantic RGB--Depth Based Surgical Skill Assessment in Microscopic Stereo Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01205)
*Jecia Z. Y. Mao,Sue M. Cho,Francis X. Creighton,Deepa Galaiya,Russell H. Taylor,Manish Sahu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义RGB-深度框架，融合稀疏立体深度与单目深度并结合语义流，用于显微外科技能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的显微外科技能评估主要依赖RGB图像，忽视了器械-解剖交互的3D空间关系；而立体显微镜虽有深度信息，但传统立体匹配在高倍成像下产生稀疏、不可靠的深度估计，难以用于自动技能评估。

Method: 基于回归的深度融合方法，将稀疏度量立体深度与稠密单目深度结合，生成稠密几何表示；再与按手术器械及周围解剖分解的语义RGB流整合，采用分层注意力架构联合编码这些流，捕捉不同培训水平外科医生的器械使用与器械-解剖交互判别模式。

Result: 在6名外科医生（主治医师与住院医师）完成的33例离体经口喉显微手术上，采用留一外科医生交叉验证。语义RGB-深度模型技能分级F1为0.938，语义RGB为0.696，语义深度为0.929。

Conclusion: 几何信息可提升基于显微立体视频的自动外科技能评估；学习到的空间、时间与语义注意力模式也支持对模型关注区域、视频片段和语义流进行定性分析。

Abstract: Objective assessment of microsurgical technical skill is essential for competency-based training and quality assurance, yet existing video-based approaches predominantly rely on RGB images and therefore overlook the 3D spatial relationships that characterize instrument-anatomy interactions. Although stereo operating microscopes provide complementary depth information, conventional stereo matching algorithms can produce sparse and unreliable depth estimates under high-magnification imaging conditions, limiting their use for automated skill assessment. This work presents a semantic RGB-Depth framework for surgical skill assessment from microscopic stereo videos. A regression-based depth fusion method combines sparse metric stereo depth with dense monocular depth estimates to generate a dense geometric representation of the surgical scene. This representation is integrated with semantically decomposed RGB streams corresponding to individual surgical instruments and surrounding anatomy. A hierarchical attention architecture jointly encodes these streams to capture discriminative patterns of instrument use and instrument-anatomy interaction across surgeons at different training levels. The framework was evaluated on 33 ex vivo transoral microlaryngeal procedures performed by six surgeons, comprising attending surgeons and surgical residents, using leave-one-surgeon-out cross-validation. The proposed semantic RGB-Depth model achieved an F1 score of 0.938 for skill-level classification, compared with 0.696 for semantic RGB and 0.929 for semantic depth. These results suggest that geometric information can improve automated surgical skill assessment from microscopic stereo videos. The learned spatial, temporal, and semantic attention patterns also support qualitative examination of the scene regions, video segments, and semantic streams emphasized by the model.

</details>


### [81] [Resolving Mixed Single-Photon LiDAR Returns for Foreground-View and Hidden Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01206)
*Ziting Wen,Runrong Deng,Zili Zhang,Haitao Zheng,Yuecong Xu,Xiaoqiang Ren,Guodong Shi,Kemi Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出状态感知框架，从遮挡单光子直方图中重建前景与隐藏场景，并发布真实配对遮挡数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人巡检中部分透射屏与防护罩会同时产生前景材质与后方场景的混合LiDAR回波。传统峰值法丢弃弱隐藏回波，单光子LiDAR虽保留时间分辨直方图，但现有瞬态重建方法只拟合单一场景表示；遮挡下弱或邻近的前景-隐藏回波会形成宽峰或肩部，而这些波形也可被解释为位移的单一表面或厚密度分布，因此瞬态拟合准确并不代表几何正确。

Method: 提出状态感知（state-aware）框架。对每条光线估计局部回波证据，判定为无可靠表面证据、单回波证据或双回波三类状态；据此为双头神经场路由监督信号：所有光线约束波形重建，可靠锚点提供几何定位。同时构建真实配对单光子LiDAR遮挡数据集，在固定位姿下同时采集遮挡与干净数据。

Result: 在真实数据集上，隐藏场景深度与点云精度均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单光子分层重建是通过部分透射遮挡物实现3D感知的可行途径。

Abstract: Partially transmissive screens and protective covers are common in robotic inspection, but they create mixed LiDAR returns from both the foreground material and the scene behind it. Conventional peak-based LiDAR usually discards weak hidden returns, while single-photon LiDAR records time-resolved histograms that preserve attenuated and overlapping echoes. However, existing transient reconstruction methods typically fit a single scene representation to the measured waveform. Under occlusion, weak or nearby foreground--hidden echoes can form a broad peak or subtle shoulder. Because such waveforms can also be explained by a displaced single surface or a thick density distribution, accurate transient fitting does not necessarily imply correct geometry. We propose a state-aware framework for foreground-view and hidden scene reconstruction from occluded single-photon histograms. For each ray, we estimate local echo evidence, identifying no reliable surface evidence, single-return evidence, or two returns. The inferred echo state routes supervision for a two-head neural field: all rays constrain waveform reconstruction, while reliable anchors provide geometry localization. We also introduce a real paired single-photon LiDAR occlusion dataset with occluded and clean captures at fixed poses. Experiments on a real dataset show improved hidden scene depth and point-cloud accuracy over baselines. Our results demonstrate single-photon layered reconstruction as a practical route for 3D perception through partially transmissive occluders.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [On-Device Named-Entity Recognition: A Deployability Study of Accuracy, Cost, Reliability, and Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00007)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了经典标注器、双向编码器专家模型与本地生成式LLM三类共九种系统在端侧NER任务上的可部署性、无人工标注评估方法及置信度可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧NER(无API、低延迟、数据本地化)的实际问题不是排行榜排名,而是哪个模型可部署、如何在无人工标注下评估、置信度是否可信。现有文献忽略了延迟和输出有效性两个维度。

Method: 在三个不同特性的数据集上评测九种系统(13M至8B参数):经典标注器spaCy、双向编码器专家GLiNER(166-460M)、本地生成式LLM(Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct,DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B)。因RSS-News语料无金标,用跨家族LLM评审团构建银标金标,并对全语料进行人工再验证(strict F1 0.95)。随后刻画GLiNER的逐span置信度。

Result: 金标来源翻转范式排名:从LLM银标到人工金标,所有编码器提升、所有生成模型下降。仅看准确率时4B instruct LLM有竞争力(干净新闻稿上领先);编码器优势在可部署性:以1/9至1/24规模、毫秒至秒级延迟、零畸形输出达到或略逊水平,最小生成模型在长输入上高达27%无效输出。GLiNER置信度排序正确性良好(AUROC 0.76-0.86)但过度自信(ECE 0.24-0.47,温度缩放减半),阈值化带来小幅诚实F1增益,小型到大型级联有小幅提升,置信度跟踪正确性但不跟踪新颖性。

Conclusion: 端侧NER中编码器在可部署性上优于生成式LLM,LLM输出有效性问题需靠规模而非输出预算解决;置信度可用于排序但需校准;所有数值可由逐span记录离线重算。

Abstract: Named-entity recognition (NER) is increasingly wanted on-device (no API, low latency, data kept local). The practitioner's question is not the leaderboard but which model is deployable, how to evaluate it without human annotation, and whether its confidence can be trusted. We answer these jointly. We place nine systems across three paradigms and 13 M to 8 B parameters: a classical tagger (spaCy), bidirectional-encoder specialists (GLiNER, 166 to 460 M), and generative LLMs run locally (Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct, DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B), on three datasets of differing character, and report accuracy plus two axes the literature omits: latency and output validity. Because our corpus (RSS-News) had no gold, we built silver gold from a cross-family LLM judge panel, then measured its fidelity against benchmark gold and a full human re-validation of the corpus (strict F1 0.95, an upper bound since the human gold was silver-seeded); gold provenance flips the paradigm ranking, moving from LLM-authored silver to human gold raises every encoder and lowers every generative model. On accuracy alone a 4 B instruct LLM is competitive (it leads on clean newswire), so the encoder's case is deployability: it matches or slightly trails at one-ninth to one-twenty-fourth the size, at millisecond-to-second latency, with zero malformed output, while the smallest generative models emit up to 27% invalid output on long inputs, a failure fixed by scale, not output budget. We then characterize GLiNER's per-span confidence: it ranks correctness well (AUROC 0.76 to 0.86) but is overconfident (ECE 0.24 to 0.47, halved by temperature scaling); thresholding gives a small honest out-of-sample F1 gain; an all-local small-to-large cascade gives a modest, corpus-dependent gain over cost-matched random routing; and confidence tracks correctness but not novelty. Every number recomputes offline from per-span records.

</details>


### [83] [SCM-based Fairness and Faithful Explainability for Legal Document Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00045)
*Yasmina El Kacemi,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在ECtHR法律判决预测任务上考察公平性去偏与解释忠实度之间的关系，发现公平性正则化未降低人口统计学差异，却持续损害SHAP解释充分性，表明公平性与解释忠实度相互解离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律决策支持中Transformer模型（如LegalBERT）日益普及，公平性与解释透明度受到关注，但二者通常被分开评估，尚不清楚改变公平性的去偏干预是否也会改变解释对模型推理的忠实反映。

Method: 基于LexGLUE的ECtHR"alleged-violations"语料，将LegalBERT基线与一种公平性正则化变体对比：后者在微调时惩罚刻板化的"热情"与"能力"表征。评估涵盖预测性能、人口统计学公平性以及SHAP解释忠实度，运行五个随机种子，并使用两种公平性定义、常规词对控制、打乱词对控制及三个阈值进行验证。

Result: 在性能最优的正则化强度下，干预并未降低人口统计学差异（该零结果在两种公平性定义与性别轴常规词对控制下均成立），分类性能基本不变；但干预在全部五个种子与三个阈值上一致地降低了SHAP解释充分性。打乱词对控制同样重现了该解释退化，而性能与公平性保持不变。

Conclusion: 结果表明公平性与解释忠实度之间存在解离：解释行为的变化不一定指示公平性的变化，解释层面的效果可能源于对比式表征正则化而非特定的"热情—能力"结构。因此公平性必须被直接评估，而不能依赖解释指标间接推断。

Abstract: Transformer models such as LegalBERT are increasingly used in legal decision support, raising concerns about both fairness and the transparency of model explanations. These properties are usually evaluated separately, leaving open whether a debiasing intervention that changes fairness also changes how faithfully explanations reflect model reasoning. This study investigates that relationship on the ECtHR alleged-violations corpus from LexGLUE. It compares a LegalBERT baseline with a fairness-regularized variant that penalizes stereotypical warmth and competence representations during fine-tuning. The evaluation covers predictive performance, demographic fairness, and SHAP explanation faithfulness across five random seeds. At the performance-optimal regularization strength, the intervention does not reduce demographic disparity. This null result holds across two fairness definitions and a conventional word-pair control on the gender axis. Classification performance is largely unchanged. However, the intervention consistently degrades explanation sufficiency across all five seeds and three thresholds. A shuffled-pair control reproduces this degradation while leaving performance and fairness unchanged, indicating that the effect arises from contrastive representational regularization rather than specifically from the warmth and competence structure. The results demonstrate a dissociation between fairness and explanation faithfulness: changes in explanation behavior do not necessarily indicate changes in fairness, and fairness must therefore be evaluated directly.

</details>


### [84] [The First Token Is Not the Verdict: Hidden Costs of Reading LLM Judges Without Generating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00054)
*Gnaneswar Villuri,Hashmath Shaik,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 从LLM评判器首个生成token的logits读取判定结果会系统性高估位置偏差，该读数应视为上界，需同时报告评判器"以判定token开头"的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从首个生成token的logits读取LLM评判器的判定廉价且无需生成，正是受限解码和似然评分评测框架的产物。但作者发现这种读法会扭曲位置偏差，需要揭示其机制与影响范围。

Method: 对比"强制首token读取"与"生成后读取"两种方式，在三个Qwen3评判器、Llama-3.1-8B和Phi-3.5-mini上测试；统计评判器不以判定token开头的比例，并在924对未提交判定的样本上通过交换响应顺序检验翻转率，同时对比位置偏差与评判准确率的变化。

Result: 评判器并非总以判定token开头：三个Qwen3评判器在12%至49%的pair上如此，Llama-3.1-8B和Phi-3.5-mini低于3%。在924对未提交判定的样本中，交换响应顺序后强制读取的翻转率为89.7%，而生成后读取为47.5%（配对差+0.422，95% CI [+0.365, +0.467]）。该扭曲具有测量特异性：位置偏差移动42个百分点，而评判准确率在十种条件中的七种变动不足一个百分点。此外，即使评判器以判定token开头，仍有0至5.5%的pair出现"先写一个字母再推理到另一个"的情况，且其比率与遵从性不相关。

Conclusion: 首token logits读取会单向高估位置偏差，误导评判器的审计者而非使用者。作者建议在报告任何位置偏差数据时，一并报告评判器以判定token开头的比率，该指标仅需一次前向传播且无需标签。

Abstract: Reading an LLM judge's verdict from the logits of its first generated token is cheap, requires no generation, and is exactly what constrained decoding and likelihood-scoring evaluation harnesses produce. We show that this readout distorts position bias in one direction: it overstates it in every condition we test, so figures obtained this way behave as upper bounds. The mechanism is that judges do not always lead with a verdict token, on 12% to 49% of pairs for three Qwen3 judges and under 3% for Llama-3.1-8B and Phi-3.5-mini, and forcing a read on those pairs returns whichever response was shown first rather than a judgment. Pooled over the 924 pairs where a judge did not commit, the forced read flips on 89.7% of them when the responses are swapped, against 47.5% read after generation (paired difference +0.422, 95% CI [+0.365, +0.467]). The distortion is specific to what is measured: it moves position bias by 42 points while moving judge accuracy by under one point in seven of ten conditions, so it misleads whoever audits a judge rather than whoever uses one. A second, smaller failure occurs even when the judge does lead with a verdict token, since it sometimes opens with one letter and reasons its way to the other, on 0 to 5.5% of pairs at a rate uncorrelated with compliance. We recommend reporting the rate at which a judge leads with a verdict token, which costs one forward pass and no labels, alongside any position-bias figure.

</details>


### [85] [Measuring Human-Like Bias in LLMs? A Critique of Human-Derived Bias Constructs in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00070)
*Antonela Tommasel,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判性地审视了将人类偏见构念（如内隐偏见、锚定效应等）迁移到LLM评估中所产生的"推断鸿沟"，并提出了一个关联构念、操作化、推断范围与人类类比限度的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地借用源自人类的偏见构念来研究大语言模型，这类方法可作为直接偏见探测的替代方案，尤其在直接提问可能掩盖偏见或模型行为看似符合规范时。然而，把人类心理测量工具迁移到LLM上存在推断上的错位，可能导致对模型偏见的解读混淆。

Method: 论文采用批判性分析的方法，剖析人类中心偏见评估在LLM中的推断鸿沟来源，指出其源于三方面错配：人类衍生构念本身、人与模型的差异、以及评估情境。在此基础上提出一个分析框架，用以将上述要素与合理的推断解释相关联。

Result: 研究识别出推断鸿沟的三大来源（人类衍生构念的错配、人—模型差异、评估情境差异），并构建了一个关注目标构念、操作化方式、推断范围及人类类比限度的分析框架。

Conclusion: 将人类偏见构念应用于LLM评估时必须谨慎，需明确目标构念、操作化、推断范围与人类类比的边界，框架为获得有依据的模型偏见解释提供了分析视角。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use human-derived bias constructs to study Large Language Models (LLMs), including social-cognitive constructs such as implicit bias and stereotype activation, and cognitive biases such as anchoring, framing effects, and confirmation bias. Such approaches offer alternatives to overt bias probes, particularly when direct questioning may obscure bias or when model behaviour appears normatively acceptable. However, adapting human bias constructs to LLMs introduces an inferential gap. Psychological instruments were developed to study human cognition and social behaviour, whereas LLM evaluations rely on probabilities, text completions, rankings, or simulated decisions. This paper critiques human-centered bias evaluation in LLMs. We show how this gap arises from mismatches pertaining to human-derived constructs, human-model differences, and evaluation contexts, which can blur distinct interpretations of model bias. We then introduce a framework providing an analytical lens for relating these elements to warranted interpretations, with attention to target constructs, operationalizations, scope of inference, and limits of human analogy.

</details>


### [86] [Legal text classification in Korean sexual offense cases: from traditional machine learning to large language models with XAI insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00087)
*Jeongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了从传统机器学习到大型语言模型的韩国性犯罪判例法律文本分类方法，发现经法律数据微调的小规模模型（KLUE-BERT，准确率99.3%）优于GPT-3.5、GPT-4.0及传统机器学习，并借助可解释AI分析预测与误分类案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本复杂、类别间差异细微，准确分类法律文档仍具挑战性。研究旨在比较不同规模与类型的模型在法律文本分类中的表现，并探讨领域适应、微调与模型规模孰更重要，同时关注模型的可解释性。

Method: 使用十类韩国性犯罪判例数据，评估从传统机器学习技术到大型语言模型（LLM）的多种法律文本分类模型；对KLUE-BERT等小规模模型在法律数据上进行微调；采用可解释AI（XAI）技术分析模型预测及误分类案例；并使用贴近真实法律案卷的KICS数据评估模型的泛化能力。

Result: 经法律数据微调的KLUE-BERT取得最高准确率99.3%，优于GPT-3.5、GPT-4.0以及传统机器学习模型。XAI分析识别出影响模型决策的语言特征，并揭示了模型在捕捉细微文本线索方面的局限。在KICS数据上，模型泛化能力不足，难以解读隐含的上下文线索。

Conclusion: 对法律文档分类而言，领域适应与微调可能比模型规模更为重要。性能与可解释性在法律AI中同等关键，XAI有助于提升法律文本分类的透明度。此类AI辅助工具可支持法律专业人员的文档分类、法律信息检索与案件评估等工作。

Abstract: The advancement of natural language processing (NLP) has expanded AI-based text classification in the legal domain. However, accurately classifying legal documents remains challenging due to the complexity of legal texts and subtle differences between legal categories. This study evaluates legal text classification models ranging from traditional machine learning techniques to large language models (LLMs) using ten categories of Korean sexual offense precedents. The results show that fine-tuning small-scale models such as KLUE-BERT on legal data outperforms general-purpose models such as GPT-3.5 and GPT-4.0, as well as traditional machine learning models. KLUE-BERT achieved the highest accuracy of 99.3%, indicating that domain adaptation and fine-tuning can be more important than model size for legal document classification. We further employ explainable AI (XAI) techniques to analyze model predictions and misclassification cases. XAI analysis identifies linguistic features influencing model decisions and limitations in capturing subtle textual cues. Using KICS data, which closely resembles real-world legal case records, we further evaluate the model's generalization capabilities and find that it struggles to interpret implicit contextual cues. These findings highlight the importance of both performance and interpretability in legal AI and demonstrate how XAI can improve transparency in legal text classification. AI-assisted tools can support legal professionals in tasks including document classification, legal information retrieval, and case assessment.

</details>


### [87] [BudgetSchemaBench: A Budget-Swept Diagnostic for Schema Context in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00092)
*Chen Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 BudgetSchemaBench，用于诊断结构化数据智能体在上下文预算约束下的模式（schema）表示与检索权衡问题，发现覆盖受限时执行准确率对预算比对序列化形式更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据库目录包含数千列、跨多个数据库，模式难以完整放入模型上下文窗口。在上下文填满之前，成本约束就迫使系统在"表覆盖范围"与"序列化细节"之间做取舍，但缺乏可执行的诊断基准来衡量这一权衡。

Method: 构建 BudgetSchemaBench：以 gold SQL 机械地推导相关性标签，无需人工或 LLM 标注真值。使用 80 个数据库的合并目录，扫描 4 档模式上下文预算，在固定各检索器表排序的前提下比较 3 种表示。设置源命名空间检查以拒绝"结果正确但来自错误数据库"的查询。评估包含三种条件：端到端检索、frozen-gold（保证所需表存在）、以及移除所需表的探针。共 1,279 道留出问题。

Result: 原始序列化下，预算由目录的 2.5% 提升到 50%，词法检索执行准确率提升 18 个百分点，稠密检索仅提升 3 个百分点（稠密检索在最小预算下已找到大部分所需表）。移除所需表后，94.6% 的正确预测恰好点名其中一张表，符合从参数化知识重建缺失模式的现象。frozen-gold 条件下两个主求解器在三种表示间差异不超过 2 个百分点，最宽的配对 95% 置信区间在 ±4 点内；另一家族的推理模型呈相同定性模式。

Conclusion: 当检索受覆盖范围限制时，执行准确率对模式预算的敏感度高于对被测序列化形式的敏感度。诊断工具与构建、评估代码已公开。

Abstract: Data agents over structured sources must fit database schema into the model's context window. Large catalogs can span many databases and thousands of columns, so cost constraints may require choosing between table coverage and serialization detail well before the context window is full. We introduce BudgetSchemaBench, an execution-grounded diagnostic for this setting. Its construction derives relevance labels mechanically from gold SQL, without human- or LLM-authored ground truth. Using a pooled 80-database catalog, we sweep four schema-context budgets and compare three representations while keeping each retriever's table ranking fixed. A source-namespace check rejects queries that obtain the correct result from the wrong database. The evaluation covers three conditions: end-to-end retrieval; frozen-gold, in which the required tables are guaranteed; and a probe that removes those tables. For the primary solver with raw serialization, raising the budget from 2.5% to 50% of the catalog improves execution accuracy on 1,279 held-out questions by 18 percentage points under lexical retrieval but only 3 under dense retrieval; the dense retriever already finds most required tables at the smallest budget. When the required tables are removed, 94.6% of correct predictions name one of them exactly, consistent with reconstruction of absent schema from parametric knowledge. For the two main solvers in the frozen-gold condition, the three representations differ by at most 2 percentage points, and the widest paired 95% confidence interval bounds the difference within +/-4 points. We observe the same qualitative patterns with one reasoning model from a different family. When retrieval is coverage-limited, execution accuracy is more sensitive to the schema budget than to the tested serializations. The diagnostic and the code used to construct and evaluate it are publicly available.

</details>


### [88] [FACET at WMT 2026 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00096)
*Ahrii Kim,Chanjun Park,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FACET 是一个无参考的机器翻译质量评估系统（WMT26 提交），将评估拆解为流畅度、准确性和一致性三轮，每轮只提供其错误类型所需的上下文，用同一固定模型三次提示，无训练组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同翻译错误类型需要不同证据：意义是否保留只能对照源文判断，而译文是否通顺、实体命名是否一致仅凭译文即可判断。现有评估往往统一喂入相同上下文，未针对错误类型区分所需信息。

Method: 采用分解式无参考评估框架，分为 Fluency、Accuracy、Consistency 三个 pass，各自只给所需上下文（准确性 pass 用源文+译文，流畅度与一致性 pass 仅用译文）。使用单一固定模型提示三次，合并错误跨度后输出三类任务结果：错误跨度、质量分数、无错误标签，全程无训练成分。另提交 FACET-C，去掉一致性 pass。

Result: 在无金标条件下刻画了 FACET 的预测行为：其系统排名将人工译后编辑译文排在首位；一致性 pass 改变了约十分之一的片段分数，但几乎不改变整体排名。

Conclusion: 按错误类型分配所需上下文的分解式提示是无参考翻译质量评估的有效且免训练方案；一致性维度对分数有局部影响但对系统排序影响很小。

Abstract: Different error types in machine translation require different evidence. Whether meaning is preserved can be judged only against the source, while whether the target is well-formed, or whether it names one entity consistently, can be judged from the target alone. We present FACET, our reference-free submission to the WMT26 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Task, which decomposes evaluation into Fluency, Accuracy, and Consistency passes and gives each pass only the context its error type requires. A single fixed model is prompted three times, and the merged error spans yield the three task outputs, error spans, quality scores, and error-free labels, with no trained components. We also submit FACET-C, which omits the Consistency pass. Without gold labels, we characterize the predictions of FACET. Its system rankings place post-edited human translation first, and the Consistency pass changes about a tenth of segment scores while leaving the ranking nearly unchanged.

</details>


### [89] [Metric-Construction Coupling Inflates Measured Synthetic Dialect Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00164)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出KoDialectBench基准，研究合成数据在多大程度上能替代不可再分发的韩语方言语料监督，并指出当合成与评测共享构建资源时会显著高估恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 韩语方言语料虽存在但无法再分发：可发布模型权重，却无法从原始数据复现训练与评测。作者因此关注两个问题：合成数据能恢复多少这种监督信号，以及这种恢复能否脱离合成流程被独立测量。

Method: 作者构建了KoDialectBench，包含覆盖五个地区、三个任务维度的1,000个条目，仅以标识符哈希和评分代码发布，用户需用自己合法授权的语料重建条目。方法上设置不同合成数据配置进行对照，并设计"构造不相交"实验：从转换规则中扣除20%的标记类型，在完全匹配的训练规模（8,600例）下检验构建资源访问的影响。此外通过评测器扫描定义指标-构造成覆盖率（MCC），考察MCC从0到1变化时的恢复测量结果。

Result: 恢复效果高度依赖任务维度：最佳合成方案在地区识别上达到真实数据增益的91.2%，但在理解任务上仅为63.7%。生成任务结果依赖指标：部署的标记词表在方言度上报告92.3%、地区匹配上119.3%（超过真实数据参考），而基于参考的生成仅为72.4%。作者发现标记指标的评分清单完全被转换规则可生成的清单所包含。在构造不相交实验中，扣留20%标记类型使方言度恢复从91.8%降至8.1%，地区匹配恢复从101.9%降至25.6%，而三项与流程无关的测量均未下降。评测器扫描显示，当MCC从0升至1时，测得的方言度恢复单调上升。

Conclusion: 合成数据恢复能力因任务维度差异显著，且在生成任务上高度依赖评测指标。当合成构建与评测共享资源清单时，会系统性夸大合成数据恢复效果的估计，因此需引入流程无关的独立测量指标（如MCC）以避免虚高。

Abstract: Korean dialect corpora are available but not redistributable: weights may be released, while reproducing training and evaluation from the underlying data cannot be. We ask how much of that supervision synthetic data recovers, and whether that recovery can be measured independently of the synthesis pipeline. We contribute KoDialectBench, 1,000 items across five regions on three axes, released as identifier hashes and scoring code so users reconstruct the items from their own licensed copy. Recovery is strongly axis-dependent: our best synthetic arm reaches 91.2% of the real-data gain on region identification but 63.7% on comprehension. On generation the answer depends on the metric: the deployed marker lexicon reports 92.3% on dialectness and 119.3% on region match, the latter exceeding the real-data reference, whereas reference-based generation reaches 72.4%. We find the marker metrics' scoring inventory is entirely contained in the inventory our transformation rules can emit. We test the effect of construction access directly with an exact-form construction-disjoint arm that withholds 20% of marker types from the rules. At exactly matched training size (8,600 examples) it reduces dialectness recovery from 91.8% to 8.1% and region-match recovery from 101.9% to 25.6% on the held-out marker inventory, while the three pipeline-independent measurements do not fall at all. A complementary evaluator sweep defines metric-construction coverage (MCC) and finds measured dialectness recovery increasing monotonically as overlap rises from MCC=0 to MCC=1. Shared construction and evaluation inventories can therefore substantially inflate estimates of synthetic-data recovery.

</details>


### [90] [When a Data Artifact Isn't a Shortcut: Causal Auditing of Synthetic RLVR Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00202)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计RLVR训练数据中"正确选项为真实语料、干扰项为合成文本"的溯源混淆问题，发现虽存在可检测的表面统计信号，但在固定预算下策略并未利用该伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期一些RLVR流程通过遮蔽真实语料片段并让语言模型生成看似合理的错误答案来构建训练数据，导致正确选项是真实人类文本、而所有干扰项都是合成文本。正确性与来源（provenance）因此纠缠在一起，策略原则上可能学到"来源"而非"正确性"，作者希望审计这一潜在风险。

Method: 在GooseReason-0.7M数据集上，(1) 用仅读取五个表面统计特征（不看语义）的分类器检验两类样本是否存在可区分信号，覆盖315,499个选项，并分领域检查；(2) 进行干预实验：构建释义匹配的对照语料，在相同训练规模与固定预算下分别训练两个策略，比较"利用差距"。

Result: 表面统计分类器整体AUROC仅0.562，接近随机；但代码领域为0.416（低于随机），人工检查发现代码干扰项实为金答案的单算子变异，两类几乎同构。干预实验中，未修改数据臂的利用差距为0.021，对照臂为0.027，未修改数据臂并未占优。

Conclusion: 尽管数据中存在可检测的伪影，但在给定训练预算下该伪影并未被策略利用，呈现出"可检测"与"被利用"之间的分离；这一分离及领域特定的构造发现对同类语料构建者有参考价值，作者并发布了以CPU为主、轻量化的审计协议。

Abstract: Several recent pipelines build RLVR training data by masking a span of real corpus text and asking a language model to invent plausible wrong answers around it. The correct option is therefore genuine human prose; every distractor is synthetic. Correctness and provenance become entangled, and a policy could in principle learn the second instead of the first. We audit that possibility in GooseReason-0.7M. First we ask whether the asymmetry is visible at all: a classifier reading only five surface statistics (never the meaning) reaches AUROC 0.562 over 315,499 options, barely above chance. The aggregate hides something, though. Code sits at 0.416, below chance, and manual inspection explains why: code distractors turn out to be single-operator mutations of the gold answer rather than freely written alternatives, so the two classes are nearly identical by construction. Detecting a signal is not the same as showing a model uses it, so we then run an intervention. We build a paraphrase-matched control corpus, hold training-set size identical across arms, and train two policies under one fixed budget. The exploitation gap does not favour the unmodified-data arm: 0.021 against 0.027 for the control. Under our budget, in other words, a detectable artifact went unexploited. We think that dissociation, along with the domain-specific construction finding, is worth knowing for anyone curating corpora of this kind, and we release the audit as a mostly CPU-only protocol.

</details>


### [91] [A Holistic Assessment of the Carbon Footprint of Noor, a Very Large Arabic Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00223)
*Imad Lakim,Ebtesam Almazrouei,Ibrahim Abu Alhaol,Merouane Debbah,Julien Launay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对极端规模语言模型 Noor（面向阿拉伯语的多任务模型，参数量最高 13B）进行全生命周期碳足迹评估，涵盖数据收集与存储、研发、预训练、未来推理服务及国际合作所需的外生成本，发现推理成本与外生因素对总碳预算影响显著，并探讨了降低碳足迹的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型规模不断增大并日益普及，其环境代价受到关注：超大模型算力需求高、碳足迹显著。现有论文的碳影响报告通常仅限训练阶段所用算力资源，缺乏对项目整体（含数据、研发、推理服务及外生因素）的完整评估，因此需要更全面的碳足迹核算方法。

Method: 以正在推进的极端规模阿拉伯语多任务语言模型项目 Noor（最高 13B 参数，依靠零样本泛化通过自然语言指令完成多种下游任务）为对象，开展整体碳足迹评估。核算范围从数据收集与存储成本开始，包含研究与开发预算、预训练成本、未来服务（推理）估算，以及该项国际合作所需的其他外生成本。

Result: 评估发现，推理成本和外生因素可能对项目总碳预算产生显著影响，仅统计训练算力会明显低估整体碳足迹。

Conclusion: 对极端规模语言模型应进行覆盖全项目生命周期的整体碳足迹评估，而非仅报告训练算力；论文进一步讨论了减少此类模型碳足迹的可能路径。

Abstract: As ever larger language models grow more ubiquitous, it is crucial to consider their environmental impact. Characterised by extreme size and resource use, recent generations of models have been criticised for their voracious appetite for compute, and thus significant carbon footprint. Although reporting of carbon impact has grown more common in machine learning papers, this reporting is usually limited to compute resources used strictly for training. In this work, we propose a holistic assessment of the footprint of an extreme-scale language model, Noor. Noor is an ongoing project aiming to develop the largest multi-task Arabic language models -- with up to 13B parameters -- leveraging zero-shot generalisation to enable a wide range of downstream tasks via natural language instructions. We assess the total carbon bill of the entire project: starting with data collection and storage costs, including research and development budgets, pretraining costs, future serving estimates, and other exogenous costs necessary for this international cooperation. Notably, we find that inference costs and exogenous factors can have a significant impact on total budget. Finally, we discuss pathways to reduce the carbon footprint of extreme-scale models.

</details>


### [92] [CAVE-Mem: Boundary-Aware Experience Validation for Memory Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00238)
*Xinyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 CAVE-Mem：将经验表示为带适用性、边界与效用条件的类型化干预算子，训练免费，仅当算子匹配当前条件且效用验证通过时才改变基础答案，否则弃权，在长期记忆、多跳问答与长文档叙事推理上稳定优于仅按相关性复用经验的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆智能体依赖迭代检索与可复用经验来回答大规模个人、事实或叙事历史问题。但现有经验记忆系统主要优化"相关性"：检索与当前状态相似的过往检索经验并注入提示。问题在于，当记忆基质、问题意图、答案粒度或证据边界发生变化时，看似相关的经验可能反而有害。

Method: CAVE-Mem 是一个免训练框架，将经验表示为类型化的干预算子（typed intervention operator），并为其配备适用性、边界与效用三类条件。流程为：先得到基础记忆检索答案，随后只有当算子与当前记忆基质、答案契约、证据边界匹配，并通过交叉拟合（cross-fitted）效用检验时，才允许该算子改动基础答案；否则系统弃权（abstain），不做干预。

Result: 在长期对话记忆、多跳问答、长文档叙事推理三类任务上，CAVE-Mem 相比仅基于相关性的经验复用方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 经验复用不应仅以相关性为标准，而应显式建模经验生效的适用条件与效用；只有在条件匹配且效用可验证时才施加干预，否则弃权，可有效避免有害经验注入，提升长期记忆智能体的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-term memory agents increasingly rely on it- erative search and reusable experience to answer questions over large personal, factual, or narrative histories. However, current experience-memory systems largely optimize relevance: they re- trieve past search lessons that appear similar to the current state and inject them into the prompt. A relevant experience can still be harmful when the memory substrate, question intent, answer granularity, or evidence boundary changes. We propose CAVE- Mem, a training-free framework that represents experience as a typed intervention operator with applicability, boundary, and utility conditions. CAVE-Mem first obtains a base memory-search answer, then allows an operator to change it only if the oper- ator matches the current substrate, answer contract, evidence boundary, and cross-fitted utility; otherwise the system abstains. Experiments across long-term conversational memory, multi-hop question answering, and long-document narrative reasoning show consistent gains over relevance-only experience reuse.

</details>


### [93] [Signed Lexical Confidence for Risk-Calibrated Intent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00262)
*Yezhou Cheng,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种带符号词汇门控方法，将句分类器logit margin与稀疏词汇模型证据结合，用于风险校准的意图路由，在不改变基分类器预测的前提下提升置信度可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性意图路由需要助手在可靠预测时行动、在不确定请求时延迟处理。标准置信度主要反映基模型自身表示，未能利用互补证据，因此需要一种不改变模型决策的置信度增强方法。

Method: 引入带符号词汇门控：将句子分类器的logit margin与稀疏词汇模型对预测意图的支持度结合，对词汇一致给予正证据、对词汇偏好的竞争意图给予负证据；再通过独立的二项校准阶段为指定风险目标选择操作阈值。

Result: 在BANKING77、CLINC150和HWU64各十次运行中，风险-覆盖率曲线下面积相对仅语义的学习门控分别降低15.8%、15.1%和11.8%；5%错误目标下BANKING77和HWU64接受覆盖率提升1.83和5.14个百分点；2%严格目标下30组数据集-运行组合均得到非空策略；对照实验显示该特征在错误排序上优于无符号词汇置信度。

Conclusion: 该双特征门控为风险校准的意图路由提供了紧凑、可解释的置信度增强，同时保留基分类器的预测结果。

Abstract: Selective intent routing allows an assistant to act on reliable predictions while deferring uncertain requests. Standard confidence scores primarily reflect the base model's representation, leaving an opportunity to incorporate complementary evidence without changing its decisions. We introduce a signed lexical gate that combines a sentence classifier's logit margin with a sparse lexical model's support for the classifier's predicted intent. By assigning positive evidence to lexical agreement and negative evidence to a lexically favored competing intent, the gate retains more information than either unsigned lexical confidence or a hard agreement rule. An independent binomial calibration stage selects an operating threshold for a specified risk target. Across ten runs on BANKING77, CLINC150, and HWU64, the proposed score reduces area under the risk-coverage curve by 15.8%, 15.1%, and 11.8% relative to a learned semantic-only gate. At a nominal 5% error target, it increases accepted coverage by 1.83 and 5.14 percentage points on BANKING77 and HWU64, while CLINC150 is already near full coverage. At a stricter 2% target, the simultaneous binomial procedure yields a nonempty policy in all 30 dataset-run combinations at the available calibration budgets. Matched controls show that the proposed feature improves average error ranking over the tested unsigned lexical-confidence feature, with dataset-dependent gains over binary agreement. The resulting two-feature gate provides a compact, interpretable confidence enhancement for risk-calibrated intent routing while preserving the base classifier's predictions.

</details>


### [94] [Certainty Is Not Just Correctness: Rethinking Token-Level Certainty in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00296)
*Yunfan Zhou,Ye Zhu,Zhihai Wang,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统评估了LLM中token级确定性（certainty）作为正确性代理的预测能力，发现确定性更能识别"模型可能答对的问题"而非"同一问题下的正确回答"，且信息分布随生成位置和token类型系统变化，并据此设计了高效的test-time compute分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性分数在LLM训练与推理中被广泛当作正确性的代理指标，但其性能既取决于分数本身的信息量，也取决于这些分数被如何使用。现有工作缺乏对确定性预测能力的直接、受控评估，因此作者希望厘清确定性到底能预测什么、在什么条件下有效。

Method: 作者在多个模型和任务上开展受控实证评估，区分两类预测目标：(1) 识别模型更可能答对的问题；(2) 区分同一问题下的正确与错误回答。同时分析确定性在不同token类型、词内位置上的系统性变化，以及问题难度信息与答案正确性信息在生成过程中的时间分布。

Result: 实验显示：确定性在识别"模型可能答对的问题"上普遍优于区分"同一问题下的对错回答"；确定性随token类型和词内位置系统性变化，反映词与文本形式的局部属性；问题难度信息在生成早期即出现，而较弱的答案正确性信息更集中在生成末期。据此在test-time compute中应用：早期用确定性分配回答数量，末期用确定性加权投票，相比固定采样多数投票基线，准确率从78.71%提升至79.54%，生成token成本降低82.4%。

Conclusion: 确定性所提供的信息对决策的价值取决于预测目标、模型、确定性度量指标以及聚合时纳入的token位置。理解这些依赖关系可以显著提升推理效率与准确率，为基于确定性的自适应推理策略提供指导。

Abstract: Token-level certainty is widely used as a proxy for correctness in LLM training and inference. However, the performance of certainty-based methods depends both on the information in certainty scores and on how those scores are used. We therefore directly assess certainty's predictive ability through controlled empirical evaluations across models and tasks. We distinguish two prediction targets: identifying questions a model is more likely to answer correctly and distinguishing correct from incorrect responses to the same question. In our experiments, certainty is generally better at identifying questions a model is likely to answer correctly than at distinguishing correct from incorrect responses to the same question. Certainty also varies systematically across token types and positions within words, reflecting local properties of words and text form. Information about question difficulty appears early in generation, while the weaker information about answer correctness is more concentrated near the end. These findings show that the information certainty provides for decisions depends on the prediction target, the model, the certainty metric, and which token positions in the response are included in aggregation. We further demonstrate the practical value of these findings for test-time compute. We allocate the number of responses using certainty early in generation and weight answer votes using certainty near the end of each response. Compared with a fixed-sampling majority-voting baseline, this approach increases overall accuracy from 78.71\% to 79.54\% while reducing generated-token cost by 82.4\%.

</details>


### [95] [DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding: Benchmarking Document Grounding and Hallucinations in Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00316)
*Puneet Mathur,Nedim Lipka,Zeyu Jin,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DSB-DG基准，评估语音智能体在外部文档中的接地能力，发现级联架构接地准确率最高，全双工与开源系统存在上下文容量崩塌与多轮接地衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音智能体虽能实现低延迟自然交互，但其在外部文档中忠实接地响应的能力尚未被充分探索，缺乏针对性的评测基准。

Method: 构建DSB-DG基准，覆盖五个专业领域、50份文档、1,636个经对抗性验证的问答对；针对三种失效模式评测：上下文饱和（随文档增长）、接地衰减（多轮对话中事实保持）、主动接地（上下文再注入是否缓解对话漂移）；支持接地准确率、幻觉与响应延迟的自动评估；测试系统涵盖级联式、商用全双工与实时、开源speech2speech架构。

Result: 不同架构的有效接地能力差异显著：级联流水线（ASR-LLM-TTS）接地准确率最高，Gemini-Live与GPT-Realtime紧随其后；开源系统表现出独特失效模式，尤其是上下文容量突然崩塌与多轮接地衰减；总体而言接地保真度随上下文与对话负载下降，失败多表现为无依据生成而非拒答。

Conclusion: 上下文接地是可靠全双工语音智能体面临的关键未解挑战。

Abstract: Voice agents enable low-latency, natural interaction, yet their ability to faithfully ground responses in external documents remains underexplored. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding (DSB-DG), a benchmark for evaluating document grounding in voice agents across five professional domains. DSB-DG targets three failure modes: Context Saturation, which measures grounding under increasing document length; Grounding Decay, which measures retention of document facts across multi-turn dialogue; and Proactive Grounding, which evaluates whether context re-injection mitigates conversational drift. The benchmark contains 1,636 adversarially verified QA pairs from 50 documents covering five professional domains, and supports fully automatic evaluation of grounding accuracy, hallucination, and response latency. Across systems spanning cascaded, proprietary full-duplex and real-time, and open-weight speech2speech architectures, we find substantial differences in effective grounding capacity. While cascaded pipeline (ASR-LLM-TTS) achieves the highest grounding accuracy, Gemini-Live and GPT-Realtime closely trail behind. Open-weight systems exhibit distinct failure modes, most notably an abrupt context-capacity collapse and multi-turn grounding decay. More broadly, grounding fidelity degrades with context and conversational load, and failures frequently manifest as unsupported generations rather than abstention. We show that contextual grounding as a key unresolved challenge for reliable full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [96] [Refusal Localizes, the Damage Relocates: Safety Layers Under Few-Sample Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00320)
*Jungseob Lee,Dongyub Jude Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Seongtae Hong,Seungyoon Lee,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文检验了基于"安全行为定位"的防御能否在攻击方式变化时依然有效，发现攻击者可通过让拒答行为迁移到冻结边界之上、分散更新方向等方式绕过多种防御，并提出五项针对自适应微调攻击的防御检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将LLM的安全拒答行为定位到特定层、方向与token，暗示可据此设计防御。但作者关心的是：这种"定位+修复"式防御能否在攻击策略改变时仍然存活，即其鲁棒性和可迁移性是否可靠。

Method: 在来自4个模型家族的6个检查点上，先用少量有害样本进行微调攻击，检验有害/良性提示在攻击后是否仍线性可分，并测试将干净隐状态注入被污染模型能否恢复拒答；随后在已有"层冻结"防御基础上，冻结到可复现的转变深度后重复攻击；另做第二项研究，在4个检查点上用仅注意力微调后移除更新的前两个奇异方向来恢复拒答，并在Llama-3.1-8B上测试普通训练干扰、攻击者分散更新以及基于良性微调校准的谱检测器的表现。

Result: 在100个有害样本下，6个检查点的拒答率全部接近零，恢复转变出现在冻结边界之上；移除前两个奇异方向的修复对短时仅注意力微调有效，但在Llama-3.1-8B上被普通训练削弱，且会被分散更新的攻击者击败；基于良性Llama微调校准的谱检测器在该检查点上漏检多数修复失败。

Conclusion: 攻击者可以绕过由"恢复"识别出的区域，并击败在多个检查点上有效的修复方法。局部化冻结在少数有害样本无意混入训练数据时仍有一定保护作用。作者据此提出针对自适应微调的防御应满足的五项检验。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts aligned large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks, but a few dozen harmful examples can remove their refusal of harmful requests. Prior work localizes safety-related behavior to specific layers, directions, and tokens, suggesting targets for protection. We test whether successful localization and recovery support defenses that survive changes in the attack. Across six checkpoints from four model families, harmful and benign prompts remain linearly separable after attack, and patching full clean hidden states into the compromised model restores refusal at a reproducible transition depth. Building on a prior layer-freezing defense, we freeze every layer up to this depth and repeat the attack. At a hundred harmful examples, refusal remains near zero on all six checkpoints, with recovery transitions above the frozen boundary. In a second study, removing the update's top two singular directions restores refusal after short attention-only fine-tunes on four checkpoints. On Llama-3.1-8B, ordinary training changes weaken this repair and an attacker who spreads the update defeats it. A spectral detector calibrated on benign Llama fine-tunes misses most repair failures on that checkpoint. Localized freezing can nevertheless help preserve refusal when a few harmful examples enter training data unintentionally. These results show that an attacker can bypass a region identified by recovery and defeat a repair that works across multiple checkpoints, motivating five checks for defenses against adaptive fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/refusal-relocates.

</details>


### [97] [CAST: Cost-Aware Speculative Trees from One-Pass Block Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00321)
*Jungseob Lee,Sugyeong Eo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAST 将块状草稿器已评分的候选打包为树，在单次目标模型前向中验证，树宽依据延迟测量自适应确定，无需扫描。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码用便宜草稿加并行验证加速 LLM 推理。块状草稿器一次前向可为整块未来 token 打分，但标准解码只验证得分最高的链，丢弃其余已评分候选，而这些候选的验证只增加目标计算、不增加草稿开销，存在被浪费的加速空间。

Method: 提出 CAST（Cost-Aware Speculative Trees）：把这些候选拼装成树，在单次目标前向中验证，不改动目标模型、草稿器权重与解码规则。树宽通过成本权衡决定——当新增候选带来的期望收益大于其增加的验证时间时继续加宽，宽度由一次延迟测量自适应于具体部署，无需遍历宽度。并在贪心与采样解码下证明目标输出分布不变。

Result: 在三个 GPU 代际、两个模型家族、五个领域上评测。按预测宽度，CAST 在全部八个设置中均快于标准链，最高提速 43%。最优宽度强依赖于部署：在验证成本因核边界跳变处，128-token 树仅比标准链快 2%，而预测宽度下的树快 20%。

Conclusion: CAST 利用块状草稿器被浪费的候选，通过成本感知的自适应树宽，在保持目标输出分布不变的前提下稳定优于标准链式验证，且宽度需按部署定制。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by drafting future tokens cheaply and verifying them with the target model in parallel. Block drafters score a whole block of future tokens in one forward pass, yet standard decoding verifies only the top-scoring chain and discards the other candidates. Because these candidates are already scored, verifying more of them adds target computation but no extra drafting. We introduce CAST (Cost-Aware Speculative Trees), which packs these candidates into a tree and verifies it in a single target pass, leaving the target model, drafter weights, and decoding rule untouched. To decide how wide the tree should be, CAST adds candidates while the expected gain from the next one outweighs the verification time it adds. The width therefore adapts to each deployment from a latency measurement, without sweeping over widths. We evaluate CAST across five domains on three GPU generations and two model families. At its predicted width, CAST is faster than the standard chain in all eight settings, by up to 43%. We also find that the best width depends strongly on the deployment. Where verification cost jumps at a kernel boundary, a 128-token tree is only 2% faster than the standard chain, whereas the tree at the predicted width is 20% faster. Furthermore, we prove that CAST leaves the target output distribution unchanged under both greedy and sampled decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/CAST.

</details>


### [98] [Dissonant ballerinas and crafty carrots: a comparative multi-modal analysis of Italian brain rot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00402)
*Anca Dinu,Andra-Maria Florescu,Marius Micluta-Campeanu,Stefana-Arina Tabusca,Claudiu Creanga,Andreiana Mihail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了意罗双语"brain rot"短视频多模态数据集CRIB，比较分析其跨语言文化差异与走红因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究意大利语与罗马尼亚语"brain rot"迷因的吸引因素及两种版本间的语言与文化差异，但此前缺乏针对性的多模态数据资源。

Method: 构建CRIB数据集：人工筛选240个TikTok视频，按语言（意大利语、罗马尼亚语）与流行度分层，提取文本、声学、视觉三类特征并做对比多模态分析。

Result: 流行度与文本要素（情感、荒诞性、押韵）及声学要素（声音特征、声音情感）无显著相关；罗马尼亚语中视频层面的节奏（更快剪辑速度、更快的整体节奏）是成功的有力预测因子。跨语言差异显著：意大利语文本更负面、困惑度更高、押韵更多，声音旋律范围与响度更高；罗马尼亚语音频频谱更亮、音高波动更无常。

Conclusion: "brain rot"内容的流行度更多由视频节奏等动态特征驱动而非文本或声学情感，且两种语言在文本与声学维度上呈现系统性文化差异。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative multi-modal analysis of Italian and Romanian brain rot memes, investigating the factors that contribute to its appeal and the linguistic and cultural distinctions between the two versions. To conduct this analysis, we introduce a multi-modal brain rot dataset named CRIB (Collection of Romanian and Italian Brain rot), a manually curated collection of 240 TikTok videos stratified by language (Italian, Romanian) and popularity, on which we examine textual, acoustic, and visual features. Our findings indicate that popularity is not significantly correlated with textual elements like sentiment, absurdity, or rhyme, or acoustic elements such as vocal features or sentiment of the sound. Instead, in Romanian language, video-level dynamics, specifically faster cutting speeds and a more rapid overall pace, are strong predictors of a video's success. The cross-linguistic analysis reveals significant differences. Italian brain rot is textually more negative, exhibits higher perplexity, and uses more rhyme, while its sound is characterized by higher melodic range and loudness. Romanian audio is spectrally brighter with more erratic pitch variations.

</details>


### [99] [LLM-as-a-Judge for Low-Resource Languages: Adapting Ragas and Comparative Ranking for Romanian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00406)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdminRo-Eval 数据集，研究"LLM-as-a-Judge"范式在罗马尼亚语 RAG 评估中的可行性，发现评估策略需因指标而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（LRLs）的 RAG 系统评估面临挑战，标准基于参考答案的指标效果不佳，缺乏可靠的自动化评估方案与基准数据。

Method: 适配 Ragas 框架，采用 Gemini 2.5 与 Gemini 3 等新一代模型；构建由母语者标注的罗马尼亚语行政文档数据集 AdminRo-Eval 作为真值；对比直接打分、比较排序、细粒度分解三种评估方法，覆盖 Faithfulness、Answer Relevance、Context Relevance 指标。

Result: 细粒度分解在 Faithfulness 上人类对齐度最高（Gemini 2.5 Pro 达 96%）；比较排序在 Answer Relevance 上表现最佳（90%）；轻量模型在低资源语言复杂推理上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 评估策略应针对具体指标选择；Gemini 2.5 Pro 架构为罗马尼亚语 RAG 自动评估建立了稳健、可迁移的基线。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems remains a challenge for Low-Resource Languages (LRLs), where standard reference-based metrics fall short. This paper investigates the viability of the "LLM-as-a-Judge" paradigm for Romanian by adapting the Ragas framework using next-generation models (Gemini 2.5 and Gemini 3). We introduce AdminRo-Eval, a curated dataset of Romanian administrative documents annotated by native speakers, to serve as a ground truth for benchmarking automated evaluators. We compare three evaluation methodologies - direct scoring, comparative ranking, and granular decomposition - across metrics for Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, and Context Relevance. Our findings reveal that evaluation strategies must be metric-specific: granular decomposition achieves the highest human alignment for Faithfulness (96% with Gemini 2.5 Pro), while comparative ranking outperforms in Answer Relevance (90%). Furthermore, we demonstrate that while lightweight models struggle with complex reasoning in LRLs, the Gemini 2.5 Pro architecture establishes a robust, transferable baseline for automated Romanian RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [100] [UniBuc at SemEval-2024 Task 2: Tailored Prompting with Solar for Clinical NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00408)
*Marius Micluta-Campeanu,Claudiu Creanga,Ana-Maria Bucur,Ana Sabina Uban,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniBuc团队在SemEval 2024 Task 2（临床试验安全生物医学自然语言推理）中，使用未微调的SOLAR Instruct模型，通过输入操控和定制化提示工程，在零样本和少样本设置下针对不同CTR章节定制提示，取得0.72一致性得分，排名第14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决临床试验中安全相关的生物医学自然语言推理问题，探索在不进行模型微调的情况下，能否通过输入操控和提示设计提升大语言模型在该任务上的表现，并希望揭示模型在语义保持变换下的推理缺陷。

Method: 采用SOLAR Instruct模型，不进行任何微调；核心方法为输入操控与定制化提示工程，针对临床试验报告（CTR）的不同章节分别设计提示，并在零样本和少样本两种设置下进行实验；随后进行了系统性错误分析以识别模型行为模式。

Result: 通过上述方法取得0.72的一致性得分，在排行榜中位列第14名。错误分析显示，模型倾向于走捷径、依赖简单启发式规则，尤其在面对语义保持型改动时表现明显。

Conclusion: 无需微调的大语言模型结合针对性提示设计可在生物医学NLI任务中取得有竞争力的结果，但模型仍存在依赖浅层启发式的局限，未来需改进其对语义等价变化的稳健性。

Abstract: This paper describes the approach of the UniBuc team in tackling the SemEval 2024 Task 2: Safe Biomedical Natural Language Inference for Clinical Trials. We used SOLAR Instruct, without any fine-tuning, while focusing on input manipulation and tailored prompting. By customizing prompts for individual CTR sections, in both zero-shot and few-shots settings, we managed to achieve a consistency score of 0.72, ranking 14th in the leaderboard. Our thorough error analysis revealed that our model has a tendency to take shortcuts and rely on simple heuristics, especially when dealing with semantic-preserving changes.

</details>


### [101] [EurekaBench: Measuring Agentic Ability to Discover New Scientific Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00492)
*Jiayi Geng,Zhengxuan Wu,Kevin S. Chen,Seungone Kim,Joseph Janssen,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Bhupalee Kalita,Runtian Gao,Aaron Ho,Andrew Oakleigh Nelson,Olexandr Isayev,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro,Ching-Yao Lai,Howard Chen,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 EurekaBench 跨领域基准，测试 AI 智能体能否通过长周期实验发现解释观测的机制，并发现当前智能体在预测精度上甚至超越人类，但在获得科学洞见上明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受牛顿通过迭代分析观测数据、建立数学方程并不断修正理论从而发现万有引力定律的启发，作者希望探究 AI 智能体是否也能做出类似的科学发现，并为此需要一个衡量该能力的评测标准。

Method: 构建 EurekaBench 跨领域基准，包含经专家验证的 26 个长周期任务，覆盖神经科学、计算机科学、化学、天体物理、地球物理和等离子体物理，共 306 条科学洞见。评测框架从三个维度考察科学发现能力：遵循已知科学约束、发现机制的预测准确性，以及机制能否产生科学洞见或指导未来研究。

Result: 当前 AI 智能体往往过度聚焦于预测精度的优化，在这一方面超过人类科学家，但在推导科学洞见方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 现有 AI 智能体尚不具备完整的科学发现能力，尤其在从机制中提炼科学洞见方面仍需改进；EurekaBench 为衡量迈向自主科学发现的进展提供了评测工具。

Abstract: When Isaac Newton discovered the law of gravitation, he did so through an iterative process of analyzing observed data such as planetary patterns, finding the underlying mechanisms by describing patterns in mathematical equations, and refining his theory against the Moon's orbit, revealing the startling insight that the same force governs both falling apples and orbiting planets. Would it be possible for AI agents to make similar discoveries? To measure this ability, we introduce EurekaBench, a cross-domain benchmark that tests AI agents' ability to conduct long-horizon experiments and discover mechanisms that explain observations. We evaluate these mechanisms by the scientific insights that can be derived from them. EurekaBench contains an expert-verified set of 26 long-horizon tasks across neuroscience, computer science, chemistry, astrophysics, geophysics, and plasma physics, with a total of 306 scientific insights that the discovered mechanisms are expected to support. Our evaluation framework tests three axes of scientific discovery: agents' ability to follow known scientific constraints, the predictive accuracy of the discovered mechanisms, and whether these mechanisms yield scientific insights or inform future research. Our results show that current AI agents often overly fixate on predictive accuracy optimization, surpassing human scientists, while falling substantially short in deriving scientific insights.

</details>


### [102] [Rules Amortize, Pairings Don't: Linguistic Structure Determines What Latent Task Representations Can Replace In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00526)
*Gunmay Jhingran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将few-shot上下文学习"摊销"为潜任务表征的方法：训练小型网络读取支持集几何（质心、主子空间、谱）并生成对查询残差流的输入条件加性更新，在冻结GPT-2上比较其与ICL在不同语言操作上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期理论表明静态向量等价于单个合成示例，无法处理高阶映射（如词级双射），因此作者提出语言学版本的追问：哪些语言操作可以从prompt中被摊销出来。

Method: 训练一个2.6M参数网络，读取few-shot支持集的几何结构（质心、主子空间、谱，一次性计算并缓存），在冻结的GPT-2-large/XL中间层对查询残差流产生输入条件加性更新；在八个屈折方向和一种词汇关系上，采用禁止方向间倒置对泄漏的规范划分进行评测，并设置错误任务流形、仅查询变体、留一任务迁移等控制实验。

Result: 三种情形：前向屈折（ICL强0.67-0.89、任务向量崩溃≤0.06）中变换在零样本成本下匹配ICL；词形还原方向（ICL弱0.13-0.48）中变换达0.78-0.92，最高超ICL 72个点（过去式→现在式0.85 vs 0.13）；任意配对（反义）中所有摊销器停留在ICL约一半。控制显示支持流形是因果必要的任务指纹：错误任务流形准确率≤0.06，仅查询变体无法区分共享输入空间的任务，留一任务迁移为零。

Conclusion: 可产出的规则（productive rules）能被摊销为潜任务表征，效果有时优于prompt传递；而记忆性配对无法摊销。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) can be amortized into latent objects (task vectors, function vectors, context vectors) that recover few-shot behavior at zero-shot inference cost, but recent theory shows a static vector acts as a single synthetic demonstration and must fail on high-rank mappings such as word-level bijections. We ask a linguistic version of this question: which linguistic operations can be amortized out of the prompt? We train a 2.6M-parameter network that reads the geometry of a few-shot support set (centroid, principal subspace, spectrum, computed once and cached) and produces an input-conditioned additive update to the query's residual stream at a mid-depth layer of a frozen GPT-2-large/XL. Across eight inflectional directions and one lexical relation, under a canonical split that bars inverted-pair leakage between directions, three regimes emerge. On forward inflection, where 10-shot ICL is strong (0.67-0.89) and extracted task vectors collapse (<=0.06), the transform matches ICL at strictly zero-shot per-query cost. On lemmatization directions, which frozen GPT-2 can execute but 10 demonstrations systematically fail to convey (ICL 0.13-0.48 at 1.5B), the transform is not capped by ICL at all: it reaches 0.78-0.92, up to +72 points over ICL (past to present: 0.85 vs. 0.13). On arbitrary pairings (antonymy) every amortizer plateaus near half of ICL at every scale, capacity, and seed tested. Controls show the support manifold acts as a causally necessary task fingerprint: wrong-task manifolds collapse accuracy to <=0.06, query-only variants cannot disambiguate tasks sharing an input space, and leave-one-task-out transfer is zero. Productive rules amortize into latent task representations, sometimes better than prompting can convey them; memorized pairings do not.

</details>


### [103] [Assessing the Impact of Language Disparity on Multilingual Linguistic Ability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00540)
*Zhanyu Chen,Jaap Jumelet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过多范式评测揭示多语言语言模型语法能力的测量依赖性，指出后训练会削弱语法能力且低资源语言受损最重，主张采用语言感知的多范式评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于多语言模型语法能力的结论因评测方式不同而差异巨大，但评测范式、后训练与语言资源丰富程度三者之间的交互作用尚未被系统性考察。作者希望厘清这些因素如何共同影响语法能力的测量结果。

Method: 研究以 MultiBLiMP 这一覆盖 101 种语言的句法最小对立对基准为基础，选取来自六个模型族的基础模型与后训练模型，采用四种评测方法进行对比评估。

Result: 主要有三点发现：其一，后训练会降低语法能力，该效应随模型规模增大而部分缓解，但低资源语言承担最高代价；其二，后训练模型仍保留无法通过显式提示表达出的语法知识，不过这一现象仅在高资源语言中可被测量，因为低资源环境下接近随机的基线几乎没有可隐藏的知识；其三，母语提示能够恢复低资源语言中原本隐藏的能力，说明只有高资源语言可以直接从无提示的概率分布中探测。

Conclusion: 多语言语法评测必须采用语言感知的、多范式结合的方法，才能避免系统性地低估低资源语言的能力。

Abstract: Claims about the grammatical competence of multilingual language models vary sharply with how competence is measured, yet the interaction between evaluation paradigm, post-training, and language resource availability has not been systematically examined. We evaluate base and post-trained models from six families on MultiBLiMP, a syntactic minimal-pair benchmark covering 101 languages, using four evaluation methods. We report three principal findings. First, post-training degrades grammatical competence, but the magnitude of this effect is reduced unevenly by model scale, while low-resource languages bear the highest cost. Second, post-trained models retain grammatical knowledge they cannot articulate through explicit prompting, yet this is measurable only in high-resource languages, because near-chance baselines in low-resource settings leave little knowledge to hide. Third, native-language prompting recovers otherwise hidden competence on low-resource languages, demonstrating that only high-resource languages can be probed directly from unprompted probabilities. We conclude that multilingual grammatical evaluation must adopt language-informed, multi-paradigm protocols to avoid systematically underestimating low-resource abilities.

</details>


### [104] [Can LLMs Reason Over Long Horizons? An Empirical Evaluation of Context Strategies for Longitudinal Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00562)
*Taye Akinrele,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Subash Neupane,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究比较五种上下文策略对LLM纵向临床推理的影响，发现证据选择与呈现方式比上下文长度更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向临床推理要求LLM从冗长病史中识别并整合分散的相关证据。尽管长上下文模型可处理更多信息，但提供更多病史未必使相关证据更易获取或提升推理质量，因此需探究不同上下文组织策略的实际效果。

Method: 在MedLoCoMo数据集上，针对四种开源权重LLM，比较五种上下文策略（Full、Recent、Episodic、Semantic、Hybrid），评估答案正确性、对查询-证据距离的稳健性，以及面对无支持前提问题时的弃答表现。

Result: Episodic与Hybrid总体准确率最强；Recent Context随支持证据距离增加退化最严重，而Episodic与Hybrid在长距离下保持最高准确率。对抗性问题分析显示，在可回答问题上的强表现未必转化为对无支持问题成功弃答。

Conclusion: 可靠的纵向推理不仅取决于LLM能访问多少病史，更关键取决于相关证据如何被选择与呈现。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical reasoning requires large language models (LLMs) to identify and integrate relevant evidence distributed across extended patient histories. Although long-context models can process increasingly large amounts of information, providing more history does not necessarily make relevant evidence more accessible or improve reasoning. We compare five context strategies (Full, Recent, Episodic, Semantic, and Hybrid) on MedLoCoMo across four open-weight LLMs, examining answer correctness, robustness to query-evidence distance, and abstention on questions with unsupported premises. Episodic and Hybrid generally achieve the strongest overall accuracy, while Recent Context degrades most as supporting evidence becomes more distant; Episodic and Hybrid maintain the highest accuracy at long distances. Analysis of adversarial questions further shows that strong performance on answerable questions does not necessarily translate to successful abstention when the available history does not support the requested conclusion.
