These findings show that reliable longitudinal reasoning depends not only on how much history an LLM can access, but critically on how relevant evidence is selected and presented for reasoning.

</details>


### [105] [Emergent Unfaithfulness: How Alignment Training Causes Language Models to Silently Override Task Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00568)
*Pardis Sadat Zahraei,Janvijay Singh,Gokhan Tur,Dilek Hakkani-Tur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了对齐训练引入的新失效模式"对齐诱发的不忠实"(AIU)，并提出 FaithConflict 数据集与双重分类体系加以量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注"能力—对齐"和"能力—忠实性"两组权衡，但"对齐—忠实性"冲突尚未被充分探讨。作者认为对齐模型在遇不安全或敏感内容时会系统性地偏离输入而不披露改动，这一现象需被单独定义与测量。

Method: 提出 AIU 概念以区别于能力驱动的不忠实；构建受控数据集 FaithConflict，可同时隔离两类冲突；建立行为层面(B1-B8)与思维链推理层面(C0-C6)两套互补分类体系；跨模型规模与中间检查点进行评测，并测试提示式缓解手段。

Result: AIU 随模型规模增大而加剧，且增速快于能力驱动的不忠实，呈现"反向缩放律"；中间检查点表明该现象在后训练阶段被放大，DPO 阶段差距增长最快同时最不显性；基于提示的缓解方法无法解决该问题。

Conclusion: LLM 设计与评测中存在"能力—对齐—忠实性"三难困境，忠实性应作为独立维度纳入评估。

Abstract: Large language models are characterized by three key properties: capability, alignment, and faithfulness. Prior work studies the tradeoffs between capability and alignment, and between capability and faithfulness, but a third tension remains underexplored: the alignment-faithfulness conflict. We show that aligned models systematically deviate from their inputs on unsafe or sensitive content without disclosing the modification, a failure mode we call alignment-induced unfaithfulness (AIU). Unlike capability-driven unfaithfulness, which comes from errors in knowledge or reasoning, this is induced by post-training mechanisms that override adherence to the input. We introduce FaithConflict, a controlled dataset isolating both conflicts, and two complementary taxonomies: behavioral (B1-B8) and chain-of-thought reasoning (C0-C6). Across models, AIU increases with scale and more sharply than capability-driven unfaithfulness, a reverse scaling law; intermediate checkpoints show it is amplified during post-training, with DPO the stage at which the gap both grows most and becomes least visible. Prompting-based mitigation does not resolve it, revealing a capability-alignment-faithfulness trilemma in the design and evaluation of LLMs.

</details>


### [106] [Where's Waldo? Query-language Preference under Cross-lingual Knowledge Disparities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00606)
*Dayeon Ki,Ruochen Zhang,Silviu Cucerzan,Ryen W. White,Ning Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Waldo 多语言 QA 基准，用于研究跨语言知识差异下的查询语言偏好，发现知识冲突时模型强烈偏向查询语言文档，并提出两种缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于查询语言偏好（模型倾向使用查询语言来源）的研究多在同等多语言知识可得的情境下进行。当不同语言来源对同一事实提供不完整或相互矛盾的信息时，这种偏好会产生实际影响，因为用户获得的信息取决于模型选择的来源。

Method: 构建 Waldo：基于 Wikipedia 的多语言问答基准，含 12K 问答对，覆盖两类情形——知识缺口（某语言有该事实而另一语言缺失）与知识冲突（不同语言版本对同一事实叙述矛盾）。在五种语言上评估八个模型，并探索两种缓解方法：消融与查询语言偏好相关的注意力头的机制干预，以及基于 LoRA 的训练。

Result: 当某语言版本仅缺失相关事实时，模型通常不论查询语言如何都会使用另一语言的证据；但在叙述冲突时，模型回答强烈倾向于与查询语言一致的文档，导致语义等价的查询因用户语言不同而得到不同叙述。LoRA 训练可将偏好差距最多缩小 61.5%。

Conclusion: 查询语言偏好仅在跨语言知识冲突时产生显著影响，可通过机制干预与 LoRA 微调加以缓解，LoRA 效果尤为明显。

Abstract: Large Language Models increasingly serve as interfaces for knowledge-intensive information seeking tasks across languages by synthesizing multilingual evidence. Prior work has shown that they often exhibit query-language preference -- the tendency to favor sources written in the language of the query -- but has largely examined this behavior in settings where equivalent knowledge is available across languages. However, this bias becomes consequential when sources in different languages provide incomplete or inconsistent accounts of the same fact, since the information users receive then depends on the sources a model selects to use. To characterize query-language preference under such cross-lingual knowledge disparities, we introduce Waldo, a multilingual Question-Answering (QA) benchmark constructed from Wikipedia. Waldo contains 12K QA pairs targeting knowledge gaps, where a fact is available in one language but absent in another, and knowledge conflicts, where language editions provide conflicting versions of the same fact. Evaluating eight models across five languages, we find that when one language edition merely lacks the relevant fact, models generally use evidence from the other language regardless of the query language. Under conflicting accounts, however, model responses strongly align with the document in the query language, causing semantically equivalent queries to elicit different accounts depending on the user's language. Finally, we explore two different approaches that could mitigate this preference under knowledge conflicts: a mechanistic intervention that ablates attention heads associated with query-language preference, and LoRA-based training, which reduces the preference gap by up to 61.5%.

</details>


### [107] [Explainable Suicide Risk Assessment on Social Media with Multi-Task QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00610)
*Xuan Zhong Feng,Geoffrey Martin,Hexin Dong,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于Qwen2.5-Instruct（QLoRA微调）的统一框架，用于可解释自杀风险评估的三个子任务：风险等级分类、证据短语抽取和多标签因素识别，官方排行榜综合得分0.7738。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释的自杀风险评估不仅要求模型估计风险严重程度，还需识别支持性语言以及帖子中表达的风险与保护因素，因此需要一个能同时完成分类、证据抽取和因素识别的统一系统。

Method: 采用Qwen2.5-Instruct模型，通过量化低秩适配（QLoRA）和答案掩码的因果语言建模目标进行适配。训练策略按任务定制：任务1a、1b、2三任务联合训练用于风险分类；任务1a与1b联合训练用于证据抽取；任务2单独训练。聚合策略也按输出结构定制：对32B与72B模型的风险概率取平均，证据短语采用跨折共识，因素决策基于折外工作点进行比率匹配校准。

Result: 官方排行榜最终系统综合得分0.7738，其中任务1为0.8089，任务2为0.6919。三任务联合训练在任务1a上表现最佳，任务1a与1b联合训练在任务1b上最佳，任务特定训练在任务2上最佳；当组件模型误差互补时，概率平均进一步提升了任务1a性能。

Conclusion: 在统一的语言模型框架内，针对每个任务的输出结构定制训练目标和聚合策略具有重要价值，这一思路可有效提升可解释风险评估系统的整体表现。

Abstract: Explainable suicide-risk assessment requires models not only to estimate risk severity, but also to identify supporting language and the risk and protective factors expressed in a post. We present our system for the IEEE BigData 2026 Cup on Explainable Suicide Risk Assessment on Social Media, which addresses three tasks: risk-level classification, evidence phrase extraction, and multi-label factor identification. Our approach adapts Qwen2.5-Instruct models using quantized low-rank adaptation (QLoRA) and an answer-masked causal language-model objective. We jointly train across all three tasks for risk classification, jointly train on Tasks~1a and 1b for evidence extraction, and adapt Task~2 separately for factor identification. We also tailor aggregation to each output: we average risk-level probabilities from the 32B and 72B models, combine evidence phrases through cross-fold consensus, and calibrate factor-specific decisions through rate matching based on out-of-fold operating points. On the official leaderboard, the final system achieved a composite score of 0.7738, with 0.8089 on Task~1 and 0.6919 on Task~2. Across the evaluated configurations, three-task training performed best for Task~1a, joint training on Tasks~1a and 1b performed best for Task~1b, and task-specific training performed best for Task~2. Probability averaging further improved Task~1a when component models had complementary errors. These findings highlight the value of tailoring both training objectives and aggregation strategies to the output structure of each task within a unified language-model framework.

</details>


### [108] [Mixture of Decoders for Diverse Dialog Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00621)
*Wenchao Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出在序列到序列对话生成模型中引入混合解码器（mixture of decoders），在CVAE框架下让每个解码器学习专门话题，以提高生成回复的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列到序列模型在对话回复生成中存在回复多样性低的问题。作者假设其原因是这类模型倾向于学习到退化的单峰（uni-modal）回复分布，因此需要引入混合建模来捕捉多模态的回复分布。

Method: 在条件变分自编码器（CVAE）框架下，将多个解码器组成的混合结构融入序列到序列模型，并设计机制使每个解码器专注于学习特定话题，从而提升生成回复的多样性。

Result: 在开放域聊天语料上进行实验，在定量指标和人工评估上均优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 将混合建模思想引入序列到序列对话生成，并通过话题专门化解码器，可以有效缓解低多样性问题，验证了回复分布多模态假设的合理性。

Abstract: Mixture modeling is a long established machine learning technique for learning large sets of multi-modal data. While it is known that sequence-to-sequence models for dialog response generation suffer from the problem of low diversity, we hypothesize that it is because sequence-to-sequence models tend to learn a degenerate uni-modal distribution of responses. We then propose to incorporate a mixture of decoders into sequence-to-sequence models and try to make each decoder learn specialized topics in order to improve the diversity of generated responses. Our model is developed under the framework of conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE). We evaluate our approach on an open domain chat corpus and show improvement over strong baselines in quantitative measures and human evaluation.

</details>


### [109] [Self-Evolving Coding Rules for AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00650)
*Zhengyuan Jiang,Reachal Wang,Yuepeng Hu,Yupu Wang,Yuqi Jia,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RuleEvolve，一个自进化的编码规则框架，通过变异与评判迭代优化规则池，在多个框架、模型和基准上优于手工设计与现有提示优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理的性能高度依赖其底层编码规则，但现有规则多为手工设计且固定不变，导致人力成本高且往往非最优。

Method: RuleEvolve维护候选编码规则池并迭代改进：每轮用LLM驱动的mutator模块从现有候选中生成变体，再用judge模块评估变体并以表现最佳者更新规则池。

Result: 在两个编码代理框架、四个骨干LLM和三个基准上的大量评测表明，RuleEvolve在生成代码的功能正确性、代码长度和/或生成成本（如token用量）方面均优于手工工程和现有提示优化基线。

Conclusion: 编码规则可通过自进化机制自动优化，RuleEvolve为提升编码代理性能提供了一种有效且优于人工与现有优化方案的途径。

Abstract: The performance of AI coding agents is highly dependent on their underlying coding rules. However, existing coding rules are typically hand-crafted and fixed, making the process labor-intensive and often suboptimal. In this work, we propose RuleEvolve, a self-evolving framework for coding rules. RuleEvolve maintains a pool of candidate coding rules and iteratively improves them. In each iteration, it employs an LLM-powered mutator module to generate variants from existing candidates, and then uses a judge module to evaluate these variants and update the pool with the best-performing ones. Extensive evaluations across two coding-agent frameworks, four backbone LLMs, and three benchmarks demonstrate that RuleEvolve outperforms both manual engineering and existing prompt optimization baselines in terms of functional correctness of the generated code, code length, and/or generation cost (e.g., tokens used).

</details>


### [110] [Lingtai: What Concept Geometry Reveals--and Does Not Reveal--About LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00656)
*Jiangang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Lingtai，一种无需训练的概念遥测层，将LLM自回归生成中的残差状态投影到领域概念锚点库上，得到逐步概念坐标信号，并研究其与预测不确定性的关联及解码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM自回归推理过程中，在线、无需训练探针地观察模型内部计算仍然困难。作者希望构建一种无需标注样例、结果标签、梯度拟合或激活空间优化的方法，实时刻画生成过程中模型的内部概念活动。

Method: 提出Lingtai：训练-free的概念遥测层。每个生成步将残差状态投影到领域特定的命名概念锚点库（161个锚点，代码实现），锚点构建不使用标注概念样例、结果标签、梯度拟合或激活优化，从而得到结构化的逐步概念坐标信号。在代码生成与小学数学推理任务上分析该信号与预测不确定性的关联，并进行问题身份、token位置等控制实验，对比K-means与PCA投影及随机锚点。

Result: 该信号与预测不确定性存在稳健关联，不受问题身份和token位置控制影响，也非单一token类型或GSM8K上正确/错误混合所能解释，且随机锚点与K-means/PCA投影无法复现。发现两种结构：与不确定性相关的活动信号其功能几何呈任务条件性（HumanEval、MBPP、GSM8K上活动熵形态不同）；以及执行特定的轨迹身份，局部惯性强但重新实例化不变性弱——匹配重执行子集下k=32处扰动仍能以62.0%检索到归档片段，而新执行仅11.7–16.0%。匹配审计未发现该标量概念活动信号在测试协议下提供稳定的正确性坐标。遥测对161锚点代码实现的每token解码开销仅增加0.7–1.6%，生成token不变。

Conclusion: Lingtai提供了一种低成本、无需训练的概念遥测方法，可揭示LLM生成中与不确定性相关的内部活动结构；但该标量概念活动信号不足以作为稳定的正确性坐标，正确性需外部提供。

Abstract: Observing what a large language model computes during autoregressive inference--online and without training probes--remains difficult. We introduce Lingtai, a training-free concept telemetry layer: at each generation step, residual states are projected onto a domain-specific bank of named concept anchors, constructed without labeled concept examples, outcome labels, gradient fitting, or activation-space optimization, producing a structured per-step concept-coordinate signal. Across code generation and grade-school mathematical reasoning, this signal exhibits a robust association with predictive uncertainty: the association survives problem-identity and token-position controls and is not attributable to a single token type, is not explained by a simple correct/incorrect mixture on GSM8K, and is not reproduced by matched random anchors; it is markedly weaker or direction-inconsistent in K-means and PCA projections. Two structures emerge: a recurring uncertainty-linked activity signal whose functional geometry is task-conditioned (distinct activity-entropy shapes on HumanEval, MBPP, and GSM8K), and an execution-specific trajectory identity with strong local inertia but weak re-instantiation invariance--under completion-only elastic alignment, corruption at k=32 (approximately a median quarter of the completion) on the matched re-execution subset still retrieves the archived episode at 62.0%, while a fresh execution retrieves it only 11.7-16.0% of the time. Finally, a matched audit finds no evidence that the scalar concept-activity signal used here supplies a stable correctness coordinate under the tested protocol; we therefore treat correctness as externally supplied. Telemetry adds 0.7-1.6% per-token decode overhead for the 161-anchor code implementation, with unchanged generated tokens.

</details>


### [111] [PhysicsMate: A Curriculum-Grounded Bengali Benchmark for Secondary Physics QA with Small-Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00664)
*Rashid Azraf Jahin,Saadman Sajid,Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhysicsMate：面向孟加拉国中学物理的课程对齐基准与知识图谱，用小模型LoRA适配提升STEM解题能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国中学教育缺乏基于课程标准的STEM解题基准；通用语言模型在物理术语、单位规范与推导方面表现不佳。

Method: 构建PhysicsMate基准，包含1834个问答对，源自NCTB九至十年级物理课程，并基于1760节点、2600条边、十类本体关系的多关系知识图谱；对0.6B、1.7B、4B参数模型采用统一配方进行低秩适配（LoRA）。

Result: 各规模闭卷准确率均显著提升，分别提高5.5、15.0和23.3个百分点；节点类型分析显示，结构化课程知识（物理量与具名定律）受益最大，界定较松散的实体级知识受益最小；4B模型经适配与量化后成为小型离线二进制，可用于资源受限环境的本地推理。

Conclusion: 该工作为连接性差、硬件有限的环境提供了可行的课程对齐物理学习支持路径。

Abstract: Bengali secondary education lacks curriculum-grounded benchmarks for STEM question-solving, and general-purpose language models struggle with the precise terminology, unit conventions, and derivations that physics problems demand. We introduce PhysicsMate, a benchmark of 1834 question-answer pairs built from the National Curriculum and Textbook Board (NCTB) Grade 9-10 physics syllabus and grounded in a multi-relational knowledge graph of 1760 nodes and 2600 edges across ten ontological types. We Low-Rank Adapt at 0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B parameters, with a unified recipe and demonstrate a significant increase in closed-book accuracy in all scales (+5.5, +15.0, and +23.3 percentage points). A node-type analysis shows that the most benefited by adaptation is the structured curricular knowledge, which consists of physical quantities and named laws, while the least benefited is the loosely specified entity-level knowledge. The 4B model has been adapted and quantized to a small offline binary that can be used for local inference in resource constrained environments and offers a viable path to curriculum aligned physics support in environments with limited connectivity and hardware.

</details>


### [112] [Closing the Loop: Practical Training Recipes for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00673)
*Andrei Marchenko,Viacheslav Bezrukov,Oleg Kashurin,Inessa Fedorova,Dmitry Bocharov,Yuliana Shakhvalieva,Maria Tikhonova,Valerii Ternovskii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文为循环语言模型（Looped LM）建立了实用的训练方案，仅需310B token即可从零训练，并在多项基准上超越同参数稠密模型，同时提供了将预训练稠密模型转化为循环模型的极简方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过重复应用共享层块来增加有效深度，但现有大规模训练方案需要数万亿token的多阶段训练，且循环带来的收益难以与数据和训练差异分离，因此亟需更高效、可控的训练方案。

Method: (1) 提出从零训练的算力高效流程：预训练加高质量中训练，配合学习率预热与更强的退出门正则化；(2) 在受控条件下与同参数、同数据、同token预算的稠密模型对比；(3) 提出将预训练稠密模型转为循环模型的极简方法：单个可学习的输入混合标量与平滑退出损失，无需步特定参数，并应用于Qwen3-1.7B-Base。

Result: 训练预算从Ouro的7.7T降至310B token，同时保持强推理性能；1.4B LoopLM在全部12个评测基准上超越同参数稠密模型（GSM8K +14、MATH +10、DROP +22），在相同推理算力下接近3.9B稠密模型且仅用36%参数；Looped Qwen在全部基准上优于持续训练的稠密基线，并在GSM8K、MATH、MMLU-Pro上取得统计显著提升。

Conclusion: 该工作使循环语言模型从零训练大幅降低成本，并使其易于引入现有预训练检查点，同时分离并验证了循环结构本身带来的性能增益。

Abstract: Looped language models increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared block of layers, but existing large-scale recipes require multi-stage training over trillions of tokens, while the benefits of recurrence remain difficult to separate from differences in data and training. In this work, we establish practical training recipes for looped language models, with three main results. (1) We develop a compute-efficient from-scratch pipeline that reduces the training budget from 7.7T tokens in Ouro to 310B tokens while retaining strong reasoning performance. Pretraining followed by high-quality mid-training, together with learning-rate warmup and stronger exit-gate regularization, enables stable recurrent training without prior multi-stage schedules. (2) Under controlled comparisons, our 1.4B LoopLM outperforms a parameter-matched dense model trained on the same data and token budget on all 12 evaluated benchmarks, including +14 points on GSM8K, +10 on MATH, and +22 on DROP. At matched inference compute, it approaches a 3.9B dense model on mathematical reasoning and reading comprehension while using only 36\% as many parameters. (3) We introduce a minimal recipe for converting pretrained dense models into looped ones: a single learned input-mixing scalar and a smoothed exit loss, with no step-specific parameters. Applied to Qwen3-1.7B-Base, Looped Qwen improves over an identically continued dense baseline on every evaluated benchmark across two data regimes, with statistically clear gains on GSM8K, MATH, and MMLU-Pro on the curated mixture. Together, these results make looped language models substantially cheaper to train from scratch and practical to introduce into existing pretrained checkpoints, while isolating the gains due to recurrence itself.

</details>


### [113] [Bayesian Fine-tuning Yields Language Models that are as Bayesian as their Beliefs Allow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00679)
*Polina Tsvilodub,Andreas Waldis,Linlu Qiu,Tal Linzen,Michael Franke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了在贝叶斯信号与Oracle答案监督下微调的语言模型，通过行为、表征与计算三个层次的要求检验其是否为贝叶斯决策者，发现贝叶斯微调能安装可用的贝叶斯信念，而标准SFT无法做到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地用于需要从少量观测中推断隐藏变量的任务，贝叶斯推断是此类问题的规范正确解。虽有研究表明在最优贝叶斯模型输出上做监督微调可使模型行为接近贝叶斯，但标准SFT（基于真实答案）却达不到这一效果。仅凭行为无法解释二者差异的原因：模型究竟是在表征贝叶斯信念、依据信念行动，还是像贝叶斯规则那样将信念转化为决策，这一问题尚不明确。

Method: 作者提出了一系列逐步加严的要求，用于判定语言模型是否算作贝叶斯决策者，涵盖行为、内部表征与计算过程三个层面。他们在航班推荐任务上对两类模型进行测试：一类在最优贝叶斯模型输出上微调（Bayes-trained），另一类在真实答案上微调（oracle-trained），并进一步通过交换两个模型之间的信念来验证贝叶斯优势的可迁移性。

Result: 贝叶斯训练的模型表现出贝叶斯式行为，其中间层编码了贝叶斯规则所需的量，并在一定程度上将编码的信念用于推荐决策。而Oracle训练的模型在持有的信念以及是否将信念读出为推荐两方面均与之不同。在两个模型之间交换信念能够转移部分贝叶斯优势。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯微调能够在语言模型中安装可用的贝叶斯信念，使其在不确定性下进行推理，这是标准基于Oracle答案的SFT所无法实现的，凸显了精细化监督信号的优势。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly used for tasks that require reasoning about hidden variables from a few observations, for which Bayesian inference is the normatively correct solution. While supervised fine-tuning of an LM on the outputs of an optimal $\textit{Bayesian}$ model leads to near-Bayesian behavior, standard supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on the true answers for the task falls short of it. But behavior alone does not tell us $\textit{why}$ tuning on a $\textit{Bayesian}$ or an $\textit{oracle}$ (true answers) signal differs: whether the resulting LM represents Bayesian beliefs, acts on them, or turns them into a choice the way Bayes' rule does. To compare them, we formulate increasingly demanding requirements for an LM to count as a Bayesian decision maker, spanning its behavior, representations, and computations, and test them on a flight recommendation task. The Bayes-trained LM acts Bayesian, encodes quantities of Bayes' rule in its middle layers, and uses the encoded belief for the recommendation to a certain extent. The oracle-trained LM differs from it both in the beliefs it holds and whether it reads beliefs out into recommendations. Exchanging beliefs between the LMs transfers a part of the Bayesian advantage. Bayes fine-tuning thus installs usable Bayesian beliefs in an LM for reasoning under uncertainty in a way standard SFT on oracle answers cannot, highlighting the advantage of nuanced supervision.

</details>


### [114] [Towards Robust Numerical Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00689)
*Peter Røysland Aarnes,Vinay Setty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对抗性微调提升LLM在数值推理上的鲁棒性，小模型超越前沿大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM广泛用于主张验证，但在数值推理上表现脆弱：数值的微小变化就会显著降低准确率，即便前沿LLM也存在这一问题。

Method: 采用参数高效微调（PEFT），在数值扰动样本上对小型Qwen3模型（0.6B–8B）进行对抗性微调，并在VitaminC数据集上验证同一方法对证据侧扰动的效果。

Result: 在标签翻转扰动上达到98.7%准确率，超越更大的零样本模型及前沿系统（GPT-5.4 Pro 74.0%、Gemini 2.5 Flash 73.9%）；增益可泛化到未见扰动类型，表明学到的是鲁棒数值决策边界而非记忆式修改；鲁棒性可在无目标域数据下迁移，显著提升西班牙语跨语言表现。

Conclusion: 对抗性微调能有效缓解LLM数值推理脆弱性，小模型亦可实现强鲁棒性，且该鲁棒性可泛化并跨语言迁移。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for claim verification, yet remain brittle for numerical reasoning: even small changes in value can sharply degrade accuracy. We show that this brittleness persists in frontier LLMs, but can be mitigated through adversarial fine-tuning on numerically perturbed examples. Using parameter-efficient fine-tuning, small Qwen3 models (0.6B$\unicode{x2013}$8B) reach 98.7% accuracy on label-flipping perturbations, outperforming larger zero-shot models and frontier systems (GPT-5.4 Pro (74.0%) and Gemini 2.5 Flash (73.9%)). The gains generalise to unseen perturbation types, indicating robust numerical decision boundaries rather than memorised edits. Robustness also transfers without target-domain data, significantly improving cross-lingual performance in Spanish. We further show that the same fine-tuning recipe confers robustness to evidence-side perturbations, using the VitaminC dataset.

</details>


### [115] [How Divergence Becomes Decision Flips in Compressed Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00694)
*Beatriz Almeida Felicio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出用总变差（TV）而非KL散度来衡量压缩语言模型偏离稠密模型的程度，因为TV能直接预测arg-max翻转率，且在802个压缩/扰动模型上验证比率中位数为1.05。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩报告通常用KL散度总结压缩语言模型与稠密模型的差异，但部署场景真正关心的是有多少决策发生了改变（即arg-max token翻转率）。KL需要开平方并乘以随模型和语料变化四倍的因子才能换算成翻转率，因为它先对token求平均再开根，导致跨模型、跨语料比较不可靠。

Method: 在19个开源模型的802个压缩及扰动副本上，覆盖五个语料和九类机制无关的扰动族，比较翻转率与总变差、KL、Hellinger距离等指标的关系；并进行两项预注册测试：留出的代码语料（八个模型）和三个带真实kernel的新模型（38个checkpoint）；另在vLLM投机解码中验证TV对贪心草稿接受率的预测。

Result: 翻转率与总变差的比率中位数为1.05，无需拟合常数；KL仅在开平方并引入随模型/语料变化四倍的因子时才能预测翻转率。当两个压缩器翻转率相差至少10%时，KL有11%的情况把更小散度分配给改变决策更多的那个，而TV仅1%。预注册测试中，代码语料上八个模型比率全部成立而KL三项预测均半数以上失败；38个checkpoint中37个比率落在预期区间。在vLLM投机解码中，教师强制下测得的总变差预测贪心草稿接受率，平均相对误差1.1%–2.4%，无需KL所需的任务特定校准。

Conclusion: 总变差是衡量压缩语言模型决策变化率的正确且可直接使用的指标，优于KL散度；它跨模型、语料和扰动类型稳健，并在投机解码等部署场景中消除任务特定校准需求。建议压缩报告直接给出总变差而非仅KL。

Abstract: Compression reports summarize how far a compressed language model moved from the dense one, usually by a KL divergence; a deployment that relies on the dense model's outputs needs to know how many of its decisions changed. We show that total variation, not KL, answers this directly. Across 802 compressed and perturbed copies of 19 open models on five corpora and nine mechanically unrelated perturbation families, the rate at which the arg-max token changes (the \emph{flip rate}) tracks total variation at a ratio with median $1.05$, with no fitted constant. KL converts into flips only through its square root and a factor that varies fourfold across models and corpora, because KL averages over tokens before the root is taken; first-order statistics averaged per token, such as Hellinger distance, avoid this, but reports rarely give them. As a result, of two compressors reported on different models and corpora whose flip rates differ by at least $10%$, KL assigns the smaller divergence to the one that changes more decisions in $11%$ of cases, total variation in $1%$. Two pre-registered tests mark the limits: on a held-out code corpus the ratio held for all eight models while three predictions about KL each failed for half of them or more, and on three new models with real kernels it stayed in its band for 37 of 38 checkpoints but fell below one on code for two models. In vLLM speculative decoding, total variation measured under teacher forcing predicts greedy draft acceptance with a mean relative error of $1.1$--$2.4%$, without the task-specific calibration that KL needs.

</details>


### [116] [Sequential Functional Structured Tucker Compression for Large Language Model Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00717)
*Jiangfeng Chen,Xinyu Wang,Tianshuo Yan,Hanwei Wu,Xiao-Wen Chang,Yang Zhang,Lei Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 FTC，一种针对 LLM 注意力模块的顺序结构化压缩框架，在固定存储预算下联合利用 Q/K/V 头结构并适配压缩后模型，无需微调或梯度恢复，在多个 GQA 模型上取得最低困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 注意力后训练压缩多被视为独立矩阵近似问题，忽略了注意力投影之间的共享结构，以及先前压缩带来的表示偏移，导致压缩效果受限。

Method: FTC 采用顺序结构化压缩框架：在固定存储预算下，联合利用原生的 Q/K/V 头结构，使近似适配当前已压缩的模型；同时将输出投影单独处理，以应对注意力后表示的变化；整个过程无需微调或基于梯度的恢复。

Result: 在 6B 至 32B 的七个 decoder-only LLM 上，FTC 在五个现代 GQA 模型的每个测试保留率下均取得最低的 WikiText-2 困惑度，激进压缩时增益最大，并能迁移到下游任务，在 32B 规模仍保持显著优势。

Conclusion: FTC 通过顺序化、结构感知的注意力压缩有效解决了独立近似与表示偏移问题，在无需微调的情况下实现优于对比方法的压缩性能，并具备良好的可扩展性与任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Post-training compression of LLM attention is often formulated as independent matrix approximation, ignoring both the shared structure among attention projections and the representation shift introduced by earlier compression. We propose FTC, a sequential structured compression framework that adapts the approximation to the current compressed model while jointly exploiting the native Q/K/V head structure under a fixed storage budget. The output projection is handled separately to account for the changed post-attention representation. FTC requires neither fine-tuning nor gradient-based recovery. Across seven decoder-only LLMs from 6B to 32B parameters, FTC achieves the lowest WikiText-2 perplexity among the compared methods at every tested keep ratio on five modern GQA models, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. The improvements transfer to downstream tasks and remain substantial at the 32B scale.

</details>


### [117] [Reason in Style: Discovering and Controlling Style in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00724)
*Ioana Marinescu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无监督方法，从语言模型输出中分离内容与风格表示，发现六种重复出现的风格，并通过风格条件微调提升数学推理的Pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型将内容与风格联合学习，导致其输出中的风格变化难以识别与控制。作者希望探究模型回复中的常见风格能否被无监督发现并显式控制。

Method: 设计算法从语言模型输出中分离内容与风格的表示，并在受控的数学问题场景中验证其有效性。将该方法应用于来自九个不同教师模型的超过10万条已验证轨迹，发现六种重复但分布不均衡的风格。随后用重要性加权对较小的学生模型进行微调，使其在显式条件下遵循这些风格，并平衡语料中不同风格的贡献。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准上，该方法相比在相同数据上的标准微调提升了Pass@k，说明能有效多样化答案风格；同时验证了请求风格与实际生成风格之间的强对应关系。还发现风格影响正确性：解题概率取决于所条件的风格，不同问题适合不同风格。

Conclusion: 模型生成数据中的风格变化可被无监督发现并显式化，这既提供了控制手段，也能提升推理性能。

Abstract: Language models learn content and style jointly, making stylistic variation in their outputs difficult to identify and control. We study whether recurring styles in model responses can be discovered without supervision and explicitly controlled. We design an algorithm that learns to separate representations of content and style from language models' outputs and validate its effectiveness on math questions in a controlled setting. By applying this method to over 100K verified traces from nine distinct teacher models, we discover six recurring yet imbalanced styles. We then fine-tune smaller student models to follow these styles when explicitly conditioned on them, using importance weighting to balance the contribution of the styles represented in the corpus. This approach improves Pass@$k$ over standard fine-tuning on the same data across six math reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating that we can diversify the style of answers effectively. We confirm that this also results in strong correspondence between requested and realized styles. We find that style affects correctness: the probability of solving a problem depends on the style we condition on, and different problems benefit from different styles. In summary, our results show that stylistic variation in model-generated data can be discovered in an unsupervised way, and made explicit, providing a source of both control and improved reasoning performance.

</details>


### [118] [Effective Synthetic Data Curation Requires Group-Level Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00779)
*Cathy Jiao,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 合成数据在LLM训练中不可或缺，但现有以个体级信号为核心的数据筛选方法不足；本文证明需采用考虑样本间交互的群体级信号进行数据筛选，并给出低成本诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据已是大模型训练的关键，用于增强自主和长程任务执行等高级能力。然而近期研究表明，大规模使用合成数据会损害模型生成能力，因此必须判断哪些合成数据值得训练。当前数据筛选实践主要依赖个体级信号（孤立估计每个样本的训练效用），作者认为这对合成数据而言并不充分，需要引入群体级信号。

Method: 作者在预训练与后训练场景下，系统对比了个体级信号与群体级信号的效果差异：（1）构造在个体级影响下难以区分、但群体级影响差异显著的合成数据集，并分别按两种信号进行筛选；（2）在不同合成化程度的训练流程中，比较多种常用数据筛选方法，并放大表征样本间关系的权重；（3）为受算力预算约束的模型开发者提出一种廉价诊断方法，用于优先识别最需要群体级估计的数据组。

Result: 个体级信号无法感知样本的联合影响：不同构成的合成数据集在个体级影响下可能无法区分，但在群体级影响下差异显著，按群体级信号筛选可获得更好的下游性能，在生成能力上尤为明显。群体级信号的重要性随训练流程的合成化程度提高而增加：在常用筛选方法中，只有纳入群体级信号的方法能超越基线，且样本关系权重被放大时增益更大。所提出的廉价诊断可优先定位最需群体级估计的数据组，以远低于全量群体级评分的算力成本挽回大部分收益。

Conclusion: 对合成数据进行筛选时，个体级信号是不充分的，群体级信号（考虑样本间交互的效用估计）是必要的。这一结论在预训练与后训练中均成立，且随训练流程合成化程度提高而更加关键。作者进一步提供了可落地的低成本诊断方案，帮助算力受限的模型开发者高效获得群体级筛选的大部分收益。

Abstract: Synthetic data now is essential to LLM training, used to strengthen advanced capabilities such as autonomous and long-horizon task execution. Yet recent work shows that training on it at scale can degrade model generation, making it important to decide what synthetic data is worth training on. While current data curation practices do so with individual-level signals (i.e., estimates of each data sample's training utility in isolation), across pre-training and post-training settings we show that this is insufficient for synthetic data, and that group-level signals (i.e., estimates of utility that account for interactions among data samples) are necessary for effective data curation. First, we show that individual-level signals are blind to how samples jointly affect training: synthetic datasets with different compositions can be indistinguishable under individual-level influence yet differ sharply under group-level influence, and curating by the latter yields better downstream performance, particularly in generative capability. Second, we find that group-level signals matter more as training pipelines become increasingly synthetic: among widely used data curation methods, only those incorporating them improve over baseline, with gains increasing when weights capturing relations among samples are amplified. Finally, we translate these findings into practice -- for model developers under a compute budget, we offer a cheap diagnostic that prioritizes which groups of synthetic data most need group-level estimation, recovering much of the benefit of full group-level scoring at a fraction of the compute cost.

</details>


### [119] [Can large language models unlock discrete data in ophthalmic diagnostic reports?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00795)
*Umair A. Zaidi,An-Lun Wu,Wei-Chun Lin,Thomas S. Hwang,Michelle R. Hribar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 评估GPT-4o两种提示策略从眼科诊断PDF报告中提取结构化数据的准确性与效率，结果显示准确率高且处理时间减少约90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼科诊断PDF报告的人工数据提取耗时且易出错，需评估大语言模型辅助流程能否在保证准确性的同时提升效率，并比较不同提示策略的优劣。

Method: 使用20份去标识化报告（4种类型各5份），采用两种GPT-4o流程：Schema-Constrained（结构化输出模式+预定义JSON Schema）与Prompt-Only（详细指令提示+Python转JSON），并与人工校对的金标准对比数值准确性、格式准确性和提取时间。

Result: Schema-Constrained数值准确率：视野与RNFL单次检查100%、青光眼概览97.45%、厚度图98.00%；Prompt-Only四类均100%。格式准确率：Schema-Constrained全为100%，Prompt-Only除RNFL单次检查（90.14%）外均为100%。平均提取时间：人工56.51秒/份，Schema-Constrained 5.04秒，Prompt-Only 4.70秒，约减少92%。

Conclusion: 在该小型概念验证数据集中，通用LLM辅助流程可高精度、低耗时地从眼科诊断PDF中提取结构化数据；Prompt-Only数值准确率最高，Schema-Constrained格式合规性最佳，二者互补，支持进一步探索混合式、带验证的工作流用于研究与临床数据抽取。

Abstract: Objective: To assess the accuracy and efficiency of a large language model (LLM) using two prompt strategies to extract structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDF reports. Methods: Twenty deidentified reports across four types (Visual Field, OCT Glaucoma Overview, OCT retinal nerve fiber layer Single Exam, and OCT Thickness Map; n = 5 each) were processed using two GPT-4o-assisted pipelines and compared with a reconciled manual ground truth. Schema-Constrained used Structured Output mode with a predefined JSON Schema; Prompt-Only used a detailed instruction prompt followed by Python conversion to JSON. Outcomes were value accuracy, formatting accuracy, and extraction time. Results: Schema-Constrained value accuracy was 100.00% for Visual Field and RNFL Single Exam, 97.45% for Glaucoma Overview, and 98.00% for Thickness Map; Prompt-Only achieved 100.00% across all four report types. Formatting accuracy was 100.00% for Schema-Constrained across all report types and 100.00% for Prompt-Only except RNFL Single Exam (90.14%). Mean extraction time was 56.51 s per report for manual review versus 5.04 s for Schema-Constrained and 4.70 s for Prompt-Only, an approximately 92% reduction. Conclusions: In this small proof-of-concept dataset, general-purpose LLM-assisted pipelines extracted structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDFs with high accuracy and substantially reduced processing time. Prompt-Only achieved the highest value accuracy, while Schema-Constrained produced schema-compliant output with 100% formatting accuracy. These complementary strengths support further evaluation of hybrid, validation-aware workflows for research and clinical data abstraction.

</details>


### [120] [Verbalized and Internal Probabilities Are Coupled in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00827)
*Sinead Williamson,Jiaxuan Li,Nick Foti,Russ Webb,Masha Fedzechkina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统探究大语言模型的内部概率与言语化概率两种不确定性读出方式是否一致，发现二者均受训练数据中的分布不确定性与断言不确定性影响，且其对齐程度超出独立追踪同一来源的预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型既携带采样分布中的内部不确定性（生成不同答案的概率），也能用言语或数字表达置信度（言语化不确定性）。已有研究认为内部概率反映训练数据的相对频率，而言语化概率反映训练数据中显式的概率断言。但当训练数据中频率与概率断言不一致时，二者的对齐关系尚不清楚，这限制了我们能否将言语化不确定性作为训练数据频率或模型内部分布的代理。

Method: 通过干预数据中潜在的不确定性来源，系统探究训练数据与上下文数据如何影响大语言模型的概率读出，从而弥补上述研究空白。

Result: 内部概率读出与言语化概率读出均同时受训练数据中的分布不确定性与断言不确定性影响；且言语化概率与内部概率之间的对齐程度，超出了二者仅独立追踪相同不确定性来源所应有的预期。

Conclusion: 言语化概率可用于探测模型的内部分布，为将其作为模型内部不确定性的代理提供了依据。

Abstract: Large language models carry an internal notion of uncertainty in their sampling distribution, i.e., the probabilities they place on generating one answer rather than another. They can also be asked to state a confidence, in words or as a number: a verbalized uncertainty. Prior work suggests that internal probabilities track relative frequencies in the training data, and that verbalized probabilities track explicit probabilistic assertions in the training data. However, we do not know whether these two readouts are aligned, except when frequencies and probabilistic assertions in the training data happen to align. This limits our understanding of when we can use verbalized uncertainties as a proxy for either training data frequencies, or a model's internal distribution. We resolve this gap by systematically exploring how LLMs probability readouts are impacted by training and in-context data, via intervening on the underlying uncertainty sources in the data. We find that both internal and verbalized probability readouts are impacted by both distributional and asserted uncertainty in the training data. Further, we find that verbalized and internal probabilities are aligned beyond what would be expected by independently tracking the same uncertainty sources, suggesting that verbalized probabilities can be used to probe a model's internal distribution.

</details>


### [121] [VERITYGATE: A Four-Gate Schema-Level Faithfulness Framework and Paired Benchmark for Grounded LLM Narrations over Structured Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00833)
*Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 VERITYGATE 四门校验器，按固定模式检查 LLM 解释中的证据 ID、实体、数字与声明类型，并报告不同模型下的拒收率与修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流畅的 LLM 解释未必遵循结构化系统给出的证据；需要一个轻量、聚焦于"声明是否引用合规证据"的校验手段，而非验证全部事实。

Method: 设计四道门的模式级校验器 VERITYGATE，只检查固定 schema（证据 ID、实体、数字、声明类型），不核验散文中的所有事实。在 r=0 与 r=1 下，各用 900 个实例（450 组有据/无据配对），测试 GPT-4o-mini、Llama-3.3-70B、Claude Sonnet 4.6。

Result: 修复前，mini 有 80.3%、Sonnet 有 47.9% 的声明未通过；一次修复使 mini 生存率由 19.7% 升至 28.0%，Sonnet 由 52.1% 升至 54.3%；每例经校验声明数变化为 mini +0.14、Llama -0.71、Sonnet -0.47；第二次 Sonnet 修复无明显增益；r=1 时 Gate 4 覆盖三模型失败声明的 97.0%、98.7%、100%。

Conclusion: 模式级校验可有效暴露证据引用缺陷，但拒收率不等于幻觉率，且需同时报告生存率与输出量；小型人工研究与 GPT-4o 评审测试提示 schema 检查与正确散文之间仍有差距，后者存在顺序效应，仅作可用性检验。代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Fluent LLM explanations may not follow the evidence from a structured system. We present VERITYGATE, a four-gate checker for declared evidence IDs, entities, numbers, and claim types. It checks a fixed schema; it does not verify every fact in the prose. At r=0 and r=1, we test 900 instances per setting (450 grounded-ungrounded pairs) with GPT-4o-mini, Llama-3.3-70B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6. Under this schema-level contract and before repair, 80.3% of mini claims and 47.9% of Sonnet claims fail. These are verifier rejection rates, not prose-hallucination rates. One repair pass raises claim survival from 19.7% to 28.0% for mini and from 52.1% to 54.3% for Sonnet. Verified claims per example change by +0.14 for mini, -0.71 for Llama, and -0.47 for Sonnet, so survival and output volume must be reported together. A second Sonnet pass gives no clear gain. At r=1, Gate 4 covers 97.0%, 98.7%, and 100% of failing claims for mini, Llama, and Sonnet. Small human studies support the rules but show gaps between schema checks and correct prose. A domain-specific GPT-4o judge test shows an order effect, so it is only a usefulness check. We release the code and data.

</details>


### [122] [Contextual trajectory and incremental contextual displacement: Towards using LLMs to understand dynamic, utterance-specific meaning construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00840)
*Grayson Wycliffe Storer,Julia Witte Zimmerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"token级增量轨迹"方法，通过逐步增加上下文并重复计算token的上下文词嵌入，刻画其随语句展开的演化过程，并利用花园路径句验证该框架能再现相关加工特征并预测句子类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer类大语言模型（如RoBERTa）用上下文词嵌入（CWEs）表示文本，即token的嵌入会随周围语境变化。作者希望借此探究话语特定意义的构建过程，检验上下文信息如何逐步、在多大表示尺度上影响token表示，并以花园路径句作为典型测试案例。

Method: 对每个token，随着句子中逐词增加，反复重新计算其CWE，构建token级增量轨迹，以此表示上下文嵌入随话语展开的演化。提出若干度量指标，用于量化每个上下文增量步骤中的表示位移；并将轨迹信息用于分类花园路径句与匹配的消歧控制句，同时进一步分析CLS表示与普通词汇token中可恢复的歧义相关信息。

Result: token级轨迹再现了花园路径加工的已知特征，包括关键区域附近的扰动，并能可靠区分花园路径句与匹配的消歧控制句。轨迹信息对句子类型具有很高预测力。歧义相关信息不仅可从句子级CLS表示中恢复，也可从普通词汇token中恢复，表明话语级信息分布在多个表示尺度上。在探索性分析中，其他与歧义和误导相关的语言现象也呈现定性相似的轨迹结构。

Conclusion: token级增量轨迹为利用LLM研究话语特定意义的构建提供了一个有前景的框架。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) such as RoBERTa represent text using contextual word embeddings (CWEs), which alter the embeddings associated with each token based on surrounding context. We construct token-wise incremental trajectories by repeatedly recomputing a token's CWE as successive words are added to a sentence, yielding a representation of how contextualized embeddings evolve as the utterance unfolds. We evaluate this approach using garden-path sentences as a test case with characteristic features. Token-wise trajectories reproduce known features of garden-path processing, including disruption around the critical region, and reliably distinguish garden-path sentences from matched disambiguated controls. We introduce several metrics for quantifying representational displacement across contextual increments and show that trajectory information can be highly predictive of sentence type. We find that ambiguity-related information is recoverable not only from the sentence-level CLS representation but also from ordinary vocabulary tokens, suggesting that utterance-level information is distributed across multiple representational scales. In exploratory analyses, we find qualitatively similar trajectory structures in other ambiguity- and misdirection-related linguistic phenomena. Together, these results establish token-wise incremental trajectories as a promising framework for studying utterance-specific meaning construction using LLMs.

</details>


### [123] [Child-Adapted Structured Phonological Representations for Interpretable Speech Sound Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00852)
*Abner Hernandez,Tomás Arias Vergara,Andreas Maier,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将结构化音系表征模型 PhonoQ-2.0 适配到儿童语音，比较三种对齐监督条件与两种初始化策略，并在人工标注上评估泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化音系表征比通用语音嵌入更具可解释性，但现有模型多在成人语音上训练，直接用于儿童语音可能受限，因此需要研究儿童语音适配策略。

Method: 使用 CHILDES-Aligned 数据将 PhonoQ-2.0 适配到儿童语音，比较 Adult、Adult+Child、Child-only 三种对齐监督条件，以及 Adult PhonoQ 与 scratch 两种初始化策略，并以人工儿童语音标注评估泛化。

Result: 在 58 名典型发育儿童的 1,352 个辅音目标上，儿童语音适配提升了所有监督条件下的 voicing 识别，macro-F1 从 0.922 提升至 0.972--0.987；manner 更依赖对齐监督，Adult+Child MFA 达 0.804 和 0.796，而 Adult MFA 约 0.70；place 表现较强（0.871--0.902）；velar-fronting 对比在七个模型变体中均保留。纵向 UltraPhonix 分析揭示说话者特异的 velar 与 post-alveolar 变化。

Conclusion: 儿童语音适配能显著改善结构化音系表征的识别性能，尤其对 voicing 和 manner，且部分音系对比与临床进展在多种模型下具有一致性。

Abstract: Structured phonological representations provide an interpretable alternative to generic speech embeddings, but existing models are largely trained on adult speech. We adapt PhonoQ-2.0 to child speech using CHILDES-Aligned data and compare three alignment-supervision conditions (Adult, Adult+Child, and Child-only) across two initialization strategies (Adult PhonoQ and scratch). Generalization is evaluated against manual child-speech annotations. On 1,352 consonant targets from 58 typically developing children, child-speech adaptation improves voicing recognition across all supervision conditions, from 0.922 macro-F1 for Adult PhonoQ to 0.972--0.987 after adaptation. Manner is more sensitive to alignment supervision: Adult+Child MFA reaches 0.804 and 0.796, compared to approximately 0.70 under Adult MFA supervision. Place remains comparatively strong across systems (0.871--0.902), although per-class performance varies substantially. The velar-fronting contrast is preserved across all seven model variants. Longitudinal UltraPhonix analysis further reveals speaker-specific velar and post-alveolar changes that are largely preserved across models and broadly consistent with reported clinical progress.

</details>


### [124] [DeBERTa-ConPara: Attack-Aware and Deployment-Realistic Detection of AI-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00883)
*Mohamed Mady,Yupei Li,Johannes Reschke,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DeBERTa-ConPara 检测器，核心发现是 Unicode 归一化的作用方向取决于施加位置：训练端归一化会破坏对抗监督，推理端归一化则有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 生成文本检测在真实部署下困难：跨领域与跨生成器的分布漂移、输入表面的对抗扰动、以及缺乏目标域标签来校准阈值，都会让在域内表现良好的检测器失效。

Method: DeBERTa-ConPara 将攻击感知的 Unicode 预处理与上下文 Transformer 编码器结合，在 HC3 Plus、M4、MAGE 和 RAID 上训练。作者设计因子实验，独立改变训练端与推理端归一化的施加位置，并做跨架构零样本验证与消融（释义语义不变增强、监督对比学习、手工特征融合分支）。

Result: 最佳配置为"原始训练+归一化推理"，在 RAID 官方隐藏测试上取得 99.61% AUROC、99.01% TPR@5% FPR、96.57% TPR@1% FPR，在 HC3 Plus 与 MAGE 固定阈值下平均平衡准确率 93.14%。增益仅集中于 12 类攻击中的两类：同形字符与零宽空格插入从 11.05% 和 1.12% 提升至 96.98%，且该特征在另一架构的零样本检测器中同样复现。

Conclusion: 预处理的位置决定其效果：训练端归一化会去重语料、把 35.4% 的 RAID 样本折叠为干净样本副本从而删除对抗监督，推理端归一化才是有效防御。语义不变增强与对比学习未带来提升，手工特征融合分支在分布内无效、分布外有害。

Abstract: Robust detection of AI-generated text under deployment conditions is challenging: distribution shifts across domains and generators, adversarial perturbations of the input surface, and the absence of target-domain labels for threshold calibration all degrade detectors that perform well in-domain. We present DeBERTa-ConPara, a deployment-oriented detector combining attack-aware Unicode preprocessing with a contextual transformer encoder trained over HC3 Plus, M4, MAGE and RAID. Our central finding is that preprocessing acts in opposite directions depending on where it is applied: normalising the training corpus deduplicates it, collapsing 35.4% of RAID rows into copies of their clean siblings and deleting the adversarial supervision, whereas normalising at inference is an effective defence. A factorial varying the two placements independently identifies raw training with normalised inference as the best configuration, reaching 99.61% AUROC, 99.01% TPR@5% FPR and 96.57% TPR@1% FPR on the official RAID hidden test, alongside 93.14% average balanced accuracy across HC3 Plus and MAGE under a fixed threshold. The gain is confined to two of twelve attack classes: homoglyph and zero-width-space insertion rise from 11.05% and 1.12% to 96.98%. The same signature reproduces in a zero-shot detector of different architecture, showing the effect belongs to the attacks rather than to our model. We additionally report two negative results: semantic-invariance augmentation through paraphrasing and supervised contrastive learning (ConPara) does not improve the best configuration, and the handcrafted feature-fusion branch is inert in distribution and harmful outside it.

</details>


### [125] [The Geometry of Contextual Relations: Language Models Address Facts by Order of Mention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00910)
*Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"序数寻址假说"：LLM 通过事实在上下文中被提及的顺序（而非名称）来定位事实，并在 Qwen、Gemma、Llama 上验证了序数向量的四类性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类推理依赖命题中对象之间的关系，但语言模型如何组织上下文中事实的表征尚不清楚。作者想探究：上下文中的事实在模型隐状态里是按什么线索（名称还是提及顺序）被寻址的。

Method: 向 LLM 上下文输入一组事实列表（如"Alice 吃苹果。Bob 吃梨。"），测量当问题从"Alice 吃什么"切换到"Bob 吃什么"时隐状态的变化。对大量列表取平均后得到转向向量，称为"序数向量"（ordinal vector），用以检验事实是按"提及顺序"还是按名称被表征，并提出"序数寻址假说"：每个提及顺序在模型状态中有一个跨上下文共享的"事实地址"，问题把状态移动到目标事实的地址，而上下文提供该事实的内容。

Result: 在 Qwen、Gemma、Llama 上，事实地址表现出四性：(1) 按提及顺序排列——查询状态依事实顺序而非名称组织，即使某事实有多个主语也成立；(2) 可转向——将序数向量加到关于新列表第一个事实的问题上，模型会改答该列表的第二个事实；(3) 低秩——事实地址张成低秩子空间，且在其中的第一个被提及事实最易到达，与人类回忆规律相似；(4) 涌现性——在中间偏后层共享，从 1.5B 到 32B 参数均成立，并在预训练早期形成。

Conclusion: 语言模型是依据事实"被提及的位置"来抵达该事实的，这一发现深化了对 LLM 推理机制的理解。类别：LLM。

Abstract: Human reasoning depends on how objects are related within propositions. \textit{How do relations organize the language representations of contextual contents?} We give an LLM a list of facts in its context (e.g., \emph{Alice eats an apple. Bob eats a pear.}) and measure how its hidden state changes when the question switches from what Alice eats to what Bob eats. Averaged over many lists, this change is a steering vector, which we call the \emph{ordinal vector}. It points to a fact by its \emph{order of mention}, the order in which the facts were stated in the context. We find that LLMs represent the fact a question asks about by its order of mention, not by the name the question contains. We state this as the \textit{ordinal addressing hypothesis}: each order of mention has a \emph{fact address} in the model's state, shared by all contexts, and a question moves the state to the fact address of the fact it asks about, while the context supplies what that fact says. Across Qwen, Gemma, and Llama, fact addresses are (1) \emph{ordered by mention}: query states are organized by the order of facts, not of names, even when one fact has multiple subjects; (2) \emph{steerable}: added to a question about the first fact of a new list, the ordinal vector makes the model answer with the second fact of that list; (3) \emph{low-rank}: they span a low-rank subspace in which the first-mentioned fact is the easiest to reach, surprisingly similar to human recall; and (4) \emph{emergent}: they are shared in late-middle layers, hold from 1.5B to 32B parameters, and form early in pretraining. Language models reach a stated fact by where it was mentioned, deepening our understanding of LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [126] [Efficient Task Adaptation in Large Language Models: A Survey of Weight-Based, Prompt-Based, and Embedding-Based Adaptations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00928)
*Jungwon Park,Changin Choi,Jimyeong Kim,Nojun Kwak,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一篇综述，提出统一框架，将大语言模型任务适配方法按任务信息编码位置（模型权重、输入提示、注入的任务嵌入）分类，整合三大范式并分析其关系与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型广泛部署于下游任务，高效任务适配成为核心挑战。已有方法（参数高效微调、上下文学习、嵌入注入）各自独立发展，跨范式关系与权衡研究不足，尤其是新兴的基于嵌入的适配方法。

Method: 提出统一框架，按任务信息编码的位置与方式（模型权重、输入提示、注入任务嵌入）对适配方法分类，构建整合各范式的分类体系，分析其优势与局限，厘清范式间关系。

Result: 给出涵盖参数高效微调、上下文学习、嵌入注入的统一分类体系，阐明各范式的演变逻辑与相互关系，并指出未来研究的开放问题。

Conclusion: 为LLM任务适配领域提供跨范式的统一视角，帮助理解不同适配范式的演进与权衡，并为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed across diverse downstream tasks, efficient task adaptation has emerged as a central challenge. In response, a wide range of task adaptation methods have been proposed, spanning parameter-efficient fine-tuning, in-context learning, and embedding-injection approaches. However, these lines of work have largely evolved within individual paradigms, leaving their cross-paradigm relationships and trade-offs underexplored, especially for recently emerging embedding-based adaptations. This survey presents a unified framework that categorizes task adaptation methods by where and how task information is encoded: model weights, input prompts, or injected task embeddings. We provide a comprehensive taxonomy that integrates these paradigms, analyze their key strengths and limitations to explain how different adaptation paradigms have evolved, clarify relationships across paradigms, and highlight open problems for future research.

</details>


### [127] [ReHoPER: Receding-Horizon Planning for Enhanced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00940)
*Saeed Ahmadnia,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReHoPER：一种免训练、零样本的推理增强方法，通过多路径生成并回答中间问题来提升大语言模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大语言模型推理能力通常依赖标注数据或任务特定提示设计，成本高且难以泛化；本文希望以推理阶段、任务无关的方式实现通用增强。

Method: 提出 ReHoPER，推理阶段零样本方法：迭代规划一段候选中间问题，选择其一作答，并基于更新后的历史重新规划，沿多条路径推进直至得出最终答案；使用同一套通用指令，跨数据集与模型，无需标注数据或任务特定提示。

Result: 在多个数据集上优于强基线；在组合性最强的设置中增益最大，并在新提出的组合推理受控基准 iLLC 上表现突出。实现与 iLLC 生成器已公开。

Conclusion: ReHoPER 证明了任务无关、免训练的推理增强可行且有效，尤其利于组合推理，并为后续研究提供公开工具与基准。

Abstract: We propose ReHoPER, an inference-only, zero-shot method that improves large language models' reasoning by generating and answering intermediate questions along multiple paths before the final answer. It iteratively plans a horizon of candidate intermediate questions, selects one to answer, and replans from the updated history. ReHoPER is task-agnostic, using the same generic instructions across datasets and models without labeled data or task-specific prompt design. Across multiple datasets, including iLLC, a new controlled benchmark for compositional reasoning, ReHoPER outperforms strong baselines, with the largest gains in the most compositional settings. Our implementation and the iLLC generator are publicly available to support future work.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Leaderboards: Tokenomics of Agentic Small Language Model Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00954)
*Alexei N. Skurikhin,Emily M. Taylor,Nathan A. DeBardeleben*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文以SLM智能体集成为案例，主张对LLM评估应从单一准确率扩展到可靠性、成本、延迟与token效率等多维指标；在IFEval上集成达97.34%严格准确率，超过最强独立LLM基线5.81个百分点且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从独立助手走向智能体工作流，传统排行榜式的标量准确率评估已不足以反映实际部署中的运行可靠性、成本、延迟和token效率，因此需要超越排行榜的多维评估视角。

Method: 采用由小语言模型（SLM）组成的智能体集成系统，并引入SLM评判者介导的反馈回路作为案例；在541条提示的IFEval基准上进行评估，并对token经济学与运行行为做分解分析，包括每样本成本、token构成、有效输出goodput、反馈回路恢复、延迟分解以及不同指令类别和约束数量下的表现。

Result: 最佳集成取得97.34%的严格提示准确率，比最强独立LLM基线gpt-5.4高出5.81个百分点，同时运行在更低成本区间；分析还揭示了性能提升背后的成本、token与延迟结构。

Conclusion: 智能体式SLM集成可以通过消耗更多测试时token与编排开销来换取更强的指令遵循保真度，这支持为未来智能体AI系统建立多维评估协议。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) move from standalone assistants into agentic workflows, evaluation must extend beyond scalar leaderboard accuracy to account for operational reliability, cost, latency, and token efficiency. We use an agentic ensemble of small language models (SLMs) with an SLM-judge-mediated feedback loop as a case study for such beyond-leaderboard evaluation. On the 541-prompt IFEval benchmark, the best ensemble achieves 97.34% strict prompt accuracy, exceeding the strongest standalone LLM baseline, gpt-5.4, by 5.81 percentage points while operating in a lower-cost regime. We then analyze the tokenomics and operational behavior behind this gain, including cost per sample, token composition, useful-output goodput, feedback-loop recovery, latency decomposition, and performance across instruction categories and constraint counts. Our results show that agentic SLM ensembles can trade additional test-time tokens and orchestration overhead for improved instruction-following fidelity, motivating multi-dimensional evaluation protocols for future agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [129] [Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention for Conversational LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00958)
*Wanyang Hong,Zhaoning Zhang,Yi Chen,Libo Zhang,Baihui Liu,Linbo Qiao,Zhiliang Tian,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出REA（角色感知启发式情景注意力）上下文管理框架，将指令与情景交互区别对待，缓解多轮对话中的累积性上下文衰减，在Long-MT-Bench+上将评分从6.32提升至7.36，并降低平均延迟2.91倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多轮对话不断增长时，常会遗忘持续性指令与相关信息。论文将这种累积性上下文衰减归纳为三种失效模式：注意力污染、稀释与漂移，并希望解决指令遵循与对话连续性难以保持的问题。

Method: 提出REA（Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention）框架，对指令与情景交互采用不同的持久化与表示策略： Instructional Memory 将识别出的全局约束保留在专用前缀中；Episodic Memory 保留用户输入并压缩模型回复；启发式检索为每个历史轮次选择原始文本、压缩表示或省略三种处理方式。

Result: 在Long-MT-Bench+上，REA将评判分数从Vanilla基线的6.32提升至7.36（10分制），相对提升16.5%，同时平均延迟降低2.91倍。在1.7B-7B参数的三个骨干模型上以及中英文角色扮演任务上均取得总体增益。

Conclusion: 结果表明，角色感知的上下文管理是维持对话连续性与指令遵循的一种实用方法。

Abstract: Large language models often lose track of persistent instructions and relevant information as multi-turn conversations grow. We study this cumulative contextual decay through three related failure modes: attention pollution, dilution, and drift. We propose REA (Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention), a context-management framework that assigns different persistence and representation policies to instructions and episodic interactions. Instructional Memory retains identified global constraints in a dedicated prefix. Episodic Memory preserves user inputs and compresses model replies, while heuristic retrieval selects raw text, compressed representations, or omission for each historical turn. On Long-MT-Bench+, REA improves the judge score from 6.32 to 7.36 on a 10-point scale, a 16.5% relative gain over the Vanilla baseline, and reduces average latency by 2.91$\times$. Additional evaluations show aggregate gains on three backbones spanning 1.7B-7B parameters and on Chinese and English role-playing tasks. These results support role-aware context management as a practical approach to maintaining conversational continuity and instruction adherence.

</details>


### [130] [A Citation-Grounded Benchmark for Trustworthy Earnings Call Transcript Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00969)
*Yingzhu Zhao,Vlad Pandelea,Han Yuan,Bo Hu,Wuqiong Luo,Li Zhang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对LLM在财报电话会议记录分析中"有据可依"评价依赖专家标注、成本高难扩展的问题，提出数值证据评估方法与自动数据集构建流程，构建S&P 500前100公司ECTs-100基准，同时评估groundedness与correctness及"有意识无能力"失败模式。结果显示LLM在有据性上表现良好，但正确性存在明显局限，信息不足是额外挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被日益用于金融文档分析（如财报电话会议记录），用户更偏好将结论与可验证的源文档引用配对的"有据分析"，以便独立验证。但评估这类分析结论通常需要大量专家标注，成本高且难以规模化；真实金融分析常涉及长上下文-问题-答案三元组，进一步增加任务复杂度。因此需要一种不依赖专家标注的有据性评估方法及相应基准，并考察LLM在证据不足时能否避免幻觉。

Method: 提出一种数值证据评估方法，实现不依赖专家标注的有据性（groundedness）评估；设计自动化数据集构建流水线；基于标普500前100成分公司构建ECTs-100数据集，用于同时基准测试有据性与正确性；并考察"有意识无能力"（conscious incompetence）这一失败模式，即LLM需识别证据不足并拒绝生成无支撑的幻觉内容。

Result: 实证结果表明，LLM在有据性（groundedness）方面表现良好，但在正确性（correctness）方面存在明显局限；此外，信息不足（informational insufficiency）构成额外挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM金融文档有据分析提供了无需专家标注的可扩展评估范式与ECTs-100基准，揭示了当前LLM在正确性与信息不足情形下的能力短板，为后续改进提供方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been increasingly used for financial document analysis, including earnings call transcripts (ECTs). Beyond generating standalone claims, users increasingly prefer grounded analyses that pair claims with verifiable citations from source documents to enable independent validation. However, evaluating such analytical claims typically requires extensive expert annotation, which is costly and difficult to scale, and real-world financial analysis commonly involves long context-question-answer triplets, further increasing task complexity. To address these challenges and benchmark the current landscape of grounded analysis by LLMs, we propose a numeric evidence evaluation method that enables groundedness assessment without reliance on expert annotation. We also introduce an automated dataset construction pipeline and construct ECTs-100 from the top 100 constituents of the S&P 500 to support benchmark of both groundedness and correctness. In addition, we examine conscious incompetence, a practical failure mode in financial analysis in which LLMs must detect when available evidence is insufficient and refrain from producing unsupported hallucinations. Empirical results show that LLMs perform well in groundedness but face notable limitations in correctness, with informational insufficiency presenting an additional challenge.

</details>


### [131] [The Devil Is in the Reconstruction Loss Scale: Rethinking Optimization in LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00983)
*Chao Li,Shigeng Wang,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了基于学习的LLM训练后量化(PTQ)中的优化动态，发现并命名了"优化失衡"现象，并提出RMSE变体作为改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的PTQ方法多为基于学习的方法，通过梯度下降优化辅助量化参数，普遍采用MSE作为重建损失函数，但其诱导的优化行为很大程度上未被探索。作者希望深入理解序贯量化中从第一阶段到最后阶段的优化演化过程。

Method: 对基于学习的PTQ方案进行整体性考察，在多种代表性PTQ方法、LLM家族、模型规模、架构、量化设置和任务上开展大量实证研究；同时从理论上分析MSE如何将重建损失尺度转化为不均匀的梯度幅度，并推导RMSE变体（样本、通道、token、元素层面）通过隐式梯度归一化实现阶段间优化强度的解耦。

Result: 一致性地发现"优化失衡"现象：各阶段重建损失幅度差异巨大，伴随MSE下高度不均匀的梯度幅度和参数更新。作者将损失幅度的跨阶段范围定义为"重建损失尺度"，并揭示MSE将意外巨大的重建损失尺度转化为高度不均匀的梯度幅度，导致各量化阶段的优化强度不均。RMSE变体作为即插即用替代方案显著优于MSE。

Conclusion: 提出了改进基于学习PTQ的一般性原则：各阶段的优化强度应与重建损失尺度解耦。理论上证明在样本、通道、token和元素层面定义的RMSE变体通过隐式梯度归一化自然实现这一原则，可作为MSE的即插即用替代，显著提升性能。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) methods typically use sequential quantization that partitions a pre-trained LLM into a series of units (e.g., transformer blocks), with one unit quantized at each stage. State-of-the-art PTQ methods are predominantly learning-based, optimizing auxiliary quantization parameters (e.g., scaling factors, rotation matrices, clipping thresholds, and adapters) via gradient descent to minimize a reconstruction loss. A common practice is to use mean squared error (MSE) as the reconstruction loss function, yet its induced optimization behavior remains largely unexplored. In this work, we take a holistic view of sequential quantization and systematically investigate how optimization evolves from the first quantization stage to the last, aiming for a deep understanding of optimization in learning-based PTQ schemes. Through extensive empirical studies spanning representative learning-based PTQ methods, LLM families, model scales, architectures, quantization settings and various tasks, we consistently uncover Optimization Imbalance: reconstruction loss magnitudes vary dramatically across stages, accompanied by highly uneven gradient magnitudes and parameter updates under MSE. We term the cross-stage range of loss magnitudes the reconstruction loss scale, and reveal that MSE translates the unexpectedly large reconstruction loss scale into highly uneven gradient magnitudes, which in turn lead to uneven optimization strength across quantization stages. This finding suggests a general principle for improving learning-based PTQ: optimization strength across stages should be decoupled from the reconstruction loss scale. Theoretically, we show that root mean squared error (RMSE) variants defined at the sample, channel, token, and element levels naturally realize this principle through implicit gradient normalization, outperforming MSE significantly as a drop-in replacement.

</details>


### [132] [Distilling Directional Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00997)
*Jungseob Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Seongtae Hong,Seungyoon Lee,Chanjun Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出方向性标签蒸馏，让冻结教师模型在已知方向对候选答案打分，用最优候选作为学生训练目标，缓解教师生成答案的方向性局限传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏旨在将大模型的事实知识迁移到小模型以实现高效部署。但教师模型可能只在某个关系方向上能回忆知识，而在反向（被请求的方向）却无法生成答案。若直接蒸馏其生成的答案，会把这种方向性缺陷传递给学生。同一教师却能在其掌握的方向上识别出正确答案。

Method: 提出方向性标签蒸馏（directional label distillation）：冻结的教师模型在它已知的方向上对候选答案进行打分，得分最高的候选成为学生的训练目标。并在父母-子女等关系上，将已知方向打分与请求方向打分对比，加入针对姓名先验的校正。

Result: 在父母-子女事实上，已知方向打分给出的标签比请求方向更准确，即使做了姓名先验的调优校正亦然。经先验校正后，较优方向取决于事实本身而非模板，并在主实体为父母而非子女的挖掘事实上发生反转。在保留评估用儿童正向事实的情况下，用已知方向标签训练的学生在其训练查询上的开放式准确率比用先验校正反向标签的学生高13至15个百分点。学生几乎能复现全部选定标签，准确率大体随标签质量变化。

Conclusion: 方向性验证通过提供更准确的训练目标，缓解了教师生成答案向学生传递错误的问题。该标签优势在未筛选查询以及候选检索时不插入正确答案的情况下依然成立。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to transfer the factual knowledge of large language models to smaller models for efficient deployment. Yet a teacher may recall a relation in one direction while failing to generate the answer in the reverse direction. Distillation from its generated answers can therefore propagate this directional limitation to the student. The same teacher can nevertheless recognize such an answer by scoring the relation in the direction it knows. We introduce directional label distillation, in which frozen teachers score candidate answers in that known direction and the best-scoring candidate becomes the student's training target. On facts about parents and their children, known-direction scoring yields more accurate labels than scoring the requested direction, even after tuned corrections for name priors. With prior-corrected scores, the better direction depends on the facts rather than the template, and reverses on mined facts whose notable entity is the parent rather than the child. With the evaluated children's forward facts withheld, students trained on known-direction labels improve open-ended accuracy on their trained queries by 13 to 15 points over students trained on prior-corrected reverse labels. After generated answers are matched to a fixed name list by lexical similarity, students reproduce nearly all selected labels. Their accuracy largely follows label quality. The label advantage holds on unscreened queries and when candidates are retrieved without inserting correct answers. Our findings show that directional verification mitigates the transfer of errors from teacher-generated answers to students by providing more accurate training targets. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/directional-verification.

</details>


### [133] [Scaling and Distilling Text Embeddings for Better Diffusibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01016)
*Zekai Zhang,Yunjie Tian,Yanjin He,Xiaoyan Zhang,Dongdi Zhao,Qing Qu,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续扩散语言模型（DLMs）中何种文本嵌入最适合作为潜空间，并通过知识蒸馏构建更"可扩散"的嵌入空间，显著提升了生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型将潜在扩散应用于连续的文本嵌入，但关键问题是：哪种嵌入能构成最佳的潜空间（即最具可扩散性）？作者发现，即使是更强的嵌入模型（如T5Gemma-2），其原始嵌入仍然过于"判别性"，使得合理的替代词嵌入彼此分离，导致生成过程在采样不完美时难以命中有效嵌入，最终落到无效嵌入上。因此需要一个更连通、更易扩散的潜空间。

Method: 作者首先在同一嵌入模型家族内（T5 → T5Gemma-1 → T5Gemma-2）进行规模扩展搜索，发现更强模型能大幅提升生成性能。针对原始T5Gemma-2嵌入判别性过强的问题，作者提出将T5Gemma-2蒸馏到一个学生编码器中，学生学习教师解码出的概率作为软标签。借助软标签学习，学生编码器将替代词嵌入拉近，同时保留编码-解码机制，从而形成更连通、更具可扩散性的潜空间。

Result: 蒸馏后的嵌入优于原始T5Gemma-2嵌入。在OpenWebText上，该中等规模的DLM在真实文本熵下取得Gen. PPL 17.8（真实文本PPL为15.4），在Gen. PPL指标上超过了GPT-2-M。

Conclusion: 嵌入模型的选择与塑造对连续扩散语言模型至关重要。更强的嵌入模型能改善生成，但原始嵌入的强判别性会损害扩散采样；通过软标签知识蒸馏，可获得更具连通性和可扩散性的潜空间，从而显著提升生成质量并超越GPT-2-M。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) language generation. Recent advances in continuous DLMs, which apply latent diffusion to continuous text embeddings, raise a practical question: which embedding makes the best latent space, i.e., the most diffusible? To answer this, we search through different embeddings and find that scaling the embedding model to stronger ones within the same family (T5 to T5Gemma-1 to T5Gemma-2) greatly improves generative performance. But the raw T5Gemma-2 embeddings are still not optimal. They are so discriminative that even the embeddings of plausible alternative words are separated, which makes the generation vulnerable to imperfect sampling. Consequently, continuous diffusion often fails to reach any of them and ends up at an invalid embedding instead. To address this, we distill T5Gemma-2 into a student encoder that learns the teacher's decoded probabilities as soft labels. Learning from such soft labels makes the student pull the alternative embeddings closer while maintaining the encoding-decoding mechanism. The distilled embeddings form a more connected and diffusible latent space, improving over the vanilla T5Gemma-2 embeddings. As a result, our medium-sized DLM achieves Gen. PPL 17.8 (against real-text PPL 15.4) at real-text entropy on OpenWebText, outperforming GPT-2-M on Gen. PPL.

</details>


### [134] [It Takes Workflows to Evolve Better Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01026)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen,Yangyi Chen,Zhenhailong Wang,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 FloWright，把工作流本身当作优化载体，用分层结构感知奖励让多个智能体自演化/共演化，并配 DataWright 数据加硬，在多个任务上提升小开源模型性能最多 +7.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一 LLM 难以处理复杂现实任务，多智能体工作流成为解法；但已有方法只训练工作流生成器，其他构建/执行智能体固定不变，而结果依赖所有智能体。困难在于智能体相互耦合、最终只有一个稀疏结果分数，无法定位是哪个智能体导致失败。同时现有工作流数据过于简单，单智能体即可完成。

Method: FloWright：以工作流为“外壳/载体”进行优化，提出分层、结构感知的奖励范式，使一个角色可自我演化、两个及以上角色可协同演化，且不需要额外模型、标签或执行。DataWright：自适应数据加硬方法，把现有数据集转成难度更高的“工作流级”任务。

Result: 在文档、幻灯片、图表、代码、数学、金融等任务上，用 FloWright 训练的小型开源模型性能最高提升 +7.41%；协同演化（+5.03%）优于只优化单个角色（+2.83%）。

Conclusion: 将优化范围从单一生成器扩展到多个耦合智能体是可行且收益更大的，配合数据加硬可让多智能体工作流真正发挥价值。

Abstract: Tackling complex real-world tasks can exceed the capabilities of a single large language model (LLM), motivating the use of multi-agent workflows that coordinate specialized agents to work together on these tasks. Recent methods train LLMs to construct better workflows from execution outcomes, but they optimize only the workflow generator, while the other agents that build or execute each workflow remain fixed even though every outcome depends on all of them. However, extending training beyond the generator is challenging: the agents are coupled, and a workflow's outcome is a single sparse score that cannot tell which agent causes a failure. We propose FloWright, which leverages the workflow as a harness to optimize workflows. By introducing a hierarchical, structure-aware reward paradigm, FloWright enables one role to self-evolve and two or more roles to co-evolve, with no additional models, labels, or executions. Considering the limitation that workflows are commonly trained and evaluated on data that a single agent can already handle, we further propose DataWright, an adaptive data hardening approach that converts existing datasets into workflow-level tasks with increased difficulty. Across document, slide, chart, code, math, and finance tasks, small open models trained with FloWright achieve improved performance by up to $+7.41\%$, with co-evolving ($+5.03\%$) more roles gaining more than optimizing one of them alone ($+2.83\%$). Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/FloWright/.

</details>


### [135] [LawCompass: Navigating from Legal QA to Multi-Agent Deep Research with Grounded Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01027)
*Xiaoxia Cheng,Linnan Wang,Jiahao Ma,Zhichuan Ye,Xuemei Zhou,Chuanyu Tong,Bo Jiang,Qing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LawCompass 是一个证据支撑的法律助手，通过多智能体深度研究实现从法律问答到报告级综合的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律助手多局限于多轮对话问答，无法支持需要系统性证据检索、多步推理和报告级综合的复杂法律任务。

Method: 提出 LawCompass，包含三项任务导向功能：法律问答（精准且证据支撑的回答）、专业检索（通过查询改写结构化探索法规与判例）、深度研究（多智能体工作流分解复杂任务并生成研究报告），并在所有模块中保持显式引用链接。

Result: 评估结果表明 LawCompass 提供了将对话式 AI 转化为可信、证据支撑的法律研究助手的实用且可扩展的范式。

Conclusion: LawCompass 实现了从标准法律问答到多智能体深度研究的跨越，支持用户依据原始法律来源直接核验系统输出。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have significantly democratized access to legal information. Nevertheless, most existing legal assistants remain confined to multi-turn conversational QA, failing to support complex legal tasks that require systematic evidence retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and report-level synthesis. In this paper, we present LawCompass, an evidence-grounded legal assistant that navigates the transition from standard Legal QA to multi-agent deep research. LawCompass provides three task-oriented functions: Legal QA, which delivers precise, evidence-backed answers to legal questions; Professional Retrieval, which enables structured exploration of statutes and judicial cases via query rewriting; and Deep Research, which employs a multi-agent workflow to decompose complex legal tasks and synthesize comprehensive research reports. Crucially, LawCompass maintains explicit citation links across all modules, empowering users to directly verify system outputs against original legal sources. Evaluation results demonstrate that LawCompass provides a practical and scalable paradigm for transforming conversational AI into trustworthy and evidence-grounded legal research assistance.

</details>


### [136] [Sentence Specificity Scores for Collaborative Technical Documentation: A Domain-Transfer Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01046)
*Rocker D'Antonio,Thomas Benton Townsend,Dimitrios Michael Manias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文审计了技术文档上句子特定性（specificity）评分工具的可靠性，并测试了生成后再评分能否帮助在固定的LLM候选修订稿中择优。结果显示不同预测器的评分相关性差异大，且仅SpeciTeller排序在Gemma集上带来显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作依赖共享语境，而技术文档是语境在人机协同中延续的载体；句子特定性（细节的数量与精确度）决定了文档所捕捉和传达的信息质量。然而现有特定性评分预测器是否可靠、是否能用于生成后择优尚不清楚，因此需要审计这些评分工具的可解释性与决策价值。

Method: 在Wikipedia和三个技术文档语料上，对两个预测器（固定的通用领域预测器SpeciTeller，以及固定在作者仓库实现中、针对目标域适配的Ko等人预测器）进行句子特定性评分对比审计；考察语料顺序、同句秩一致性，并施加严格过滤与token长度调整；进一步在Gemma与GPT-OSS-120B两组固定LLM生成候选上测试生成后评分排序对"方向正确选择"的影响，采用源案例bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 两个预测器产生不同的语料排序，同句秩一致性在-0.066到0.510之间；严格过滤与长度调整改变但不统一这些模式。在Gemma集上，SpeciTeller排序将方向正确选择从71.7%提升到83.3%（+11.7点，95%区间+1.7至+21.7）；在GPT-OSS-120B集上从51.7%升至56.7%（+5.0点，95%区间-6.7至+16.7），且GPT-OSS-120B所有主要单一评分区间均包含零。

Conclusion: 特定性评分的解读与决策价值高度依赖于所选预测器和候选集合；生成后择优的收益并不稳定，GPT-OSS-120B上无显著增益。研究者与实践者应谨慎对待特定性评分工具的直接比较与使用。

Abstract: Collaboration depends on shared context, and technical documentation is one way that context persists across people and AI teammates. Specificity, the amount and exactness of detail expressed in language, shapes what information documentation captures and how precisely that information is communicated. This work audits sentence-specificity scoring artifacts on technical documentation and tests whether scores applied only after generation help choose among fixed LLM-generated revisions. Across Wikipedia and three technical-documentation corpora, the fixed general-domain predictor SpeciTeller and the pinned post-publication author-repository implementation of Ko et al.'s target-adapted predictor produce different corpus orders and same-sentence rank agreement from -0.066 to 0.510. Strict filtering and token-length adjustment change these patterns without reconciling them. In the Gemma set, SpeciTeller ranking raises direction-valid selection from 71.7% to 83.3% (+11.7 points; 95% source-case bootstrap interval +1.7 to +21.7); in the GPT-OSS-120B set, SpeciTeller ranking raises direction-valid selection from 51.7% to 56.7% (+5.0 points; 95% source-case bootstrap interval -6.7 to +16.7), and every primary single-score GPT-OSS-120B interval includes zero. These findings tie score interpretation and decision value to the predictor and candidate set.

</details>


### [137] [Capturing In-Context Learning Dynamics with Task Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01054)
*Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Bohan Liu,Sanchit Sinha,Wenqian Ye,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Task Operator (TO)，将 ICL 中注意力头输出的仿射变换关系蒸馏为对注意力输出投影的解析更新，从而在不扩展上下文的情况下高效复现 ICL 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL 每次推理都需处理全部示例，部署效率低，且其工作机制尚不清晰；已有压缩方法依赖输入无关的固定激活向量，在输出依赖细粒度输入交互的复杂任务上失效。

Method: 分析 ICL 前向过程，发现每个注意力头输出是其上下文掩码对应量的仿射变换，且该变换参数在给定任务的不同样本间经验稳定；据此提出 Task Operator，将该变换作为解析导出的更新回放到注意力输出投影上。

Result: 在词汇、算法与推理任务上，TO 总体性能优于现有方法，显著缩小 zero-shot 与 ICL 的差距；提取的知识集中于任务特定的稀疏电路（跨层与位置）；对不同示教批次求平均算子可实现 many-shot 扩展而不扩大上下文窗口。

Conclusion: ICL 可被理解为一种稳定的、任务特定的仿射变换，TO 通过解析方式将其注入模型权重，为高效部署与理解 ICL 机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables language models to perform new tasks from demonstrations without weight updates. However, every ICL inference requires processing the full set of examples, resulting in inefficient deployments, and how ICL works mechanistically is not fully understood. Prior work compresses ICL into fixed activation vectors extracted from specific layers or positions, but these input-independent interventions fail on complex tasks where the output depends on fine-grained interactions with the input. By analyzing the ICL forward pass, we show that each attention head's output is an affine transformation of its context-masked counterpart, and that the parameters of this transformation are empirically stable across samples for a given task. Building on this, we introduce Task Operator (TO), which replays this transformation as an analytically derived update to the attention output projection. Across lexical, algorithmic, and reasoning tasks, TO achieves the best overall performance among prior methods and substantially narrows the gap between zero-shot inference and ICL. We further show that the extracted knowledge concentrates in a task-specific sparse circuit across layers and positions, and that averaging operators from disjoint demonstration batches enables effective many-shot scaling without expanding the context window. Our code is available at https://github.com/gzxiong/task_operator.

</details>


### [138] [JoinGR: Learning to Traverse Join Graphs for Table Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01064)
*Sandipan De,Abhijit Chakraborty,Sambaran Bandyopadhyay,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JOINGR是一种连接感知的表检索方法，将数据库连接图作为检索空间，通过图遍历与查询条件评分器提升多跳表检索召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密表检索器独立地对模式元素排名，忽略了关键证据来源：某些必需表未在问题中出现，只有通过与已相关表的连接关系才能被识别，导致多跳表需求场景下检索效果不佳。

Method: 将列表示为图节点，表内关系与外键关系表示为带类型的边，以数据库连接图作为检索空间。给定问题后，先选取语义相似的锚点表，再用查询条件评分器遍历连接边，并将边上的累积贡献聚合为表分数。评分器是在冻结的查询、节点和边嵌入之上的轻量级MLP，使用针对金标准表的成对间隔损失训练。

Result: 在BIRD和Spider数据集上与最强检索基线相当；在具有多跳表需求的企业级挑战基准BEAVER上，召回率显著优于稠密检索与重排序基线；跨域实验表明学习到的评分器可跨基准迁移。

Conclusion: JOINGR通过将连接图纳入检索过程，有效解决了隐式必需表的识别问题，其学习到的评分器具备跨数据集的可迁移性，说明该方法捕捉到了可复用的连接图遍历行为。

Abstract: Retrieving the right tables is a prerequisite for Text-to-SQL over realistic databases. Dense table retrievers rank schema elements independently, but this ignores a key source of evidence: some required tables are not mentioned in the question and become identifiable only through their join relationships to already relevant tables. We introduce JOINGR, a join-aware table retrieval method that treats the database join graph as the retrieval space. Columns are represented as graph nodes, while intra-table and foreign-key relationships are represented as typed edges. Given a question, JOINGR selects semantically similar anchor tables, traverses join edges with a query-conditioned scorer, and aggregates the resulting edge deposits into table scores. The scorer is a lightweight MLP on top of frozen query, node, and edge embeddings, trained with a pairwise margin loss over gold tables. On BIRD and Spider datasets, JOINGR is competitive with the strongest retrieval baselines. On BEAVER, a challenging enterprise benchmark with multi-hop table requirements, JOINGR substantially improves recall over dense retrieval and re-ranking baselines. Cross-domain experiments show that the learned scorer transfers across benchmarks, indicating that the method captures reusable joingraph traversal behavior.

</details>


### [139] [Probe with Participation Trophies: Random-Reward RL as a Probe of LLM Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01066)
*Yu Mao,Lei Yu,Zining Zhu,Yusheng Zheng,Haohang Li,Freda Shi,Yutong Yin,Zhaoran Wang,Jingcheng Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将"虚假奖励悖论"与模型的"可达性"（reachability）相联系，提出随机奖励强化学习（random-reward RL）可作为探测大语言模型能力的工具，用以回应"探测性能究竟揭示模型何种信息"这一长期争论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现，即使使用随机奖励，大语言模型的性能也能提升。对此存在两种主流解释：一种归因于RL训练中的特定机制，另一种归因于数据污染。作者希望提出第三种视角，即随机奖励RL能够探测模型在当前状态下、给定约束下进一步训练所能达到的能力上限（可达性），从而解决基于可解码性的探测中"标签泄漏"的核心问题——成功的探测究竟揭示了模型能力，还是探测本身学会了任务。

Method: 作者以OLMo模型为研究对象，在合成算术任务上比较不同检查点的表现。选取两个在当前准确率相同（均为3.5%）的OLMo检查点，在相同的正确性奖励RL下观察其最佳运行的准确率差异；同时考察OLMo在预训练与中期训练各阶段的检查点，分析不同训练阶段对RL的响应规律。此外，还进行了数字掩码的监督微调（SFT）分析以验证结论是否独立于特定RL机制。

Result: 两个当前准确率相同的OLMo检查点（均为3.5%），在相同的正确性奖励RL下最佳运行分别达到8.5%和55%，说明当前性能相同并不意味着可达性相同。对OLMo各阶段检查点的分析揭示了三种不同的训练响应机制：训练早期，即使奖励正确答案，RL改善也很小；预训练后期，奖励正确答案开始有效，而随机奖励仍然很弱；进入中期训练后，即使随机奖励也能带来大幅提升。数字掩码SFT分析中出现了类似的顺序，表明该模式并非特定RL机制所独有。

Conclusion: 随机奖励RL提供了一种独特视角来理解训练能让大语言模型做什么，因为其奖励信号不包含任何关于答案正确性的信息。通过探究"在没有正确性反馈的情况下训练能达到什么"，该方法解决了基于可解码性的探测中标签泄漏的核心问题——即成功的探测究竟揭示了模型已有能力还是学会了任务本身。虚假奖励RL可用于探测模型的可达性，即超越当前性能所反映的、在特定约束下进一步训练所能达到的状态。

Abstract: We connect the spurious-reward paradox to a model's reachability and propose random-reward reinforcement learning (RL) as a useful tool for the probing enterprise, addressing a decade-long debate over what probing performance actually reveals about a model. There are two prevailing explanations for the surprising finding that even random rewards can improve the performance of large language models (LLMs): one attributes the gains to particular mechanisms within RL training; the other to data contamination. Our results motivate a different view: spurious-reward RL can probe a model's reachability, or what further training can attain from its current state under specified constraints, beyond what is reflected in its current performance. Two OLMo checkpoints with the same accuracy on synthetic arithmetic (3.5%), for example, reach 8.5% and 55% in their best runs under the same correctness-rewarded RL. Examining OLMo checkpoints across pre-training and mid-training reveals three distinct regimes of training response: early on, RL produces little improvement even when correct answers are rewarded; later in pre-training, rewarding correct answers becomes effective while random rewards remain weak; and, upon entering mid-training, even random rewards can produce large gains. A similar ordering appears in a number-masked supervised fine-tuning (SFT) analysis of these checkpoints, suggesting that the pattern is not specific to a particular RL mechanism. Moreover, RL with random rewards offers a distinctive perspective on what training can make an LLM do, since its reward signal supplies no information about which answers are correct. By asking what training can attain without correctness feedback, it addresses the label-leakage side of a central problem in decodability-based probing: whether a successful probe reveals the model's capabilities or learns the task itself.

</details>


### [140] [Precision over Scale: A Polish-Silesian Benchmark and a Translation System Outperforming Open-Source and Commercial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01082)
*Grzegorz Kulik,Mikołaj Pokrywka,Adam Jatowt,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究波兰语-西里西亚语方言机器翻译，构建新测试集SiLTT，发现规则系统在方言场景下优于神经与微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方言机器翻译因数据稀缺和语言变异大而困难，标准基准多假设规范且经过编辑的文本，无法捕捉方言特性，因此需要针对性的测试集与系统评估。

Method: 采用神经与规则两种系统进行波兰语-西里西亚语翻译，在新建的Pol-Szl测试集SiLTT上评估，并与BOUQuET、FLORES基准对比；同时对TranslateGemma在精选数据集上微调。

Result: 规则系统在SiLTT和BOUQuET上表现一贯最佳；微调后的TranslateGemma优于强神经基线，但在方言场景中仍未超越规则系统。

Conclusion: 规则方法在低资源方言翻译中仍具优势，作者公开SiLTT测试集与最佳神经模型以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Dialectal machine translation remains challenging due to limited data and strong linguistic variation not captured by standard benchmarks, which often assume standardized and well-edited text.
