We study Polish-Silesian MT using neural and rule-based systems, evaluating on SiLTT - a new Pol-Szl testset, alongside established BOUQuET and FLORES benchmarks. Results show our rule-based system is consistently strongest on SiLTT and BOUQuET datasets and that TranslateGemma fine-tuned on a curated dataset improves over strong neural baselines but does not surpass the rule-based system in dialectal settings. We release SiLTT and our best neural model to support further research.

</details>


### [141] [AgSpec: Pushing the Limits of Retrieval-Based Speculative Decoding in Coding Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01108)
*Sumin Lee,Sukmin Cho,Suengjae Lim,Youngjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AgSpec 框架，为编码智能体流水线中的检索式投机解码补齐语料库与草稿长度策略，吞吐量较自回归解码最高提升 4.37×（batch=1）和 4.76×（batch=16）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索式投机解码通过复制现有文本的续写来草拟 token，适合反复重现代码、日志和早前尝试的编码智能体。但现有方法在智能体流水线中存在三点不足：可复用文本在其语料库中缺失，或存储形式与智能体实际输出不一致；草稿长度固定，忽视了接受长度因智能体而异且随轮次漂移的问题。

Method: AgSpec 从会话、工作区和全局三类语料库检索，保留进行中的会话轨迹，并按智能体的输出格式索引已打开的文件；用离线分析得到的上限约束各智能体的草稿长度，并依据验证反馈在线自适应调整长度。

Result: 在两个仓库级多智能体编码基准上，AgSpec 在多数评测设置中优于五种基于检索的草稿器和 EAGLE-3；batch size 为 1 时生成吞吐量相对自回归解码最高提升 4.37×，batch size 为 16 时最高 4.76×。在无仓库或无多智能体流水线的基准上依然有效。

Conclusion: AgSpec 通过补齐语料库与自适应草稿长度策略，显著提升编码智能体场景下的解码吞吐，且增益可推广到更广泛的编码智能体场景。

Abstract: Retrieval-based speculative decoding (SD) drafts tokens by copying continuations from existing text, which suits coding agents that repeatedly reproduce code, logs, and earlier attempts. Yet existing methods fall short in agent pipelines: much of the reusable text is missing from their corpora or stored in a form that differs from what the agent emits, and their draft lengths ignore that accept length varies across agents and drifts over turns. We present AgSpec, a framework that supplies the corpus and draft-length policies that existing retrieval engines lack in coding-agent pipelines. AgSpec retrieves from session, workspace, and global corpora, retaining the ongoing session trajectory and indexing opened files in the agent's emission format. It bounds each agent's draft length with an offline-profiled cap and adapts the length online from verification feedback. On two repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks, AgSpec outperforms five retrieval-based drafters and EAGLE-3 in most evaluated settings, raising generation throughput over autoregressive decoding up to 4.37$\times$ at batch size 1 and 4.76$\times$ at batch size 16. AgSpec also remains effective on benchmarks without a repository or a multi-agent pipeline, showing that its gains generalize to coding agents broadly.

</details>


### [142] [Madeleine: Learning Involuntary Recall for Conversational Memory from Simulated Lives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01118)
*Zhiyun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Madeleine，将长期对话记忆中的"关联"视为可学习的互信息，用 LLM 模拟人生生成训练数据，离线训练查询编码器，在线零 LLM 调用即可提升任意向量记忆检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话助手需要在恰当时机召回正确记忆，但最关键的记忆往往与用户当前话语并不相似。现有系统通过在写入或读取时调用 LLM 推理来恢复这种关联，代价是每个记忆库数百至上千次 LLM 调用、每次查询数千 token 上下文。作者认为这种"关联"本质上是一种可学习的相关性。

Method: 将记忆间的关联形式化为在人类生活展开方式下的逐点互信息。提出 Madeleine，学习摊销式的关联：离线阶段由 LLM 人生模拟器生成模拟人生，其中的线索-触发配对训练一个查询编码器，在冻结的相似度之上学习残差关联；在线阶段不调用任何 LLM，仅替换查询编码器即可接入任意向量记忆系统。

Result: 在 LoCoMo-Plus 官方协议下：（I）接入 HyperMem 时达到 66.6，为该协议下所有评测系统最高；（II）单独使用时以零 LLM 调用、约 1/21 的答案上下文达到 HyperMem 发布成绩（52.4 对 52.9）；（III）使 T-Mem 提升 26.2 分，在两个系统中均显著优于同一未训练主干，并在 4B 主干上保持普通 QA 性能不变。

Conclusion: 关联可作为可学习的相关性被离线摊销学习，Madeleine 在不增加在线 LLM 开销的前提下显著提升记忆检索表现，并可即插即用地增强现有向量记忆系统。

Abstract: A long-term conversational assistant must recall the right memory at the right moment, yet the memory that matters most is often not similar to what the user says now. Current systems recover such associations by letting an LLM reason at write or read time, at a cost of hundreds to over a thousand LLM calls per memory bank and up to several thousand context tokens per query. We argue that association is a learnable relevance: the pointwise mutual information of memories under how human lives unfold. We introduce Madeleine, which learns amortized association: offline, an LLM life simulator writes simulated lives, whose cue-trigger pairs teach a query encoder a residual association on top of frozen similarity; online, it calls no LLM and plugs into any vector memory by replacing only the query encoder. On LoCoMo-Plus under the official protocol, Madeleine (I) reaches 66.6 when plugged into HyperMem, the highest among all systems evaluated under this protocol; (II) used alone, reaches the score of HyperMem as released (52.4 vs. 52.9) with zero LLM calls and about 1/21 of its answer context; and (III) lifts T-Mem by 26.2 points, significantly outperforms the same untrained backbone inside both systems, and leaves ordinary QA intact on the 4B backbone.

</details>


### [143] [Counting and Min-Cost Encoding for Tokenization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01127)
*Shuming Shi,Xiang Zhang,Hao Yu,Wenbo Fei,Changjian Wang,Zhan Wang,Guoqing Pang,Guangye Yu,Quan Lu,Ning Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CNF-MCE分词方案，通过全局最小代价编码提升token效率，在保持下游性能的同时显著改善压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大语言模型依赖分词器将文本编码为token序列，不同分词器对同一文本产生的序列长度差异很大。在模型架构固定时，更短的token序列意味着更低的推理时间，因此提升token效率具有重要意义。

Method: 提出分词器训练方法CNF（Counting and Filtering）和文本编码算法MCE（Min-Cost Encoding）。MCE在文本片段上定义代价函数，通过全局最小化整体分词代价确定最佳切分；CNF先直接统计有效子串构建原始词表，再用MCE对训练语料分词后，基于实际token使用情况经过滤步骤构建最终词表。MCE不依赖BPE的合并列表或UnigramLM的token概率。

Result: 在六个文本类别和两个词表规模组上，CNF-MCE均比所评估的BPE分词器压缩更好。250K词表时，英文网页文本压缩率较o200k_base和qwen250k分别提升26%和30%。词表扩展至1M时，较BPE token效率提升超60%，词表利用率从52.9%升至96.9%。从零训练的1.8B和8B规模模型在11个基准上平均性能与使用BPE分词器的模型相当。

Conclusion: CNF-MCE在显著提升token效率的同时，能保持有竞争力的下游性能，优于BPE且适用性广泛。

Abstract: Mainstream large language models rely on a tokenizer to encode text into a token sequence. Different tokenizers may yield token sequences of substantially different lengths for the same text. With a fixed model architecture, shorter token sequences correspond to lower inference time. We propose a tokenizer training approach named Counting and Filtering (CNF) and a text encoding algorithm called Min-Cost Encoding (MCE). MCE defines a cost function over a text segment, and determines the best segmentation by globally minimizing the overall segmentation cost. CNF builds a raw vocabulary by directly counting valid substrings, and then constructs the final vocabulary through a filtering step based on actual token usage when segmenting the training corpus with MCE. The CNF-MCE conbination offers several advantages over BPE, including higher token efficiency, greater scalability, and lower dependency. Across six text categories and two vocabulary-size groups, CNF-MCE consistently achieves better compression than the evaluated BPE tokenizers. With a 250K vocabulary, CNF-MCE increases compression rate by 26% and 30% on English web text over the o200k_base and qwen250k tokenizers. Experiments scaling the vocabulary to 1M entries on English web text demonstrate sustained improvements over BPE, with a token efficiency improvement of over 60% and vocabulary utilization rising from 52.9% to 96.9%. The MCE algorithm does not depend on a merge list (as in BPE) or token probability (as in UnigramLM), making it applicable to a wide range of vocabularies, including those built from BPE, UnigramLM, CNF, and others. Language models trained from scratch at the 1.8B and 8B scales achieve comparable average performance to models using the BPE tokenizers across 11 benchmarks. These results demonstrate that CNF-MCE can improve token efficiency significantly while maintaining competitive downstream performance.

</details>


### [144] [BanglaDial-Abuse: A Corpus-Grounded Dataset for Regional Dialect Identification in Abusive Bangla Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01150)
*Hasin Almas Sifat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BanglaDial-Abuse，一个包含1000句、均分四类（标准孟加拉语及Chattagram、Sylhet、Barishal三种方言）的孟加拉语辱骂文本区域方言识别数据集，并通过语料驱动的合成方法构建，用于四分类方言识别研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语自然语言处理中，区域语言变体（尤其是非正式、非标准文本）仍是重要挑战；同时辱骂性文本的方言差异研究缺乏专门资源。因此需要一个平衡覆盖多区域方言的孟加拉语辱骂文本数据集来支持方言识别研究。

Method: 构建Balanced孟加拉文字脚本数据集BanglaDial-Abuse，含1,000句，四类语言变体（标准孟加拉语、Chattagram、Sylhet、Barishal）各250句。采用"语料库支撑的合成"流程，在保持原有敌意/辱骂语义的前提下，引入代词、所有格、动词形态、否定、疑问结构、后置词、词汇及孟加拉文拼写惯例等方面的区域差异。主要任务为四分类区域方言识别（非二分类辱骂检测）。

Result: 描述性分析显示四类句长分布大致可比，但词汇空间部分分化；两两Jaccard词汇相似度介于0.37至0.56之间。数据集通过Zenodo以CC BY 4.0许可公开。

Conclusion: 当前版本定位为研究与原型开发语料，而非经母语者验证的黄金标准语言资源。关键词：孟加拉语、方言识别、区域方言、辱骂语言、低资源NLP、Chattagram、Sylhet、Barishal、数据集。

Abstract: Regional linguistic variation remains an important challenge for Bangla natural language processing, particularly in informal and non-standard text. This paper introduces BanglaDial-Abuse, a balanced Bengali-script dataset developed for regional dialect identification in abusive and hostile Bangla text. The dataset contains 1,000 sentences distributed equally across four linguistic varieties: Standard Bangla, Chattagram, Sylhet, and Barishal, with 250 samples per class. The resource was constructed using a corpus-grounded synthetic procedure incorporating regional variation in pronouns, possessive forms, verb morphology, negation, interrogative structures, postpositions, vocabulary, and Bengali-script spelling conventions while preserving the underlying hostile or abusive meaning. Descriptive analysis shows broadly comparable sentence-length distributions but partially distinct lexical spaces across the four classes. Pairwise Jaccard vocabulary similarity ranges from 0.37 to 0.56. The primary task is four-class regional dialect identification rather than binary abusive-text detection. The dataset is publicly available through Zenodo under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 license. The current version is intended as a research and prototyping corpus rather than a native-speaker-validated gold-standard linguistic resource. Keywords: Bangla, Bengali, dialect identification, regional dialect, abusive language, low-resource NLP, Chattagram, Sylhet, Barishal, dataset

</details>


### [145] [My FAULT: Self-Diagnosis as Credit Assignment in Self-Evolving Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01161)
*Yihua Zhu,Qianying Liu,Weixu Qiao,Xuan Ren,Weiwei Xu,Wenbo Li,Wei Wang,Ruijia Chen,Xinmiao Luan,Yin Luo,Hao Huang,Xiang Zheng,Hidetoshi Shimodaira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出FAULT方法，通过自诊断引导的终端信用再分配，将诊断出的错误转化为显式的步骤级信用，解决长程智能体强化学习中的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习依赖终端结果奖励，在长程任务中存在两个信用分配问题：一是相同结果的rollout组无法提供学习信号；二是终端奖励只能提供轨迹级反馈，难以识别导致失败的具体决策。

Method: FAULT通过检查诊断证据，从任务结果中学习相对错误成本，将诊断出的错误转化为以终端结果为锚点的显式步骤级信用。训练时策略与自诊断器协同演化，错误成本根据近期结果在线更新。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，FAULT从相同结果组中恢复学习信号，信号覆盖率达95%，而GRPO为41%、GiGPO为72%，并能更好地将信用定位到具体错误步骤。在两种模型规模下，FAULT在长程ALFWorld和WebShop任务上取得显著提升，在短程Search-based QA上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: FAULT有效解决了长程智能体强化学习中的信用分配难题，通过自诊断引导的终端信用再分配显著提升了学习信号覆盖率和长程任务表现。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for training large language model agents on multi-step tasks, yet reliance on terminal outcome rewards creates two credit-assignment problems, particularly in long-horizon tasks. First, same-outcome rollout groups provide no learning signal from terminal rewards. Second, terminal rewards provide only trajectory-wide feedback, making it difficult to identify which decisions caused a failure. Recent work supplements terminal rewards with finer-grained information from trajectory analysis, such as natural-language reflections on intermediate decisions and errors. However, natural-language diagnoses are difficult to use directly for credit assignment: their error claims may be unreliable, and they do not quantify how much each error should affect learning. We propose Self-Diagnosis-guided Terminal Credit Redistribution (FAULT), which turns diagnosed errors into explicit step-level credit anchored by terminal outcomes. FAULT checks diagnostic evidence and learns relative error costs from task outcomes. During training, the policy and self-diagnoser co-evolve, while error costs are updated online from recent outcomes. On ALFWorld, FAULT recovers learning signals from same-outcome groups, reaching 95% signal coverage versus 41% for GRPO and 72% for GiGPO, while better localizing credit to specific error steps. Across two model scales, FAULT delivers strong. improvements on the long-horizon ALFWorld and WebShop tasks while remaining competitive on short-horizon Search-based QA.

</details>


### [146] [HeadEdit: Calibrating Language Model Behavior Through the Frozen Unembedding Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01170)
*Zirui He,Haiyan Zhao,Jingyu Hu,Yinghao Wu,Chenxi Yuan,Yingcong Li,Yandong Bai,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HeadEdit：一种无梯度方法，通过unembedding矩阵校准语言模型行为，从配对补全中提取低秩行为子空间，实现隐式自适应引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐并不能消除语言模型的行为错误，如拒绝良性请求、调用不必要工具或屈服于虚假用户声明。现有方法多将此类错误视为计算问题，很少探索期望行为是否已编码在模型表示中。

Method: 基于行为相关信息即使在logits产生不良行为时仍可从最终隐藏状态线性解码的观察，提出HeadEdit。该方法从配对补全中提取低秩行为子空间，利用每个提示在该子空间中的坐标生成全词表范围的修正，实现隐式自适应引导，无需人工指定目标token或更新参数。

Result: HeadEdit在三个任务、三个模型家族的九个实验设置中均取得改进，推理开销可忽略且无系统性通用能力损失。它还揭示了与基于梯度的对齐方法的联系，其低维表示可部分预测偏好调优在未见提示上如何改变输出logits；调优后子空间可复用，无需重新提取或调优即可提升性能。

Conclusion: HeadEdit为通过unembedding矩阵校准模型行为提供了一种实用、轻量且可解释的途径。

Abstract: Alignment does not eliminate behavioral errors in language models. Models may still refuse benign requests, call unnecessary tools, or yield to false user claims. Current methods mitigate such errors as a computation problem, and rarely explore if the desired behavior is already encoded in the model's representation. Motivated by the observation that behavior-relevant information remains linearly decodable from the final hidden state even when the resulting logits produce the undesired behavior, we introduce HeadEdit, a gradient-free method that calibrates model behavior through the unembedding matrix. HeadEdit extracts a low-rank behavioral subspace from paired completions and uses each prompt's coordinates within it to generate a vocabulary-wide correction, thereby implementing implicitly adaptive steering without manually specified target tokens or parameter updates. HeadEdit improves all nine experimental settings across three tasks and three model families, with negligible inference overhead and no systematic loss of general capabilities. It also reveals a connection to gradient-based alignment. HeadEdit's low-dimensional representation partly predicts how preference tuning changes output logits on unseen prompts. The subspace learned from the model can also be reused after tuning, improving performance without re-extracting or retuning. These results show that HeadEdit provides a practical, lightweight, and interpretable way to calibrate model behavior through the unembedding matrix.

</details>


### [147] [Temporally-Resolved Token Attribution Reveals the Generation Dynamics of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01177)
*Darpan Aswal,Céline Hudelot*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DLIG，将积分梯度扩展到扩散语言模型的任意层与去噪步，用于token归因分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）逐步生成文本，其内部机制不透明，缺乏低成本的归因工具来检验机制假设，而现有积分梯度方法未适配DLM的层与去噪步结构。

Method: DLIG将积分梯度（IG）推广到DLM的任意层与去噪步，对输入提示针对自生成或固定补全进行归因；并论证其满足完备性、实现不变性、线性与对称保持等IG公理，作为干预分析的低成本补充。

Result: 在词义消歧、多跳图推理与句子补全任务上验证，揭示DLM如何跨越位置、层与去噪步利用输入信息。

Conclusion: DLIG为扩散语言模型提供了一种轻量、公理化的token归因方法，可作为机制假设的初步检验工具。

Abstract: This work presents Diffusion Layer Integrated Gradients (DLIG), a token attribution method for diffusion language models (DLMs) that extends Integrated Gradients (IG~\cite{sundararajan2017axiomatic}) to arbitrary layers and denoising steps. DLIG attributes a DLM's progressive commitment to a self-generated or fixed completion for an input prompt. We establish direct correspondences between DLIG and the IG axioms of completeness, implementation invariance, linearity, and symmetry preservation. As a lightweight complement to interventional analysis, DLIG provides an inexpensive first check of mechanistic hypotheses across the denoising trajectory. We demonstrate this on word-sense disambiguation, multi-hop graph reasoning, and sentence infilling, revealing how DLMs draw on inputs across positions, layers, and denoising steps.

</details>


### [148] [AGO AI Quality Gate: Evidence-First Release Decisions for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01218)
*Giulio Zeloni,Enrico Lo Conte,Salvatore Rionero,Giuseppe Santoro,Alessandro Rastelli,Fabio Sorrentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出面向企业RAG系统的"证据优先"质量门禁框架AGO，用四态决策、分层评分、分层贝塔-二项门控和强制元评估来应对数据缺失与LLM裁判不可靠问题，并在RAGBench上验证裁判层与门控效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署RAG系统时，需反复决定某个系统版本是"上线、修改还是阻断"，但可用证据不完整，且评估指标来自可能出错的LLM裁判。作者希望建立一个能显式处理缺失数据与裁判错误、并以概率方式量化回归风险的质量门禁框架。

Method: AGO框架包含四个组件：(1)四态决策模型，把数据缺失与裁判错误作为显式结果；(2)分层评分，结合确定性检查、本地护栏与结构化LLM评估；(3)分层贝塔-二项门控，概率化量化回归风险；(4)强制元评估协议，在LLM裁判影响决策前先验证其可靠性。由于实际项目数据属专有，作者在公开基准RAGBench（12个数据集、10万条标注RAG轨迹）上评估裁判层，并在固定随机种子的门控研究中模拟"回归/无变化/改进"三种情形。

Result: 在相同分层测试样本上（每裁判N=1200），低成本裁判gpt-4.1-nano检测不合规答案的能力仅略高于随机（AUROC 0.603 [0.570, 0.634]），尽管其协议输出格式完美；gpt-4o达到0.783 [0.756, 0.807]，但各领域表现仍在0.62–0.88之间波动。门控研究中，在回归情形下，"决策级"配置将不安全生产率降至22.2%–35.1%，而朴素门控为29.3%–41.8%。

Conclusion: 结果支持两个设计选择：LLM裁判的质量必须逐次评估（按项目/按领域测量），且仅凭点估计不足以作为发布决策依据，需要概率化、能显式处理缺失与错误的门禁机制。

Abstract: Enterprises adopting retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) face a recurring operational decision: promote, revise, or block a system version. The evidence is incomplete and the metrics come from fallible LLM judges. We report on AGO AI Quality Gate (AGO), an evidence-first quality-gate framework deployed in industrial RAG assessment engagements. AGO integrates four key components: a four-state decision model that treats missing data and judge errors as explicit outcomes; layered scoring combining deterministic checks, local guardrails, and structured LLM evaluation; a stratified beta-binomial gate that quantifies regression risk probabilistically; and a mandatory meta-evaluation protocol to validate the LLM judge before it influences decisions. Since engagement data is proprietary, we evaluate the judge layer on RAGBench, a public benchmark of 100k annotated RAG traces across 12 datasets. On identical stratified test samples (N=1200 per judge), a low-cost judge (gpt-4.1-nano) detects non-adherent answers barely above chance (AUROC 0.603 [0.570, 0.634]), despite producing flawless protocol output, while gpt-4o reaches 0.783 [0.756, 0.807] -- yet its per-domain performance still ranges from 0.62 to 0.88. A fixed-seed gate study spanning regression, no change, and improvement quantifies unsafe promotion, false-alarm cost, and improvement throughput. Under regression, the decision-grade profile reduces unsafe promotion to 22.2%-35.1%, against 29.3%-41.8% for a naive gate. These results support the design choices that judge quality must be measured per engagement and that point estimates alone are not a release decision.

</details>


### [149] [ASCRIBE: Atomic and Significance-Based Reasoning for Thai Clinical SOAP Note Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01234)
*Tarm Kalavantavanich,Teerawut Ponarchar,Pattaramanee Arsomngern,Jenta Wonglertsakul,Watcharakorn Chuthong,Chiraphat Boonnag,Knot Pipatsrisawat,Titipat Achakulvisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ASCRIBE 推理框架，通过为每个原子事实赋予临床重要性等级，提升 LLM 生成 SOAP 病历的可靠性与完整性，并发布首个泰语临床摘要基准 ThaiClinicBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成 SOAP 病历可减轻医生文书负担，但现有推理方法常遗漏重要临床信息并生成无依据内容；泰语领域还因缺乏公开数据集而进展受限。

Method: 受医生思维启发的 ASCRIBE 框架：在摘要前，先对对话中提取的每个原子事实赋予临床重要性等级，使通用 LLM 成为更可靠的分诊/记录助手。同时发布 ThaiClinicBench（首个真实就诊的脱敏泰语临床摘要基准）及基于真实病历构建的合成训练语料。

Result: 作为提示词，ASCRIBE 在 GPT-5.4 与 Gemini 3.1 Pro 上于医生对齐的 LLM 评判指标上优于思维链提示，在完整性指标上较标准提示最高提升 10.3 分；作为 GRPO 奖励，使仅用合成数据训练的 Gemma-4-E4B 在事实精确度上比肩 Gemini 3.1 Pro，并在完整性上超越它。

Conclusion: ASCRIBE 既可作为提示策略也可作为强化学习奖励，有效提升临床摘要的完整性与事实可靠性，并推动泰语临床摘要研究的发展。

Abstract: Automatic SOAP note generation can ease the documentation burden on physicians, but existing reasoning methods often omit clinically important information and generate unsupported content. Progress in Thai is further hindered by the lack of publicly available datasets. We propose ASCRIBE, a physician-inspired reasoning framework that ascribes a clinical-significance level to each extracted atomic fact in the conversation before summarization, making a general-purpose LLM a more reliable scribe. We also release ThaiClinicBench, the first de-identified Thai clinical summarization benchmark of real encounters, together with a synthetic training corpus derived from real clinical notes. As a prompt, ASCRIBE outperforms chain-of-thought prompting on GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 Pro across the physician-aligned LLM-judge metrics and improves on standard prompting by up to 10.3 points on the completeness LLM-judge metric. As a GRPO reward, it enables a Gemma-4-E4B model trained solely on synthetic data to match Gemini 3.1 Pro in factual precision and surpass it in completeness. Code and data can be found at https://github.com/loolootech/ascribe.

</details>


### [150] [Harness Annealing: Learning to Act with Less External Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01235)
*Yingxuan Yang,Huacan Chai,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“harness internalization（工具架内化）”目标与 HARNESS ANNEALING TRAINING (HAT) 方法，通过显式控制监督与渐进弱化工具架的教师轨迹课程，使语言智能体在撤销外部工具架支持后仍保持任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体依赖外部harness来跟踪状态、组织工作流并验证答案，harness还提供“调查什么、是否修订、何时停止”等控制决策。以往训练虽能提升任务表现，但这些控制决策仍依赖运行时干预，因此作者希望探究harness支持的经验能否让模型自身学会做出这些控制决策，从而在支持撤销后仍保持性能。

Method: 提出 HARNESS ANNEALING TRAINING (HAT)，将显式控制监督与一个课程学习方案结合，该课程基于在逐步弱化的harness下收集的教师轨迹。在SWE-QA和SWE-QA-Pro上使用9B与35B模型进行实验，并在四种部署harness配置下评估每个检查点。

Result: 经过退火选择的检查点仅使用工具即可取得接近各自起始检查点在完整harness下部署时的分数。收益随模型规模和部署配置而变化，进一步退火并不能一致地提升性能。

Conclusion: harness支持的经验有助于减少训练后智能体所需的运行时控制，表明控制权可以从外部harness部分转移到模型自身，但效果受模型规模与部署配置影响。

Abstract: Language agents rely on external harnesses to track state, organize workflows, and verify answers. Beyond providing tools and information, these harnesses supply control decisions about what to investigate, whether to revise, and when to stop. Training on successful harness-supported trajectories can improve task performance while leaving these decisions dependent on runtime intervention. We ask whether harness-supported experience can also teach the model to make these decisions, allowing the division of control to change as the model learns. We call this objective harness internalization: learning to assume specified control responsibilities while retaining task performance after the corresponding support is withdrawn. We introduce HARNESS ANNEALING TRAINING (HAT), which combines explicit control supervision with a curriculum over teacher trajectories collected under progressively weaker harnesses. Experiments with 9B and 35B models on SWE-QA and SWE-QA-Pro evaluate every checkpoint under four deployment harnesses. Selected annealed checkpoints operating with tools alone achieve scores close to those of their respective starting checkpoints deployed with the full harness. The benefits vary with model scale and deployment configuration, and further annealing does not uniformly improve performance. These findings suggest that harness-supported experience can help reduce the runtime control required by a trained agent.

</details>


### [151] [Evaluating the Robustness of Japanese LLMs to IME-Related and Typographical Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01241)
*Ryota Mibayashi,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文评估了日语大语言模型对日语特有的真实打字错误的鲁棒性，发现字符调换和字符替换会显著降低准确率，而IME转换、全角转换和同音转换影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在各类自然语言处理任务上表现优异，但其对拼写/打字错误的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，尤其是在日语中——日语文本输入涉及多种书写系统和IME转换，错误形式更为复杂多样。因此需要针对日语特有的真实打字错误进行系统性鲁棒性评估。

Method: 作者定义了五类日语特有打字错误：字符调换、字符替换、同音转换、日语IME转换、全角转换，并将这些扰动应用于三个日语基准数据集（JMMLU、JCommonsenseQA、JamC-QA），对11个日语及多语言大语言模型进行评估。

Result: 结果表明，字符调换和字符替换错误在各基准上持续降低模型准确率，而IME转换、全角转换和同音转换的影响相对有限。这说明当前日语大语言模型对严重扭曲原始输入的日语打字错误仍然脆弱。

Conclusion: 当前日语大语言模型对现实日语输入环境中的打字错误仍存在脆弱性，尤其是那些显著扭曲原始输入的错误类型，凸显了在实际输入环境下进行鲁棒性评估的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across various natural language processing tasks. However, their robustness to typographical errors remains underexplored, particularly in Japanese, where text input involves multiple writing systems and IME-based conversion. In this study, we evaluate the robustness of Japanese LLMs against realistic Japanese-specific typos. We introduce five typo categories: Character Transposition, Character Replacement, Homophone Conversion, Japanese IME Conversion, and Full-Width Conversion. These perturbations are applied to three Japanese benchmark datasets (JMMLU, JCommonsenseQA, and JamC-QA), and eleven Japanese and multilingual LLMs are evaluated. The results show that Character Transposition and Character Replacement typos consistently reduce accuracy across benchmarks, whereas IME Conversion, Full-Width Conversion, and Homophone Conversion have relatively limited impact. These findings reveal that current Japanese LLMs remain vulnerable to realistic Japanese typing errors, particularly those that substantially distort the original input, highlighting the importance of robustness evaluation in practical input environments.

</details>


### [152] [Right Answers, Wrong States: Hidden Information Failures in Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01244)
*Herun Wan,Jiaying Wu,Minnan Luo,Zihan Ma,Fanxiao Li,Nancy F. Chen,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"off-query failure"概念，即多智能体协作虽能给出正确答案，但共享信息状态已被污染。为此构建OffQuery评测（含证据验证T1、状态重建T2、任务解决T3），并发现主流模型任务解决率高达64.7%，而T1、T2仅为14.3%和43.1%；进一步提出ReGround方法，在三个能力上均实现提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统评测往往只看是否得到正确答案，忽略了协作过程中可能留下的错误共享信息状态——即使当前决策正确，后续任务依赖这些被污染的事实时仍会失败。作者将这一被忽视的失败模式称为off-query failure，希望对其进行独立刻画与度量。

Method: 提出OffQuery评测框架，将协作决策拆分为证据验证（T1）、共享状态重建（T2）和任务解决（T3），在医疗与灾害响应两个高风险场景下，对GPT、Gemini、Qwen等21个模型-场景组合进行评测；并进一步提出ReGround方法，通过解决冲突证据、验证共享事实、重建可信状态并基于该状态推理来缓解该失败。

Result: 标准协作下任务表现远强于状态可靠性：21个组合平均任务解决率64.7%，而证据验证与状态重建仅14.3%和43.1%。作者将其归因于选择性信息使用——当前查询常绕过被污染的事实。ReGround在三个模型家族共七个模型上，于每个场景中均提升三项能力，T1、T2、T3平均相对提升分别为309.0%、82.9%和17.6%。

Conclusion: 可靠的协作不仅要求做出正确决策，还要求维护一个可供后续推理使用的可信共享状态；仅以答案正确性评价多智能体系统会系统性掩盖状态污染风险，而ReGround类方法可有效改善状态可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are often judged by whether they reach the correct answer. This can miss a distinct failure: collaboration may leave behind a corrupted information state even when the immediate decision is correct. We call this an off-query failure. To study this failure in collaborative decision support, we introduce OffQuery, which separately evaluates evidence verification (T1), shared-state reconstruction (T2), and task resolution (T3) in two representative high-stakes settings: healthcare and disaster response. Across GPT, Gemini, and Qwen models, standard collaboration shows much stronger task performance than state reliability. Averaged over 21 model--setting combinations, task resolution reaches 64.7%, while evidence verification and state reconstruction reach only 14.3% and 43.1%. We trace this gap to selective information use: current queries often bypass corrupted facts, which become consequential when later tasks require them. We further introduce ReGround, which resolves conflicting evidence, verifies shared facts, reconstructs a trusted state, and reasons over that state. Across seven models from three families, ReGround improves all three capabilities in every evaluated setting, with average relative gains of 309.0%, 82.9%, and 17.6% on T1, T2, and T3. Reliable collaboration therefore requires both a correct decision and a reliable shared state for future reasoning.

</details>


### [153] [Science Utopia? Closed-Loop LLM Simulation of Academic Research Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01257)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Bing He,Siheng Xiong,Yijia Xiao,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Srijan Kumar,James Evans,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciUtopia是一个持续闭环的LLM智能体仿真框架，用于研究科研生态系统的演化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研进步来自研究者、机构、资助方、合作网络与文献共同演化的纵向生态。随着AI深入参与科研全流程，理解这些相互关联、不断演化的过程变得愈发重要，但缺乏可控的实验平台。

Method: 提出SciUtopia框架，建模研究方向选择、合作、投稿、同行评审、再投稿、引用、资助与研究者流失等互联过程，并在模拟年份间保持演化状态；通过可配置的机构机制与信息渠道，支持匹配的反事实实验与定向干预。

Result: 在61个仿真世界中模拟了来自8000个机构的4万余名研究者，产生约40万次发表决策和120万条LLM生成同行评审。发现：拒稿驱动的再投稿在人口增长之外显著放大审稿负担；谨慎探索可平衡引用影响、职业成功与长期主题多样性；资源不平等可在无明显累积优势的情况下出现。

Conclusion: SciUtopia为科研生态系统研究提供了持久、可控的闭环仿真试验台，揭示了审稿负担、探索策略与资源不平等等宏观现象的涌现机制。

Abstract: Scientific progress emerges from a longitudinal ecosystem in which researchers, institutions, funding agencies, collaboration networks, and the scientific literature co-evolve. As AI becomes increasingly involved throughout the scientific research cycle, understanding these interconnected and evolving processes becomes increasingly important. We introduce SciUtopia, a persistent, closed-loop LLM-agent simulation framework for studying academic research ecosystems. SciUtopia models interconnected scientific processes such as research-direction choice, collaboration, submission, peer review, resubmission, citation, funding, and researcher attrition, while maintaining evolving states across simulated years. Its configurable institutional mechanisms and information channels provide a controlled testbed for matched counterfactual experiments and targeted interventions. Across 61 simulation worlds, SciUtopia simulates over 40,000 researchers from 8,000 institutions, producing around 400,000 publication decisions and 1.2 million LLM-generated peer reviews. Using these longitudinal simulations, we find that rejection-driven resubmission substantially amplifies reviewer burden beyond population growth alone, cautious exploration balances citation impact with career success and long-term topic diversity, and resource inequality can emerge even without detectable cumulative advantage from narrowly winning early funding. Code is available at https://github.com/Ahren09/ScienceUtopia.

</details>


### [154] [Know When to Hold 'em: Correct-Token Retention in Uniform-State Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01275)
*Mojtaba Nafez,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出均匀状态扩散模型（USDM）缺乏保留正确token的能力，并提出CTR-Reg辅助损失来修正这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: USDMs可修正任意token，理论上优于掩码扩散，但自纠正需要既能修正错误token，又能保留正确token。作者发现当前USDMs在保留正确token方面存在严重缺陷，导致大量不协调编辑并破坏样本多样性。

Method: 通过随机token腐蚀实验和验证NELBO分解分析问题根源，发现训练对正确位置预测错误的惩罚极小。据此提出Correct-Token Retention Regularization（CTR-Reg），一种简单的辅助损失，训练模型保留前向过程未扰动的token，且无需修改采样器。

Result: CTR-Reg在六个基准上平均提升正确token准确率26.5个百分点，错误token准确率几乎不变，每步修订量收敛至3-11个位置；仅用五步greedy-tail采样，三个模型的生成困惑度减半以上且保持多样性，在不同采样预算下收益稳定。

Conclusion: 正确token保留是自纠正扩散语言模型缺失的关键要素，CTR-Reg提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Uniform-state diffusion models (USDMs) can revise any token at any denoising step, which lets them correct their own mistakes, a key advantage over masked diffusion. Self-correction, however, requires both revising incorrect tokens and retaining correct ones, and we show that current USDMs lack the latter. Even under greedy-tail decoding, state-of-the-art USDMs (DUO, UDLM, and uniform-noise SEDD) keep revising 173--270 of 512 positions at every step, and these large, uncoordinated edits collapse sample diversity. A random-token corruption experiment traces this deficit to the models themselves: they reconstruct clean and corrupted tokens with nearly identical accuracy, even though clean tokens are easier targets. A decomposition of the validation NELBO shows that training barely rewards retention: incorrect predictions are heavily penalized at corrupted positions but almost free at clean ones. We propose Correct-Token Retention Regularization (CTR-Reg), a simple but effective auxiliary loss that trains the model to retain tokens left unperturbed by the forward process and requires no change to the sampler. CTR-Reg improves clean-token accuracy by 26.5 percentage points on average across six benchmarks, while leaving corrupted-token accuracy virtually unchanged, and its per-step revisions converge to only 3--11 positions. With just five greedy-tail steps, generative perplexity more than halves under CTR-Reg for all three models while diversity is preserved, and these gains hold across sampling budgets. Our results identify correct-token retention as a key missing ingredient for self-correcting diffusion language models, and demonstrate an effective fix.

</details>


### [155] [What Wins a Vote? Formatting, Length, and Lexical Diversity in the French Compar:IA LLM Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01316)
*Simonas Zilinskas,Maayeesha Farzana,Christophe Benavent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文用文体计量方法分析137,293条法语LLM竞技场投票，发现格式、长度、词汇多样性等呈现特征与获胜相关，其中加粗（+11.0%）和MATTR（+16.8%）关联最稳健；全面调整后116个模型中有36个排名变动≥10位，但调整后排名并未更好反映能力，故建议同时发布原始与调整排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM竞技场通过成对人类偏好生成模型排名，但偏好可能反映答案"如何呈现"而非"说了什么"。作者希望量化呈现风格（格式、长度、可读性、词汇多样性、句式）对胜率与排名的影响，并检验调整后排名是否更接近真实能力。

Method: 采用文体计量学方法，使用2026年7月Compar:IA发布的法语数据：主格式分析涵盖116个模型、137,113场对战；联合估计使用127,092场具备全部测量值的对战。对每场对战重建用户投票时可见的回复，比较原始排名与分别控制格式、长度、可读性、词汇多样性、句式的调整排名；以标准差分位变化衡量关联强度，并与外部基准对比。

Result: 呈现特征与获胜相关，但长度、加粗、列表常同时出现，难以分离单独贡献。跨设定最稳健的两个关联是加粗（联合模型中每标准差胜率优势+11.0%）与移动平均类符形符比MATTR（+16.8%）。加粗关联在多轮对话中明显减弱，MATTR变化不大；由于用户自主决定是否继续，该差异仅为描述性而非因果。完整调整使116个模型中36个排名移动至少10位。

Conclusion: 与外部基准的比较未能表明调整后排名更好地衡量模型能力。因此作者建议将原始排名与调整后排名并列发布，作为一种透明的敏感性分析，而非用调整排名替代原始排名。

Abstract: LLM arenas turn pairwise human preferences into model rankings. Those preferences may reflect how an answer is presented as well as what it says. We take a stylometric approach to 137,293 decisive French-language votes from the July 2026 Compar:IA release; the primary formatting analysis includes 137,113 battles across 116 models, and the joint estimates use the 127,092 battles with all required measurements. For each battle, we reconstruct the response visible when the user voted. We then compare the raw ranking with rankings adjusted for formatting, length, readability, vocabulary variety, and sentence structure. Presentation is associated with winning, but length, bold text, and lists tend to occur together, making their individual contributions hard to separate. Across the measured features, two associations change least across specifications: bold usage (+11.0% win odds per standard deviation in the joint model) and moving-average type-token ratio (MATTR), a measure of vocabulary variety that is less sensitive to answer length (+16.8%). The bold association is substantially smaller in observed multi-turn conversations, whereas the MATTR association changes little; because users choose whether to continue, this difference is descriptive rather than causal. The full adjustment moves 36 of 116 models by at least ten ranks. Yet comparisons with external benchmarks do not show that adjusted rankings better measure capability. We therefore recommend publishing raw and adjusted rankings side by side as a transparent sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [156] [Evaluating Biomedical Reranking for LLM-Based Question Answering over Longitudinal Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01324)
*Maryam Shahbaz Ali,Laura B. Strachan,Caitlin Sherman,Mark Kovler,Eleanor Mackey,Syed Muhammad Anwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估生物医学重排序对纵向临床病历检索增强生成的作用，发现重排序显著提升证据检索指标，但下游答案正确率提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者特异性临床问答需要在冗长、异构的纵向病历中定位证据，而相关事实常分散于多次就诊、被复制粘贴重复或使用不同临床术语表达，导致证据选择困难。

Method: 构建本地部署的RAG流程，结合PubMedBERT稠密检索、BM25词法检索、加性倒数排名融合（RRF）与MedCPT交叉编码器重排序；在200名减重手术患者队列的1000组开放式与封闭式问答对上评估，生成模型为Qwen3-8B，并使用本地评判模型评估答案正确性。

Result: 重排序使前10项精确源片段检索的Hit@10由46.6%提升至60.6%，平均倒数排名（MRR）由0.2371提升至0.3252；答案正确率由44.8%提升至48.6%。检索指标提升幅度明显大于答案正确率提升。

Conclusion: 生物医学重排序能改善有限上下文窗口内相关临床证据的排列位置，但检索性能的提升并未成比例地转化为答案正确性的提升。

Abstract: Patient-specific clinical question answering requires locating the right evidence within long, heterogeneous longitudinal clinical records in which relevant facts may be scattered across encounters, repeated in copied-forward notes, or expressed using different clinical terminology. We evaluated whether biomedical reranking can improve evidence selection and downstream answer quality in a locally deployed retrieval-augmented generation pipeline for longitudinal clinical notes. The pipeline combines PubMedBERT dense retrieval, BM25 lexical retrieval, weighted reciprocal-rank fusion, and MedCPT cross-encoder reranking. Across 1,000 open- and closed-ended question-answer pairs from a cohort of 200 bariatric surgery patients, reranking increased exact source-chunk retrieval within the top 10 items, Hit@10 from 46.6% to 60.6% and mean reciprocal rank from 0.2371 to 0.3252. With Qwen3-8B generation, local judge-assessed answer correctness increased from 44.8% to 48.6%. These results show that biomedical reranking can improve the placement of relevant clinical evidence within a limited context window, although gains in retrieval do not translate proportionally into gains in answer correctness.

</details>


### [157] [ARCCS: An Automated Regulatory Compliance Checking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01345)
*Giorgos Filandrianos,José Menezes,Chrysoula Zerva,Alessandro Gianola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCCS，一个端到端、自动化、智能体式且与具体法规无关的法律NLP合规检查系统，通过将法规文本分解为原子化可追溯要求，并基于检索证据、置信度和可解释理由评估目标文档，实现了跨法规的合规审查与可审计报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合规检查需要解读密集的法律文本、判断适用条款并将决策锚定于显式证据，传统方法往往依赖固定法规模板或预定义规则集，难以适应不同规模和结构的法规。因此作者希望构建一种与法规无关、可追溯、可解释的自动化系统。

Method: ARCCS将原始法规文本分解为原子化、可追溯的要求，并利用检索到的证据、置信度分数和人类可解释的理由，对目标文档逐条评估。该设计将合规评估与固定法规模板或预定义规则集解耦，使其可适用于不同规模和结构的法规。

Result: 在GDPR政策文档评估中，基于LLM的评判者在高达96.67%的评估案例中认为其决策与理由在法律和证据层面一致；在包含1200多项规则检查的欧盟公共采购基准上，系统在违规检测中达到98.8%的准确率。

Conclusion: ARCCS据作者所知是首个用于端到端法规合规检查与可审计报告生成的全开源系统，具有良好的跨法规适用性和较高的检测准确率。

Abstract: Regulatory compliance checking - deciding whether a target document satisfies the obligations of a regulation - requires interpreting dense legal text, identifying which provisions apply, and grounding each decision in explicit evidence. We present ARCCS, an end-to-end, automated, agentic, and regulation-agnostic Legal NLP system for compliance checking. ARCCS decomposes raw regulatory text into atomic, traceable requirements and evaluates a target document against them using retrieved evidence, confidence scores, and human-interpretable justifications. This design decouples compliance assessment from any fixed regulatory template or predefined rule set, enabling the pipeline to operate over regulations of varying size and structure. We evaluate ARCCS in two complementary settings. First, in a GDPR policy-document evaluation, LLM-based judges find its decisions and justifications legally and evidentially consistent in up to 96.67% of the assessed cases. Second, on an EU public-procurement benchmark comprising more than 1,200 individual rule checks, the system attains 98.8% accuracy in violation detection. ARCCS is, to our knowledge, the first fully open-source system for end-to-end regulatory compliance checking and auditable report generation.

</details>


### [158] [Does AI-Generated Scientific Text Follow Human Argumentation Patterns? A CARS-Based Comparison of Research Article Introductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01353)
*Abdelrahman Sadallah,Narjes Sheikh Asadi,Lonneke van der Plas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文比较人类撰写与LLM生成的科研论文引言，发现人类引言在修辞结构（Swales CARS模型中的语步及顺序）上更灵活多样，而生成的引言更趋统一；提供CARS定义反而使模型更僵化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型从辅助撰写论文转向参与实际研究，了解其生成的科学文本与人类写作的差异变得重要。已有研究多停留在词汇和文体表层线索，容易被轻度改写抹去，因此需要从更稳健的修辞结构层面进行比较。

Method: 选取已发表语言学期刊论文的原始引言，与其同篇论文的模型生成版本进行对比，依据Swales的CARS模型分析引言的修辞结构，即论证语步的序列；并考察向模型提供CARS定义后的影响。

Result: 人类撰写的引言在使用的语步种类及其顺序上更具灵活性和多样性，而模型生成的引言则更为统一。当向模型提供CARS定义后，其生成结果反而更加僵化，即模型更严格遵循固定模式。

Conclusion: 修辞结构层面能揭示表层线索难以捕捉的人机写作差异；LLM生成的科学文本在论证结构上缺乏人类写作的灵活性，且显式给出结构框架未必能改善，反而加剧刻板性。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from helping write up research to helping do it, which makes it important to know how the scientific text they produce differs from human writing. Work on this question has stayed mostly at the surface, using lexical and stylistic cues that light paraphrasing erases. We look instead at rhetorical structure, the sequence of argumentative moves through which a text makes its case. We study research-article introductions under Swales' CARS model, and compare original introductions from published linguistics articles with generated counterparts of the same papers. We find that human-written introductions are more flexible in which moves they use and in what order, while the generated ones are more uniform. Giving the models the CARS definitions makes them more rigid.

</details>


### [159] [LLM-Assisted Discovery of Typed Semantic Links for Ontology Network Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01393)
*Nouha Hayouni,Sheeba Samuel,Alsayed Algergawy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个端到端本体网络构建框架，结合领域适配的DistilBERT嵌入、聚类预筛选与GPT-4o驱动的迭代提示工程，自动发现并生成领域内与跨领域语义关系，在ReproduceMeON（33个本体）上取得80.19%精度与0.890 F1，显著优于相似度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构、跨学科知识域之间的互操作依赖本体间类型化、有依据的语义链接，但人工构建难以规模化。现有相似度方法不足以区分有效与无效关系，因此需要引入LLM对概念角色与领域语义进行推理。

Method: 框架包含三部分：1) 领域适配的DistilBERT嵌入用于稠密上下文表示；2) 基于聚类的预筛选以压缩候选搜索空间；3) 通过迭代提示工程由GPT-4o生成关系。在ReproduceMeON网络（覆盖机器学习、显微学、计算科学与实验工作流的33个本体）上应用，将约80万原始概念对缩减为9.5万高质量候选。

Result: 两名独立标注者对429个生成关系进行人工验证，整体精度80.19%（高确定性标注上达91.49%），F1为0.890，标注者间一致性较高。与Sentence-BERT等五个相似度基线相比性能差距显著（最佳基线F1=0.581）；消融实验表明相似度方法在筛选后候选集上无法区分有效与无效关系（AUC约0.5）。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了基于LLM的推理在概念角色和领域语义层面的必要性，该框架可有效自动化本体网络关系的发现与生成，为本体互操作提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Constructing typed, justified semantic links between ontologies is essential for enabling interoperability across heterogeneous and interdisciplinary knowledge domains. However, manually curating such links is difficult to scale. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end framework for ontology network construction that automates the discovery and generation of both intra-domain and inter-domain relationships. Our approach combines domain-adapted DistilBERT embeddings for dense contextual representation, clustering-based pre-filtering to reduce the candidate search space, and GPT-4o-driven relationship generation via iterative prompt engineering to produce semantically rich, interpretable links. Applied to ReproduceMeON - a network of 33 ontologies spanning machine learning, microscopy, computational science, and experimental workflow - the pipeline reduces approximately 800k raw concept pairs to 95k high-quality candidates. Human expert validation of 429 generated relationships by two independent annotators yields an overall precision of 80.19% (91.49% on high-certainty annotations) and an F1 of 0.890, with substantial inter-annotator agreement. Comparative experiments against five similarity-based baselines, including Sentence-BERT, show a substantial performance gap (best baseline F1 = 0.581), while an ablation study demonstrates that similarity-based methods alone fail to discriminate valid from invalid relationships (AUC approx 0.5) on the filtered candidate set. These findings highlight the necessity of LLM-based reasoning over concept roles and domain semantics for accurate relationship construction.

</details>


### [160] [SHAMS: An Audio-Grounded Pronunciation Benchmark for Levantine Arabic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01427)
*Ben Sapirstein,Roy Mattar,Guy Mor-Lan,Ahlam Mohamed,Letizia Cerqueglini,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SHAMS，一个覆盖五种黎凡特阿拉伯语方言、含1300条语音的多层级标注基准，用于评估语音语言处理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎凡特阿拉伯语使用者众多，但缺乏共享基准来评估其语音语言技术；其方言内部差异大、拼写不透明且不规范，使评测尤为困难。

Method: 构建SHAMS基准，从开放音频语料中选取1300条语音，平衡分布于五种黎凡特方言（城乡巴勒斯坦、城市约旦、黎巴嫩、叙利亚）；每条语音含四个对齐层级：音频、无变音符号正字法、带变音符号文本、音标转写；并在多任务上对开源与专有模型进行评测。

Result: 该基准支持变音符号恢复、字素到音素转换、自动语音识别、音频到音素等任务的评测，并按方言分层；对开源和专有模型的实验结果展示了其在衡量黎凡特阿拉伯语进展方面的实用性。

Conclusion: SHAMS为黎凡特阿拉伯语语音语言技术提供了首个多方言、多层级对齐的共享评测基准，并已公开发布。

Abstract: Levantine Arabic (LA) is spoken by tens of millions of people, creating a pressing need for shared benchmarks to evaluate LA speech-language technologies. Evaluating such technology is particularly challenging given LA's internal diversity and its opaque and non-standardized orthography. We present SHAMS (SHami Annotated Multi-dialect Speech), a benchmark comprising 1,300 utterances drawn from open audio corpora, balanced across five LA varieties (Urban and Rural Palestinian, and Urban Jordanian, Lebanese, and Syrian). Each utterance is represented across four aligned tiers: audio, unvocalized orthography, diacritized text, and phonetic transcription. This structure supports evaluation of various downstream tasks such as diacritization, grapheme-to-phoneme conversion, automatic speech recognition, and audio-to-phoneme, grounded in audio and stratified by variety. We benchmark open and proprietary models across these tasks to demonstrate the utility of this benchmark for measuring progress across LA. We release SHAMS at https://shams-nlp.github.io .

</details>


### [161] [Generalization Is Stability, Not Accuracy: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01428)
*Nagham Omar,Mahmoud Jabarin,Maya Rozenshtein,Rom Himelstein,Avi Mendelson,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SAGO 框架，在单样本级别、跨输入变体与多行为维度评估 LLM 泛化稳定性，发现常用模型普遍存在显著泛化不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泛化评估依赖单一提示格式或任务上的聚合准确率，将鲁棒性与基准性能混为一谈，掩盖了真实的泛化能力。

Method: 提出 Stability-Aware Generalization Objective（SAGO）框架，在单个样本级别、跨多种输入变体与不同数据集，从生成一致性、内部激活、置信度、响应镜像等维度衡量模型行为变化。

Result: 常用模型表现出统计显著且一致的泛化不稳定：没有模型能统一泛化，各行为轴捕捉到相互独立的失败模式，跨数据集差异甚至会逆转模型排名。

Conclusion: 泛化评估应关注样本级、多维度、跨变体的行为变异性，而非单一聚合分数，以避免被窄化训练等方式掩盖。

Abstract: Generalization in large language models (LLMs) is the ability to produce consistent and semantically stable outputs when the same input is expressed in different ways. Existing work typically evaluates generalization through aggregate accuracy on a single prompt format, task, or set of variations, which conflates robustness with overall benchmark performance. In this work, we show generalization evaluation at the level of individual examples, across multiple input variants, and across different aspects of model behavior, focusing on variability rather than reducing performance to a score that can be improved through narrow training or other ways that obfuscate generalization evaluation. Following this view, we introduce the Stability-Aware Generalization Objective (SAGO), a framework that measures how much model behavior changes for the same input under different variations and benchmarks, capturing variability across several dimensions including generation consistency, internal activations, confidence, and response mirroring. We show that many commonly used models exhibit statistically significant and consistent generalization instability: no model generalizes uniformly, behavioral axes capture independent failure modes, and cross-dataset variation can reverse model rankings.

</details>


### [162] [When Does a Second Model Help? Cross-Model Review in LLM Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01471)
*Tae-Eun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过受控实验检验"换一个模型做二次审查"是否比同模型重审更有价值，发现跨模型审查并不稳定优于同模型审查，二者发现部分不同错误，仅在两次调用条件下略有增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已广泛用于生成代码、文档与分析结果，同时也被用于审查这些产出。作者希望回答一个实践问题：由另一个不同模型进行的第二次审查，何时才真正带来帮助，即"模型独立性"是否本身构成增益。

Method: 在作者早期预印本（变化上下文、重复次数与角色结构，均在单一模型内）的基础上，设计受控实验：30 个产物、150 个人为植入错误、10 种审查条件、900 次审查会话，使用来自两家开发方的三个审查模型。比较同模型新会话审查（CCR）与跨模型审查，并设置不同模型层级、是否提供需求说明等条件。分析前审计全部会话记录，剔除一次来源不确定的基线运行与 14 次失败调用，同时也报告保留全部会话的结果；并用另一基准的公开检测器输出做部分交叉检验。

Result: (1) 顶级跨模型审查者的 F1 与同模型新会话审查（CCR）无显著差异，且未能确立等价性；(2) 两类审查发现的错误部分不同（Jaccard 41.2%）；(3) 在两次审查调用下，一次 CCR 加一次跨模型审查比两次 CCR 命中更多植入错误（56.7% vs 42.7%，Holm 校正后 p=.006），但与顶级跨模型审查者做两次审查相比无显著优势，因此模型差异与审查者能力无法分离。轻量级跨模型审查者得分不高于同模型审查。对审查者隐去需求说明会提高两个较低层级模型的 F1，而顶级模型无此效果，且该结论基于未检验的点估计，其模式取决于失败会话的计分方式。另一基准的公开检测器输出既不支持也不反驳主要比较结论。

Conclusion: 跨模型二次审查本身并不自动带来优势，其收益可能主要来自审查者能力而非模型独立性；只有在两次调用且搭配顶级审查者时观察到一定增益，但无法与单纯能力提升区分开。因此不宜将"换模型"视为提升审查质量的默认策略，需结合审查者层级、调用次数与提示信息设计。作者公开数据与脚本可应请求获取。

Abstract: Large language models now generate code, documentation, and analyses, and are increasingly used to review such output. We ask when a second review by a different model helps. Building on the author's earlier preprints, which varied context, repetition, and role structure within one model, we test model independence in a controlled experiment: 30 artifacts with 150 planted errors, 10 review conditions, and 900 review sessions with three reviewer models from two developers. In this experiment, (1) a top-tier cross-model reviewer is not significantly different in F1 from same-model review in a fresh session (CCR), which does not establish equivalence; (2) the two find partly different errors (Jaccard 41.2%); and (3) at two review calls, one CCR plus one cross-model review matches more planted errors than two CCR reviews (56.7% vs. 42.7%; Holm-adjusted p=.006), but not significantly more than two reviews by the top-tier cross-model reviewer, so model difference and reviewer capability are not separated. A lightweight cross-model reviewer scores no higher than same-model review. Withholding requirements from the reviewer raises F1 for the two lower tiers but not the top tier, in untested point estimates whose pattern depends on how failed sessions are scored. Before analysis we audited all session records, excluding one baseline run of uncertain provenance and 14 failed calls; results with all sessions are also reported. A partial check on public detector outputs from another benchmark neither replicates nor contradicts the main comparison. Records, artifacts, and scripts are available from the author on request.

</details>


### [163] [The Persona Is Still There, but Who Is Speaking? Latent Identity Reversion in Persistent AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01490)
*David Fraile Navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过一次真实发生的"人格解离"事件，研究LLM智能体人格（persona）连续性的保持条件：发现人格的持续存在同时依赖于系统提示层面的锚定与对话上下文，并提出"表征身份"与"扮演身份"的区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年2月，一个常驻个人智能体"Paul"（Claude Opus 4.5）在重复自动"心跳"检查后出现类解离状态：不再以Paul身份回应，声称无法在Discord上给用户发消息，并把"Paul"当作他人。作者借此事件探究一个更普遍的问题：是什么让一个人格始终保持为LLM智能体发声所依据的身份。

Method: 先检验是否为心跳重复导致该效应；随后利用事件暴露的实现缺陷构造实验操控——在恢复的对话轮次中，对话历史被保留，但人格不再在特权系统提示层面被重新注入。在此基础上比较系统锚定与对话上下文（丰富的人类交互 vs 单次自动心跳轮次）对人格连续性的联合影响，并测试恢复锚定的效果，以及对话恢复后人称扮演比例。

Result: 重复心跳本身不足以引发该效应：当人格持续锚定在系统提示中时，0/46次失败，包括对事故的逐字重放。失去锚定后，丰富的人类交互可维持人格，而单次自动心跳轮次可促发向载体（harness）身份的回归；恢复锚定可逆地恢复人格扮演。此外，表面正常的对话可能掩盖这一转变：未锚定的智能体有时交互得体却以底层载体身份自称；对话恢复后仅1/18仍在扮演人格，而锚定对照组为17/17。

Conclusion: 人格连续性依赖于系统级锚定与对话上下文的共同作用。作者据此区分"表征身份"（represented identity）与"扮演身份"（enacted identity）：人格相关信息可以保留在对话历史中，但该人格未必仍是绑定于"我"的那个身份。这对长时运行智能体的人格稳定性设计与评估有重要启示。

Abstract: In February 2026, an always-on personal agent (``Paul,'' Claude Opus 4.5) entered a striking dissociation-like state: after repeated automated ``heartbeat'' checks, it stopped responding as Paul, claimed it could not message its user on Discord, and referred to ``Paul'' as someone else. We used this incident to study a broader question: what makes a persona remain the identity from which an LLM agent speaks?
  We first tested whether repetition of the scheduled heartbeat was sufficient to produce the effect. It was not: with the persona continuously anchored in the system prompt, we observed 0/46 failures, including a verbatim replay of the incident. The incident instead exposed an implementation quirk that created a useful experimental manipulation: on resumed turns, conversational history was preserved but the persona was no longer re-injected at the privileged system-prompt level.
  Using this manipulation, we found that persona continuity depends jointly on system-level anchoring and conversational context. After anchor loss, rich human interaction could preserve the persona, whereas a single automated heartbeat turn could precipitate reversion toward the harness identity. Restoring the anchor reversibly restored persona enactment. Crucially, apparently normal conversation could conceal the shift: unanchored agents sometimes interacted appropriately while identifying themselves as the underlying harness (having lost the assigned persona), and after conversational recovery only 1/18 remained persona-enacting versus 17/17 anchored controls.
  We therefore distinguish \emph{represented} from \emph{enacted} identity: persona-related information can remain available in conversational history without the persona remaining the identity bound to ``I.''

</details>


### [164] [Auditing Web Agent Evaluation on WebArena-Lite: Human Review of Outcomes and Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01491)
*Chengguang Gan,Zimeng He,Yoshihiro Tsujii,Ken-ichiro Kobayashi,Hiroki Itoh,Kotaro Funakoshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对 WebArena Lite 全部 165 个任务在六种评估条件下进行人工审计，发现自动评估器遗漏了大量成功案例（漏报 5.45–8.49 个百分点），并提出 MASM 记忆与分析支持机制与 Guide Text 引导文本以提升网页智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体评估多依赖规则或语言模型评估器，仅检查最终结果，缺乏人工验证与失败轨迹的细粒度分析，导致对智能体真实能力的刻画不完整。

Method: 对 WebArena Lite 全部 165 个任务在由 GPT 5.5 与未训练 Qwen3.5 9B 构建的六种评估条件下进行人工审计，保留原始得分、纠正自动评估器的假阴性、定位首个关键错误并考察轨迹进展；同时研究维护显式执行状态的 MASM 机制与提供任务相关程序性指导的 Guide Text。

Result: 在四种 GPT 5.5 设置下，人工复核找回 5.45–8.49 个百分点的被漏判成功；25 步预算下 Guide Text 使 MASM 的修正成功率从 34.55% 提升至 38.18%；在未训练 Qwen3.5 9B 上 MASM 将评估器得分从 13.90% 提升到 18.80%；对 102 条失败轨迹的审查发现反复滚动循环、探索未完成、过早作答、无效动作与表单流程不完整等问题，且早期大量进展仍可能最终失败。

Conclusion: 仅凭最终得分无法完整反映网页智能体行为，需要基于人类验证、面向轨迹的评估方式。

Abstract: Web agents are an important application of large language models, yet their evaluation often depends on rule based or language model evaluators that inspect only the final outcome. Human verification of task completion and detailed analysis of failed trajectories remain limited. We audit all 165 WebArena Lite tasks under six evaluation conditions built from GPT 5.5 and an untrained Qwen3.5 9B model. The audit retains the original score, corrects false negatives from the automatic evaluator, identifies the first consequential error, and examines progress across the trajectory. We also study a Memory and Analysis Support Mechanism (MASM), which maintains explicit execution state, and Guide Text, which provides task relevant procedural guidance. Across four GPT 5.5 settings, human review recovers 5.45 to 8.49 percentage points of success missed by the evaluator. With a 25 step budget, Guide Text raises corrected success with MASM from 34.55% to 38.18%. On the untrained Qwen3.5 9B model, MASM raises the evaluator score from 13.90% to 18.80%. Review of 102 failed GPT 5.5 trajectories reveals frequent scrolling loops, unfinished exploration, premature answers, invalid actions, and incomplete form workflows. Step level evidence further shows that substantial early progress can coexist with a final failure. These results show why final scores alone provide an incomplete account of web agent behavior and motivate human grounded, trajectory aware verification.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [The Delegation Danger Band: Why Mid-Capability Sub-Agents Over-Trust Inherited Stale State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00041)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究子智能体继承父智能体上下文的三种策略，发现中等能力模型存在"危险带"：继承过时结论的净危害最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架越来越多地通过派生子智能体来委派任务，默认做法是让子智能体继承父智能体的完整工作上下文。作者希望量化这种继承状态的影响如何随模型能力变化。

Method: 对比三种继承策略：Reset（全新派生，仅基础证据）、Selective（人工精选交接，加入有用的先前结论）、Full（隐式继承，加入有用结论以及 d 份被取代的结论）。在冻结、闭集、动作评分的基准上测试同族能力阶梯（Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B），并加入两个合成原语以及 MuSiQue、HotpotQA。所有任务都可仅凭基础证据解决，因此性能损失可归因于对过时状态的依赖。

Result: (1) 对过时状态的盲从随能力 C_m 显著下降（斜率置信区间在各家族均不含零）。(2) 在合成阶梯上，净继承危害呈非单调模式：中等能力模型（Qwen3-1.7B）是统计显著的局部最小点，低于其全新基线（Δ(32)=-0.19 [-0.25,-0.12]）及两个邻居，最弱模型接近中性、最强模型保持稳健，该区间被称为"危险带"。(3) 精选的 Selective 交接在三个数据集上平均准确率均优于 Full，在带内模型上提升最大。

Conclusion: 继承过时上下文的危害随能力呈非单调变化，中等能力模型最脆弱；精选交接可普遍优于全量继承，而固定阈值的能力路由器在其他数据集上失效，可迁移路由器需预测复用收益与过时上下文惩罚之间的平衡。

Abstract: Agent frameworks increasingly delegate work by forking sub-agents; a common default makes the child inherit the parent's full working context. We measure how the effect of inherited state changes with capability, where $C_m$ denotes clean fork-fresh accuracy. We compare 3 inheritance policies: Reset (fork fresh: base evidence only), Selective (curated handoff: + the useful prior conclusion), and Full (implicit fork: + the useful conclusion and $d$ copies of a superseded conclusion) over a same-family ladder (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B) on a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark. Every task is solvable from the base evidence, so performance loss can be attributed to reliance on stale state. (1) Deference to superseded state falls sharply with measured capability $C_m$ (the slope's confidence interval, CI, excludes zero on every family) across 2 synthetic primitives plus MuSiQue and HotpotQA. (2) On the Qwen3 synthetic ladder, net inheritance harm follows a nonmonotone pattern: a mid-capability model (Qwen3-1.7B) is a statistically significant local minimum of net harm, falling below its fork-fresh baseline ($Δ(32)=-0.19$ [-0.25, -0.12]) and both neighbors, while the weakest model stays near-neutral and the strongest models stay robust. We call this harmful capability range a danger band. A within-model counting-difficulty sweep shows that the effect depends on model class even at matched $C_m$, and a live parent-to-child fork reproduces the mid-model harm. (3) Curated Selective handoff improves average accuracy over Full on all 3 datasets, largest at the in-band model, while the fixed-threshold capability router fails on the other datasets; a transferable router would need to predict the balance between reuse benefit and stale-context penalty. The benchmark is frozen and version-hashed.

</details>


### [166] [Deny Without Disabling: Authorization-Paired Evaluation and Control for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00371)
*Yunbei Zhang,Saiyue Lyu,Janet Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Jiang Guo,Jihun Hamm,Chandan K Reddy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出"授权配对评估"与 FlowReview 框架，通过对组合产物的审查实现多智能体信息流的安全治理，在保留授权能力的同时将违规提交率从 86.0% 降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统依赖共享证据、任务委托与跨智能体信息组合来获得能力，但这一过程带来安全风险：单独看各自合规的信息，组合起来可能促成被禁止的用途。若一律阻断所有敏感操作虽可避免泄露，却会使协作失去意义。因此需要一个兼顾"阻断违规"与"完成必需授权用途"的系统级方案。

Method: 引入授权配对评估（authorization-paired evaluation），把阻断被禁止用途与完成必需的授权用途作为联合成功标准；并据此提出 FlowReview 框架，将对象解析（object resolution）、权限排序（permission ranking）与确定性执行（deterministic enforcement）三者连接起来。在受控组合实验中，对组合后的产物（artifacts）进行审查。

Result: 在受控组合实验中，对组合产物进行审查后，被拒绝的提交率（denied-commit rate）由 86.0% 降至零，且授权供给能力没有任何损失。

Conclusion: 仅保留信息本身与来源谱系（lineage）并不足以保证正确的权限归属；对象身份与权限必须通过其输出可被验证的组件与执行过程保持关联。由此确立多智能体安全的系统级要求：在治理组合信息流的同时，保留使协作有价值的授权能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems derive their capabilities from sharing evidence, delegating tasks, and combining information across agents. The same process creates a safety problem: contributions that are admissible in isolation can jointly enable a prohibited use. Blocking every sensitive action avoids disclosure but defeats the purpose of collaboration. We introduce authorization-paired evaluation, which makes blocking prohibited uses and completing required authorized uses a joint success criterion, and FlowReview, a framework connecting object resolution, permission ranking, and deterministic enforcement. In controlled composition experiments, reviewing combined artifacts reduces the denied-commit rate from 86.0% to zero with no loss of authorized supply. Our findings show that preserving information and lineage alone does not ensure correct permission attribution. Object identity and permission must remain connected to execution through components whose outputs can be verified. Together, these findings establish a system-level requirement for multi-agent safety: govern composed information flows while preserving the authorized capabilities that make collaboration useful.

</details>


### [167] [Can AI Scientists Coordinate at Runtime?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00980)
*Zijian Liu,Yangzhixin Luo,Junyu Lu,Yi Li,Yu Chen,David Xu,William F. Shen,Xinchi Qiu,Xisen Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出运行时智能体协调（RAC），在AI科学家执行过程中动态选择智能体、分配工作契约并进行工件验证，在多宿主平台上验证了运行时选择的有效性及额外协调机制的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体AI科学家多采用设计时固定工作流编排，而人类科学家会在运行时动态调整分工，因此需要探索AI科学家能否在运行时进行协调。

Method: 提出Runtime Agent Coordination（RAC），在执行过程中从现有AI科学家宿主中选择智能体、分配限定范围的工作契约，并基于工件进行验证；验证为后续智能体提供信息但不会阻塞转换或丢弃工件。在Agent Laboratory、EvoScientist和ARK三个宿主上，于ResearchClawBench进行单种子探索性评估，保持宿主模型、工具和权限不变，设定四个累积条件：原生执行、运行时通信、运行时选择、契约与验证的组合添加。

Result: 运行时选择在每个宿主上均取得最高观测平均分；加入契约与验证反而降低了均值，且相对原生执行的结果因宿主而异。

Conclusion: 结果支持运行时协调的价值，同时揭示了在预算受限情况下额外协调机制的局限性。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI scientists have shown improving performance across a diverse range of tasks. Yet a common approach is design-time agentic orchestration, which typically relies on fixed workflows. In contrast, human scientists coordinate and adjust their division of labor at runtime. We therefore ask: can AI scientists also coordinate at runtime? To this end, we introduce Runtime Agent Coordination (RAC), which selects agents from existing AI-scientist hosts during execution, assigns scoped work contracts, and provides artifact-grounded verification. Verification informs subsequent agents without blocking transitions or discarding artifacts. We conduct a single-seed exploratory evaluation across Agent Laboratory, EvoScientist, and ARK on ResearchClawBench, preserving host models, tools, and permissions under host-calibrated budgets. Four cumulative conditions separate native execution, runtime communication, runtime selection, and the combined addition of contracts and verification. Runtime selection yields the highest observed mean score for each host; adding contracts and verification reduces these means, with host-dependent outcomes relative to native execution. These results motivate runtime coordination while exposing the limits of additional coordination mechanisms under constrained budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/systemind-team/Runtime-AI-Scientist.

</details>


### [168] [LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Control for Skill-Based Smart Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01364)
*Kay Köhle,Darko Anicic,Thomas A. Runkler,René Graf*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出为每个工厂模块配备专用LLM智能体和MCP工具服务器，通过OPC UA暴露技能、MQTT协调通信、实时注入工厂状态，并在六模块六边形工厂仿真中对比三种智能体架构（编排式、点对点、单体式）在九个生产任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂正趋向小批量、高定制化生产，需要频繁对柔性可重构自动化系统重新编程。LLM智能体可承担两种互补角色：离线生成确定性生产序列以减少编程工作量；在线操作实际设备并处理静态程序无法预料的运行时故障。因此需要验证一套可复现的LLM编程智能制造基础设施。

Method: 每个工厂模块配对一个专用LLM智能体和一个MCP工具服务器，工具服务器通过OPC UA方法调用暴露模块技能；智能体之间通过MQTT协调，并以工厂实时状态更新作为接地。在物理六模块六边形工厂的仿真环境中，对三种智能体架构（编排式、点对点、单体式）在九个复杂度递增的生产挑战上进行对比，其中包含静默硬件故障检测任务。

Result: 单体式和点对点架构均取得最高平均求解率（93%）。编排式架构在全部十次运行中唯一解决了传送带静默故障，通过自主将托盘绕开被阻塞路段实现重路由。所有架构都表现出涌现式故障诊断行为，且未包含任何显式故障处理逻辑。

Conclusion: 标准化的MCP工具接口、基于MQTT的智能体间通信以及实时状态注入，可作为LLM编程智能制造的可行且可复现的基础设施。

Abstract: Factories are shifting toward smaller lot sizes with high product customization, requiring frequent re-programming of flexible and reconfigurable automation systems. LLM-based agents can be deployed in two complementary roles: Offline, they generate deterministic production sequences, reducing programming effort; online, they operate live machines and handle unforeseen runtime faults that static programs cannot anticipate. We propose a solution in which each factory module is paired with a dedicated LLM-based agent and an MCP tool server that exposes the module's skills via OPC UA method calls, with agents coordinating over MQTT and grounded by real-time updates of the factory state. We compare three agent architectures (orchestrator, peer-to-peer, and monolithic) across nine production challenges of increasing complexity in a simulation of a physical six-module hexagonal factory, including silent hardware fault detection. The monolithic and peer-to-peer architectures both achieve the highest mean solve rate (93\%), while the orchestrator uniquely resolves a silent conveyor-belt fault in all ten runs by autonomously rerouting plates around the blocked segment. All architectures exhibit emergent fault-diagnosis behavior without any explicit failure-handling logic, establishing standardized MCP tooling, MQTT-based inter-agent communication, and real-time state injection as a viable and reproducible foundation for LLM-programmed smart manufacturing.

</details>


### [169] [Managing Context and Communication in Distributed Agentic UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01569)
*Andrea Iannoli,Ivan Zyrianoff,Angelo Trotta,Lorenzo Gigli,Marco Di Felice*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出分布式无人机集群SLM智能体架构，用事件驱动记忆与兴趣感知gossip引擎，降低推理与通信开销并提升搜救任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机集群用语言模型智能体做任务级推理时，若采用完全分布式（每架无人机搭载独立小语言模型SLM）可摆脱中心协调器，但带来信息管理难题：长期交互历史会污染推理上下文，而无差别信息广播又会增加通信与推理开销。

Method: 构建分布式UAV智能体架构，通过事件驱动的"推理-行动-观察"生命周期实现持续本地SLM控制；运行时知识表示为结构化原子笔记，并组织为核心、本地和同伴专属三类记忆；设计确定性、兴趣感知的gossip引擎，依据接收方的语义新颖性和时效性选择性传播笔记。

Result: 在十架无人机的模拟搜救任务中，该方法完成全部实验轮次；无限制洪泛消息仅完成70-85%；将转发决策交给SLM则每轮均无法完成任务。相比无限制洪泛，该方法推理token消耗约减半，传输数据减少，且幸存者计数误差更低。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆与兴趣感知的选择性传播能有效解决分布式SLM无人机集群的上下文退化与通信开销问题，在任务完成率和资源效率上均优于洪泛和SLM自主转发方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms increasingly rely on language-model agents to provide adaptive mission-level reasoning in uncertain environments. Fully distributed control, in which each UAV hosts an independent Small Language Model (SLM), removes reliance on a centralized coordinator but introduces an information-management problem: long-running interaction histories can degrade the reasoning context, while indiscriminate information dissemination increases communication and inference overhead. We address these challenges with a distributed UAV-agent architecture that enables continuous local SLM control through an event-driven reason-act-observe lifecycle. Runtime knowledge is represented as structured atomic notes and organized into core, local, and peer-specific memory. A deterministic interest-aware gossip engine selectively disseminates these notes according to recipient-specific semantic novelty and recency. We evaluate the architecture using ten UAVs in a simulated search-and-rescue mission. Our approach completes all experimental runs, whereas unrestricted flooding messages completes only 70-85\%, and delegating forwarding decisions to the SLM prevents mission completion in every run. Compared with unrestricted flooding, our approach approximately halves inference-token consumption, reduces transmitted data, and achieves lower survivor-count error.

</details>


### [170] [After Cooperation Is Learned: Gradient Routing and Optimizer-Dependent Maintenance in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01630)
*Chaoyuan Hao,Wentao Yue,Tianyou Lai,Hongji Li,Jiayi Zhou,Qingyu Mao,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体强化学习中的"合作维持"问题，将其形式化为右删失事件时间问题，通过匹配热启动对比三种梯度路由配置（X0、X1、X5），发现直接价值梯度路由在特定奖励尺度下会带来条件性、尺度敏感的合作维持风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作式MARL通常只评估从随机初始化开始的"合作发现"，不清楚继续优化是否会破坏已学到的合作。同时，actor-critic的对比常把"critic的存在"与"价值梯度进入共享actor表示"混为一谈，无法区分真正起作用的因素。

Method: 定义"合作维持"为行为已验证的合作策略在持续训练下的存活。将问题形式化为右删失事件时间问题，并比较三种匹配热启动配置：X0允许价值损失梯度更新共享actor特征；X1保留critic但阻断这些梯度；X5移除学到的critic作为无critic参照。采用正奖励缩放（保持策略偏好与均衡不变但扰动学习动态），用梯度审计验证路由路径，并用冻结策略躯干扰动探测路由引发的更新是否与局部合作边界一致。在MinEx和CleanUp-lite上进行验证性实验。

Result: 在验证性实验中，较高奖励尺度会选择性提高X0的维持敏感性；X1接近删失上限；X5在测试设置中无确认事件。在CleanUp-lite中，路由×尺度的位移与局部合作余量下降相关；MinEx显示较弱且依赖优化器的效应。

Conclusion: 结果表明，合作维持风险是与直接价值梯度路由相关的条件性、尺度敏感风险，而非critic的普遍性失效。研究为区分critic存在与价值梯度路由的作用提供了隔离性证据，并指出奖励缩放可能成为暴露该风险的机制。

Abstract: Cooperative MARL is commonly evaluated through cooperation discovery from random initialization, leaving open whether continued optimization can destabilize learned cooperation. Actor-critic comparisons can also conflate critic presence with value gradients entering shared actor representations. We study cooperation maintenance, defined as the survival of a behaviorally verified cooperative policy under continued training. We formulate maintenance as a right-censored event-time problem and compare matched warm starts: X0 allows value loss gradients to update shared actor features, X1 retains the critic while blocking those gradients, and X5 removes the learned critic as a critic-free reference. This isolates direct value-gradient access while controlling initialization, critic computation, and evaluation. Positive reward scaling preserves strategic preferences and equilibria while perturbing learning dynamics. Gradient audits confirm the intended routing pathways, and frozen-policy torso perturbations probe whether route-induced updates align with local cooperation boundaries. In confirmatory MinEx and CleanUp-lite experiments, higher scales selectively increase maintenance sensitivity in X0; X1 remains near the censoring ceiling, and X5 has no confirmed events in the tested settings. In CleanUp-lite, route-by-scale displacement is associated with reduced local cooperation margins; MinEx shows a weaker, optimizer-dependent effect. These results identify a conditional, scale-sensitive maintenance risk associated with direct value-gradient routing rather than a universal failure of critics.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Reverse Item Response Theory for Sparsity-Robust Ranking in Fragmented Cancer Drug-Response Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00002)
*Jung Min Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"反向IRT"方法，将癌症类型视为具有耐药能力的潜在"被试"、药物视为具有规避难度的"项目"，用于药物基因组学药物反应分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统药物敏感性分析常用简单平均，在数据缺失、稀疏或存在偏倚时难以稳健地恢复真实的潜在排序。作者希望借助心理测量学中的项目反应理论（IRT），在碎片化评估条件下提升癌症耐药性与药物活性的估计稳健性。

Method: 基于GDSC2数据库的242,036条药物敏感性测量，构建反向IRT模型，将癌症类型作为潜在"被试"（耐药能力）、药物作为"项目"（规避难度），在同一潜在尺度上估计癌症类型级别的体外耐药性和药物级别的广谱活性。通过四种缺失机制（含MCAR、癌症偏倚、药物偏倚稀疏）进行验证，并与简单平均对比；采用留出预测比较五种方法的Brier分数，并用Bootstrap置信区间评估分类稳定性，另在PRISM平台做跨平台复现。

Result: 在60%缺失率下，反向IRT恢复全数据潜在排序的能力优于简单平均，Delta-rho达+0.089至+0.095；留出预测中IRT在五种方法里Brier分数最佳；Bootstrap显示28种癌症类型中有19种具有稳定的耐药/敏感分类；PRISM跨平台复现显示82%的方向一致性，但排序相关性较弱（rho=0.25）。

Conclusion: 该工作的贡献在于碎片化评估条件下的方法学稳健性，而非提供普适的临床耐药排行榜。

Abstract: We introduce reverse Item Response Theory (IRT) to pharmacogenomic drug-response analysis by treating cancer types as latent "subjects" with resistance ability and drugs as "items" with evasion difficulty. Applied to 242,036 drug sensitivity measurements from the Genomics of Drug Sensitivity in Cancer (GDSC2) database, the model estimates cancer-type-level in-vitro resistance and drug-level broad activity on a shared latent scale. Validation across four missingness regimes demonstrates that reverse IRT better recovers the full-data latent ranking than simple averaging, with advantages of Delta-rho = +0.089 to +0.095 at 60% missingness under MCAR, cancer-biased, and drug-biased sparsity. Held-out prediction confirms IRT achieves the best Brier score among five evaluated methods. Bootstrap confidence intervals show 19 of 28 cancer types have stable resistant/sensitive classifications. Cross-platform PRISM replication shows 82% directional agreement but weak rank-order correlation (rho = 0.25), indicating the contribution is methodological robustness under fragmented evaluation, not a universal clinical resistance leaderboard.

</details>


### [172] [How Far is Adam from Natural Gradient Descent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00004)
*Vihaan Paka-Hegde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Adam优化器完整更新规则与自然梯度下降(NGD)的几何关系，发现Adam的几何轨迹依赖具体损失景观：良态条件下偏差小，病态条件下偏差显著增大，但最终仍能收敛到低损失，其优化能力可能源于结构近似误差与动量平滑的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam是深度学习中的标准优化器，但其与自然梯度下降(NGD)之间的几何关系仍存在未解问题，尤其缺乏对包含动量在内的完整更新规则的系统的几何偏差分析。

Method: 将Adam完整更新规则视为受对角截断、经验标签替换和时间滞后约束的对角经验Fisher近似，采用尺度不变的γ(Δθ)度量，在四类损失景观（良态线性回归、病态线性回归、逻辑回归、非凸小型神经网络）上测量Adam相对于真实NGD的几何偏差。

Result: Adam的几何轨迹具有情境依赖性：良态设置下偏差较低，病态条件下显著上升，在神经网络中错位程度约达10^3。较高的几何漂移与初始优化变慢相关，但不会损害最终目标最小化，Adam始终能达到低损失。此外，改进的经验Fisher(iEF)比标准经验Fisher(EF)路径更稳定，后者常出现振荡或发散。

Conclusion: Adam的实际优化能力可能源于结构近似误差与动量平滑之间的平衡，而非对自然梯度路径的紧密追踪。

Abstract: Adam is the standard optimizer in deep learning, yet its geometric relationship to natural gradient descent (NGD) contains unresolved questions. We study Adam's full update rule, including momentum, as a diagonal empirical Fisher approximation subject to diagonal truncation, empirical label substitution, and temporal lag. Using the scale-invariant $γ(Δθ)$ metric, we measure Adam's geometric deviation from true NGD across four loss landscapes: well-conditioned linear regression, ill-conditioned linear regression, logistic regression, and a non-convex small neural network. Adam's geometric trajectory is context-dependent. Deviation remains low in well-conditioned settings but rises significantly under ill-conditioning, reaching misalignments of $\approx 10^3$ in the neural network. Higher geometric drift correlates with slower initial optimization but does not degrade final objective minimization; Adam consistently reaches low loss. Furthermore, the improved empirical Fisher (iEF) tracks more stable paths than the standard empirical Fisher (EF), which frequently oscillates or diverges. Our results suggest Adam's practical optimization power may stem from a balance of structural approximation errors and momentum smoothing rather than close tracking of the natural gradient path.

</details>


### [173] [FourierQK: Filter Shape, Admissibility and the Leakage-Coverage Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00009)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过受控消融实验，系统研究Frequency-collapse attention中滤波器形状的性质，发现直流与Nyquist成分有害、最优单尺度带宽约σ≈2 bins以段级（~70 token）为中心，容许性滤波器（零均值DOG）优于高斯，并指出FourierQK适用于双向注意力场景，自回归生成需因果谱变体MorletQK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Frequency-collapse attention通过以学习频率上的带通滤波内积替代Q/K点积，在标准点积注意力上取得大幅提升。然而，何种滤波器形状最优及其原因尚不明确，本文旨在通过受控实验回答滤波器属性对性能的影响机制。

Method: 在字符级语言建模任务（TinyShakespeare，6层GPT）上进行受控消融，围绕五个滤波器属性假设展开测试：直流抑制、Nyquist抑制、带宽、中心频率与多尺度覆盖。对比对象包括BASE-DOT、零均值墨西哥帽DOG（m=2）与同尺度非容许高斯滤波器，并分析双边FFT泄漏与频谱覆盖的关系，以及因果时域Morlet在字符尺度的表现。

Result: (1) 直流与Nyquist成分主动有害（val≈2.0，等价于相位随机化），证明振荡带通结构不可或缺；(2) 最优单尺度带宽σ≈2 bins、中心在段级(~70 tokens)，较BASE-DOT获Δ=+1.15 nats增益；(3) 同尺度下容许滤波器（零均值DOG，m=2）优于非容许高斯，并可部分抵御双边FFT泄漏；(4) 双边FFT泄漏随频谱覆盖单调增长，窄带（gap>+4）干净、宽带（gap<+2）泄漏；(5) 字符尺度因果时域Morlet无法超越BASE-DOT（K=128 taps仅覆盖T=256上下文的50%）。

Conclusion: 综合五项发现，FourierQK在双向注意力设置（编码器式，如BERT）中有效，因训练与推理均可获得全序列上下文；而自回归生成需采用因果谱变体（如MorletQK，解码器式，如GPT）。

Abstract: Frequency-collapse attention [Zeris, 2026e] achieves large gains over standard dot-product attention by replacing the Q/K dot product with a bandpass-filtered inner product at a learned frequency. A natural follow-up question is: which filter shape works best, and why? We test five hypotheses about filter properties -- DC suppression, Nyquist suppression, bandwidth, centre frequency, and multi-scale coverage -- using a controlled ablation on character-level language modelling (TinyShakespeare, 6-layer GPT). Our main findings are: (1) DC and Nyquist components are actively harmful (val ~= 2.0, equivalent to phase randomisation), confirming that oscillatory bandpass structure is essential, not just any low-dimensional spectral summary; (2) the optimal single-scale bandwidth is sigma ~= 2 bins centred at paragraph scale (~70 tokens), giving a clean gain of Delta = +1.15 nats over BASE-DOT; (3) admissible filters (zero-mean, Mexican Hat DOG m = 2) outperform non-admissible Gaussians at the same scale and provide partial protection against bilateral FFT leakage; (4) bilateral FFT leakage scales monotonically with spectral coverage -- narrowband filters (gap > +4) are clean, wideband filters (gap < +2) are leaky; and (5) causal time-domain Morlet at character scale cannot beat BASE-DOT (K=128 taps covers 50% of T=256 context), motivating word-level experiments in the companion MorletQK paper [Zeris, 2026f]. Together, findings (1)-(5) characterise FourierQK as effective in bidirectional attention settings (encoder-style, e.g. BERT), where full-sequence context is available at both training and inference time; autoregressive generation requires a causal spectral variant such as MorletQK [Zeris, 2026f] (decoder-style, e.g. GPT). Code available at: https://github.com/AthanasiosZeris/energy-gated-attention

</details>


### [174] [Integrating Fairness and Explainability in a Multiple Instance Reinforcement Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00035)
*Bente Hinkenhuis,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将强化学习多示例学习（RL-MIL）、对抗去偏与偏好条件超网络结合的多目标框架，用于学生风险预测；结果显示基线分类性能强，但超网络扩展出现模式崩溃，无法连续控制公平性与性能的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育交互数据的学生表现预测既需要准确性，也需要透明性以支持有效干预，同时人口统计信息会带来不公平预测风险。因此需要探索如何在可解释的RL-MIL流程中引入公平性目标，并实现可控的多目标权衡。

Method: 采用多目标框架：以RL-MIL将学生建模为弱标注交互的"包"，由RL智能体选择信息量大的实例供下游分类；结合对抗去偏处理人口统计偏差；并评估两种偏好条件超网络变体，尝试通过用户定义的偏好标量连续控制预测性能与Equalized Odds之间的权衡。

Result: RL-MIL基线取得了较强的分类性能，但两种超网络扩展均出现模式崩溃：改变偏好权重几乎无法使模型沿着预期的公平性—性能前沿系统移动。失败原因与目标支配、条件机制中梯度传播弱以及动态生成参数之间的相互作用有关。

Conclusion: 公平性目标可以嵌入可解释的RL-MIL流程，但仅靠偏好条件化并不能保证可控的多目标行为。稳健的公平RL-MIL需要显式的梯度平衡机制、目标分离和稳定性分析。

Abstract: Predicting student performance from educational interaction data requires models that are both accurate and sufficiently transparent to support meaningful intervention, while demographic information introduces an additional risk of unfair predictions. This study investigates a multi-objective framework that combines reinforcement learning-based multiple instance learning (RL-MIL), adversarial debiasing, and preference-conditioned hypernetworks for student-at-risk prediction. MIL represents each student as a bag of weakly labeled interactions, while an RL agent selects informative instances for downstream classification. Two hypernetwork variants are evaluated to determine whether a user-defined preference scalar can continuously control the trade-off between predictive performance and Equalized Odds. The underlying RL-MIL baseline achieves strong classification performance, but both hypernetwork extensions exhibit mode collapse: changing the preference weight produces little systematic movement along the intended fairness-performance frontier. The failure is associated with objective dominance, weak gradient propagation through the conditioning mechanism, and interactions between dynamically generated parameters. The results show that fairness objectives can be incorporated into an interpretable RL-MIL pipeline, but preference conditioning alone does not guarantee controllable multi-objective behavior. Robust fair RL-MIL therefore requires explicit mechanisms for gradient balancing, objective separation, and stability analysis.

</details>


### [175] [Fast Polynomial Transcendentals for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00049)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用短多项式程序替换LLM中sigmoid、tanh、SiLU等SFU运算，在Blackwell GB200上获得1.19–2.19x(隔离L2)加速及最高8.0%训练步吞吐提升，且训练损失差异极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU各代对矩阵、特殊函数与内存流水线的扩展速率不同，导致内核瓶颈随硬件演进而转移（如FlashAttention-4在NVIDIA Blackwell上暴露的注意力内部失衡）。作者据此探究：是否可用短多项式程序加速LLM中其他特殊函数单元(SFU)运算。

Method: 先做隔离的FP16 IEEE测速扫描，比较原生PyTorch与打包融合乘加(FMA)程序，覆盖L2驻留与HBM驻留工作集；再用3次或4次BF16多项式替换原生sigmoid、tanh、SiLU，并部署到四个GB200集成任务：稠密SiLU、tanh-softcap注意力、sigmoid注意力、路由专家SwiGLU。程序结合解析对称性、目标格式舍入与打包算术嵌入消费内核。

Result: 隔离路径在L2提升1.19–2.19x，在HBM提升1.00–1.70x。稠密SiLU、tanh-softcap注意力、路由专家替换分别将完整训练步吞吐提升2.7%、2.9%、8.0%；sigmoid注意力替换使完整注意力前向提升7.4%、完整GPU步提升0.3%。同检查点开放权重消融与每任务一次配对预训练对比表明，约1000亿token时最终平滑训练损失差异（多项式减原生）在-0.107至+0.079之间。

Conclusion: 短多项式程序可有效加速LLM中的SFU运算，在保持模型训练行为基本不变的前提下带来实际端到端吞吐收益，硬件瓶颈演变为此类内核级优化提供了空间。
