Abstract: Graphics processing unit (GPU) generations scale matrix, special-function, and memory pipelines at different rates, so kernel bottlenecks move as hardware evolves. FlashAttention-4 exposed this imbalance inside attention on NVIDIA Blackwell. We test whether short polynomial programs can accelerate other special-function-unit (SFU) operations in large language models (LLMs). We first compare native PyTorch evaluation with packed fused multiply--add (FMA) programs in an isolated IEEE binary16 (FP16) sweep spanning L2-resident and high-bandwidth-memory (HBM)-resident working sets. We then replace native sigmoid, tanh, and sigmoid linear unit (SiLU) with degree-3 or degree-4 bfloat16 (BF16) programs in four GB200 integration tasks: dense SiLU, tanh-softcapped attention, sigmoid attention, and routed-expert Swish-gated linear unit (SwiGLU). The programs combine analytical symmetry, target-format rounding, and packed arithmetic inside consuming kernels. The isolated paths improve by 1.19--2.19x in L2 and 1.00--1.70x in HBM. The dense-SiLU, tanh-softcapped-attention, and routed-expert substitutions improve complete training-step throughput by 2.7\%, 2.9\%, and 8.0\%, respectively. The sigmoid-attention substitution improves complete-attention forward by 7.4\% and the complete GPU step by 0.3\%. Same-checkpoint open-weight ablations and one paired pre-training comparison per task extend the evaluation to model behavior. At common horizons near 100 billion tokens, the final smoothed training-loss differences (polynomial minus native) range from $-0.107$ to $+0.079$ across the four tasks.

</details>


### [176] [SW-KAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Stieltjes-Wigert q-Orthogonal Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00050)
*Amirhosein Azarpour,Seyyed Moein Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SW-KAN，用Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式替代KAN中的固定激活，解决无界输入与多项式有界支撑的域不匹配问题，在图像分类与函数逼近中实现更优的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始KAN使用B样条计算开销大；已有基于多项式的KAN变体虽降低了计算成本，但忽略了无界实数输入与正交多项式有界/半无限支撑之间的域不匹配这一根本问题，限制了其表达能力与稳定性。

Method: 提出SW-KAN架构，采用定义在半无限域(0, ∞)上的Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式；设计平滑的exponential-of-tanh映射以稳定桥接域差距并保持良好梯度条件；使用数值稳定的三项递推关系，在O(N)操作内完成多项式展开评估，无需调用特殊函数。

Result: 在图像分类与连续函数逼近的广泛实验中，SW-KAN在多种任务上取得更优的精度-效率权衡；其对数正态权重结构与可学习q参数形成独特归纳偏置，在特征维度降低、训练数据有限等资源受限条件下表现稳健；超越现有多项式KAN基线，并能以极少参数逼近复杂多元函数。

Conclusion: SW-KAN通过引入Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式及稳定的域映射与递推计算，有效解决了多项式KAN的域不匹配问题，是资源受限场景下高效函数逼近与分类的有前景替代方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) represent a paradigmatic shift in deep learning by replacing fixed node activations with learnable univariate functions on edges, offering enhanced interpretability and parameter efficiency. While recent polynomial-based KAN variants have addressed the computational overhead of original B-spline implementations, they introduce a fundamental yet underexplored challenge: the domain mismatch between unbounded real-valued inputs and the bounded or semi-infinite support of orthogonal polynomial bases. To address this limitation, we propose the Stieltjes-Wigert Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (SW-KAN), a novel architecture that employs Stieltjes-Wigert q-orthogonal polynomials defined on the semi-infinite domain (0, infinity). We introduce a smooth exponential-of-tanh mapping that stably bridges the domain gap while preserving well-conditioned gradients, and leverage a numerically stable three-term recurrence that evaluates polynomial expansions in O(N) operations without special-function calls. Through comprehensive experiments spanning image classification and continuous function approximation, we demonstrate that SW-KAN achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across diverse tasks. The log-normal weight structure and learnable q-parameter of Stieltjes-Wigert polynomials provide a distinct inductive bias that enables robust performance under resource-constrained conditions, including reduced feature dimensionality and limited training data. The proposed architecture not only outperforms established polynomial KAN baselines on standard benchmarks but also exhibits strong representational capacity for approximating complex multivariate functions with remarkably few parameters, making it a compelling alternative for efficient function approximation and classification in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [177] [Format-Aware Fusion for Fast FP4 Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00053)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"格式感知融合"方法，通过在FP4量化中协同设计缩放域与消费端布局，显著提升Llama-3 8B预训练速度并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4 Tensor Core虽能加速矩阵乘法，但缩放计算、操作数打包、布局构建及反向状态保存等开销会抵消其收益，因此需要系统性协同优化。

Method: 提出format-aware fusion，将每个量化生产者与其缩放域和消费布局协同设计，支持原生mxfp、全局nvfp及CTA-local nvfp三种格式；结合行梯度随机舍入与固定符号32值Hadamard权重梯度预条件。

Result: 在Llama-3 8B、160B token预训练中，bfloat16与Transformer Engine nvfp分别为18.8K和27.6K tokens/s/GPU，最快定制路线达37.9K；mxfp方案达37.2K（86.3% bfloat16模型FLOP利用率），训练损失终点比bfloat16高2.11%；TE配方末端四个bfloat16块方案高0.87%（27.1K）。下游排名与训练损失排名不一致。

Conclusion: FP4训练效果取决于缩放契约、操作数与执行路径的联合设计，格式感知融合可在保持精度的同时大幅提升训练吞吐。

Abstract: Four-bit floating-point (FP4) Tensor Cores accelerate matrix multiplication, but scale computation, operand packing, layout construction, and saved backward state can erase the gain. We present \emph{format-aware fusion}, which co-designs each quantization producer with its scale domain and consumer layout for native \mxfp{}, global \nvfp{}, and cooperative-thread-array-local \nvfp{}. We evaluate Llama-3-family 8B pretraining through 160 billion tokens using bfloat16 output projections and compiled cross entropy. In matched same-accelerator probes, bfloat16 and Transformer Engine \nvfp{} reach 18.8K and 27.6K tokens/s/GPU, while our fastest custom route reaches 37.9K. \mxfp{} with row-gradient stochastic rounding and fixed-sign 32-value Hadamard weight-gradient preconditioning reaches 37.2K tokens/s/GPU (86.3\% bfloat16 model FLOP utilization) and ends 2.11\% above the raw bfloat16 training-loss endpoint. A Transformer Engine recipe with four final bfloat16 blocks ends 0.87\% above bfloat16 at 27.1K tokens/s/GPU. Downstream rankings differ from training-loss rankings, showing that FP4 outcomes depend jointly on scale contract, operand, and execution path.

</details>


### [178] ["very likely" Means "uncertain"? How LLMs Diverge from Humans in Linguistic Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00083)
*Jinhao Duan,Zicheng Liu,Zijie Liu,Kaidi Xu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM与人类在语言不确定性表达上的差异，并提出METHODNAME算法从LLM输出中直接学习不确定性标记的最优不确定性分布，实现人类与LLM置信语义的标记级对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化多依赖代价较高的似然或一致性信号，而人类通过"可能""很可能"等语言标记表达不确定性，这反映元认知监控与知识边界。论文动机是探究LLM在语言不确定性量化上与人类的偏差，以及语言标记能否可靠地量化LLM不确定性。

Method: 从心理学与决策科学文献中整理人类不确定性标记语料，并以此对LLM进行基准测试；随后提出METHODNAME，一种基于优化的算法，直接从LLM输出中学习不确定性标记的最优不确定性分布，通过拟合标记—不确定性映射以最好地解释实际正确性，而非依赖重复采样估计不确定性。

Result: 观察到LLM对语言不确定性所编码的数值水平与人类存在显著差异；METHODNAME能够为每个语言标记分配应传达的概率质量，并支持人类与LLM在标记层面的置信语义直接比较，从而分离不匹配并揭示语言表达中的系统性置信差异。

Conclusion: 语言标记可作为量化LLM不确定性的可靠信号；METHODNAME提供了一种直接、标记级的分析框架，揭示人类与LLM在语言置信表达上的系统性差异，为LLM不确定性量化与元认知对齐提供新思路。

Abstract: Humans express uncertainty verbally via markers (e.g., "possible," "likely"), yet most LLM uncertainty quantification (UQ) relies on costing likelihood- or consistency-based signals. From a cognitive perspective, accurate verbal uncertainty reflects metacognitive monitoring, representing knowledge boundaries ("knowing that you don't know") to support regulation and information seeking. In this paper, we investigate how LLMs diverge from humans in verbal uncertainty quantification and whether verbal markers can reliably quantify LLM uncertainty. We curate a corpus of human uncertainty markers from psychology and decision-science literature and benchmark LLMs against it. We observe that LLMs encode verbal uncertainty with numerical levels that differ substantially from those of humans. We then introduce METHODNAME, a novel optimization-based algorithm that learns an optimal uncertainty profile over uncertainty markers directly from LLM outputs. By fitting a marker-uncertainty mapping to best explain empirical correctness, METHODNAME discovers how much probability mass each verbal marker should convey, rather than estimating uncertainty via repeated sampling. METHODNAME enables a direct, marker-level comparison of confidence semantics between humans and LLMs, disentangling mismatch and revealing systematic confidence disparities in verbal expressions.

</details>


### [179] [Nous: Learning and Certifying Memory Decisions Before Source Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00094)
*Pranav Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于信念的智能体记忆中的决策问题，区分学习、校准与修订认证，证明学习并认证有用决策所需的样本量可远少于源可靠性校准，并提出有限样本证书与鲁棒扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于信念的智能体记忆需要可靠的当前状态判断，但其证据可能噪声大、被复制或已过时。核心疑问是：记忆是否必须先校准其来源，才能改进决策。作者希望厘清学习、校准与修订认证之间的关系，判断在何种条件下记忆决策可以被学习并得到证明，而无需恢复源可靠性。

Method: 作者在一个四模型隐马尔可夫族上分离学习、校准与修订认证，推导样本复杂度界；将决策速率与源估计下界推广到更广的模型类。在未知身份加背景的报告信道下，刻画策略改进的尖锐可识别区间，并利用可观测见证区域导出有限样本证书，不依赖纯类锚点。进一步给出鲁棒性扩展，容忍有界的历史相关错误设定与条件复制，分裂训练的见证适用于任意历史空间并给出显式功效条件。最后将策略界收据与 Nous Dimensions 集成，在 45,000 条留出可变状态历史和三个外部 MiniGrid 记忆环境（引入噪声报告接口）的 9,000 个回合上测试。

Result: 理论上，学习未知贝叶斯决策需 Theta(l^-2) 条记录，认证其相对有信息量基线的改进需 O(l^-2) 条同分布新记录，而固定精度源估计在持续性 l 趋于零时需 Theta(l^-4)，即学习与认证有用决策可比源校准少用二次方量级的记录。实验上，新证书在 MiniGrid 中接受 9/9 相对常数基线的改进、4/9 相对 last-write-wins 的改进，而早先的证书一个也不接受；但强基线推理方法仍具竞争力或更好。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一个统计学解释，说明在哪些条件下记忆决策可以被学习并得到证明，而无需恢复源可靠性；其贡献不是提出普遍更优的记忆算法，而是厘清校准与决策认证之间的样本复杂度差异及可识别性边界。

Abstract: Belief-based agent memory needs reliable decisions about current state, yet its evidence may be noisy, copied, or stale. Must a memory calibrate its sources before it can improve its decisions? We separate learning, calibration, and revision certification. On one four-model hidden Markov family, learning an unknown Bayes decision requires Theta(l^-2) records and certifying its improvement over an informative incumbent takes O(l^-2) fresh records from the same observation law, while fixed-precision source estimation requires Theta(l^-4) as persistence l vanishes. Thus learning and certifying useful decisions can require quadratically fewer records than source calibration. A broader model class retains the decision rate and source lower bound. Under an unknown identity-plus-background report channel, we characterize the sharp identified interval for policy improvement and derive a finite-sample certificate using observable witness regions, without pure-class anchors. A robustness extension tolerates bounded history-dependent misspecification and conditional copying; split-trained witnesses apply to arbitrary history spaces with explicit power conditions. We integrate policy-bound receipts with Nous Dimensions and test 45,000 held-out mutable-state histories and 9,000 episodes in three external MiniGrid memory environments with an introduced noisy-report interface. The new certificate accepts 9/9 improvements over a constant incumbent and 4/9 over last-write-wins, versus none for the earlier certificate in MiniGrid. Strong established inference baselines remain competitive or better. The result is a statistical account of when memory decisions can be learned and justified without recovering source reliability, not a universally superior memory algorithm.

</details>


### [180] [One Mastery Threshold Does Not Fit All Knowledge Tracing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00095)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识追踪（KT）模型中掌握阈值的选择问题，发现同一数值阈值在不同模型下会产生截然不同的教学决策，因此阈值需随模型和教学优先级重新校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅导系统依赖掌握阈值来决定学生何时可以停止练习并进阶，但同一数值阈值在不同知识追踪模型下会导致差异很大的决策，因此需要系统评估阈值选择的影响。

Method: 在四个公开教育数据集上考察六种知识追踪模型，使用12个从0.50到0.99的阈值，并从进阶后表现、进阶覆盖率、练习负担以及不同先前表现群体间的差异四个维度进行评估；同时在30种预设教学情境下识别能平衡表现、额外练习与进阶的阈值。

Result: 贝叶斯知识追踪（BKT）对阈值变化相对不敏感，而神经模型随阈值升高变得选择性更强，部分原因在于BKT估计潜在掌握概率、神经模型估计下一次答对的概率。最佳平衡阈值在不同模型和情境间差异显著；半数情境中神经模型与BKT的选定阈值相差超过0.10。更严格的阈值并不能可靠缩小表现差距，反而可能过度限制进阶，先前表现较强的学生进阶频率可达较弱学生的3.26倍。

Conclusion: 当知识追踪模型或教学优先级改变时，掌握阈值应重新校准，并应通过其对表现、练习、进阶和可及性的影响进行评估。

Abstract: Tutoring systems use mastery thresholds to decide when students can stop practicing and advance, but the same numerical threshold can lead to very different decisions when the underlying knowledge tracing (KT) model changes. We examine six KT models across four public educational datasets and evaluate 12 thresholds from 0.50 to 0.99 using post-advancement performance, advancement coverage, practice burden, and disparities across prior-performance groups. We also identify thresholds that balance performance, extra practice, and advancement under 30 predefined instructional settings. Bayesian Knowledge Tracing (BKT) is relatively insensitive to threshold changes, while neural models become much more selective as thresholds increase. This partly reflects different model outputs: BKT estimates latent mastery probability, whereas neural models estimate the probability of a correct next response, so the same cutoff does not represent the same level of mastery. The best-balanced threshold varied substantially across models and settings. In half of the tested settings, neural models and BKT differed by more than 0.10 in their selected thresholds, although this gap became smaller when greater priority was placed on reducing extra practice and allowing more students to advance. Stricter thresholds also did not reliably reduce performance gaps and could disproportionately restrict advancement, with stronger-prior students advancing up to 3.26 times as often as weaker-prior students. These results show that mastery thresholds should be recalibrated when the KT model or instructional priorities change and evaluated by their effects on performance, practice, advancement, and access.

</details>


### [181] [Fully Online Decentralized Learning in Stochastic Games with Unknown Independent Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01181)
*S. Rasoul Etesami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有独立受控链和未知转移核的随机博弈，提出了一个完全在线、去中心化且无协调的对偶镜像下降算法，以逼近平稳纳什均衡策略，并给出O(T^{-1/2})的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机博弈学习通常依赖联合状态空间覆盖、同步回合或集中协调，且复杂度易随玩家数量及联合状态/动作空间规模指数增长。本文动机是设计可扩展、完全在线且仅依赖局部信息的分散式学习框架，处理未知独立受控链下的均衡求解问题。

Method: 在占用测度的对偶空间中运行完全在线、去中心化、无协调的镜像下降算法；每个原始时间步仅使用单个转移/奖励样本，仅依赖局部信息，无需联合状态空间覆盖或同步回合。在一致遍历性与有限覆盖假设下分析时间平均固定比较器遗憾。

Result: 高概率下时间平均固定比较器遗憾以O(T^{-1/2})速率衰减（至多对数因子和关于博弈参数的多项式依赖）；复杂度取决于各局部状态空间的覆盖时间而非乘积状态空间，避免对玩家数量及联合状态、动作空间规模的指数依赖。有限时间遗憾界进一步给出近似粗略相关均衡保证；在额外全局变分稳定性条件下，同一算法在最后一次迭代渐近收敛到平稳ε-纳什均衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为未知独立链随机博弈提供了完全在线且可扩展的学习框架，也可视为利用玩家受控转移链独立性与局部结构的马尔可夫博弈原始-对偶框架。

Abstract: We consider stochastic games with independent controlled chains and unknown transition kernels, where players observe only their local states and realized payoffs. We develop a fully online, decentralized, and uncoordinated mirror-descent algorithm that operates in the dual space of occupancy measures for approximating stationary Nash equilibrium (NE) policies. The algorithm uses a single transition/reward sample at every primitive time step, relies only on local information, and requires neither coverage of the joint state space nor synchronized episodes. Under uniform-ergodicity and finite-coverage assumptions, we show that, with high probability, the time-averaged fixed-comparator regret decays at the canonical $O(T^{-1/2})$ rate, up to logarithmic factors and polynomial dependence on the game parameters. In particular, the complexity depends on the cover times of the individual local state spaces rather than the product state space, avoiding exponential dependence on the number of players and the sizes of the joint state and action spaces. The resulting finite-time regret bound further yields an approximate coarse-correlated-equilibrium guarantee, which is natural for arbitrary reward functions since computing a stationary $ε$-NE is PPAD-hard in this setting. Under an additional global variational-stability condition, we show that the same fully online algorithm converges asymptotically in the last iterate to a stationary $ε$-NE. Our results provide a fully online and scalable learning framework for stochastic games with unknown independent chains. The algorithm can also be viewed as a primal-dual framework for Markov games that exploits the independence and local structure of the players' controlled transition chains.

</details>


### [182] [Uncertainty-Aware Learning from Multi-Expert Interval Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00102)
*Samira Alkaee Taleghan,Younghyun Koo,Andrew P. Barrett,Farnoush Banaei-Kashani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出保留个体专家区间标签的概率建模方法，分离标签内不精确性与专家间差异，并验证预测不确定性分解的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML应用依赖专家标注，专家对同一观测可能有不同但合理的解释，这种差异反映真实分歧或模糊性而非标注错误；当专家还报告区间而非精确值时，监督中同时存在标签内不精确性和专家间差异两种标签不确定性。现有方法（多专家方法、区间目标方法、预测不确定性方法）分别处理这两类不确定性，很少将预测不确定性估计与观察到的专家分歧进行验证。

Method: 1) 将异构标签词表统一到公共概率标签空间，分离编码差异与专家判断；2) 保留个体标签区间，用Beta混合分布建模，采用适当的Cramér距离目标训练，保留不同专家报告的标签；3) 将预测不确定性分解为成分内、成分间和模型不确定性，评估其是否分别对应标签内不确定性、标签间不确定性和模型误差；4) 由于该对应关系不保证成立，引入"分解匹配"（decomposition matching）将预测成分对齐到其预期的标签侧来源。

Result: 在海冰浓度（sea-ice concentration）数据上，模型相比硬标签将MAE降低31%，并优于聚合、区间分布和区间回归等基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过保留个体专家区间、分离标签内不精确性与专家间差异并验证预测不确定性成分的对应关系，所提方法能更准确地建模含专家分歧的区间监督，并在海冰浓度任务上显著提升性能。

Abstract: Many machine learning (ML) applications rely on expert labels, and qualified experts may provide different but plausible interpretations of the same observation. Such variation across expert labels may reflect genuine disagreement or ambiguity rather than annotation error. When individual experts additionally report intervals rather than exact values, the supervision contains two distinct sources of label uncertainty: within-label imprecision and between-expert variation. Existing methods treat these forms separately: multi-expert approaches collapse labels to a consensus, interval-target methods often yield a single prediction, and predictive-uncertainty methods rarely validate their uncertainty estimates against observed expert disagreement. To address this problem, we propose an approach that preserves individual expert intervals, separates within-label imprecision from between-expert variation, and validates the corresponding predictive uncertainty components. First, heterogeneous label vocabularies are harmonized into a common probabilistic label space, separating encoding differences from expert judgement. Second, individual label intervals are retained and modeled with a mixture of Beta distributions trained using a proper Cramér-distance objective, preserving distinct expert-reported labels. Third, we decompose predictive uncertainty into within-component, between-component, and model uncertainty, and evaluate whether these components correspond to within-label uncertainty, between-label uncertainty, and model error, respectively. Because this correspondence is not guaranteed, we introduce decomposition matching, which aligns the predictive components to their intended label-side sources. On sea-ice concentration the model reduces MAE by 31\% over hard labels and outperforms aggregation, interval-distribution, and interval-regression baselines.

</details>


### [183] [The Hidden Costs of 99% Accuracy: A Trustworthiness Audit of the Telco Customer Churn Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00118)
*Soumyadeep Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文审计了IBM Telco客户流失基准中四类被忽视的可信度问题（SMOTE泄漏、冗余特征、校准失效、阈值次优），并提出四组件报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户流失预测文献普遍报告95%以上测试准确率（最高99.01%），但以准确率和F1为中心的评估掩盖了数据泄漏、特征冗余、概率校准和决策阈值等可信度问题，导致结果被高估且解释被误导。

Method: 在n=7,043的IBM Telco Churn基准上进行审计：跨10个分类器和15个随机种子比较预划分SMOTE与类加权；用R2诊断TotalCharges与tenure×MonthlyCharges的近似确定性关系并对比TreeSHAP排名；开展15种子校准审计比较等渗回归与温度缩放；在50美元挽留成本与24个月CLV假设下比较成本最优与F1最优阈值；并在伊朗电信与银行客户流失数据集上做域内与跨域复现。

Result: 预划分SMOTE使流失类F1虚高13.1个百分点（Wilcoxon p<10^-4），约36%合成点来自测试集样本的最近邻插值，而类加权无此膨胀；TotalCharges的R2=0.999，删除后准确率变化不足0.2个百分点却被TreeSHAP排第9；等渗回归为最强默认校准，温度缩放在类加权树集成上失效；成本最优阈值比F1最优阈值低约5-10倍，每千客户节省约77,000美元；F1在域内跨域均泛化，F2仅在电信域内泛化。

Conclusion: 该基准的高准确率部分源于泄漏与冗余而非真实预测能力，需以流水线披露、冗余诊断、校准审计和成本敏感阈值四组件报告清单加以规范，并公开可复现实现。

Abstract: Customer churn prediction on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (n = 7,043) routinely reports test accuracies above 95%, with the most cited published study reporting 99.01%. We audit this benchmark for four trustworthiness failures invisible to the accuracy- and F1-centred reporting that dominates the literature. First, pre-split SMOTE inflates churn-class F1 by 13.1 percentage points across ten classifiers and fifteen seeds (Wilcoxon p < 10^-4 per classifier); the same leaky pipeline ordering paired with class weighting yields no inflation, isolating the effect to SMOTE's geometric construction. We measure the mechanism directly: approximately 36% of synthetic training points are nearest-neighbour interpolations of test-set instances. Second, the TotalCharges field is approximately determined by tenure multiplied by MonthlyCharges (R2 = 0.999); removing it changes accuracy by less than 0.2 percentage points, yet TreeSHAP ranks it ninth in mean absolute attribution - a pattern that materially corrupts SHAP-based interpretation. We propose an R2 > 0.95 pre-modelling diagnostic. Third, in a 15-seed calibration audit, isotonic regression is the strongest default; temperature scaling fails on class-weighted tree ensembles whose predicted-probability distribution is bimodal. Fourth, the cost-optimal decision threshold (under a 50 USD retention offer and 24-month CLV proxy) is approximately 5-10 times lower than the F1-optimal threshold, saving approximately 77,000 USD per 1,000 customers. We replicate F1 and F2 on Iranian Telecom Churn (within domain) and Bank Customer Churn (across domain): F1 generalises; F2 generalises only within telecom. We synthesise these findings into a four-component reporting checklist - pipeline disclosure, redundancy diagnostic, calibration audit, and cost-sensitive thresholds - and release a reproducible implementation.

</details>


### [184] [Generalized Biomedicine Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00120)
*Luyao Tang,Yingkai Yang,Hanqi Chen,Jiewei Zheng,Chaoqi Chen,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通用生物医学发现（GBD）任务与统一基准，并提出SCAN方法应对医学影像中的开放世界分布偏移（长尾、异常、层级分类）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床中医学影像面临开放世界偏移：长尾罕见病、正常解剖主导的细微病灶、以及层级分类体系。而多数开放世界范式假设扁平、均衡的标签空间，无法满足这些生物医学需求。

Method: 论文提出GBD任务及覆盖长尾、异常、分类感知发现的统一基准。核心洞察是主导性已知模式形成视觉流形，掩盖了细微的新颖性。受专家诊断启发，提出SCAN（Surprise-evoked Complementary AccommodatioN），遵循认知启发的感知进程：先用预测抑制过滤预期常态，再以惊讶诱发显著性突出意外偏差，最后通过互补适应将这些偏移整合进全局表示。

Result: 大量实验表明，SCAN在提升新概念发现能力的同时，总体上保留了既有临床知识；且可嵌入现有架构，更好地平衡医学影像中的已知-未知权衡。

Conclusion: SCAN为医学影像开放世界发现提供了一种兼顾新概念发现与既有知识保留的有效方案，并可即插即用地集成到现有架构中。

Abstract: In real-world clinical practice, medical images face open-world shifts: (i) long-tailed rare diseases, (ii) subtle lesions dominated by normal anatomy, and (iii) hierarchical taxonomies. Yet most open-world paradigms assume flat, balanced label spaces, leaving these biomedical demands unresolved. We introduce Generalized Biomedicine Discovery (GBD) and a unified benchmark spanning long-tail, anomaly, and taxonomy-aware discovery. Our key insight is that dominant known patterns form a visual manifold that masks subtle novelty. Inspired by expert diagnosis, we propose SCAN (Surprise-evoked Complementary AccommodatioN), which follows a cognition-inspired perceptual progression: it applies predictive suppression to filter expected norms, triggers surprise-evoked salience to highlight unexpected deviations, and performs complementary accommodation to integrate these shifts into global representations. Extensive experiments show that SCAN improves novel concept discovery while generally preserving established clinical knowledge, and it plugs into existing architectures to better navigate the known-unknown trade-off in medical imaging. Code is available at https://github.com/lytang63/generalized-biomedicine-discovery.

</details>


### [185] [Classification Based on Association Rules Algorithm for Breast Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00174)
*Ali Alsalama,Ahmed Kubba,Ghaith Jamjoum,Zaher Al Aghbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于加权分类的关联规则数据挖掘技术，用于乳腺癌分类，包含规则生成、规则剪枝和规则预测三个核心算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌是全球女性死亡的重要原因，发病率高，因此早期检测需求迫切。关联规则分类方法因结果易于被医学专业人员理解而受到青睐，本文据此提出改进方法。

Method: 采用基于加权分类的关联规则挖掘技术，包含三个核心算法：Rule Generation（发现频繁项集并生成关联规则）、Rule Pruning（按特定标准剔除规则，并依据其对训练数据的影响分为主要和次要组）、Rule Prediction（利用剪枝后的规则对测试数据进行分类）。

Result: 在多个测试样本上验证了所提方法的可行性与性能。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于乳腺癌分类，具有一定的可行性和性能表现，但其具体实验指标未在摘要中给出。

Abstract: Breast cancer is a significant contributor to female mortality across the world, displaying one of the highest oc currence rates among the various cancer types. In response to the need for early breast cancer detection, researchers have increasingly turned to association rule-based classification as a favored method. Association Rule mining is a data mining approach which offers the benefit of yielding results that are readily understandable for medical professionals. This paper introduces a novel association rule-based data mining technique for breast cancer classification based on a weighted classification approach. This implementation employs three core algorithms: Rule Generation, Rule Pruning, and Rule Prediction. Rule Generation identifies frequent itemsets and creates association rules. Rule Pruning eliminates rules using specific criteria and separates them into major and minor groups based on their influence on training data. Rule Prediction applies the pruned rules to classify test data. The final prediction algorithm was tested on several testing samples to show the feasibility and performance of the approach.

</details>


### [186] [Four Ways to Grow a Classifier and Why One of Them Cannot Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00180)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一实验协议下考察树结构与构造式分类器中四种"生长决策"的实际收益，重点对"将软决策树每个叶子变成门"这一最自然的加深方式给出精确诊断：新门梯度恒为零、子节点梯度相同，导致该层永远无法学习；并提出添加微小随机扰动的修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造式分类器在训练中不断添加结构（树加层、隐层加单元、叶子分裂），但这些生长决策究竟带来什么收益缺乏统一度量；尤其最自然的加深方式可能毫无作用，需要精确诊断并修复。

Method: 在固定的统一协议下，对树结构与构造式模型中的四种生长决策进行测量对比。针对"叶子转门"的加深方式，从梯度角度进行理论证明（新门梯度恒为零、门在1/2时两子节点梯度相同），并与Net2Net的噪声破对称、最陡下降分裂的二阶逃逸进行对比；修复方案是对子节点施加小随机扰动。另外三种决策（拟合残差的隐单元、按最大期望误差分裂叶子、显著性检验后分裂）分别在多种数据集上评估其影响。

Result: "叶子转门"构造在五折交叉验证三个种子下，相比同深度从头训练分别损失 Iris 19.6、Wine 19.1、Digits 55.6 个准确率点；小随机扰动即可修复，扰动幅度几乎无关。拟合残差的隐单元在各数据集上得到更小网络但精度未提升，在 Digits 上显著损失精度。按最大期望误差分裂叶子带来稀疏性（0.885 仅需 3.7 次分裂，而完整深度六树需 63 次），但在更难问题上损失 4.3 点。要求统计显著性后再分裂无任何收益，树更大且更不准确。

Conclusion: 四种生长决策中，"叶子转门"式加深不带来任何收益且因梯度缺失而无法学习，需用随机扰动修复；其余三种各有所得（小网络、稀疏性），但均不提升精度甚至可能损害精度。实用建议：添加参数后应断言其梯度非零。

Abstract: Constructive classifiers add structure while they train: a level to a tree, a unit to a hidden layer, a split at a leaf. This paper asks what each of four such growth decisions actually buys, measured under one fixed protocol in tree-structured and constructive models, and gives an exact diagnosis and a fix for the one that buys nothing.
  The diagnosis concerns the most natural way to deepen a soft decision tree: turn every leaf into a gate whose two children inherit the parent's class distribution, so that the function is unchanged. I prove that this leaves the gradient of every new gate identically zero and, with the gate at 1/2, gives the two children identical gradients, so the added level can never learn. Unlike the symmetry that Net2Net breaks with noise or the saddle point that splitting steepest descent escapes with second-order information, first-order information here is not weak but absent. Over three seeds of five-fold cross-validation the construction loses 19.6 accuracy points on Iris, 19.1 on Wine and 55.6 on Digits against the same depth trained from scratch. The fix is a small random perturbation of the children, whose size barely matters. The practical rule is one line in a test: after adding parameters, assert that their gradient is nonzero.
  The other three decisions each buy one thing. Fitting a new hidden unit to the residual error before installing it buys a smaller network on every dataset, though not a more accurate one, and on Digits it costs accuracy significantly. Splitting the leaf with the largest expected error buys sparsity, reaching 0.885 with 3.7 splits where a complete depth-six tree uses 63, but loses 4.3 points on a harder problem. Requiring statistical significance before a node receives a more expressive split buys nothing: the tree gets larger and less accurate.
  Every number in the paper is inserted from the measurement script.

</details>


### [187] [Uncertainty-Aware RL-Controlled Adaptive 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00188)
*Alpay Ozkan,Tunc Ozan Aydin,Marc Pollefeys,Jelena Trisovic,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于语义熵、几何曲率和纹理丰富度的自适应体素细分框架，并用强化学习代理在用户指定内存预算下学习细分策略，实现多分辨率TSDF建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定分辨率体素建图内存效率低，均匀区域浪费内存、复杂区域丢失细节。现有自适应方法（如MAP-ADAPT）依赖用户定义的语义类别列表和专家调参，难以泛化到未见物体，且无法显式控制内存使用。

Method: 利用语义熵（标签不确定性）结合几何曲率与纹理丰富度作为场景复杂度线索，进行有原则的分辨率分配，不依赖语义分类体系；并引入强化学习代理，在用户指定的目标内存预算下学习体素细分策略，将手工阈值替换为单一直观控制参数。

Result: 所得多分辨率TSDF在合成与真实数据集上，相比MAP-ADAPT和固定分辨率基线，取得更高的几何精度、更好的语义一致性以及更优的内存-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法无需语义分类体系且内存可控，可有效提升三维重建中精度与内存的权衡表现，代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Voxel-based volumetric mapping is fundamental to 3D reconstruction, yet fixed-resolution grids remain inherently inefficient - wasting memory in uniform regions and losing detail in complex ones. Existing adaptive methods, such as MAP-ADAPT, partially address this by varying resolution based on geometry and user-defined semantic class lists, but these heuristics require expert tuning, lack generalization to unseen objects, and provide no explicit mechanism to control memory usage. We propose an adaptive framework that refines voxels based on semantic entropy, which captures label uncertainty, together with geometric curvature and texture richness as scene complexity cues, yielding principled resolution allocation without reliance on semantic taxonomies. To make the accuracy-memory trade-off explicit and user-controlled, we further introduce a reinforcement learning agent that learns voxel subdivision policies under a user-specified target memory budget, replacing hand-tuned thresholds with a single intuitive control parameter. The resulting multi-resolution TSDF achieves higher geometric accuracy, better semantic consistency, and improved memory-accuracy trade-offs compared to MAP-ADAPT and fixed-resolution baselines on both synthetic and real-world datasets. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/alpayozkan/UnRL.

</details>


### [188] [Energy Time-Series Imputation with Differentially Private Diffusion Models via Clipping-Aware Objective Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00209)
*Huizhen Huang,Yu Li,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向隐私敏感能源时间序列插补的扩散模型目标函数条件化方法，通过v-prediction与扩散调度感知的动态加权，缓解DP-SGD在低信噪比晚期时间步的梯度裁剪问题，在五个真实数据集上一致提升插补效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源时间序列的细粒度测量常含敏感时间信息，缺失值恢复对监测与分析很重要。基于DP-SGD训练的扩散模型是实现隐私敏感插补的有前景框架，但在余弦扩散调度下，晚期时间步对应低信噪比条件，标准ε-prediction会产生较大的裁剪前梯度，易被裁剪从而损失优化信号。

Method: 将目标函数与梯度裁剪的交互形式化为固定阈值DP-SGD下的目标优化问题，并开发时间步感知的目标条件化方法：采用v-prediction缓解晚期时间步梯度放大，使用静态损失加权作为统一缩放控制，并引入扩散调度感知的动态加权以在裁剪前实现更强衰减。

Result: 在五个真实能源时间序列数据集上，覆盖随机点缺失、连续块缺失、持续中断及多种缺失严重程度，在匹配的DP-SGD设置下，所提方法在插补效用上一致优于ε-prediction基线。梯度诊断显示裁剪前梯度范数上尾更低、裁剪比例下降、晚期低信噪比时间步衰减更强。

Conclusion: 裁剪感知的目标条件化能有效改善隐私保护下的能源时间序列插补性能，验证了从目标-裁剪交互角度优化DP扩散插补的可行性。

Abstract: Reliable recovery of missing measurements is important for monitoring and analysis in energy time-series systems, where fine-grained measurements may contain sensitive temporal information. Diffusion models trained with differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) provide a promising framework for privacy-sensitive energy time-series imputation. Under cosine diffusion schedules, late timesteps correspond to low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions, where standard $\varepsilon$-prediction can induce large pre-clipping gradients. Such gradients are more likely to be clipped, reducing the retained optimization signal. The artificial intelligence (AI) contribution lies in formulating this objective--clipping interaction as an objective optimization problem under fixed-threshold DP-SGD and developing timestep-aware objective conditioning for diffusion-based energy time-series imputation. The method adopts $v$-prediction to mitigate late-timestep gradient amplification, uses static loss weighting as a uniform-scaling control, and introduces diffusion-schedule-aware dynamic weighting for stronger attenuation before clipping. For the engineering application, we evaluate the method on five real-world energy time-series datasets across random point missingness, contiguous block missingness, persistent outages, and multiple missing-data severities. Under matched DP-SGD settings, the proposed method consistently improves imputation utility over the $\varepsilon$-prediction baseline. Gradient diagnostics reveal lower upper-tail pre-clipping gradient norms, reduced clipping fractions, and stronger attenuation at late low-SNR timesteps, supporting the effectiveness of clipping-aware objective conditioning for energy time-series imputation.

</details>


### [189] [Useful to Whom? Sample Value Is Defined Only Relative to the Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00221)
*Yangze Liu,Xiao-Long Yin,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 数据选择策略（易学优先 vs 几何覆盖）的优劣分界并非由数据固定，而是依赖目标学习器及其网络结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心集选择中，易学优先与几何覆盖在不同预算区间各有优势，其交叉边界此前被视为数据属性；本文质疑该边界是否随目标学习器变化。

Method: 冻结已选子集、只改变训练学习器的受控实验；在低分辨率ImageNet-100上调整ResNet-18宽度，改变输入网格与步幅，并在native-224px ImageNet-1k协议下考察宽度效应与不同探针（LFrac、EL2N），以及将卷积系统替换为ViT。

Result: ResNet-18宽度加倍使交叉点从每类57样本右移至85；网格放大在保持图像信息不变时使边界左移，且该效应随宽度增大而减弱；仅移除最后一个下采样步幅即可复现左移；native-224px下宽度效应较小且依赖探针；换成ViT后覆盖策略在整个测量区间胜出。

Conclusion: 通过冻结子集干预确立了选择偏好的学习器依赖性，并指出可移动边界的网络结构因素，但未给出普适缩放律；实践含义是选择策略的适用预算区间必须针对目标学习器评估。

Abstract: What kind of data does a model need in order to learn? Coreset selection makes this question concrete: under a budget, keep the samples most useful for training. Easy-first and geometric coverage criteria can win in different budget regimes, separated by a crossover boundary. We ask whether this boundary is fixed by the data or changes with the target learner. Controlled experiments freeze the selected subsets and manipulate only the training learner. On low-resolution ImageNet-100, doubling ResNet-18's width moves the crossover from 57 to 85 samples per class: the learner changes the relative value of the same samples. A wider sweep reveals an interaction between input grid and capacity. Enlarging the grid while retaining the same image information shifts the boundary left, and this shift weakens as width increases. Stride controls reproduce and reverse the grid effect without changing the input grid; removing only the last downsampling stride is sufficient to recover the leftward shift. Under the native-224px ImageNet-1k protocol, width effects are smaller and depend on the probe: LFrac remains nearly flat, while EL2N shifts modestly right. Swapping the convolutional learning system for a ViT makes coverage win throughout the measured range, even when the easy subsets come from the convolutional proxy. These results establish learner dependence through frozen-subset interventions and identify network structure that can move the boundary. They do not yield a universal scaling law. Their practical implication is direct: a selection strategy's preferred budget regime must be evaluated with respect to the target learner.

</details>


### [190] [Constant-Memory Recall: Learned Associations in a Fixed Matrix State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00232)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 小型DeltaNet在固定32 KiB循环记忆下可近乎完美地召回32组键值对，但参数匹配的向量与Transformer基线仍接近随机，阻碍了记忆效率比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定大小的循环记忆可限制推理阶段存储增长，但能否成功召回取决于任务与训练设置，因此需要在小规模受控任务上系统考察其召回能力与容量边界。

Method: 研究一个带固定token特定键偏置的小型DeltaNet变体，训练其每序列记忆32组新键值对；使用32 KiB循环矩阵状态，在三个训练种子下评估多值选择准确率，并测试长填充上下文、消融首个记忆块、48对扩展实验，以及与参数量匹配的向量和Transformer基线对比。

Result: 32对设定下平均准确率99.95%；填充使查询前上下文扩展至1798个token时召回仍近乎完美；将首个记忆块置零后召回消失；48对实验在四分之一训练预算下仍接近随机，未找到容量上限；参数匹配的向量与Transformer基线（含额外训练搜索）仍接近随机。

Conclusion: DeltaNet变体在受控键值召回任务上表现近完美且对长填充鲁棒，但基线未解决导致无法进行记忆效率比较，容量上限亦有待进一步确定。

Abstract: Fixed-size recurrent memory limits storage growth during inference, but successful recall depends on the task and training. We study a small DeltaNet variant with fixed token-specific key biases, trained to remember 32 new key-value pairings per sequence. With 32 KiB of recurrent matrix state, it achieves 99.95% mean accuracy across three training seeds when choosing among the sequence's values. Recall remains near perfect when filler extends the pre-query context to 1,798 tokens without adding pairings. Zeroing the first memory block removes this recall. An exploratory 48-pair test remains near chance after one quarter of the primary training budget and does not locate a capacity limit. Parameter-matched vector and Transformer baselines remain near chance, including the Transformer after additional training searches. This unresolved baseline failure prevents a memory-efficiency comparison.

</details>


### [191] [How Many Categories Are Enough? Distribution-Free Certification Limits for Few-Shot Anomaly Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00236)
*Gia Huy Thai,Nguyen Thai Anh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了少样本异常检测中可控误报率（FAR）阈值的可认证性问题，提出类别计数可行性演算与CRESS协议，指出源域证据支持可迁移可靠性声明存在明确边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本异常检测器虽以排序指标评估，但实际部署需要具有受控误报率的报警阈值。论文追问：对于未见类别，需要多少正常样本（图像单元或类别单元）才能认证这样的阈值。

Method: 在15个MVTec与12个VisA类别、四种损坏类型下，使用冻结的DINOv2 PCA残差排序器。分析了目标域留一图像法（LOIO）校准的分辨率受限与漂移脆弱问题（秩值不低于1/(k+1)，在α=0.20、k=4时高斯损坏MVTec经验FAR达0.341）；推导了类别计数可行性演算，给出确定性、一致有效、无分布假设95%上置信界所需的最少iid类别抽样数；提出CRESS协议，将源类别分为参考、提议、认证三种不相交角色。

Result: 任何确定性、一致有效、无分布假设的95%上置信界在α=0.20、0.10、0.05时分别至少需要14、29、59个iid类别抽样（必要但不充分）。CRESS协议在仅3至4个认证类别下，全部960个冻结配置均返回失败关闭阈值τ*=0，最小类别级UCB为0.950。同一档案的图像单元分析在36.7%至60.3%的目标单元中选出正阈值，但这些界仅对所选自源混合成立，不适用于新类别抽取的边际风险。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于给出了定量可行性边界与估量感知协议，明确说明了源域证据何时能够、何时不能支持可迁移的可靠性声明。

Abstract: Few-shot anomaly detectors are judged by ranking metrics, yet deployment requires an alarm threshold with a controlled false-alarm rate (FAR). We ask how much normal evidence, in images or category units, is needed to certify such a threshold for an unseen category. Using a frozen DINOv2 principal component analysis (PCA) residual ranker on 15 MVTec and 12 VisA categories under four corruption types, we show that target-only leave-one-image-out (LOIO) calibration is resolution-limited and shift-fragile: rank values cannot fall below $1/(k+1)$, and at the attainable level $α=0.20$, empirical FAR reaches 0.341 on Gaussian-corrupted MVTec at $k=4$, 1.7 times the nominal level. A category-count feasibility calculus is then derived: even with all-zero category losses and no multiplicity charged, any deterministic, uniformly valid, distribution-free 95% upper confidence bound (UCB) requires at least 14, 29, and 59 independent and identically distributed (iid) category draws at $α=0.20$, $0.10$, and $0.05$; these counts are necessary but not sufficient. The Cross-category Reliability Estimation with Source Support (CRESS) protocol splits source categories into disjoint reference, proposal, and certification roles. With only three or four certification categories, all 960 frozen configurations return the fail-closed threshold $τ^\star=0$, and the smallest category-level UCB is 0.950. Image-unit analyses of the same archive select positive thresholds in 36.7% to 60.3% of target cells; these bounds hold for the selected source mixture, not for the marginal risk of a new-category draw. The contribution is a quantitative feasibility boundary and an estimand-aware protocol specifying when source evidence can, and cannot, support a transferable reliability claim.

</details>


### [192] [Contingent Exposure Routing for Financial AI: Outage Risk and the Cost of Indivisible Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00239)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了模型故障转移（failover）路由对金融决策误差的影响，提出基于市场冲击响应矩阵的审计式路由压力测试框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型故障转移虽恢复了可用性，却改变了哪些金融机构共享决策误差。作者希望刻画在端点故障情况下，备份路由如何影响预期平方价格位移，并识别集中度与多样化风险的下限。

Method: 通过局部市场冲击响应矩阵形式化"故障相关的路由问题"，分析预期平方价格位移；采用对称构造证明共享备份会产生一阶集中度下限；针对不可分决策推导独立随机路由的精确二阶矩与有效暴露粒度；使用条件期望舍入获得无耦合配额下的有限智能体界，并用单独的交换程序保持端点计数，并与对偶下界进行比较。

Result: 在60个合成组合网络和11,340次情景评估中，交换程序在中央反馈设置、独立误差下，对单端点与双端点移除分别降低风险6.57%和10.53%；对1,024条已录制API响应在构建的再平衡任务上回放，留出集降低为3.30%（配对自助区间2.07--4.57%）。强相关误差、劣质端点与不可分性会限制多样化收益。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献的是一套可审计的路由压力测试与实现分析，而非对真实市场崩盘概率的估计。结果表明备份路由的多样化收益受误差相关性与端点质量约束。

Abstract: Model failover restores availability, but changes which financial institutions share decision errors. We formulate outage-contingent routing through a local market-impact response matrix and study expected squared price displacement. A symmetric construction shows that a shared backup can leave an order-one concentration floor as the number of primary endpoints grows, while balanced fallback risk decreases inversely with the surviving endpoint count. For indivisible decisions, we derive the exact second moment of independent randomized routing and an effective-exposure granularity that determines its gap from fractional allocation. Conditional-expectation rounding gives a finite-agent bound without coupled quotas; a separate swap procedure preserves endpoint counts and is assessed against dual lower bounds. Across 60 synthetic portfolio networks and 11,340 scenario evaluations, the latter reduces risk by 6.57% and 10.53% for single and double endpoint removals at the central feedback setting with independent errors. A replay of 1,024 recorded API responses on constructed rebalancing tasks gives a smaller held-out reduction of 3.30% (paired bootstrap interval 2.07--4.57%). Strongly aligned errors, inferior endpoints, and indivisibility limit diversification. The contribution is an auditable routing stress test and implementation analysis, not an estimate of real-market crash probabilities.

</details>


### [193] [The Null Is the Hard Part: Exact Tests for Memorization in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00251)
*Sushovan Majhi,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出生成模型记忆化审计缺乏零假设导致结论不可靠，并提出两类校准零假设方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆化审计直接将相似度分数与阈值比较，没有零分布，导致结论可能错误；论文认为记忆化问题中零假设才是难点。

Method: 提出两种零假设：对整模型，给定样本后训练图像与留出图像可交换，重标记构成置换检验；对单图像，使用匹配对照校准，并推荐受限尺度的交集欧拉特征轮廓小尺度质量统计量。

Result: 在MemBench上，用假发现控制后，随机提示扰动下三分之二认证图像不再被检出，注意力重缩放下去掉六分之五，嵌入优化下全部消失；数据复制检验误标24个生成器中的10个；置换检验下最近邻偏好仍触发7个，但近重复尺度计数为0（McNemar p=0.016）；随机图像排名产生596个假发现（2365个对照），独立生成重采样使零假设窄三倍；校准后61张MemBench图像中认证36张（5% FDR），留出对照在0.01%校准划分中被认证，每图两次生成即可恢复该计数，校准最大值认证46张。

Conclusion: 记忆化审计必须建立正确的校准零假设，否则会严重误判；提出的置换检验和匹配对照校准方法可提供可靠的假发现控制，并推荐使用交集欧拉特征轮廓的小尺度质量作为受限尺度统计量。

Abstract: Memorization audits of generative models read similarity scores against thresholds, with no null distribution, and the conclusions they support can be wrong. By MemBench's rule, the benchmark's mitigations roughly halve Stable Diffusion's memorization; audited with false-discovery control, two thirds of the certified images are no longer detected under random prompt perturbations, five sixths under attention rescaling, and all of them under embedding optimization. The field's data-copying test, read against its own null, flags ten of twenty-four generators that reproduce nothing. We argue that for memorization the null is the hard part, and supply two. For a whole model, training and held-out images are exchangeable given its samples, and relabelling them is a permutation test, exact for any statistic when the held-out images are a random split; under it, a nearest-neighbour preference still fires on seven of those twenty-four, and a count restricted to the near-duplicate scale on none (McNemar p=0.016). For single images, the natural nulls fail twice, measurably: ranking an image among random images yields 596 false discoveries among 2,365 controls, and resampling independent generations makes the null three times too narrow. Calibrated against matched controls, the audit certifies 36 of 61 MemBench images at 5% false-discovery rate, held-out controls are certified in 0.01% of calibration splits, and on this benchmark two generations per image recover that count. A calibrated maximum, which reads occasional rather than typical copying, certifies 46. As the scale-restricted statistic we recommend the small-scale mass of the Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile, which also counts distinct images copied and tests whether two models copy the same ones.

</details>


### [194] [Sharp Oracle-Regret Tradeoffs for Projection-Free Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00254)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了在线凸优化中当仅能通过精确线性优化预言机访问可行集时的遗憾界，给出无维度 minimax 期望遗憾的精确刻画，并匹配上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在线凸优化中可行集访问受限（仅精确线性优化预言机，且有总调用量 Q 和每轮调用上限 B）时的性能极限，填补带预言机预算约束下 minimax 遗憾刻画的研究空白。

Method: 下界方面：利用普适可行性限制每轮动作落在给定球与历史预言机回复的凸包内，构造固定体实例，将新相位方向耦合到共享单纯形，使有用回复反复付出代价；上界方面：提出带交错块的计数近似梯度方法，达到匹配速率。

Result: 对 G-Lipschitz 凸损失，直径不超过 D，总预算 Q、每轮上限 B，无维度 minimax 期望遗憾为 Θ(GD·max{√T, T/(1+min{Q,BT})^{1/4}})；总预算与严格每轮保证作为特例：每轮一次调用得 T^{3/4} 速率，二次总预算可得 √T 遗憾。对给定光滑度 β，解析构造给出曲率相关的下界并确定一般刻画保持紧致的阈值。

Conclusion: 在线凸优化在有限线性优化预言机预算下存在精确的 minimax 遗憾刻画，本文方法同时给出匹配的上下界，并明确总预算与每轮调用限制等特殊情况。

Abstract: We characterize the regret attainable in online convex optimization when access to the feasible set is limited to an exact linear optimization oracle. The learner is given an inscribed ball and a diameter bound and must remain feasible on every consistent instance. For convex $G$-Lipschitz losses, diameter at most $D$, a total allowance of $Q$ oracle calls, and a strict limit of $B$ calls per round, the dimension-free minimax expected regret is $Θ(GD\max\{\sqrt T,T/(1+\min\{Q,BT\})^{1/4}\})$. The lower bound applies to arbitrary randomized learners. Universal feasibility first forces each action into the hull of the supplied ball and the preceding oracle replies. A fixed-body construction then couples fresh phase directions to a shared simplex, making useful replies costly repeatedly even though all losses have a common minimizer. A counted approximate-gradient method with interleaved blocks attains the matching rate. Total-budget and strict per-round guarantees follow as special cases, including the $T^{3/4}$ rate with one call per round and the quadratic total budget needed for $\sqrt T$ regret. For prescribed smoothness $β$, an analytic construction yields a curvature-dependent lower bound and identifies the threshold above which the general characterization remains sharp.

</details>


### [195] [Verification Pulses and the Cost of Escaping Wrong Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00256)
*Shivam Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究异步二元寄存器中固定验证预算的时机与投放方式如何影响错误共识的恢复，推导了跨越盆地边界所需的最小"燃料"及临界预算窗，并用13,392条语言模型响应实验验证了风险校准的重置调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部验证能纠正个体输出，却可能让处于错误共识盆地内的自强化群体依然存续。论文关注在固定验证预算下，验证的时机与投放对象如何决定系统能否从错误共识中恢复。

Method: 研究异步二元寄存器模型：对一般非线性响应，推导在峰值验证约束下跨越盆地边界所需的最小燃料；对有限种群，用精确生灭过程计算脉冲后出现后续错误共识的概率；给出临界预算窗的渐近结果，主导项为 N log(x0/b)、修正项为 √N 阶，方差来自重复验证目标与验证停止后的自主放大。并开展前瞻性实验，记录13,392条语言模型响应（含校准与108条留出轨迹）。

Result: 理论结果区分了均值场阈值与精确模型：16个多数更新的槽位中有14个初始错误时，9次随机检查即可跨越均值场预算阈值，而精确模型需23次才能达到95%的最终恢复率。实验中校准得到不同的拟合响应区制，但四个调整后的调度比较区间均包含零；分布审计还发现适度的均值预测误差可能掩盖对终态共识占据率的严重低估。

Conclusion: 结果支持在指定更新契约下采用风险校准的重置调度，同时明确将其与异构目标检查和不受限的证据广播区分开来。

Abstract: External verification can correct individual outputs while leaving a self-reinforcing population in the basin of a wrong consensus. We study how the timing and addressing of a fixed verification budget affect recovery in an asynchronous binary register. For a general nonlinear response, we derive the minimum fuel required to cross a basin boundary under a peak verification constraint. For a finite population, an exact birth--death calculation gives the probability of subsequent wrong consensus after a pulse. Our main asymptotic result identifies the critical budget window: a leading term $N\log(x_0/b)$ and a correction of order $\sqrt N$, with separate variance contributions from repeated verification targets and autonomous amplification after verification stops. The distinction is substantial: with 16 majority-updated slots and 14 initially wrong, 9 random checks cross the mean-field budget threshold, whereas 23 are required for 95% eventual recovery in the exact model. A prospectively specified experiment records 13,392 language-model responses, including calibration and 108 held-out trajectories. Calibration produces different fitted response regimes, but all four adjusted schedule-comparison intervals include zero. A distributional audit also finds that modest mean-prediction error can conceal a large underestimate of terminal consensus occupancy. The results support risk-calibrated reset scheduling under a specified update contract, while explicitly separating it from distinct-target checking and unrestricted evidence broadcast.

</details>


### [196] [Attention Manifolds: Steering or Blocking Language Models by Editing Learned B-Spline Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00257)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"注意力流形"（attention manifolds）：由查询-键交互调制的可学习二维B样条曲面，用于在标准Transformer注意力中动态调节每个value维度，从而让query不仅决定"关注多少"也决定"提取什么"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力中，源token向所有接收者发送相同的value向量；query只决定注意权重（how much），而不决定提取的内容（what）。作者希望通过引入几何化的调制机制，弥补这一表达力缺口。

Method: 为每个value维度引入一张张量积三次B样条曲面S_d(q_d, k_d)，基于query-key交互对该维度进行调制；曲面初始化为零以保持预训练行为不变，仅增加约0.3%参数。在LLaMA 3.2-1B-Instruct与3B-Instruct上进行实验，并测试曲面系数的机械可编辑性（如反转系数、设置系数为-1形成"注意力墙"）。

Result: WikiText-2验证困惑度降低2–2.5点；在112个多样化提示中，69%（1B）至83%（3B）的贪婪解码输出发生改变，歧义与多义输入变化率达94–100%。输出质量提升：纠正事实错误、提高精确性、增加具体性（如归属到Saint Augustine的引用在两种规模下均稳定出现）。反转某层系数可改变9/10提示的输出（KL约0.010）；层级"注意力墙"在初步实验中可将爆炸物相关提示从具体指令导向一般性教育内容。

Conclusion: 注意力流形以极小参数开销显著改善语言模型性能与输出质量，并提供一种几何化的模型引导与安全塑形机制，为可控性与安全导向的注意力调制开辟了路径。

Abstract: In standard transformer attention, a source token sends the same value vector to every receiver. The query determines \emph{how much} to attend but not \emph{what} to extract. This work introduces \textbf{attention manifolds}: learned 2D B-spline surfaces $S_d(q_d, k_d)$ that modulate each value dimension based on the query-key interaction. Each surface is a tensor-product cubic B-spline initialized to zero, preserving pretrained behavior. Applied to LLaMA 3.2-1B-Instruct and 3B-Instruct, attention manifolds reduce WikiText-2 validation perplexity by 2--2.5 points with 0.3\% parameter overhead. Across 112 diverse prompts, surfaces change greedy-decoded output for 69\% (1B) to 83\% (3B) of cases, with the strongest effects on ambiguous and polysemous inputs (94--100\% change rate). The surfaces improve output quality: correcting factual errors (\emph{``the CAP theorem has three main components''} $\to$ \emph{``it is impossible to guarantee all three''}), increasing precision (\emph{``impossible to know certain properties''} $\to$ \emph{``impossible to know both position and momentum''}), and adding specificity (a generic quote $\to$ an attributed Saint Augustine citation, consistently at both scales). The learned surfaces are also mechanically editable: inverting a layer's coefficients changes greedy output for 9/10 prompts (KL~0.010), providing a geometric mechanism for model steering. Setting surface coefficients to $-1$ creates ``attention walls'' that block value flow through specific dimensions. In a preliminary experiment, a layer-wide wall redirects an explosive-device prompt from specific instructions to general educational content, suggesting a path toward safety-oriented manifold shaping.

</details>


### [197] [Large Language Bayes Is Not Reparameterisation-Invariant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00265)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLB通过语言模型采样概率程序并按指数化证据界的权重进行平均，但该权重依赖程序的书写方式（参数化），导致相同的分布得到差异巨大的权重，且无单一写法普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLB用语言模型采样候选概率程序，并对每个程序做近似推断后加权平均，权重正比于指数化证据界。但这一加权方式可能受模型书写方式（参数化）影响，而理论上对数边缘似然应对重参数化不变。作者希望检验这种书写依赖性是否存在、有多严重，以及能否检测或修正。

Method: 以八学校模型为基准，比较中心化与非中心化程序（两者测度相同至5.7e-14）的证据界权重差异；测试重要性加权、以及与后验矩匹配的全协方差高斯（复现LLB实际推断）下的差异；跨似然族、维度与漏斗严重程度考察差异范围与符号反转；用已知的Hilbert距离锐界刻画模型后验及下游目标的诱导误差；统计六个语言模型共360个程序的中心化比例及其稳定性；并考察统计检测等价程序在不同样本预算下的表现。

Result: 中心化与非中心化程序测度相同到5.7e-14，但权重相差6.1倍；重要性加权仅降到2.2倍，全协方差高斯仍留1.9倍（八学校）和8.9倍（64维）。跨似然族、维度与漏斗严重程度差异最高达31.9倍且会反向，无单一写法普遍更优。该效应反转了与八个自然竞争者的贝叶斯因子，对模型后验及任何下游目标的诱导误差由界短差的散布Δ通过已知锐Hilbert距离界控制。360个程序中中心化比例从0%到100%，但在同一模型内稳定。统计检测等价程序在实用样本预算下可能错误合并真正不同的模型；而对我们自己生成的重参数化进行验证则可以避免，从而关闭这一窗口。

Conclusion: LLB的证据界加权对程序参数化敏感，虽真实边缘似然不变，但权重可差数十倍并改变符号，进而扭曲贝叶斯因子与模型后验；这一偏差受界短差散布Δ的锐界控制。程序写法在同一语言模型内稳定但跨模型差异极大。实践上应通过验证自行生成的重参数化而非统计等价检测来避免误合并。总体表明LLB的加权方案在实现层面存在系统性缺陷，需要谨慎对待。

Abstract: Large Language Bayes (LLB) answers an informal modelling question by sampling candidate probabilistic programs from a language model, running approximate inference on each, and averaging them with weights proportional to an exponentiated evidence bound. We show that this weighting depends on how a model is written. The log marginal likelihood is invariant to reparameterisation; the evidence bound is not. On eight schools the centered and non-centered programs are the same measure to $5.7\times10^{-14}$, yet their weights differ by $6.1\times$; importance weighting reduces this only to $2.2\times$, and reproducing the inference LLB actually runs, a full-covariance Gaussian matched to the posterior moments, still leaves $1.9\times$ on eight schools and $8.9\times$ in $64$ dimensions. Across likelihood families, dimensions and funnel severities the discrepancy reaches $31.9\times$ and reverses sign, so no single writing is uniformly preferable. It inverts Bayes factors against eight natural competitors, and the induced error in the model posterior, and in any downstream target, is controlled by the spread $Δ$ of the bound shortfalls through a known sharp Hilbert-distance bound. Across $360$ programs from six language models the parameterisation written ranges from $0\%$ to $100\%$ centered and is stable within a model. Detecting equivalent programs statistically can falsely merge genuinely different models at practical sample budgets; verifying reparameterisations we generate ourselves cannot, and closes the window.

</details>


### [198] [MOVE: Multimodal Open-world Verification and Expansion for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00268)
*Zekai Chen,Jiayang Xing,Xun Wu,Miao Zhang,Xunkai Li,Kairui Yang,Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MOVE框架，解决多模态图学习中开放世界类别验证与扩展问题，平均提升11.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图学习面临部署后新类别出现、而模型标签空间固定的根本性挑战。现有方法虽能检测未知节点并利用LLM生成候选类别描述，但无法判断已有类别是否不足以覆盖这些节点，也无法判断生成类别是否足够可靠以扩展类别空间。实证研究揭示了三个挑战：未知节点识别需要超出单一模态的多模态信息；LLM生成的类别描述可能无法充分捕捉多模态类别特征；直接添加候选类别会引入冗余类别。

Method: MOVE通过联合考虑视觉token、文本属性和图上下文来识别无法归入现有类别的节点；利用多模态大语言模型生成候选类别；仅当候选类别得到多模态证据一致支持且不会引入不必要类别时，才选择性地扩展类别空间。

Result: 在未知识别、开放域标注和下游图学习任务上，MOVE平均取得11.87%的提升。

Conclusion: MOVE是一种多模态开放世界类别验证与扩展框架，能够有效应对开放世界下新类别出现的问题，并通过有选择地扩展类别空间避免冗余类别，在多类任务上取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Multimodal graph learning faces a fundamental challenge: new classes may emerge after deployment, while models are trained with a fixed label space. Existing approaches typically detect unknown nodes and use LLMs to generate candidate class descriptions, but they do not determine whether existing classes are insufficient to cover these nodes or whether a generated class is reliable enough to expand the class space. Our empirical study reveals three challenges: multimodal information beyond individual modalities is required for unknown-node identification, LLM-generated class descriptions may not fully capture multimodal class characteristics, and directly adding candidate classes can introduce redundant categories. Based on these observations, we propose MOVE, a multimodal open-world class verification and expansion framework. MOVE identifies nodes that cannot be assigned to existing classes by jointly considering visual tokens, textual attributes, and graph context, leverages a multimodal LLM to generate candidate classes, and selectively expands the class space only when candidates are consistently supported by multimodal evidence without introducing unnecessary categories. Experiments demonstrate that MOVE achieves an average improvement of 11.87\% across unknown recognition, open-domain annotation, and downstream graph learning tasks.

</details>


### [199] [Coupling Perception and Reasoning in Federated Multimodal Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00277)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Hailin Zhang,Xunkai Li,Yu Liu,Kairui Yang,Muyan Huang,Xuaner Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCORE框架，通过共享低维隐状态联合优化多模态编码器与GNN更新，缓解联邦多模态图基础模型适配中的模块配对敏感性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图基础模型适配方法主要更新图侧模块而冻结多模态编码器，只能调整"信息如何传播"，却固定了"提取什么信息"。实证研究发现编码器与GNN的适配并非独立：编码器适配受图关系影响，且跨客户端模块交换显示二者参数化更新之间存在显著的配对敏感性。

Method: 提出FedCORE联邦适配框架，将编码器更新与GNN更新表示为共享的低维隐状态（core），从多模态信号与结构信号中联合优化该核心，并直接在共享状态空间中进行联邦演化，从而保持感知与推理适配之间的兼容性。

Result: 大量实验表明，FedCORE将编码器—GNN配对差距从30.6降至5.9，相比独立联合适配降低了80.7%。

Conclusion: 通过共享隐状态的联合演化，FedCORE有效解决了联邦多模态图基础模型中编码器与GNN适配不兼容的问题，显著提升跨模块适配的一致性。

Abstract: Federated multimodal graph foundation models (GFMs) aim to adapt pretrained multimodal models to decentralized graph data, where each client owns a private multimodal graph and cannot share raw information. These models typically combine a multimodal Encoder that extracts semantic evidence from heterogeneous modalities and a graph neural network (GNN) that performs relational reasoning over graph structures. However, existing federated GFM adaptation methods mainly update graph-side modules while keeping the multimodal Encoder frozen, limiting adaptation to \emph{how information is propagated} while fixing \emph{what information is extracted}. Through empirical studies, we reveal that Encoder and GNN adaptations are not independent: Encoder adaptation is affected by graph relations, while cross-client module swapping reveals substantial pairing sensitivity between separately parameterized Encoder and GNN updates. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{FedCORE}, a federated adaptation framework that represents Encoder and GNN updates through a shared low-dimensional latent state. FedCORE jointly optimizes this core from multimodal and structural signals and performs federated evolution directly in the shared state space, preserving compatibility between perception and reasoning adaptations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedCORE reduces the Encoder--GNN pairing gap from $30.6$ to $5.9$, corresponding to an $80.7\%$ reduction over independent joint adaptation.

</details>


### [200] [Stable and Counterfactually Robust Physical World Models from Imposed Structure and Learned Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00280)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过将通用物理结构（能量梯度+可逆算子+单向耗散端口）硬编码进世界模型，仅约九千参数即可同时满足耗散、干预响应、长时稳定与抗扰动四项性质，并在电磁腔、粒子网格和浅水流体上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型从轨迹中学习物理系统演化，但系统所遵循的物理定律既未完全提供也未被可靠遵守：模型可能凭空造能、长时发散，或在物理参数被改变时仍按训练时观测到的定律作答。因此需要探究：世界模型中必须硬编码多少通用物理结构，又有多少系统特定物理可从数据中学得。

Method: 硬编码通用结构：动力学由学习到的能量梯度经固定可逆算子生成；能量限定在约束类中；单向端口只能耗散不能注入能量；驱动通道已知；被干预参数通过可分离映射进入。模型学习能量泛函、本构关系、耗散率与耦合项。目标是四项性质同时成立：符合第二定律的耗散、对物理参数干预的正确响应、训练时域一百倍外仍稳定、对扰动鲁棒。

Result: 在电磁腔、粒子网格与浅水流体上，约九千参数的模型恢复出单位斜率的本构函数；用同一组权重以四个数量级的差距区分守恒与耗散世界；并将符号、幅度、速率和重力的改变迁移到未见取值；同等容量但无此结构的模型表现仅为随机或更差。非线性本构律的曲率被保留，其预测留出干预的效果比收敛的线性模型好 2–17 倍。

Conclusion: 少量通用物理结构（能量梯度、可逆算子、单向耗散端口）的硬编码即可让极小规模世界模型获得可靠的耗散、干预泛化、长时稳定与鲁棒性，系统特定物理可由数据学到；缺乏该结构的同等容量模型则失败。

Abstract: A world model learns to forecast how a physical system evolves from recorded trajectories, yet the systems it imitates obey physical laws that are neither fully supplied nor reliably respected. The model may create energy, drift or diverge over long rollouts, and answer a changed law query using the law observed during training. We ask how much general physical structure must be hard coded into a world model, and how much system-specific physics can then be learned from data, for four properties to hold simultaneously: second law compatible dissipation, correct responses to interventions on physical parameters, stability out to one hundred times the training horizon, and robustness to disturbances. The imposed structure is general: dynamics are generated from the gradient of a learned energy through a fixed reversible operator, the energy is restricted to a confining class, a one way port can remove energy but never inject it, the drive channel is known, and the intervened parameter enters through a separable map. The model learns the energy functional, constitutive relations, dissipation rate, and couplings. Across an electromagnetic cavity, a particle in cell grid, and a shallow-water fluid, models with roughly nine thousand parameters recover constitutive functions with unit slope, separate conserving from dissipating worlds by four orders of magnitude using a single set of weights, and transfer changes in sign, magnitude, rate, and gravity to unseen values, where equal-capacity models without the same structure perform at chance or worse. A nonlinear constitutive law is recovered with its curvature preserved and predicts a held-out intervention $2$-$17\times$ better than a converged linear model.

</details>


### [201] [Partial AUC Maximization from Positive-unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00284)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Taishi Nishiyama,Hiroshi Takahashi,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在仅有正例与无标签数据（PU数据）条件下最大化部分AUC（pAUC）的方法，通过经验风险最小化推导出仅依赖正类与边缘密度的经验估计量并加以平滑优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pAUC是二分类在特定假正率区间内的重要性能指标，广泛用于网络安全、医疗与广告等场景。但现有最大化pAUC的方法通常需要同时具备标注的正例与负例数据，而负例数据因隐私或标注专业门槛难以获取。

Method: 在经验风险最小化框架下，证明pAUC（含依赖FPR的阈值）可仅用正类密度与边缘密度表示，并据此从PU数据导出经验估计量；再通过最大化该平滑后的经验pAUC估计量来训练分类器。

Result: 在十个真实数据集上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 无需负例标注即可从PU数据中有效最大化pAUC，为负例难以获取的实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The partial area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (pAUC) is an important performance metric for binary classification that summarizes true positive rates within a specific range of false positive rates (FPRs). Classifiers that achieve high pAUC need to be obtained in many real-world applications such as cybersecurity, medical care, and advertising. Although many methods for maximizing the pAUC have been proposed, they typically require both labeled positive and negative data for training. However, in practice, labeled negative data are often difficult to collect due to privacy concerns or the need for high expertise to annotate them. In this paper, we propose a method for maximizing the pAUC from positive and unlabeled (PU) data without negative data. Within an empirical risk minimization framework, we show that the pAUC, including its FPR-dependent thresholds, can be represented using only the positive and marginal densities, and derive an empirical estimator from PU data. A classifier is then trained by maximizing the derived smoothed empirical pAUC estimator. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with ten real-world datasets.

</details>


### [202] [Bellman-Certified Rounding for Sparse Policy Deployment in MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00325)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将连续策略优化的行混合策略四舍五入为稀疏二值策略时,折扣回报能保留多少,并提出基于Bellman求解的可复用包络与秩二有理表示来给出舍入前保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署场景往往只允许少数完整的状态级策略改动,而连续策略优化会把更新分散到大量状态上,因此需要理解从连续行混合策略舍入到稀疏二值策略时折扣回报的损失。策略相关的访问分布使各行编辑相互耦合,长时域又使全局曲率界过于保守。

Method: 作者通过对2d+2次Bellman求解得到可复用的包络,支持均匀与候选特定的舍入前保证;对每次交换给出秩二有理表示,从而可沿实际轨迹做加权曲率积分;并给出关于预算缩放时线性维度依赖不可避免、以及全局曲率阈值化困难的理论结果。

Result: 候选特定界将舍入前认证覆盖率从48.2%提升到74.1%(结构化测试集);在γ=0.95时,局部积分把耦合实例上的中位界-损失比从402.3降到2.08。

Conclusion: 所提包络与轨迹上的局部曲率积分能显著收紧稀疏二值舍入的回报保证,同时理论上说明维度依赖与全局曲率阈值化的固有困难。

Abstract: Continuous policy optimization may spread an update across many states, even when deployment permits only a few complete state-level changes. We study how much discounted return can be retained when continuous row mixtures are rounded to sparse binary policies in finite MDPs. Policy-dependent visitation couples the row edits, while long horizons make global curvature bounds conservative. From $2d+2$ Bellman solves, we derive reusable envelopes that support uniform and candidate-specific guarantees before rounding. A rank-two rational representation of each exchange further permits weighted curvature integration along the realized trajectory. We prove that linear dimension dependence is unavoidable when the budget scales, and that exact global curvature thresholding is hard. Candidate-specific bounds raise pre-rounding certification coverage from $48.2\%$ to $74.1\%$ on the structured suite. At $γ=0.95$, local integration lowers the median bound-to-loss ratio from $402.3$ to $2.08$ on coupled instances.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Diagonal State Space Models: Exact Non-Abelian Group Tracking, Solvability Barriers, and Geometric Physical Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00329)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出非交换状态空间模型（NC-SSM）系列，通过将状态转移提升到紧李群 SU(2)、SO(3)、SO(N)，突破传统对角SSM在可解群上的表示瓶颈，在群跟踪、流形保持和长程记忆等任务上取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统选择性状态空间模型（如 Mamba、S4D、LRU）受限于转移矩阵可交换性与可解仿射变换群（Aff_D，导出长度≤2）。因此堆叠多层对角网络在非可解单群（如 A_5）上会因模拟非阿贝尔交换子所需的指数级电路深度而出现严重优化退化。核心动机是打破这一代数结构限制。

Method: 提出 NC-SSM 及其实数正交版本 SO(3)-SSM 和任意维度的 Cayley-SSM，将状态转移提升到紧李群 SU(2)、SO(3)、SO(N)。利用闭式 Euler-Rodrigues 映射与有理 Cayley 变换实现精确的保范等距（||U_t||=1）。引入纯 Hopf 纤维化 Bloch 射影读出（S^3/{±1}=S^2=SO(3)）消除符号歧义，采用真正的四元数并行前缀扫描（T=2048 时 9.06 倍加速），并提出 Identity-Gated Lie SSM 消除稀疏语法相位漂移。

Result: 在 14 个实验场景的基准测试中：(1) NC-SSM 在 S_3、D_4、Q_8 和单群 A_5 上达到 100% 跟踪，而 3 层深对角基线崩溃至 6.60%（p=8.81e-4）；(2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM 打破 Klein 1884 关于对称群 S_5 的上限（50.92% vs 对角 5.25%，p=0.0015），方差比 SO(3) 降低 7.8 倍；(3) SO(3)-SSM 在 300 步内保持黎曼流形（漂移<3.12e-6，优势>580,000 倍），航位推算误差 0.04 度，并支持主动切空间去噪；(4) NC-SSM 在 Dyck-2 上达 74.36%，深度 AST 作用域跟踪达 30.26%（p=0.0081）；(5) 消融实验证实严格等距对无损长程关联记忆在数学上是必要的。

Conclusion: 将状态转移提升到非交换紧李群可从根本上突破对角 SSM 的代数与优化局限，NC-SSM 在群表示、流形保持与长程记忆等任务上均展现显著优势，且严格等距是实现无损长程记忆的必要条件。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs), such as Mamba, S4D, and LRU, are bounded by transition matrix commutativity (A_t A_t' = A_t' A_t) and solvable affine transformation groups (Aff_D of derived length <= 2). Consequently, stacked multi-layer diagonal networks face severe optimization degradation on non-solvable simple groups such as A_5 due to the exponential circuit emulation depth required to simulate non-abelian commutators. We propose Non-Commutative State Space Models (NC-SSM), their real-orthogonal counterpart SO(3)-SSM, and arbitrary-dimension Cayley-SSM, lifting state transitions to compact Lie groups SU(2), SO(3), and SO(N). Via closed-form Euler-Rodrigues maps and rational Cayley transforms, NC-SSM achieves exact norm-preserving isometry (||U_t|| = 1). We introduce pure Hopf-fibration Bloch projective readouts (S^3/{+-1} =~ S^2 =~ SO(3)) to eliminate sign ambiguity, true quaternion parallel prefix scans (9.06x speedup at T=2048), and Identity-Gated Lie SSMs to eliminate sparse syntax phase drift. Extensive benchmarks across 14 experimental regimes show: (1) NC-SSM achieves 100% tracking on S_3, D_4, Q_8 and simple group A_5, where a 3-layer deep diagonal baseline collapses to 6.60% (p = 8.81e-4); (2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM breaks Klein's 1884 ceiling on symmetric group S_5 (50.92% vs diagonal 5.25%, p = 0.0015, delivering 7.8x variance reduction over SO(3)); (3) SO(3)-SSM preserves Riemannian manifolds across 300 steps (< 3.12e-6 drift, > 580,000x advantage), achieving 0.04 deg dead-reckoning error and active tangent denoising; (4) NC-SSM achieves 74.36% on Dyck-2 and 30.26% on deep AST scope tracking (p = 0.0081); and (5) ablation confirms strict isometry is mathematically necessary for lossless long-range associative memory.

</details>


### [204] [The Weakest Link: Distilling LLM Reasoning with Worst-Case Constrained Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00332)
*Matthieu Zimmer,Xiaotong Ji,Tu Nguyen,Haitham Bou-Ammar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将推理蒸馏建模为带最坏情况约束的强化学习问题，通过无增广约束MDP实现精度与保真度的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大模型推理能力蒸馏到小模型是高效部署的核心挑战。现有方法存在根本矛盾：纯任务奖励优化（如GRPO）导致奖励黑客，学生用错误中间逻辑得到正确答案；而基于KL软散度对教师的惩罚会稀释任务性能，且允许学生在某一步严重逻辑违规后用其他步的高教师一致性来"补偿"。作者认为这种平均化与推理本质不符——思维链的合法性取决于其最薄弱环节。

Method: 将推理蒸馏建模为约束强化学习问题：最大化任务奖励，同时约束教师对数似然在轨迹的每个前缀上都满足最坏情况条件。为规避对偶拉格朗日求解器的高成本以及Saute等状态增广方法的测试时教师依赖，作者推导出一个无增广的约束MDP，其奖励变换保留硬约束语义，具备低方差策略梯度分解（单步项与长期项），并可在惩罚极限下几乎必然满足最坏情况约束。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成任务上的大量实验表明，该方法显著扩展了准确率-保真度的帕累托前沿：在匹配纯RL的高最终答案正确率的同时，大幅减少教师约束违规，最终在所有评估设置下取得最高的严格推理成功率。

Conclusion: 将推理蒸馏视为以最坏情况前缀保真度为硬约束的约束RL问题，可在不依赖测试时教师的情况下同时兼顾任务性能与推理逻辑保真度，优于纯RL与KL软蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Distilling the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) into smaller students is a central challenge for efficient deployment. Current approaches face a fundamental tension: optimizing purely for verifiable task rewards (e.g., via GRPO) leads to reward hacking, where students arrive at correct final answers through flawed intermediate logic, while regularizing with soft divergence penalties against a teacher (e.g., KL-based distillation) dilutes task performance and, critically, allows the student to compensate for severe logical violations at one step with high teacher agreement at others. We argue that this averaging is fundamentally misaligned with the nature of reasoning: a chain-of-thought is only as valid as its weakest link. Motivated by this observation, we formulate reasoning distillation as a constrained reinforcement learning problem in which the task reward is maximized subject to a worst-case constraint on the teacher log-likelihood along every prefix of the trajectory. To avoid the prohibitive cost of dual Lagrangian solvers and the test-time teacher dependence of state-augmented methods such as Saute, we derive an unaugmented constrained MDP whose reward transformation preserves the hard-constraint semantics, admits a low-variance policy gradient decomposition into single-step and long-term terms, and provably satisfies the worst-case constraint almost surely in the penalty limit. Through extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks, we demonstrate that our method significantly expands the accuracy-fidelity Pareto front. By matching the high Final Answer Correctness of pure RL and drastically reducing teacher constraint violations, we ultimately achieve the highest rigorous Reasoning Success Rate across all evaluated settings.

</details>


### [205] [Benchmarking System One decision models against trained classifiers and language models for automated decision gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00346)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在匹配条件下系统比较类型化决策模型（System One）、监督分类器与生成式语言模型在决策门控任务上的表现，发现模型类别优劣依赖于具体条件，并据此给出条件相关的设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件若将分支决策交给模型，需要一个可声明的选项及其可阈值化的概率。类型化决策模型能直接返回概率而不生成文本，但监督分类器与生成式语言模型是既有替代方案，缺乏在相同条件下对这几类方法的系统对比。

Method: 在同一测试框架下，向来自六个家族（含托管模型 Jev）的八个决策模型检查点以及两个生成式对比模型发送相同的语义请求，并在相同的工作流、意图与社会科学条目上对监督分类器与零样本分类器打分；同时考察选项键似然读取、温度校准、阈值设定、是/否选项互换等条件。

Result: 有标签时小型训练分类器在意图上最准、在工作流上与最佳决策模型无显著差异；无标签时除编码器类检查点外，所有决策模型在工作流与意图上均超过零样本蕴含分类器；通过选项键似然读取，更大的生成模型在工作流与意图上与 Jev 持平，并在 5% 风险下接受更多工作流决策；微调决策检查点相对未微调骨干提升意图准确率；少量选项上拟合的温度在多选项时增大校准误差；5% 范围内风险的留出阈值下 Jev 仍接受了 0.310 的域外请求；是/否互换使 Jev 每百题翻转 50.5 个答案，而微调检查点减少了骨干的社会科学翻转；意图训练的第一阶段升级到 Jev，在满 GPU 利用率下以 0.43 成本达到其准确率。

Conclusion: 模型类别的排名取决于条件，研究结果为自动化决策门控给出一套条件依赖的设计规则。

Abstract: Software that hands branching decisions to a model needs a declared option and a probability it can threshold. Typed decision models, also called System One models, return such probabilities without generating text, while supervised classifiers and generative language models are the established alternatives. Under matched conditions, one harness sends eight decision-model checkpoints from six families, including the hosted model Jev, and two generative comparators the same semantic requests, and scores supervised and zero-shot classifiers on the same workflow, intent and social-science items. The ranking of the model classes depends on the conditions. With the task's own labels, small trained classifiers are the most accurate on intents and not significantly different from the best decision models on workflows. Without labels, every decision model except the encoder-based checkpoints exceeds a zero-shot entailment classifier on workflows and intents. Read through option-key likelihoods, a larger generative model is level with Jev on workflows and intents and accepts more workflow decisions at five percent risk, and fine-tuned decision checkpoints gain intent accuracy over their untuned backbones. Stored temperatures fitted on few options raise calibration error with many options, and a held-out threshold for five percent in-scope risk still lets Jev accept 0.310 of out-of-scope requests. Swapping yes and no flips 50.5 answers per hundred for Jev, while fine-tuned checkpoints cut their backbones' social-science flips. An intent-trained first stage escalating to Jev matches its accuracy at 0.43 of its cost at full graphics-processor utilization. The results yield condition-dependent design rules for automated decision gates.

</details>


### [206] [Deep Learning for Anomaly Detection in Railway Systems: A Structured Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00363)
*Ammar Bouketta,Smail Niar,Hamza Ouarnoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于深度学习在铁路系统异常检测中应用的综述论文，提出了统一的分类体系、梳理了主流学习范式，并给出面向部署的决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代铁路系统的安全可靠运行日益依赖数据驱动的监测与智能故障检测。铁路车辆与基础设施产生的异构传感器数据不断增多，深度学习已成为铁路异常检测的有效范式，但相关方法分散、术语与分类不统一，缺乏系统性的选型与部署参考，因此需要一篇结构化综述来整合研究现状。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法：构建统一分类体系，涵盖异常位置、数据表征与表现形式、感知模态、时间特性；将卷积、循环、注意力架构、自编码器、生成对抗网络及Transformer等方法归入基于分类、基于预测、基于重构和混合学习四类范式；并梳理以数据为中心的挑战、评估实践、性能指标及实际部署问题（边缘-云架构、计算约束、硬件感知优化）。

Result: 论文系统整理并对比了现有深度学习铁路异常检测方法，归纳了各类范式的适用场景；总结了数据、评估和部署层面的关键问题；提出一个面向决策的框架，将异常特征、数据属性与运行约束映射到合适的检测范式和部署配置。

Conclusion: 该工作为铁路异常检测中深度学习方案的选择与部署提供了结构化参考，并指出了实现可靠、可扩展智能监测系统所面临的开放挑战。

Abstract: Ensuring safe and reliable operation of modern railway systems increasingly relies on data-driven monitoring and intelligent fault detection. Deep learning has emerged as an effective paradigm for railway anomaly detection, driven by the growing availability of heterogeneous sensor data from rolling stock and infrastructure. This paper presents a structured survey of deep learning-based anomaly detection approaches for railway systems. The surveyed methods are organized using a unified taxonomy covering anomaly location, data representation and manifestation, sensing modality, and temporal characteristics. Existing approaches, including convolutional, recurrent and attention-based architectures, autoencoders, generative adversarial networks, and transformers, are structured into classification-based, prediction-based, reconstruction-based, and hybrid learning paradigms. The survey also examines data-centric challenges, evaluation practices, performance metrics, and practical deployment aspects, including edge-cloud architectures, computational constraints, and hardware-aware optimization. Finally, a decision-oriented framework links anomaly characteristics, data properties, and operational constraints to suitable detection paradigms and deployment configurations. This work provides a structured reference for selecting and deploying deep learning solutions for railway anomaly detection and highlights open challenges toward reliable and scalable intelligent monitoring systems.

</details>


### [207] [Manifold-Constrained Initial Noise Optimization for Efficient Generative Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00365)
*Jinho Chang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZeNOVA：一种无需梯度的初始噪声对齐方法，通过退火软值引导、流形约束超球Langevin动力学和Metropolis-Hastings跳跃，在图像与视频生成模型上稳定高效地提升奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏与流映射模型实现了确定性的一步或几步生成，催生了直接优化初始噪声的奖励对齐方向。但现有初始噪声优化的方法多依赖一阶梯度，在黑盒奖励场景下不适用，或存在不稳定与低效的问题，因此需要一个稳定高效的无梯度初始噪声对齐方法。

Method: 提出 ZeNOVA，一种无梯度的稳定高效初始噪声对齐方法。针对黑盒场景的主要挑战，采用三项关键技术：退火软值引导（annealed soft-value guidance）、流形约束的超球Langevin动力学（manifold-constrained hyperspherical Langevin dynamics），以及 Metropolis-Hastings 跳跃。

Result: 在图像和视频生成模型上的大量实验表明，ZeNOVA 优于所有评估的零阶基线，能以更稳定的方式将初始噪声优化至更高奖励，同时利用高斯先验的几何结构。

Conclusion: ZeNOVA 在黑盒奖励对齐场景下具有实际适用性，能够稳定有效地优化初始噪声以实现更高奖励，为奖励对齐提供了无梯度方向的可行方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in distillation and flow-map models have enabled deterministic one- or few-step generation for high-quality data, facilitating a new branch of reward alignment approaches that directly optimize the initial noise from a Gaussian distribution. However, most existing initial-noise optimization methods rely on first-order gradient information, which is either inapplicable or suffers from instability and inefficiency in black-box reward scenarios. Here, we introduce ZeNOVA, a stable and efficient initial noise alignment method in a gradient-free manner. Specifically, we address existing algorithms' major challenge in black-box scenarios through annealed soft-value guidance, manifold-constrained hyperspherical Langevin dynamics, and Metropolis-Hastings jumping. Extensive experiments on image and video generative models show that ZeNOVA outperforms all evaluated zeroth-order baselines by optimizing the initial noise toward higher rewards substantially more stably while exploiting the geometry of the Gaussian prior, demonstrating its practical applicability to various black-box reward alignment.

</details>


### [208] [MoRA: MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00367)
*Yushuai Sun,Zikun Zhou,Lin Gao,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MoRA 框架，通过可学习路由偏置与专家近似实现结构化 MoE 专家剪枝，在多个模型和零样本基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型虽能通过稀疏激活降低每 token 计算量，但部署时仍需将全部专家池载入内存；现有结构化专家剪枝方法要么排序准则与模型性能不匹配，要么子集搜索计算开销大，且忽视保留专家间的路由行为冗余。

Method: 为每个专家引入可学习路由偏置，通过最小化语言建模损失和路由多样性正则项来优化；学习到的偏置锐化路由概率分布以识别关键专家并鼓励选择路由偏好多样的专家。此外提出专家近似机制作为剪枝后增强，用剩余专家通过仿射变换近似被剪专家的输出。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite 和 Moonlight-16B-A3B 上各 MoE 层移除 25% 与 50% 路由专家，九个零样本基准测试表明 MoRA 优于最先进的剪枝算法。

Conclusion: MoRA 是一种有效的结构化 MoE 专家剪枝框架，结合路由偏置学习与专家近似，可在显著减少专家数量的同时保持或提升剪枝模型性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable parameter scaling with limited per-token computation by activating only a small subset of experts for each token, but deploying them still requires loading the complete expert pool into memory. Structured expert pruning can effectively reduce the memory usage by removing experts. However, existing pruning methods either use expert ranking criteria that are not well aligned with model performance or rely on effective expert subset searching that is computationally expensive. Moreover, these methods typically overlook the routing-behavior redundancy among the retained experts. In this paper, we propose MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation (MoRA), a framework for structured MoE expert pruning. We introduce a learnable router bias for each expert and optimize these biases by minimizing the language-modeling loss and a routing-diversity regularizer.
