The learned router biases sharpen the routing probability distributions to identify experts critical to model performance while encouraging the selection of experts with diverse routing preferences. In addition, we introduce an expert approximation mechanism as a post-pruning enhancement. It leverages the remaining experts to approximate the outputs of pruned experts by affine transformation, further improving the performance of the pruned model. We evaluate MoRA on Qwen3-30B-A3B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and Moonlight-16B-A3B, removing 25\% and 50\% of the routed experts in each MoE layer. Extensive experiments on nine zero-shot benchmarks show that MoRA outperforms state-of-the-art pruning algorithms. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [209] [M$^2$Weather: A Benchmark for Joint Multi-Station and Multi-Variable Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00370)
*Rongwen Li,Xiao Wang,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出M²Weather基准，用于联合多站点与多变量的天气预测，包含2809个高质量站点、5个物理耦合变量和三个空间尺度，并提供统一训练评估协议与即插即用适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究往往将站点间的空间依赖与变量间的物理耦合分开处理，且使用不同数据集和实验设置，难以系统评估二者各自及联合的贡献，因此需要一个统一的基准与协议。

Method: 通过多准则质量控制与站点分层，收集法国、欧洲、全球三个尺度共2809个站点、5个物理耦合天气变量；引入统一训练与评估协议以公平比较不同站点-变量建模范式；设计轻量级即插即用适配器，可在不重新训练模型的情况下补充缺失的站点或变量关系。

Result: 对16个代表性模型的系统评估表明，联合建模站点与变量关系具有收益；补充缺失关系后，所有适配模型在三个数据集上的MSE均进一步降低。

Conclusion: 站点与变量之间的互补信息是提升站点天气预测的重要资源，M²Weather为系统性研究提供了统一基准与高效工具。

Abstract: Station weather forecasting is fundamentally shaped by both complex spatial dependencies across stations and strong physical coupling among weather variables. However, existing studies often consider these relationships separately and use different datasets and experimental settings, hindering systematic assessment of their individual and joint contributions. In this paper, we introduce $M^2$Weather, a benchmark for joint multi-station and multi-variable weather forecasting. Through multi-criteria quality control and station stratification, we collect 2,809 high-quality stations with 5 physically coupled weather variables across three spatial scales: France, Europe, and Global. This multi-scale design lets us examine whether conclusions persist from national to global station networks. We also introduce unified training and evaluation protocols to enable fair comparison of different station-variable modeling paradigms. To further examine the benefits of modeling station-variable relationships, we design a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter. With a trained weather forecasting model, this adapter can introduce missing station or variable relationships without retraining the model. This enables fair and efficient investigation of station-variable relationships. Systematic evaluation of 16 representative models shows the benefits of jointly modeling station and variable relationships. Completing missing relationships further reduces MSE for all adapted models on all three datasets. Together, these results identify the complementary information across stations and variables as an important resource for improving station weather forecasting. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/M2-Weather.

</details>


### [210] [When Do Attention-Head Ablations Support Causal Claims? Projection-Level Confounds, Floor Effects, and Matched Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00373)
*Juli Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文表明，在GPT-2 small上做单头消融时，"清零一个注意力头"的实现位置、评价指标和控制组设置会显著影响结论，只有修正投影位置、使用非饱和连续指标并配合匹配对照，才能得到稳定的因果性推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力头消融被广泛用于推断语言模型哪些组件对某行为有因果责任，但这种推断的可靠性此前缺乏系统检验。作者希望验证：干预语义（如何"清零"）、评价指标以及对照组设计是否会导致消融结论不稳定甚至错误。

Method: 在GPT-2 small上进行注意力头消融实验：对比"投影后实现"与"修正的投影前消融"两种清零方式及其相关性；比较二值准确率与gold-token对数概率两种指标；采用发现集/留出集划分，并抽取1000个匹配的随机头与层级匹配头作为对照，评估每头效应排序的稳定性；在DistilGPT2上做复现验证。

Result: 投影后实现的消融与修正的投影前消融几乎不相关（Pearson r = 0.057），两者选出的top-5重要头完全不重叠；二值准确率在行为地板和天花板附近会掩盖效应，而gold-token对数概率保持梯度性。修正后的每头效应排序在划分间高度稳定（Spearman rho = 0.974），top-5头显著超过两种对照分布（Monte Carlo p = 0.001）；但任务特异性证据在GPT-2上不稳健。DistilGPT2复现了干预语义与匹配对照的结论。

Conclusion: 单头消融本身不足以支撑因果主张；可辩护的解释需要正确的干预位置、非饱和的连续评价指标，以及匹配的留出集对照。研究提示相关方法论细节必须被严格验证。

Abstract: Attention-head ablation, zeroing a head and measuring the resulting change in task performance, is a common method for inferring which components of a language model are causally responsible for a behavior. We show using GPT-2 small that this inference can be fragile unless the intervention semantics, evaluation metric, and controls are carefully validated. A natural post-projection implementation of "zeroing a head" is nearly uncorrelated with a corrected pre-projection ablation (Pearson r = 0.057) and selects a completely disjoint top-5 set of important heads. We also show that binary accuracy can hide effects at behavioral floors and near ceilings, whereas gold-token log-probability remains graded. Using a discovery/held-out split and 1,000 matched random-head and layer-matched-head control draws, the corrected per-head effect ranking is highly stable across splits (Spearman rho = 0.974), and the top-5 selected heads significantly exceed both control distributions (Monte Carlo p = 0.001). However, evidence for task specificity is not robust on GPT-2. Replication on DistilGPT2 preserves the intervention-semantic and matched-control findings. These results show that single-head ablation does not by itself justify a causal claim; defensible interpretation requires correct intervention placement, a non-saturated continuous metric, and matched held-out controls.

</details>


### [211] [A First Glance at Jev for Network Traffic Classification: Accuracy, Processing Time, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00376)
*Shenghe Xu,Lifan Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次实证评估通用决策模型Jev在网络流应用分类任务上的表现，发现少量标注示例可显著提升Jev准确率，但仍低于传统树集成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通用决策模型（以Jev为代表）能否用于网络流的应用分类，这在此前尚无实证研究。

Method: 在CESNET-QUICEXT-25数据集上，仅使用前十个数据包的包大小、方向和包间隔时间，对Jev在10个应用标签上进行评估；对比随机森林、Extra Trees，以及与OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol（高推理强度，经Azure）的配对子集测试。

Result: 训练期后26个采集周、52,000条记录中，40个固定标注示例将Jev准确率从9.80%提升至28.42%；8,000条记录训练的随机森林和Extra Trees分别达69.95%和66.80%，每周均优于Jev；增加上下文至150示例使首周测试达34.50%。配对100条子集上，Jev（40示例）准确率29%、中位请求时间0.750秒，GPT-5.6 Sol为37%、6.036秒，Jev API费用更低。

Conclusion: 标注示例能大幅提升Jev，但所测试配置仍不及训练好的树集成模型；受监督预算不等和配置固定影响，性能差距无法归因于单一原因，配对子集亦未确立任一服务的准确率优势。

Abstract: We evaluate Jev on ten dataset-defined application labels in CESNET-QUICEXT-25 using only the first ten packets' sizes, directions, and inter-packet times. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first empirical study of general-purpose decision models, represented here by Jev, for application classification of network flows. Across 52,000 records from 26 collection weeks following the training period, 40 fixed labeled examples raise Jev's accuracy from 9.80% to 28.42%. Random Forest and Extra Trees trained on 8,000 records achieve 69.95% and 66.80% and outperform Jev in every week. Increasing Jev's context to 150 examples yields 34.50% on the first test week. On a paired 100-record subset, Jev with 40 examples achieves 29% accuracy at a median request time of 0.750 s, versus 37% and 6.036 s for the generative language model OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol with high reasoning effort through Azure; Jev also incurs lower API charges. The paired subset does not establish an accuracy advantage for either service, and the timing reflects different service configurations. Thus, labeled examples substantially improve Jev, but the tested Jev configurations remain less accurate than trained tree ensembles; unequal supervision budgets and fixed configurations prevent attributing the gap to a single cause.

</details>


### [212] [STCFormer: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Modeling with Dynamic Cluster Transformer for Station-based Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00377)
*Rongwen Li,Haixin Xie,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STCFormer，一种自适应时空Transformer，在每个时间片段内根据站点局部演化动态聚类，结合簇内局部注意力与区域全局注意力，并在理论上证明其Lipschitz上界不劣于全连接注意力，在三个真实气象数据集八个任务上取得最优24小时MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站点气象预报需建模站点间复杂空间依赖。现有基于聚类的选择性建模虽有前景，但整个观测窗口共享的固定分组会掩盖站点关系的局部变化，且仅用簇内交互会丢失全局上下文；同时选择性交互相对稠密连接的理论优势尚不明确。

Method: 提出STCFormer自适应时空Transformer：在每个时间patch内依据站点局部演化动态分组；设计Cluster-Guided Attention Block，融合簇内细粒度局部注意力与基于区域状态摘要的全局注意力，使每个站点可获取簇外信息；推导出簇条件局部注意力的Lipschitz上界不超过全连接情形，并据此设计InfoLoss。

Result: 在三个真实世界气象数据集、八项温度与风速预报任务上，STCFormer在全部八项任务中取得最低的24小时MSE，在48项指标与预测时长的比较中47项排名前二。消融实验与案例研究进一步验证了局部自适应分组及局部-全局互补交互的有效性。

Conclusion: STCFormer通过局部自适应分组与局部-全局互补注意力，在提升气象预报精度的同时具备理论上更优的鲁棒性，为站点气象预报提供了有效方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Station-based weather forecasting supports daily life and economic activity, yet accurate forecasts require modeling complex spatial dependencies among stations. Recent clustering-based selective modeling offers a promising alternative to dense inter-station interactions. However, a grouping shared across an observation window may obscure local changes in station relationships, while intra-cluster interactions alone may miss important global context. The theoretical advantages of selective interactions over dense connectivity also remain insufficiently understood. We therefore propose STCFormer, an adaptive spatio-temporal Transformer that dynamically groups stations according to their local evolution within each temporal patch. Its Cluster-Guided Attention Block combines fine-grained local attention within clusters and global attention over regional state summaries, allowing each station to access information beyond its own cluster. We further show that a derived Lipschitz upper bound for cluster-conditioned local attention is no larger than its fully connected counterpart, explaining a potential robustness benefit and motivating the design of InfoLoss. Experiments on three real-world weather datasets spanning eight temperature and wind forecasting tasks show that STCFormer achieves the lowest 24-hour mean squared error on all eight tasks and ranks first or second in 47 of 48 comparisons across metrics and forecasting horizons. Ablations and case studies further confirm the benefits of locally adaptive grouping and complementary local-global interactions. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/STCFormer.

</details>


### [213] [Fusion techniques of time frequency-based images to predict the outcome of rTMS depression therapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00380)
*Wael Korani,Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Mohammed Aledhari,Reza Rostami,Reza Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于EEG时频图像预测rTMS抑郁症治疗反应，提出Montage与blending两种融合技术并设计轻量CNN；但在严格按被试划分的交叉验证下，绝大多数配置性能接近随机水平，说明此前的片段级高精度存在被试泄露导致的乐观偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症治疗结果预测对临床决策困难，rTMS是常用非侵入疗法，但用EEG预测其应答受个体差异大、单域特征不足的限制，因此需要更丰富的特征表示与融合方法。

Method: 提出montage（导联组合拼接）与blending（混合）两种融合技术，从EEG时频(TF)图像提取更丰富特征；构建轻量级定制CNN在融合TF表示上训练。数据为主要队列15例和次要队列46例。实验分两组：一是片段级10折交叉验证（同一被试片段可同时出现在训练与测试）；二是严格被试不重叠交叉验证。共测试4种时频方法、6种融合机制、14种模型架构，并做被试级置换检验。

Result: 片段级设置下Montage CWT_ST融合在主队列达99.90%准确率、次队列91.90%。严格被试不重叠设置下性能崩塌：除一个例外，两个队列所有配置AUC在0.31–0.54之间，且95%置信区间均包含0.5；最优单模型被试级置换检验p=0.703。最佳被试级结果为Montage CWT_ST在主队列，AUC 0.874±0.183、准确率82.7%。

Conclusion: 片段级交叉验证因被试泄露产生虚高精度，不能反映真实泛化能力；在严格被试不重叠评估下EEG预测rTMS疗效基本无效。研究强调抑郁症治疗反应预测中必须采用被试级评估协议，并对高精度结果保持警惕。

Abstract: Depression is a mental condition that can lead to suicide and self-harm. Predicting the outcome of depression treatment is one of the most difficult tasks for clinicians. Among various treatment options, repetitive Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (rTMS) is a widely used non-invasive method. Predicting rTMS response using Electroencephalogram (EEG) data is difficult because of high inter-subject variability and limited features from single-domain analysis. We introduce two fusion techniques, montage and blending, to overcome these limitations and extract richer features from EEG-derived Time-Frequency (TF) images. We then propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) trained on fused TF representations. \textcolor{black}{We use a primary dataset of 15 patients and a secondary dataset of 46 patients. We run two sets of experiments. The first set uses segment-level 10-fold cross-validation. In this setup segments from the same patient can appear in both training and testing. The Montage CWT\_ST fusion reaches 99.90\% accuracy on the primary dataset and 91.90\% on the secondary dataset. The second set uses strict subject-disjoint cross-validation. All segments of a patient stay in one fold and no patient appears in both training and testing. Performance collapses. We test four time-frequency methods, six fusion mechanisms, and fourteen model architectures. With one exception, every configuration on both cohorts falls between AUC 0.31 and 0.54 and every 95\% confidence interval contains 0.5. A patient-level permutation test on the best standalone method returns $p = 0.703$. The best subject-level result is Montage CWT\_ST on the primary cohort, which reaches AUC $0.874 \pm 0.183$ and 82.7\% accuracy.

</details>


### [214] [OmniMed-Jev: Calibrating LVLM Confidence for Trustworthy Medical Multimodal Decisions via System One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00381)
*Luyao Tang,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OmniMed-Jev，将医学多模态决策建模为候选集上的概率分布（Choice/Noul/Score），显著改善置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态医学模型虽扩展了感知能力，但仍以生成文本形式表达诊断、发现或细胞计数等决策，其报告的"概率"实际反映的是下一个 token 的概率，而非决策本身的概率，导致置信度与真实准确率不匹配。

Method: 受决策原生接口（如 Jev）启发，将每个医学决策表示为对运行时提供的候选集上的 Choice（互斥类别）、Noul（发现是否存在）或 Score（有界有序值）决策，并返回该候选集上的完整概率分布；用统一的候选条件概率模型覆盖多种影像模态与预测任务，使异构输出转化为可比较的概率。在相同骨干、数据和训练计划下与生成式基线做接口控制对比。

Result: 相比生成式基线，OmniMed-Jev 的报告概率更贴近实际正确率：校准误差最多降低一个数量级，可靠性误差最多降低两个数量级，点预测性能相当；仅计数任务上生成式基线更优。

Conclusion: 将决策分布作为模型输出并非格式变化，而是让所报告的数字成为名副其实的概率；结果支持显式决策建模在所评估任务中使置信度更有意义，但不构成临床可用性证据——该对比无法区分接口本身与相关训练差异。代码已开源。

Abstract: Medical models are judged not only on correctness, but on whether reported confidence matches actual accuracy. Generalist multimodal medical models have expanded what a single model can perceive, yet they still express bounded decisions such as diagnoses, findings or cell counts as generated text, so the reported probability reflects the next token rather than the decision itself. Motivated by decision-native interfaces such as Jev, we introduce OmniMed-Jev, which represents each medical decision as a Choice, Noul or Score decision over a runtime-supplied candidate set and returns a full distribution over that set: mutually exclusive classes, binary presence of a finding, or a bounded ordered value. The design is omni in three respects: it accepts diverse imaging modalities, covers different prediction tasks, and expresses them through one candidate-conditioned probability model, so heterogeneous outputs become comparable probabilities rather than task-specific strings. In an interface-controlled comparison against a generative baseline trained on the same backbone, data and schedule, OmniMed-Jev's reported probabilities track observed correctness far more closely, reducing calibration error by up to an order of magnitude and reliability error by up to two, while point-prediction performance remains comparable; counting is the one family where the generative baseline stays ahead. Making the decision distribution the model's output is not a format change but what turns reported numbers into probabilities that mean what they say. These results support explicit decision modeling as a way to make reported confidence meaningful within the evaluated tasks, and they are not evidence of clinical readiness: the comparison cannot separate the interface from associated training differences, which we state alongside the results. Code is available at github.com/lytang63/OmniMed-Jev.

</details>


### [215] [EvoGen-Harness: Learning Where and How to Evolve Image-Generation Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00383)
*Jiabin Luo,Yinan Liu,Chunlei Meng,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EvoGen-Harness 框架与 Trace 方法，在不修改冻结生成器参数的前提下，通过归因引导的多职责协同演化提升文生图系统表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有冻结生成器外部适配方法通常只优化单一预设维度（如提示词、路由或工作流），限制了可纠正生成失败的空间；而允许多个生成器外部职责共同演化虽能扩大适配空间，却带来新难题：视觉反馈只揭示"失败了什么"，却无法指出持续性演化应发生在何处、以及如何高效探索该空间。

Method: 提出生成器无关框架 EvoGen-Harness，用于多职责图像生成框架的演化；并配套 Trace（Trajectory-Relative Attribution and Coordinated Evolution）方法：在多次随机执行中聚合证据，以失败归因作为搜索先验聚焦候选更新，并逐步将残余失败重新归因以协调多个职责的演化；同时使用 No-Patch 机制与留出验证避免无必要或有害的更新。

Result: 在 GenEval2、T2I-CompBench++ 和 WISE 三个基准上，EvoGen-Harness 相较最强基线分别提升 +0.2633、+0.0720 和 +0.0752，同时取得 87.9–91.4% 的归因召回率、94.8% 的 No-Patch 准确率，回归率仅 1.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，归因引导的多职责演化能够显著增强冻结的文生图系统，效果超越单维度适配。该工作属于多模态生成方向，归为 MLLM 相关范畴。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image (T2I) systems can be improved without modifying generator parameters by adapting the external system around frozen generators. However, existing approaches typically optimize a predefined dimension, such as prompts, routing, or workflows, restricting the space in which generation failures can be corrected. Allowing multiple generator-external responsibilities to evolve provides a broader adaptation space, but introduces a new challenge: visual feedback reveals what failed, but not where persistent evolution should occur or how this space should be explored efficiently. We introduce EvoGen-Harness, a generator-agnostic framework for multi-responsibility image-generation harness evolution, together with Trace (Trajectory-Relative Attribution and Coordinated Evolution). Trace aggregates evidence across stochastic executions, uses failure attribution as a search prior to focus candidate updates, and progressively re-attributes residual failures to coordinate evolution across responsibilities, while No-Patch and held-out validation prevent unnecessary or harmful updates. Across GenEval2, T2I-CompBench++, and WISE, EvoGen-Harness improves over the strongest evaluated baselines by +0.2633, +0.0720, and +0.0752, respectively, while achieving 87.9-91.4% attribution recall, 94.8% No-Patch accuracy, and only 1.9% regression. These results demonstrate that attribution-guided multi-responsibility evolution can substantially enhance frozen T2I systems beyond single-dimension adaptation.

</details>


### [216] [FAER: Auditable Utility-Aligned Trajectory Replay for Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00385)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Tianshu Fu,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文形式化了回放选择与学习者效用之间的差距，提出可审计的全轨迹回放框架FAER及学习者感知选择器FAER-UTILITY，在GSM8K上取得优于固定、均匀与格式反馈基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回放选择器常用格式反馈、置信度、新鲜度或响应长度对缓存轨迹排序，但缓存层面的正确性与下游学习者的效用是不同目标，二者之间的选择—学习差距未被形式化研究。

Method: 提出FAER可审计全轨迹回放框架：训练无关的固定选择器作为协议基线；FAER-UTILITY为在不相交校准块上拟合的学习者感知选择器。以归一化梯度对齐作为基线，并用一次性的优化器感知虚拟更新提供幅度感知的效用曲面。审计契约在加入评估标签前冻结观测字段与回放轨迹。

Result: GSM8K + Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct上，128次更新下固定选择器质量0.6329，均匀0.5482，格式反馈0.6037；仅元数据交叉拟合校准达0.6476±0.0139（八种子，中位数0.6481，配对95%区间[+0.079,+0.122]），含校准全成本189,642 tokens、3.48 GPU小时；FAER-UTILITY达0.6624，成本62,844 tokens、4.26 GPU小时。格式反馈所选记录正确率0.6953，高于固定选择器的0.3594，但下游排序不同。

Conclusion: 缓存正确性与学习者效用确实存在不一致，学习者感知的选择可带来更优下游效果，但成本更高；可审计框架使选择—学习差距的可验证比较成为可能。

Abstract: Replay selectors often rank cached trajectories by format feedback, confidence, freshness, or response length, although cache-level correctness and downstream learner utility are distinct objectives. We formalize this selection-to-learning gap and introduce FAER as an auditable full-trajectory replay framework. Its training-free fixed selector is a protocol baseline; FAER-UTILITY is the learner-aware selector fitted on disjoint calibration blocks. The normalized gradient alignment is reported as a baseline, while a disposable optimizer-aware virtual update supplies a magnitude-aware utility surface. The audit contract freezes observed fields and replay traces before evaluation labels are joined. On GSM8K with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, the matched learner study reports quality 0.6329 for the fixed selector, compared with 0.5482 for uniform and 0.6037 for format-feedback under 128 updates. Metadata-only cross-fitted calibration reaches $0.6476\!\pm\!0.0139$ over eight seeds (median 0.6481; paired 95% interval $[+0.079,+0.122]$) at 63,276 target-run tokens; its recorded full cost is 189,642 tokens and 3.48 GPU-hours including calibration. The completed FAER-UTILITY row reaches 0.6624 at 62,844 target-run tokens and 4.26 GPU-hours. Format-feedback selects records with correctness 0.6953, compared with 0.3594 for the fixed selector, despite the different downstream ranking. The completed comparison surfaces report the learner-aware ablation, same-seed gap, policy-optimization rows, and strict zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [217] [T2SPO: Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00388)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Maoqi Liu,Feiran Li,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出T2SPO方法，利用历史交互轨迹提供步级反馈，通过TabPFN回归器估计剩余成功距离，为LLM智能体提供中间决策的辅助信用分配，在ALFWorld和WebShop上超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习让LLM智能体能通过与环境交互学习多步行为，但许多交互任务的奖励只反映最终结果，对哪些中间决策有助于推进任务缺乏指导，导致信用分配困难。

Method: T2SPO从成功轨迹中提取"剩余距离"目标，并配对沿途所访问状态的表示。以此为例，预训练的TabPFN回归器对新rollout中每个状态估计剩余成功距离。相邻状态间距离估计的变化作为智能体步骤的辅助信用，与任务级监督共同使用。训练过程中，新完成的轨迹会刷新估计器的上下文，在不更新参数的情况下纳入新经验。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用1.5B和7B语言模型的实验表明，T2SPO相较GRPO能持续提升整体任务成功率。

Conclusion: 利用历史成功轨迹提供的步级剩余距离信号，可在无需参数更新估计器的条件下有效改善LLM智能体的多步强化学习信用分配，验证了轨迹到步骤策略优化的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables large language model (LLM) agents to learn multi-step behaviors through interaction with their environments. However, rewards in many interactive tasks reflect only the final outcome, providing limited guidance on which intermediate decisions advance the task. Successful training trajectories contain intermediate states that can provide supervision for subsequent interactions. We introduce Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization (T2SPO), a method that uses past interaction trajectories to provide step-level feedback for policy learning. T2SPO derives remaining-distance targets from successful trajectories and pairs them with representations of the states visited along the way. Conditioned on these examples, a pretrained TabPFN regressor estimates the remaining distance to success at each state of a new rollout. Changes in this distance estimate across consecutive states yield auxiliary credit for agent steps alongside task-level supervision. As training proceeds, newly completed trajectories refresh the estimator's context, incorporating new experience without updating its parameters. Experiments with 1.5B and 7B language models on ALFWorld and WebShop show that T2SPO consistently improves overall task success over GRPO.

</details>


### [218] [MatrixReward: Reward from Rubric Matrix for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00389)
*Zihan Shen,Qi Liu,Zixuan Yang,Yiqun Chen,Chenglong Zhao,Xiaozhao Wang,Lei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MatrixReward，通过“rollout×评分细则”的胜率矩阵构建开放式生成任务的数据依赖型奖励机制，在 Qwen3-8B 上四个基准平均得分 63.02，超过最强基线约 2.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式查询生成缺乏标准答案，需要有效的奖励机制。而逐点评分细则仅提供有限信息，无法反映同一提示下不同回答的相对质量；将多个细则判断合并为单一分数也可能掩盖样本间差异。

Method: 构建 rollout-by-rubric 胜率矩阵：在每个评分细则下比较每一对采样回答。利用矩阵各列的离散程度衡量该细则对当前 rollout 的区分能力，利用列间相关性揭示细则重复性，从而得到数据依赖的细则权重，并与细则先验权重结合。经列归一化与加权后，以各细则观测到的最大值与最小值定义正、负理想画像，用每个 rollout 到两个理想点的距离计算“相对接近度”质量奖励。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上于四个开放式问答基准评测，MatrixReward 平均得分 63.02，比最强基线高约 2.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于相对比较构建的矩阵可为开放式生成强化学习构建更合理的奖励。

Abstract: Open-ended query generation lacks standard answers, thus necessitating an effective reward mechanism. Pointwise scoring rubrics provide limited information about the relative quality of sample answers under the same prompt; merging multiple rubric judgments into a single score may also mask the differences between these answers. We propose MatrixReward, which constructs rewards from a rollout-by-rubric win-rate matrix obtained by comparing every pair of sampled responses under each rubric. The spread of each matrix column captures how strongly that rubric distinguishes the current rollouts, while correlations between columns reveal rubric repetition; together, these statistics yield data-dependent rubric weights. We combine these weights with the prior weights of rubrics. After column normalization and weighting, the observed per-rubric maxima and minima define positive and negative ideal profiles. Each rollout's distances to these two ideals determine its relative-closeness quality reward. Evaluated using Qwen3-8B on four open-ended query-answering benchmarks, MatrixReward achieves an average score of 63.02, outperforming the strongest baseline by approximately 2.0%. These results support the idea that matrices derived from relative comparisons can be used to construct rewards more reasonably for open-ended generative reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [219] [Interpretable Synthetic Medical Tabular Data Generation for Clinical Decision Support Using Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00391)
*Michael Vasilakakis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将模糊认知图（FCM）应用于合成医疗表格数据生成，通过可解释的模糊集与因果边权重建模特征依赖，仅用CPU即可在TSTR协议下匹配或超越TVAE和高斯Copula基线，并具备良好隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实临床数据受隐私、伦理与可获取性限制，合成医疗表格数据成为开发与验证计算机医疗系统的重要手段。但现有概率模型与深度生成模型缺乏可解释性，且难以保留具有临床意义的依赖关系，不适合安全攸关场景。

Method: 提出基于模糊认知图（FCM）的合成医疗表格数据生成框架：用语言可解释的模糊集描述临床特征，以模糊集交集计算的FCM边权重编码特征间依赖；通过将随机初始化的语言激活向量在FCM中传播至收敛，再经去模糊化得到临床连贯的数值；原生支持混合数据类型与健康记录中的领域约束。

Result: 在UCI医疗基准数据集上按Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real（TSTR）协议评估：心脏病数据集准确率最高0.81、AUROC最高0.90，匹配或超越TVAE与高斯Copula基线，且仅用CPU运行；保真度指标KS Complement最高0.91、Correlation Similarity最高0.95；DCR Baseline Protection持续优于TVAE，隐私保障充分。

Conclusion: 因果基础、可解释的模糊建模为可信合成医疗数据生成提供了计算高效且透明的替代方案，优于深度生成模型，适用于计算机医疗系统。

Abstract: Synthetic medical tabular data generation has become essential for developing and validating computer-based medical systems (CBMSs) when real clinical data is restricted due to privacy, ethical, or data availability limitations. Existing probabilistic and deep generative models often lack interpretability and fail to preserve clinically meaningful dependencies, limiting their suitability for safety-critical applications. This paper proposes a novel application of Fuzzy Cognitive Maps (FCMs) in a framework for synthetic medical tabular data generation with explicit causality and privacy preservation. Clinical features are described using linguistically interpretable fuzzy sets, and inter-feature dependencies are encoded as FCM edge weights computed from fuzzy set intersections. Synthetic patient records are generated by propagating randomly initialized linguistic activation vectors through the FCM until convergence, followed by defuzzification to produce clinically coherent numerical values. The approach natively handles mixed data types, and domain constraints common in health records. Experimental evaluation on UCI medical benchmark datasets demonstrates competitive performance under a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) protocol. The proposed method achieves accuracy of up to 0.81 and AUROC of up to 0.90 on the Heart Disease dataset, matching or exceeding TVAE and Gaussian Copula baselines while running exclusively on CPU. Fidelity metrics including KS Complement (up to 0.91) and Correlation Similarity (up to 0.95) confirm strong statistical coherence, and DCR Baseline Protection scores consistently exceed those of TVAE, confirming adequate privacy guarantees. These results demonstrate that causally grounded, interpretable fuzzy modeling offers a computationally efficient and transparent alternative to deep generative models for trustworthy synthetic data generation in CBMSs.

</details>


### [220] [Forking: Sudden Overfitting Under Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00394)
*Shanbin Yu,Shaoyang Guo,Haoran Zhao,Danni Yu,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究NanoGPT自研搜索中发现的"分叉"泛化失败现象：在数据回放下，过编码n-gram记忆分支的模型在epoch边界处训练损失与验证损失急剧分离，形似叉子；该现象在受控NanoGPT与DeepSeek风格Engram模型中均可复现，并出现在短预算、高强度重复的SFT与类RL场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者注意到自动研究（autoresearch）智能体产生的大量"看似有用"的技巧可能带来隐患。分叉现象正是这类技巧的意外副产品，因此有必要在受控环境中系统研究其成因与边界条件，提醒研究者审慎对待智能体自动搜索得到的结果。

Method: 在受控的vanilla NanoGPT设置下复现分叉，并在带Engram的DeepSeek风格模型中验证；采用数据回放设定，分析epoch边界处训练/验证损失的分裂。机制分析聚焦重复更新对已见延续概率的锐化与对未见延续概率的抑制、n-gram模块形成的弱交互上下文专属子空间、低频上下文的贡献，以及训练预算与哈希表拥挤度的影响；同时考察短预算、高强度重复的SFT与类RL训练情形。

Result: 成功复现分叉现象：重复更新锐化了训练中观察到的延续并压低未见延续的概率，其损失随每轮遍历累积增长；n-gram模块造成弱交互的上下文专属子空间，放大了该效应；低频上下文贡献了大部分损失差距，而更大的训练预算与高度拥挤的哈希表可抑制分叉；该现象亦出现于短预算、高强度重复的SFT与类RL场景。

Conclusion: 分叉是继grokking与double descent之后又一耐人寻味的深度学习现象，同时也是自动研究智能体所提技巧带来的意外且不悦的副产品。尽管这类智能体能产出数量庞大的看似有用的结果，研究者仍应对其结果保持警惕与审慎。

Abstract: This paper studies forking, a generalization failure discovered in NanoGPT autoresearch. Under data replay, models with an over-encoding n-gram memory branch show a sharp separation of training and validation loss at epoch boundaries, resembling the shape of forks. We study this phenomenon in a controlled vanilla NanoGPT setting and reproduce it in a DeepSeek-style model with Engram. Mechanistically, repeated updates sharpen the continuations observed in training while suppressing the probability of unseen continuations, whose loss grows with each pass. The n-gram module creates weakly interacting context-specific subspaces, amplifying this effect. Low-frequency contexts contribute most of the gap, whereas larger training budgets and heavily crowded tables suppress it. We also observe forking in short-budget, heavily repeated SFT and RL-like regimes. The contributions of this paper are twofold: (1) Forking reveals yet another curious phenomenon in deep learning, in addition to grokking and double descent. (2) Forking is an unexpected and unpleasant by-product of tricks proposed by autoresearch agents. While these agents produce an enormous number of results that seem useful, we should always be careful with their results.

</details>


### [221] [Specificity-Aware Diffusion Steering via Variance-Reduced Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00395)
*Luran Wang,Linrui Ma,Hannes Stärk,Regina Barzilay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种特异性感知的扩散模型推理时引导方法，通过重叠度目标设计目标分布并配合SMC采样，实现对负向分布的有效抑制同时最小化对正向分布的扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时引导可在不重新训练的前提下让预训练扩散模型满足新约束，但特异性感知生成存在困难：将样本从负参考分布推开时，也会侵蚀正负分布重叠区域中的正向分布，因此核心挑战是在抑制负向质量的同时尽量少地扭曲正向分布。

Method: 将特异性感知引导形式化为目标设计问题，基于重叠度目标导出目标分布，使目标分布仅在期望参考分布相对不受欢迎参考分布具有足够偏好优势的区域保留该分布，并给出特异性的似然比解释。为从相应的时间依赖目标路径采样，提出带方差最小化局部提议的序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样器，并引入实用的固定噪声优化过程，利用与期望及非期望分数场的雅可比-向量积。

Result: 在合成任务、类别对比生成、文本到图像任务以及肽-MHC（p-MHC）结合物任务上的实验表明，相比负向引导基线，该方法能更有效地抑制非期望区域、减少模式偏移，并通过降低SMC权重坍缩提升采样稳定性。

Conclusion: 所提方法为特异性感知的扩散引导提供了似然比视角的目标设计，并结合SMC采样与固定噪声优化，在多种任务上优于负向引导基线，代码已开源。

Abstract: Inference-time steering enables pretrained diffusion models to satisfy new constraints without full retraining. However, specificity-aware generation is difficult: repelling samples from a negative reference distribution can also erode the positive distribution where the two overlap. The key challenge is to suppress negative mass while minimally distorting the positive distribution. We address this problem by formulating specificity-aware steering as a target-design problem and deriving a target distribution from an overlap-based objective. The resulting target keeps the desired reference distribution only in regions where it is sufficiently preferred over the undesired reference distribution, giving a likelihood-ratio interpretation of specificity. To sample from the corresponding time-dependent target path, we develop a Sequential Monte Carlo sampler with a variance-minimized local proposal. We further introduce a practical fixed-noise optimization procedure with the Jacobian--vector products with the desired and undesired score fields. Experiments on synthetic task, class-contrastive generation, text-to-image tasks and peptide-MHC (p-MHC) binder show that the proposed method suppresses undesired regions more effectively, reduces mode shift, and improves sampling stability by decreasing the SMC weight collapse compared with negative-guidance baselines. Code is available at: https://github.com/WangLuran/Specificity-Aware-Diffusion-Steering

</details>


### [222] [NEUROTOKEN: Joint Source and Directional AAD with Envelope Decoding via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00397)
*Ali Alavi,Donald S. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NEUROTOKEN框架，用条件流匹配建模"给定EEG的被注意语音包络的条件似然"，实现高鲁棒性的听觉注意解码（AAD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鸡尾酒会问题（噪声环境中听者关注哪位说话人）是下一代助听器与脑机接口的关键，但现有AAD研究割裂：方向性AAD只能分左右、回归式源AAD依赖单个噪声大的Pearson相关（1-5秒窗口下不稳定）、包络重构缺乏原生AAD判据。作者认为正确对象应是"给定EEG下被注意包络的条件似然"，而非任何单一统计量。

Method: NEUROTOKEN：单一网络，三个头共享一个EEG前端；其中条件流匹配头ATTUNEFLOW通过积分速度残差似然比对候选流打分。推理期集成两种无需额外代价的方案：QUADTRACK（四种互补统计量）与ENV-FLOW（z归一化的QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW），以吸收各统计量的失效模式。同时在严格试次互斥（trial-disjoint）协议下复现经典方向AAD结果。

Result: 在KU Leuven、DTU、NJU三个数据集5秒窗口下，ATTUNEFLOW将分段级源AAD相对最强非生成基线提升9%-16%，并将跨被试方差缩小约3倍；试次级融合在两个数据集上超过93%。严格试次互斥协议下，经典95-97%的方向AAD准确率下降17%-45%，揭示了真实上限。

Conclusion: 以条件似然为核心、结合流匹配与多统计量集成，是更稳健且可落地的AAD范式；同时呼吁用严格协议重新评估AAD的真实性能天花板。

Abstract: Identifying which speaker a listener is attending to in a noisy room -- the cocktail-party problem -- is the missing ingredient for next-generation hearing aids and brain-computer interfaces: it tells the device whose voice to amplify. Auditory attention decoding (AAD) reads this answer from EEG, but the literature splits into disconnected pieces: directional-AAD classifies side but does not map side to stream; regression-based source-AAD ranks candidate streams by a single Pearson correlation that is intrinsically noisy at the 1-5 s windows real devices need; and envelope reconstruction has no native AAD rule. We argue the right object is not any single statistic but the conditional likelihood of the attended envelope given EEG, and we make this practical with NEUROTOKEN: a single network whose three heads share one EEG front-end, with a conditional flow-matching head (ATTUNEFLOW) that scores candidates by an integrated velocity-residual likelihood ratio. Two inference-time ensembles -- QUADTRACK (four complementary statistics) and ENV-FLOW (z-normalised QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW) -- absorb per-statistic failure modes for free. On KU Leuven, DTU, and NJU at 5 s, ATTUNEFLOW lifts per-segment source-AAD by 9%-16% over the strongest non-generative baseline and shrinks across-subject variance by ~3x; trial-level fusion exceeds 93% on two of three datasets. In parallel reproductions we show that canonical 95-97% direction-AAD numbers collapse by 17%-45% under a strict trial-disjoint protocol, clarifying both the true ceiling and why a likelihood-based formulation is needed.

</details>


### [223] [WIPSNet: Deep Learning for Paediatric Wheeze Detection from Overnight Impedance Pneumography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00398)
*Felix Oury,Harley Day,Karina Mayoral,Ville-Pekka Seppä,Sejal Saglani,Reiko J. Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WIPSNet，一种基于3D ResNet的模型，在整夜阻抗呼吸描记信号的连续小波变换尺度图上进行喘鸣分类，AUC达0.783，优于EVI、Mamba及睡眠分期架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整夜阻抗呼吸描记（IP）用于监测儿童呼吸健康，但现有临床指标EVI将整段记录压缩为单一标量，夜间喘鸣分类AUC仅0.633，信息损失大、性能有限，亟需能保留多尺度时频结构的建模方法。

Method: 提出WIPSNet，将整夜IP信号做连续小波变换得到尺度图并堆叠为三维体数据，用3D ResNet进行学习；在15名患者（60个夜晚、281小时）队列上与EVI、状态空间模型Mamba及两种现代睡眠分期架构对比，并分析时间上下文深度的影响。

Result: WIPSNet取得AUC 0.783±0.026，优于EVI、Mamba和两种睡眠分期架构；性能在约32分钟时间上下文的体积深度处达到峰值。

Conclusion: 结构化的时频表示结合3D卷积架构，能有效从长时、不规则的生理时间序列中学习；多尺度时间聚合对建模夜间呼吸动力学很重要。

Abstract: Overnight impedance pneumography (IP) is used to monitor paediatric respiratory health. Its current clinical readout, the Expiratory Variability Index (EVI), compresses each IP recording into a single scalar and achieves an AUC of 0.633 for night-level wheeze classification. We introduce Wheeze Impedance Pneumography Scalogram Network (WIPSNet), a 3D ResNet operating on stacked continuous wavelet transform scalograms of overnight IP signals. On a 15-patient cohort (60 nights, 281 hours), WIPSNet achieves an AUC of $0.783 \pm 0.026$, outperforming EVI, a state-space model (Mamba), and two modern sleep-staging architectures. Performance peaks at a volumetric depth corresponding to 32 minutes of temporal context, suggesting that multi-scale temporal aggregation is important for modelling nocturnal respiratory dynamics. Overall, these results indicate that structured time-frequency representations combined with 3D convolutional architectures provide an effective approach for learning from long, irregular physiological time series.

</details>


### [224] [Metacognitive Reasoning in Energy Based Models using Instance Based Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00399)
*Tailia Malloy,Prateek Kumar Rajput,Serge Lionel Nikiema,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了MERITED框架，将基于实例的学习理论（IBLT）与能量模型（EBM）结合，实现LLM推理中的元认知计算资源动态分配，并开源了一个191M参数的推理EBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前依赖LLM的AI系统无法在给出输出前估计不确定性，也无法动态分配计算资源，导致类似人类元认知的推理过程难以实现。EBM虽能建模不确定性和动态分配算力，但如何基于不确定性决定算力分配这一元认知任务尚未被直接解决。

Method: 论文将IBLT（一种从经验中建模类人决策的方法）与EBM结合，提出MERITED框架。该框架在EBM中控制投入的计算量，使其能基于不确定性对推理努力进行元认知控制，同时保持计算效率。具体贡献包括：训练并开放共享一个191M参数的推理EBM，以及实现基于IBL模型的MERITED动态算力分配框架。

Result: 论文产出了两个主要成果：一是训练并开源了一个191M参数的推理能量模型；二是实现了MERITED框架，可利用IBL模型进行动态计算资源分配。原文未提供定量实验结果。

Conclusion: MERITED框架表明，将IBLT与EBM结合可在保持计算效率的同时，让LLM系统具备基于不确定性的元认知推理控制能力，为AI系统的元认知计算分配提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Metacognition involves reasoning about cognitive processes themselves. An example is in resource allocation where we choose how much time and effort to put into a reasoning task before we begin based on our confidence. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems that rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot estimate their uncertainty about an output without first responding, and cannot dynamically allocate resources to producing an output, making this type of metacognitive process difficult. A recently proposed alternative to classic transformer architectures that addresses these two concerns is the Energy Based Model (EBM) which allows for interpretable uncertainty modeling and dynamic allocation of compute resources. While EBMs can allow for control of these two processes, the actual metacognitive task of determining compute allocation based on uncertainty is not directly addressed. Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) provides an approach to modeling human-like decisions from experience that has previously been applied to predicting human metacognitive reasoning. In this paper we introduce a framework for MEtacognitive Reasoning with Instance-based Learning Theory and Energy Dynamics (MERITED). Grounded in IBLT, this framework allows for control of the computational effort allocated in an EBM to allow for metacognitive control over reasoning effort based on uncertainty while remaining computationally efficient. This work has two main contributions, the training and open weight sharing of a 191M parameter reasoning EBM, and an implementation of the MERITED framework for dynamic compute allocation using an IBL model.

</details>


### [225] [Representation Transitions Reveal Emerging Safety Risks in Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00400)
*Haoyu Wang,Wei Zhao,Yedi Zhang,Christopher M. Poskitt,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DART 运行时框架，通过去噪后的表示转移信号检测并归因多轮智能体攻击，将攻击成功率从 84% 降至 25%（MT-AgentRisk）和 97% 降至 52%（ASEval）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮攻击可将单个合规动作组合成有害结果，使按孤立动作或状态评估的防御失效。作者希望找到一种能捕捉行为累积过程的可检测内部信号，并解决良性表示漂移对安全方向判别的干扰。

Method: 发现有害行为在智能体内部表示中表现为跨上下文更新的累积表示转移，且触发上下文可由同一信号识别。提出对安全方向进行去噪：将良性流量锚定在零并移除其主要变化方向。基于此构建 DART 框架，在运行时检测与归因表示偏移并进行针对性提醒干预，无需额外模型且无运行时训练成本。

Result: 在六个模型和两个多轮基准上，DART 将 MT-AgentRisk 攻击成功率从 84% 降至 25%（平均误报率 12%，捕获全部攻击），将 ASEval 从 97% 降至 52%（良性非拒答代价 8% 和 0%）。在 MT-AgentRisk 上六个模型均优于 SOTA 防御 ToolShield（其仅达 55%）。去噪至关重要：ASEval 上未去噪监控仅捕获 7%–40% 攻击，去噪后达 60%–85%。同一监控器无需修改即可覆盖单轮间接注入，每步仅增加 0.14–0.56 秒开销。

Conclusion: 多轮攻击在智能体表示中留下可检测的累积转移签名，去噪后的表示监控是一种轻量、有效的运行时防御，可与计算密集型推测式防御互补。

Abstract: Multi-turn attacks on agentic systems can compose individually permissible actions into harmful outcomes, challenging defenses that assess actions or states in isolation. We show that such attacks leave a detectable signature in the agent's internal representations: harmful behavior emerges as an accumulated representation transition across context updates, whose triggering context can be identified from the same signal. We further find that naive aggregation is confounded by benign representation drift, as a contrastive safety direction need not assign zero to benign transitions. We address this by denoising the direction, anchoring benign traffic at zero and removing its leading variation directions, with no runtime cost.
  These findings motivate DART, a runtime framework that detects and attributes representation shifts and intervenes with targeted reminders. Across six models and two multi-turn benchmarks, DART reduces attack success from 84% to 25% on MT-AgentRisk, catching every attack at a mean false-alarm rate of 12%, and from 97% to 52% on ASEval, at costs in benign non-refusal of 8% and 0%, respectively. On MT-AgentRisk, it outperforms ToolShield, the state-of-the-art multi-turn defense, on all six models: under the same protocol, ToolShield reaches only 55%. Denoising is critical: on ASEval, the undenoised monitor catches only 7%-40% of attacks, while the denoised monitor catches 60%-85%. The same monitor covers single-turn indirect injection without modification and adds only 0.14-0.56 s overhead per monitored step without requiring an auxiliary model, making it a lightweight complement to computation-heavy speculative defenses.

</details>


### [226] [The Conflict Between Logic and Memory: Learning Higher-Order Interactions in Shallow MLPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00403)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究单隐层MLP在拟合训练样本的同时能否学到生成标签的规则，用合成任务控制交互阶数与冗余输入，揭示优化器与冗余权重学习对高阶规则实现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络可以拟合训练样本却学不到生成标签的真实规则，这一"拟合与规则恢复"的分离现象尚缺乏对浅层网络在不同交互阶数下的系统刻画，尤其是优化器选择与冗余输入学习如何影响高阶规则的问题。

Method: 构造纯奇偶校验等合成任务，控制交互阶数与独立冗余输入的存在。分析纯奇偶任务的基准性质：无低阶预测边际、存在精确贝叶斯后验、可在干净隐变量上用宽度k的ReLU网络表示。进行阶数2-4的匹配扫描，比较SGD、Adam、Muon；在混合阶任务中冻结连接冗余输入的隐层权重，并在测试时移除这些输入；同时进行偏置干预以考察目标对称性与浅层ReLU表示的关系。

Result: 阶数2时三种优化器峰值测试准确率均达100%；阶数3时SGD、Adam、Muon分别为96.25%、50.87%、76.82%；阶数4时Muon达99.21%。混合阶任务中仅冻结连接独立冗余输入的隐层权重，使AdamW第10轮准确率从44.73%升至95.07%；仅在测试时移除相同输入则升至48.38%。双信号主导的紧凑设置下SGD与Muon能学到阶数5至8，阶数9至11时SGD峰值准确率更高。

Conclusion: 优化过程与冗余权重学习共同约束浅层网络所能实现的高阶规则；冗余权重的学习会通过改变训练动力学而影响最终结果，其作用超出对预测的直接影响，偏置干预揭示了目标对称性与浅层ReLU表示之间的联系。

Abstract: A network can fit its training examples while failing to recover the rule that generated their labels. We examine this separation in single-hidden-layer multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), using synthetic tasks that control interaction order and the presence of nuisance inputs. We establish elementary benchmark properties: pure parity contains no predictive lower-order marginals, admits an exact Bayes posterior, and can be represented on clean latent inputs by a width-$k$ ReLU network. Experiments then identify distinct optimization outcomes. In a matched order-2--4 sweep, SGD, Adam, and Muon all reach 100\% peak test accuracy at order two; at order three they reach 96.25\%, 50.87\%, and 76.82\%, respectively, while Muon reaches 99.21\% at order four. In a separate mixed-order task, freezing only the first-layer weights connected to independent nuisance inputs raises AdamW's epoch-10 accuracy from 44.73\% to 95.07\%. Removing the same inputs only at test time raises it to 48.38\%. Thus, nuisance-weight learning changes the training outcome beyond its immediate effect on prediction. Bias interventions expose a connection between target symmetry and shallow ReLU representations. In a compact signal-only regime, both SGD and Muon learn orders five through eight, with higher SGD peak accuracy at orders nine through eleven. Together, the results show how optimization and nuisance learning constrain the higher-order rules realized by a shallow network.

</details>


### [227] [RACE: Residual-Aware Test-Time Adaptation for Neighbor-Rich Time-Series Foundation Model Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00405)
*Hao-Nan Shi,Tong Wu,Chen-Cong Sun,Yuan Jiang,Han-Jia Ye,De-Chuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RACE框架，在测试阶段利用同域邻居残差证据对时间序列基础模型（TSFM）的预测进行校正，无需修改骨干网络，在CGM与Web/云负载等"邻居丰富"预测场景中全面优于冻结基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSFM按单序列推理，难以利用"邻居丰富"场景中相关但非同质的历史序列（如CGM个体差异、Web/云负载的非平稳与突发）。已有方法要么针对目标域微调TSFM（成本高、跨骨干迁移性差），要么直接拼接检索序列却不验证其是否有助预测。核心难点在于邻居残差证据相互冲突，且残差模式随TSFM骨干与任务变化。

Method: 将问题形式化为"测试时邻域缩放"（test-time neighborhood scaling），在冻结骨干上利用同域邻居证据。提出RACE两阶段框架：先检索与查询兼容的邻居，将其残差对齐到查询尺度并聚合一致证据，形成免训练的RACE-TF校正；完整RACE再用轻量、领域专用的Gate判断何时应用校正有益，并提供可跨TSFM骨干复用的训练流程。

Result: 在四个TSFM骨干上，RACE在两大主领域的全部三项域级聚合指标上均有提升，在高误差查询上增益最大；在一个骨干上训练的Gate可无需适配迁移到其他骨干，最终流程在72项跨骨干指标对比中全部优于匹配的冻结基线。

Conclusion: 通过残差感知的邻居证据校正，可在不修改骨干的前提下提升TSFM在邻居丰富场景的预测精度，且校正门控具备良好的跨骨干可迁移性，为TSFM的测试时适配提供了低成本、可复用的方案。

Abstract: Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) perform strongly across forecasting tasks, but their per-series inference is ill-suited to neighbor-rich forecasting, where each query has access to related but nonidentical historical series. Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) and Web/cloud workloads exemplify this setting: CGM trajectories share physiological patterns but vary across individuals, devices, and conditions, while Web/cloud workloads combine common operating regimes with non-stationarity, heavy tails, and bursts. These histories share useful structure, yet neighbors are not equally relevant. Existing methods either fine-tune TSFMs for each target domain, incurring additional costs and offering limited transferability across backbones, or append retrieved series without verifying whether they support the current forecast. The key challenges are conflicting residual evidence from neighboring series and residual patterns that vary across TSFMs and forecasting tasks. We formulate test-time neighborhood scaling: using same-domain neighbor evidence without modifying the backbone. We propose RACE (Residual-Aware Correction of Forecasting Errors), a two-stage framework for using historical neighbors. We first retrieve query-compatible neighbors, align their residuals to the query scale, and aggregate coherent evidence into the training-free RACE-TF correction. Full RACE then uses a lightweight, domain-specific Gate to determine when applying the correction is beneficial, with a reusable training workflow across TSFM backbones. Across four TSFMs, RACE improves all three domain-aggregate metrics on both primary domains, with the largest gains on high-error queries. Within each domain, a Gate trained on one TSFM transfers to other backbones without adaptation, and the resulting pipeline improves all 72 cross-backbone metric comparisons over the matched frozen targets.

</details>


### [228] [VANDAM: Viewing a nucleotide sequence with DNA molecular priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00411)
*Jeremy Levy,Ariel Larey,Yury Nahshan,Raizy Kellerman,Elay Dahan,Amit Bleiweiss,Guy Leib,Omri Nayshool,Dan Ofer,Tal Zinger,Dan Dominissini,Gideon Rechavi,Marissa Wirth,Simon Lee,Dung Hoang,Noam D. Beckmann,Shane O'Connell,Nicole Bussola,Alexander W. Charney,Yoli Shavit,Nati Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VANDAM 通过引入DNA分子先验（regional分子性质预测与局部特征注入）增强基因组基础模型的训练，在四类架构、九项基因组任务上稳定提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因组基础模型（GFMs）将DNA视为字符串并用掩码token预测预训练，未显式建模对生物功能至关重要的生化、结构与物理性质。作者认为，许多分子性质本可由序列借助既有生物物理模型估计，其价值在于为训练目标引入可显式利用的先验，而非作为独立模态。

Method: 提出 VANDAM 框架，在GFM训练中扩展DNA分子先验：自监督阶段，从池化表示中预测区域分子性质；当存在功能标签且能奖励保留分子先验时，在输入端额外注入局部特征。该方法与基于token的目标互补。

Result: 在四类架构家族和九项留出基因组任务上，VANDAM 一致提升了下游性能；探测实验表明，分子先验的使用可泛化到其他未见过的分子性质。

Conclusion: 显式引入分子先验可有效补充字符串式token目标，提升基因组基础模型的泛化能力与下游表现，为GFM预训练提供新的先验利用范式。

Abstract: Contemporary Genomic Foundation Models (GFMs) rely on a DNA-as-a-string paradigm that employs masked token prediction objectives for pretraining. However, this abstraction does not explicitly model the biochemical, structural, and physical properties essential to biological function. Many molecular properties can be estimated from sequence using established biophysical models, so their utility lies not in providing an independent modality, but in introducing priors that training objectives can explicitly exploit. We introduce VANDAM, a framework that extends the training of GFMs with DNA molecular priors. In self-supervised training, VANDAM predicts regional molecular properties from pooled representations. When functional labels are available and can reward retaining molecular priors, local features are additionally injected at the input. VANDAM consistently improves downstream performance across four architecture families and nine held-out genomic tasks by complementing token-based objectives. Probing experiments further demonstrate that the use of molecular priors generalizes to other unseen molecular properties.

</details>


### [229] [EchoPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Pruning via Virtual Context Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00412)
*Jiawei Lin,Saibo Geng,Thomas Bourgeat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EchoPress 是一种免训练的 KV cache 剪枝方法，通过复用 prefill 阶段已有的 query/key 来近似 KVzip 的重构重要性分数，仅重构首个 chunk 以校准后续上下文的重要性，在保持任务精度的同时大幅降低压缩开销与前填充时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中 KV cache 占用大量内存，KVzip 通过逐块重构上下文来估计 key-value 对的重要性，压缩质量好但需要额外前向传播；已有的学习式近似方法虽能降低成本，却需要针对特定模型进行训练，通用性差且部署成本高。

Method: 作者首先分析 KVzip 如何识别重要缓存信息，发现其重构分数可以用 prefill 阶段已经计算出的信息来近似。据此提出 EchoPress：一种免训练方法，直接利用标准 prefill 中的 query 和 key 近似重构注意力；对每个请求仅重构第一个 chunk，用来校准剩余上下文的重要性分数，从而避免逐块重构的额外开销。

Result: 在 LongBench 和 RULER 基准上，使用 Qwen3-8B 与 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 进行实验，50% 到 90% 的驱逐率下 EchoPress 的任务准确率与 KVzip 相当，同时将压缩开销降低 1.7–19.6 倍，总 prefill 时间最多降低 2.9 倍。

Conclusion: EchoPress 表明 KVzip 的重构分数可在无需训练的情况下由 prefill 阶段已有信息高效近似，仅用首个 chunk 校准即可达到同等压缩质量，为长上下文推理提供了免训练、低开销的 KV cache 剪枝方案。

Abstract: KV cache pruning reduces long-context inference memory usage by evicting less important key-value pairs. KVzip estimates importance through context reconstruction: prompting a model to repeat the context chunk by chunk. This achieves strong compression quality at the cost of additional forward passes. Learned approximations reduce this cost but require model-specific training. We analyze how KVzip identifies important cached information and show how to approximate its reconstruction scores using information already computed during prefill. These findings motivate EchoPress, a training-free method that approximates reconstruction attention using queries and keys from standard prefill. For each request, it reconstructs only the first chunk to calibrate importance scores for the remaining context. Experiments on LongBench and RULER with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct show that EchoPress matches KVzip in task accuracy across eviction ratios from 50% to 90%, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 1.7-19.6 and total prefill time by a factor of up to 2.9. Code is available at https://github.com/ljwljwljwljw/kvpress/tree/echo-press.

</details>


### [230] [Do Better Scores Mean Better Physics? Physics-Grounded Explanations for Sim2Real Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00415)
*Somyajit Chakraborty,Xizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究机器学习代理模型在流体仿真中的评估问题：较低的预测误差不一定对应较低的物理相关流动统计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型虽能加速物理仿真，但仅以预测误差衡量性能可能误导；作者关注预测精度是否真能反映物理流动统计量的准确性。

Method: 以NACA4418翼型绕流为对象，结合CFD仿真与粒子图像测速实验数据；采用均值保持的输入干预（去除特定区域速度脉动）并对比四种神经算子；另用CNO与U-Net对比速度场误差与双分量脉动能量误差；还设计输出衰减压力测试。

Result: 四种神经算子中，去除最活跃10%有效观测单元脉动比等面积随机去除带来更大预测变化；但掩膜未匹配脉动能量，故仅衡量敏感性。CNO在速度场误差更低的同时，双分量脉动能量误差显著高于参照模型；输出衰减测试也显示基准误差与全域脉动能量不一致。

Conclusion: 单一基准结果提示应在聚合预测分数之外报告补充性物理诊断指标；这些发现并不构成反事实物理正确性的证据。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates accelerate physical simulation, but lower prediction error need not coincide with lower error in physically relevant flow statistics. We examine this question for flow around a NACA4418 airfoil using paired computational-fluid-dynamics simulations and experimental particle-image-velocimetry measurements. A mean-preserving input intervention removes velocity fluctuations from selected regions of observed flow histories. Across four neural operators, removing fluctuations from the most energetic 10% of valid observed cells changes forecasts more than equal-area random removal. Because the masks are not matched for removed fluctuation energy, this contrast measures sensitivity, not independent evidence of physical importance. Separately, a CNO has lower velocity-field error but substantially higher two-component fluctuation-energy error than the reference on both analysis subsets. An output attenuation stress test also demonstrates disagreement between benchmark errors and domain-summed fluctuation energy. These single-benchmark results motivate reporting complementary physical diagnostics alongside aggregate prediction scores; they do not establish counterfactual physical correctness.

</details>


### [231] [Source Identification Is Not Fitness Testing: Measuring the Limits of Synthetic-Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00417)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在金融风险文本上，模型生成内容的来源归因在原文上准确率高达98.7%，但改写后骤降至53.1%、风格重写后仅29.0%；而用来源信息或参考模型打分两种方式筛选生成样本进行递归训练，未发现稳定的退化差异，说明"识别数据来源"与"识别数据是否有用"是两个独立问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型生成数据上反复训练会导致后续模型性能退化（模型自噬/递归退化），一种可能的应对思路是利用数据来源信息（provenance）来决定复用哪些生成样本。但这一思路成立需要两个前提：来源能被可靠恢复，且来源信息确实能帮助挑出更好的训练数据。论文正是要检验这两个前提是否成立。

Method: 使用金融风险领域文本开展两组实验。第一组为生成器归因与生成/人类判别：在原始生成段落上做来源识别，并在改写（paraphrase）和风格重写（style rewriting）后重复测试；同时与人类对照集做生成-人类检测。第二组为三轮"生成-再训练"循环，比较两种生成样本筛选规则：一种利用来源信息，另一种使用独立参考模型给出的分数，并观察所产生模型在退化上的差异。

Result: 生成器归因在原始段落上准确率为98.7%，改写后降至53.1%，风格重写后仅29.0%；生成文本与人类的区分在测试对照集上仍接近完美。两种筛选规则选出的样本不同，但计划中的比较未能检测到所产生模型退化上的稳定差异。

Conclusion: 识别数据来源与识别哪些数据对训练有用是两个不同的问题。实验将来源身份、面向准则的样本选择与递归训练结果分离开来：无论是来源分数，还是所测试的面向准则的替代指标，都不足以决定未来的递归行为。

Abstract: Repeated training on model-generated data can degrade later models. One possible response is to use provenance when deciding which generated examples to reuse. We test both how reliably that provenance can be recovered and whether it helps identify better training data. Using financial-risk text, we first identify the source of generated passages and then repeat the test after rewriting them. Generator attribution is 98.7% accurate on the original passages but falls to 53.1% after paraphrasing and 29.0% after style rewriting. Generated-versus-human detection remains close to perfect against the tested human comparison set. We then compare two ways of selecting generated examples over three rounds of generation and retraining. One uses source information. The other uses a score from a separate reference model. The two rules select different examples, but the planned comparison does not detect a stable difference in the degradation of the resulting models. The results show that identifying where data came from and identifying which data are useful for training are separate problems. The experiment therefore separates source identity, criterion-facing selection, and recursive training outcome: neither the provenance score nor the tested criterion-facing proxy is established as sufficient for future recursive behaviour.

</details>


### [232] [CommunityKV: Efficient Long-Context Decoding via Graph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00418)
*Joe McKenna,Anastasios Alexandridis,Nathan Susanj,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommunityKV 将稀疏注意力建模为社区发现问题，在无需训练的情况下实现长上下文高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 扩展到长上下文时受限于自注意力的二次复杂度和键值缓存随长度线性增长的内存传输开销。现有稀疏注意力方法要么依赖大规模训练，要么在免训练场景下依赖语义粗糙的启发式规则或计算昂贵的聚类，且难以在解码过程中高效更新。

Method: 提出 CommunityKV 框架，将稀疏注意力形式化为社区检测问题。它利用标准预填充阶段已计算出的 QK^T 分数构建 token 图，并将图划分为社区，从而检索语义连贯的 token 组。同时设计局部更新规则，以常数时间将新生成 token 分配到已有社区，使整个流式解码过程可持续进行稀疏检索而无需全局重新划分。

Result: 在 Qwen3 和 Llama-3.1 模型上于三个长上下文基准进行评测。每个查询头使用一个图时，端到端生成吞吐量最高可达稠密注意力的 1.25 倍；采用查询组图聚合时最高可达 1.71 倍，且精度相当。

Conclusion: CommunityKV 通过在预填充阶段复用已有注意力分数并以常数时间增量维护社区结构，为长上下文推理提供了一种免训练、高效且可流式更新的稀疏注意力方案，在保持精度的同时显著提升生成吞吐量。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to long contexts is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention and the linear growth of key-value cache memory transfer. Sparse attention mitigates this by retrieving only relevant tokens, but current approaches either require large-scale training or, within the training-free regime, rely on semantically coarse heuristics or expensive clustering that is difficult to update efficiently during decoding. We introduce CommunityKV, a framework that formulates sparse attention as a community detection problem. CommunityKV constructs a token graph from the $QK^T$ scores already computed during standard prefill, and partitions the graph into communities to enable retrieval of semantically coherent token groups. A local update rule assigns newly generated tokens to communities in constant time, enabling sparse retrieval throughout streaming decoding without global re-partitioning. We evaluate CommunityKV on Qwen3 and Llama-3.1 models across three long-context benchmarks. With one graph per query head, CommunityKV delivers up to $1.25\times$ the end-to-end generation throughput of dense attention, while query-group graph aggregation yields up to $1.71\times$ with comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [233] [The Life Cycle of a Massive Activation: Stochastic Birth, Weight-Decay-Driven Growth, and Competitive Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00423)
*S. Aaron McClendon,Jorge Gallego-Feliciano,Antonios Saravanos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer中"大规模激活"（massive activations）的生命周期，发现权重衰减（weight decay）因果性地调控其全局激活尺度，并建立了AdamW预条件增长与权重衰减相平衡的理论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模激活（幅度远超普通激活的残差流坐标）与注意力汇（attention sinks）相关，但其规模在训练过程中如何被调控尚不完全清楚。作者希望揭示这些激活的产生、增长与固化机制，并找到调控其幅度的训练时杠杆。

Method: 结合训练轨迹分析与受控干预实验，追踪大规模激活的出现、增长与固化过程；通过消融实验考察梯度衰减与哪些量相关；进行受控续训，去除或保留权重衰减；提出一个平衡模型，用AdamW预条件增长对抗权重衰减；扫描衰减系数λ并测量优化器动态。

Result: 承载"汇"的通道随随机种子变化，但在每次运行中早期即稳定；长期训练后周边通道被侵蚀，汇集中于少数冗余载体上。梯度衰减遵循汇token的集体均方根幅度而非单通道。去除权重衰减会使激活尺度持续上升，保留则导致下降（即使学习率不变）。峰值时间随λ近似对数线性变化，峰值幅度近似按λ^{-1/2}缩放。优化器测量显示预条件能维持大通道群体以对抗衰减。

Conclusion: 研究将大规模激活的观测生命周期与调控尺度的训练动态联系起来，确立了权重衰减作为激活幅度的训练时调控手段，并提供了与平衡模型一致的定量验证。

Abstract: Massive activations, residual-stream coordinates with magnitudes far larger than typical activations, are associated with attention sinks in transformers, but how their scale is regulated during training remains incompletely understood. Combining training-trajectory analyses and controlled interventions, we trace their emergence, growth, and consolidation. Sink-carrying channels vary across random seeds but stabilize early within each run. Over longer training, surrounding channels erode and the sink concentrates onto a few redundant carriers. Across ablations, gradient attenuation follows the sink token's collective root-mean-square magnitude rather than any single channel, making collective scale central to understanding their effects. Our central result is that weight decay causally controls the turnover of global activation scale. In controlled continuations, removing decay near the peak allows this scale to keep rising, whereas retaining it produces decline even at constant learning rate. We develop a balance model for the rise and peak of massive-activation magnitude, in which AdamW-preconditioned growth opposes weight decay. Sweeping the decay coefficient $λ$ shifts peak timing approximately log-linearly and yields peak magnitudes scaling approximately as $λ^{-1/2}$, consistent with this balance. Optimizer measurements further show that preconditioning sustains the large-channel cohort against decay even when raw maintaining forces are too small to do so. Together, these findings connect the observed life cycle to scale-regulating training dynamics and establish weight decay as a training-time lever on activation magnitude.

</details>


### [234] [IrekoGPT: Turning Structured Pruning into Post-Hoc Slimmable LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00426)
*Pietro Moriello,Pietro Buzzega,Angelo Porrello,Simone Calderara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IrekoGPT：一种事后方法，将预训练LLM转换为可在推理时调整宽度的slimmable模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM部署需要灵活性，而传统压缩方法如SliceGPT会剪枝投影矩阵，导致单一模型无法支持多种宽度。作者希望保留投影矩阵以获得嵌套子网络，实现推理时的可变宽度。

Method: 基于SliceGPT，保留投影矩阵不剪枝以暴露不同宽度的嵌套子网络；对每层在多个压缩比下进行校准以提升鲁棒性；通过无梯度的岭回归修正下游线性层。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen模型上，初步结果显示优于基于PCA的朴素slimming方法，在高压缩率下提升最大。

Conclusion: IrekoGPT可有效将预训练LLM转为slimmable模型，代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce IrekoGPT, a post-hoc method for converting pretrained LLMs into slimmable models whose width can be adjusted at inference time. Building on SliceGPT, we retain its projection matrices without pruning them, allowing a single model to expose nested subnetworks at different widths. We improve robustness by calibrating each layer across multiple compression ratios, and correct downstream linear layers through gradient-free ridge regression. Across Llama and Qwen models, preliminary results show improvements over naive PCA-based slimming, with the largest gains at high compression. Code is available at https://github.com/aimagelab/IrekoGPT

</details>


### [235] [XOR-Trellis: Ultra-Low-Complexity Dequantization and Curvature-Aware Hadamard-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00432)
*Xiaofan Que,Nir Elkayam,Spandan Pyakurel,Shuokai Pan,Dibakar Gope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对大语言模型权重的超低位宽压缩，提出低成本网格去量化器与曲率感知的网格路径优化目标，实现了高质量、可高效并行重构且无需Hadamard变换的网格编码量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格编码量化可在超低位宽下压缩LLM权重且无需指数级大的码本，但实际部署面临两大挑战：一是压缩权重的重构需具备足够并行吞吐，否则去量化会成为推理瓶颈；二是需在不依赖昂贵非相干变换（如Hadamard变换）的情况下保持量化精度。

Method: 提出两项互补技术：(1) 超低复杂度网格去量化器，采用结构化、硬件友好的状态到数值映射，同时保留网格搜索所需的多样重构选择；(2) 将离散网格路径优化重新表述为曲率感知目标，直接在原始坐标空间中反映模型敏感度，避免Hadamard类非相干处理。

Result: 两项技术结合使得超低位宽网格量化在保持高质量的同时，实现低成本、高并行的运行时重构，且不依赖Hadamard基非相干处理。

Conclusion: 该工作为LLM权重的超低位宽网格编码量化提供了实用部署方案，兼顾重构吞吐与量化精度，去除了对昂贵非相干变换的依赖。

Abstract: Trellis-coded quantization enables high-dimensional compression of large language model (LLM) weights at ultra-low bit widths without the exponentially large codebooks required by conventional vector quantization. Practical deployment, however, presents two challenges: reconstructing compressed weights at sufficient parallel throughput to avoid making dequantization an inference bottleneck, and maintaining quantization accuracy without costly incoherence transformations. We address these challenges with two complementary techniques. First, we introduce an ultra-low-complexity trellis dequantizer that uses a structured, hardware-efficient state-to-value mapping while preserving diverse reconstruction choices for trellis search. Second, we reformulate discrete trellis path optimization with a curvature-aware objective that reflects model sensitivity directly in the original coordinate space. Together, these techniques enable high-quality ultra-low-bit trellis quantization with inexpensive, highly parallel runtime reconstruction and without relying on Hadamard-based incoherence processing.

</details>


### [236] [Every Batch Is Its Own Validation Set: Leave-One-Out Gradient Matching for Online Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00436)
*Hongyu Chen,Xinyi Luo,Ming Zhao,Lin Tang,Zihan Xu,Jing Li,Yuxuan Wang,Haoran Deng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示在线批次选择中样本内梯度匹配为何难以击败全批次训练，并提出基于留一目标与梯度信噪比的LOOM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线批次选择本应在每个候选批次中挑选最有用的样本，但基于梯度匹配的选择器虽无需验证集，却很少能超越全批次训练，作者旨在解释这一现象并提供理论修正。

Method: 作者指出样本内梯度匹配存在"协方差惩罚"：每个样本的自身梯度噪声被计入目标对角线，导致选择偏向噪声样本。为此提出留一目标，去掉对角线后成为种群梯度误差的无偏估计，其最优解按样本梯度信噪比（SNR）加权。基于此构建LOOM：在普通反向传播中按Adam预条件子度量计算Gram矩阵，贪心选择加权子集（具有(1-e^{-γ})近似保证），且无需留出数据。

Result: 在四个微调任务、七个1.5B至8B参数骨干上验证。LOOM在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上分别较全批次训练提升2.3和2.4分；超过所有样本内梯度匹配器2.4分，超过验证集引导的GREATS和OPUS 1.6-2.0分；对注入标签噪声的选择率不到其基础率的五分之一。给出了半批次优于全批次的精确条件。

Conclusion: 去除对角线使梯度匹配目标无偏，按梯度SNR加权可使部分批次产生比全批次更低误差的更新，LOOM在多种模型与任务上验证了该理论的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: Online batch selection fine-tunes a language model on the most useful part of each candidate batch. Selectors that match the gradient of the candidate batch are attractive because they need no held-out data, yet they rarely beat training on the whole batch. We show why. In-sample gradient matching uses every example as part of its own target, so its objective credits each example with its own gradient noise. This is the covariance penalty that makes training error optimistic, now sitting on the diagonal of the gradient Gram matrix: it steers selection toward the noisiest examples and makes the full batch the best solution the objective can reach. The fix costs nothing. For each example, the other candidates form an independent sample of the data distribution, so removing the diagonal turns the matching objective into an unbiased estimate of the update's error with respect to the population gradient. The minimizer of this leave-one-out objective weights examples by their gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and whenever per-example SNR is heterogeneous enough, half of a batch yields a lower-error update than the whole batch; we give the exact condition. We build \method{} on this principle. It computes the Gram matrix in the metric of the Adam preconditioner during the ordinary backward pass, selects a weighted subset greedily with a $(1-e^{-γ})$ guarantee, and uses no held-out data. Across four fine-tuning tasks and seven backbones from 1.5B to 8B parameters, LOOM improves on full-batch training by 2.3 and 2.4 points on Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, exceeds every in-sample gradient matcher by 2.4 points and the validation-guided GREATS and OPUS by 1.6--2.0, and selects injected label noise at under a fifth of its base rate.

</details>


### [237] [One pool, many targets: a conservation layer and what archival data can identify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00445)
*Zahra Khodagholi,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可微的守恒标量平衡层，用于刻画有限向导负载RISC池中配对向导-转录本分数的守恒约束，并对其检索近似、秩不变性及剂量/跨语境实验进行审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立解释配对向导-转录本分数作为占用率时，无法保证有限向导负载RISC池的守恒；需要检验这种耦合是否带来额外预测价值。

Method: 构建一个可微标量平衡层：单一守恒方程，具有唯一正根与精确隐式梯度；推导再分配定理、高资源极限、检索近似分析及条件秩不变性结果；在存档脱靶数据上审计剂量与跨语境两个实验，使用配对置换检验与构建簇自助法。

Result: 校正后的热力学亲和力与实测抑制呈弱相关且方向符合要求，但耦合未显示额外预测价值；仅GapNet存活，即平衡关联簇标准误的描述性GapNetOverSE；剂量拟合异质且常违反模型隐含的超线性指数约束，数据无法识别竞争参数；可饱和竞争者压缩在留出向导家族上保持两个精度目标，但在所测规模下并不更快。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个可复用的守恒算子，以及检验该算子所需的实验信息。

Abstract: Pairwise guide--transcript scores do not enforce conservation of a finite guide-loaded RISC pool when they are interpreted independently as occupancies. We formulate a differentiable scalar equilibrium layer: one conservation equation with a unique positive root and exact implicit gradients. It yields a redistribution theorem, a qualified high-resource limit, an analysis of the retrieval approximation, and a conditional rank-invariance result: within one construct at one dose, rankings by fractional occupancy cannot distinguish equilibrium from independent scoring. We therefore audit the two experiments that proposition leaves open, dose and cross-context, on archival off-target data. Corrected thermodynamic affinities associate weakly with measured repression in the direction a working predictor requires, but a paired permutation test and a construct-cluster bootstrap do not establish added predictive value from the coupling: what survives their differing permutation-null baselines is \GapNet{}, a descriptive \GapNetOverSE{} of the equilibrium association's cluster standard error. The dose fits are heterogeneous and frequently violate the model-implied exponent constraint, which is superlinear rather than sublinear, so these data do not identify the competition parameter. A saturable compression of the competitor set holds both accuracy targets on held-out guide families but is not faster at the size measured. The contribution is a reusable conservation operator and the experimental information needed to test it. The code for this study is available at https://github.com/shadi97kh/One-Pool-Many-Targets.

</details>


### [238] [Exact information accounting for SGD methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00446)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用信息论方法精确分析了随机梯度下降（SGD）及其变体，将预条件SGD步解释为高斯贝叶斯模型的后验均值更新，并把单步遗憾分解为内在时间代价与比较器信息变化，从而统一解释了凸收敛、鞍点逃逸、平坦性与泛化、学习率调度及多种优化器变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为对标准几何分析的替代，作者希望从信息论角度精确刻画SGD及其变体的行为，揭示各类优化现象背后的统一原理，并检验经典收敛界与泛化证书在实际网络中的有效性。

Method: 将预条件SGD步建模为高斯贝叶斯模型的后验均值更新，推导其单步遗憾的分解恒等式（内在时间代价 + 比较器信息变化），并将该恒等式扩展到目标函数本身；随后在合成与真实训练过程中测量各项，覆盖凸收敛、严格鞍点逃逸、平坦性-泛化联系、学习率调度、自适应优化器以及噪声、动量、重尾、无梯度等变体。

Result: 在真实网络上，该恒等式把经典收敛界的松弛归因于其推导中丢弃的项，并能区分达到相同训练损失的优化器（区分依据是步数与步的一致性）；但这种区分与泛化优劣的关系因网络而异。对无梯度SGD，该恒等式确定了曲率预条件器应如何进入更新。基于锐度的泛化证书在数据无关各向同性先验下，除非曲率谱在所有参数上近乎平坦，否则在网络规模下是空洞的。

Conclusion: SGD及其变体可由一个信息论恒等式统一刻画，几何现象与优化器差异均对应其中的特定项或特例；但经典泛化证书在网络尺度下往往缺乏实际约束力，需依赖曲率谱的强假设。

Abstract: As an alternative to the standard geometric analyses, we give an exact, information-theoretic analysis of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) and its variants. We show that a preconditioned SGD step is the posterior-mean update of a Gaussian Bayes model, and that its one-step regret splits into an intrinsic-time cost and a change in comparator information. The split extends to an identity for the objective itself. Convex convergence, strict-saddle-point escape, the link between flatness and generalization, the standard learning-rate schedules, adaptive optimizers, and the noisy, momentum, heavy-tailed, and gradient-free variants of SGD each correspond to a term or a special case of this identity. We measure its terms on synthetic and real training runs. On real networks it attributes the slack of classical convergence bounds to the terms their derivations drop and separates optimizers that reach the same training loss. That separation follows the number and consistency of their steps. Its relation to which of them generalizes better differs between networks. For gradient-free SGD the identity determines how a curvature preconditioner should enter the update. The sharpness-based generalization certificate it yields, with a data-independent isotropic prior, is vacuous at network scale unless the curvature spectrum is nearly flat across all parameters.

</details>


### [239] [Score the Update, Not the Token: Descent-Aligned Routing for Combinatorial LoRA Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00493)
*Priya Nair,Lukas Brenner,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore,Wen-Hsuan Liu,Tom Saliencro,Amara Okonkwo,Rohan Desai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VANE，让路由器对专家的更新进行打分（而非仅对token打分），将每个专家拆分为reader和writer，通过低秩"compass"预测token下降方向，从N_A+N_B个向量高效打分所有N_A×N_B个reader-writer对，在多个基准上超越12个PEFT/MoE-LoRA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mixture-of-LoRA-experts方法提升低秩适配容量，但几乎都将每个专家的输入侧因子与输出侧因子绑定，且路由仅对token打分（看不到专家会写出什么）。作者认为路由器应评估"更新"本身的价值：一阶近似下，某专家更新对损失的降低等于该更新与输出处负损失梯度的内积。该有用性对token是二次的，线性token路由器只能看到经过token均值的部分，而按激活范数排序的路由器完全看不到输出因子。

Method: 将每个专家拆分为reader（下投影）和writer（上投影）；在此设定下，每个reader-writer对的有用性成为共享rank-r空间中的内积，可由N_A+N_B个向量为全部N_A×N_B对打分。基于此构建VANE：用低秩compass预测每个token的下降方向；在不构造任何更新矩阵的情况下，按更新与compass的对齐度给所有对打分，用加性门激活top-k对，并为每一对提供精确的一阶路由器梯度。

Result: 在单领域常识推理及含四个领域的多任务混合上，使用Llama-3.2-3B与Llama-3.1-8B，VANE在12个PEFT与MoE-LoRA基线中取得最佳平均成绩，分别高出0.9–1.1和1.3–1.5个点，且可训练参数不到8专家MoE-LoRA的一半；其路由器打分与专家实测有用性的吻合度远高于token路由器。

Conclusion: 路由器应对专家更新而非仅对token打分；通过reader/writer拆分与低秩compass，可实现高效、精确的一阶更新评估，在更低参数预算下持续优于现有PEFT与MoE-LoRA方法，并使路由打分更贴近专家真实有用性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-LoRA-experts methods raise the capacity of low-rank adaptation by routing each token to a few low-rank experts. Nearly all of them tie one input-side factor to one output-side factor per expert, and nearly all of them route by scoring the token: the router picks experts without seeing what any of them would write. We argue that the router should score the update. To first order, adding an expert's update to a layer output lowers the loss by the inner product between that update and the negative loss gradient at the output. This usefulness is quadratic in the token, so a router that is linear in the token sees only the part of it that runs through the token mean, and routers that rank experts by the norm of their own activations never see the output factor. If each expert is split into a reader (down-projection) and a writer (up-projection), the usefulness of every reader--writer pair becomes an inner product in the shared rank-$r$ space, and all $N_AN_B$ pairs can be scored from $N_A+N_B$ vectors. We build VANE on this identity. A low-rank compass predicts the descent direction of each token. VANE scores every pair by the alignment between its update and the compass without forming any update, activates the top-$k$ pairs with additive gates, and gives every pair its exact first-order router gradient. On single-domain commonsense reasoning and a four-domain multi-task mixture with Llama-3.2-3B and Llama-3.1-8B, VANE attains the best average among twelve PEFT and MoE-LoRA baselines, by 0.9--1.1 and 1.3--1.5 points respectively, with less than half the trainable parameters of an 8-expert MoE-LoRA. Its router scores also track the measured usefulness of experts far more closely than token routers do.

</details>


### [240] [Gumbel Straight Flow: Distilling Autoregressive Models into One-step Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00497)
*Yeongmin Kim,Arnaud Doucet,Andrew Campbell,Valentin De Bortoli,Thomas Mensink,David Ruhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GSF：利用预训练自回归模型的Gumbel噪声-数据耦合构建连续流映射语言模型，实现高质量少步生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步语言生成方法在采样质量与步骤效率上存在不足，需要借助预训练自回归模型的知识来改进连续流生成的路径设计。

Method: 提出Gumbel Straight Flow（GSF）连续流映射语言模型，利用预训练AR模型诱导的Gumbel噪声与one-hot token序列之间的耦合，理论上证明其形成连接噪声与序列表示的非相交线性路径；并采用流映射半群目标，使切向（速度）条件由AR教师直接引导。

Result: 在包括预训练与下游任务的多项基准上，GSF优于当前少步语言生成基线方法。

Conclusion: GSF通过结合AR模型耦合结构与流映射半群目标，有效提升了少步语言生成的采样质量，可作为少步语言生成的有效方案。

Abstract: We present Gumbel Straight Flow (GSF), a continuous flow map language model that leverages the noise-data coupling of a pretrained autoregressive language (AR) model. We theoretically demonstrate that the coupling between Gumbel noise and one-hot token sequences induced by an autoregressive model yields non-intersecting linear paths connecting the noise to the sequence representations. To further enhance high-quality few-step path sampling, we use a flow map semigroup objective where the tangent (velocity) condition is guided directly by the AR teacher. Across various benchmarks, including pretraining and downstream tasks, GSF can outperform current few-step language generation baselines.

</details>


### [241] [Denoising Surface: Modeling and Predicting Inference Cost for Diffusion LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00499)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Liang Deng,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出去噪工作负载曲面（DWS）以准确预测扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的推理成本，并在真实服务场景中显著降低预测误差与延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散大语言模型（dLLM）从研究走向实际部署，请求管理（调度与资源分配）需要准确的单请求推理成本估计。然而现有的成本代理指标不适用：输出长度忽略了单次前向可解掩多个token，去噪步数忽略了每步成本的异质性。

Method: 作者观察到块自回归生成机制在输出块与块内去噪步上形成二维执行结构，而现有代理将其压缩为标量，丢失关键信息。据此提出去噪工作负载曲面（DWS），以概率曲面保留该二维块-步结构并加权异质的逐步成本；并设计由粗到细的训练方案，使轻量级的仅提示（prompt-only）预测器能准确预测复杂的DWS，可在单个CPU核心上高效运行，避免与serving模型争抢GPU。由于DWS将请求相关的执行行为与部署相关的成本因素解耦，预测器可跨硬件配置迁移而无需重训练。

Result: 在真实服务实验中，DWS 相比基于标量的预测器将成本预测误差降低最多 2.50 倍；DWS 引导的最短作业优先（SJF）调度器将在线聊天机器人端到端延迟降低最多 1.92 倍。

Conclusion: DWS 通过保留二维块-步结构并解耦执行行为与部署成本，实现了准确、可迁移、低开销的dLLM推理成本预测，并有效提升了在线服务的调度效率与延迟表现。

Abstract: As diffusion large language models (dLLMs) become more capable, they are moving from research settings to real-world \textit{serving}, where request management (such as scheduling and resource allocation) relies on accurate estimation of per-request inference cost. However, common cost proxies fall short for dLLMs: output length ignores that one forward pass can unmask multiple tokens, and denoising-step count ignores the \textit{heterogeneous} per-step costs. We observe that the block-autoregressive generation mechanism induces a two-dimensional execution structure over output blocks and within-block denoising steps, whereas these proxies collapse it into a scalar, discarding information essential for characterizing the cost. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Denoising Workload Surface (DWS), which preserves this two-dimensional block-step structure as a probability surface to weight the heterogeneous per-step costs. We then design a coarse-to-fine training scheme that enables a lightweight prompt-only predictor to accurately predict the complex DWS. This predictor runs efficiently even on a single CPU core, avoiding GPU contention with the serving model. Since DWS decouples request-dependent execution behavior from deployment-specific cost factors, the predictor transfers across hardware configurations without retraining. In \textit{real-world} serving experiments, DWS reduces cost-prediction error by up to $2.50\times$ over scalar-based predictors, while the DWS-guided shortest-job-first scheduler reduces end-to-end latency by up to $1.92\times$ for online chatbots.

</details>


### [242] [One-Step Generative Modeling via Training Dynamics Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00518)
*Zhangyong Liang,Ying Huang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TDAction方法，通过考虑共享参数实现代价来选择一步生成模型的传输目标，在ImageNet 256×256上无需蒸馏即达到FID低于1.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一步生成模型通过训练时的迭代传输构建静态生成器，但现有传输目标主要评估分布运动，而神经生成器需要通过共享参数更新共同实现所需的样本位移。核心问题在于：当训练成为构建最终一步映射的迭代过程时，应该优化下一次分布移动，还是有限生成器学习最终映射的路径。

Method: 引入Training Dynamics Action（TDAction），根据局部共享参数实现代价选择传输目标，同时保留规定的分布进展水平。将代价表述为软终端控制问题，推导出闭式Batch Tangent Action-to-Go值，兼顾参数努力和终端失配。该准则捕捉独立成对代价所忽略的跨样本交互；在各向同性移动下，准则与确定性平衡耦合的二次欧氏分配一致。随机切线探针提供低秩实现，构建共享分离目标而不增加推理时轨迹。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256上，TDAction无需蒸馏即可达到FID低于1.1。受控实验考察了生成器几何、传输选择与实际局部动作之间的关系。

Conclusion: TDAction通过优化共享参数实现代价而非仅关注分布运动，为一步生成模型的训练提供了新的传输目标选择准则，并在高分辨率图像生成任务上取得优异性能。

Abstract: One-step generative models construct a static generator through iterative training-time transport. Existing transport objectives primarily assess distributional motion, although a neural generator needs to realize the requested sample displacements jointly through shared parameter updates. The training-time construction raises the question: \emph{once training becomes the iterative process that constructs the final one-step map, what to optimize: the next distributional move, or the route by which the finite generator learns the final map?} To address the question, we introduce \textbf{T}raining \textbf{D}ynamics \textbf{A}ction (\textbf{TDAction}), which selects transport targets according to local shared-parameter realization cost while retaining a prescribed level of distributional progress. We formulate the cost as a soft-terminal control problem and derive a closed-form Batch Tangent Action-to-Go value that accounts for parameter effort and terminal mismatch. The criterion captures cross-sample interactions omitted by independent pairwise costs; under isotropic mobility, the criterion agrees with quadratic Euclidean assignment for deterministic balanced couplings. Randomized tangent probes provide a low-rank implementation that constructs shared detached targets without adding an inference-time trajectory. Controlled studies examine the relationship between generator geometry, transport selection, and realized local action.
