On ImageNet $256\times256$, TDAction attains an FID below $1.1$ without distillation.

</details>


### [243] [SimplexUQ: An Evaluation Framework and Benchmark for Conformal Uncertainty on Simplex-Valued Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00523)
*Liang You,Hengyu Shi,Dongwen Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimplexUQ 是首个针对单纯形值预测的 conformal 覆盖分配问题基准与可复现协议，对比多种 conformal 包装方法，发现全局校准可能掩盖分层覆盖不均，且无普遍最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conformal prediction 只保证边际覆盖，单一校准阈值会导致覆盖在易区过度、难区不足的分配不均问题；而现有研究缺乏针对单纯形值预测（概率、混合比例等）上该问题的统一基准与可复现评测协议。

Method: 提出 SimplexUQ 基准与协议，不提出新包装器而是对比已有 conformal 包装方法。任务套件 SimplexTasks-12 包含 6 个受控合成设定与 6 个冻结预测器的真实任务，涵盖类概率、主题混合、光谱丰度、细胞类型比例、年龄分布与情绪混合。每次比较固定预测器、评分函数与无响应分层映射，仅改变包装器，报告边际覆盖、最差分层覆盖、最大差异以及任务内半径与计算量。

Result: 全局校准可能看似有效却严重失败：在 CIFAR-10 上边际覆盖为 0.900，但最差熵分层仅 0.542；Mondrian 校准将该分层提升至 0.886，并把最大差异从 0.358 降至 0.022。但没有包装器占绝对优势：平滑合成异质性下多种修复方法表现接近；固定映射分析显示排序依赖评估组与协议；12 任务对比中 Mondrian 在其目标划分上全部 12 个任务差异更低，而 BatchMVP 在重叠组上 5 个任务更低。预测器偏差扫描显示，去除偏差只能部分降低全局阈值差异。

Conclusion: 该工作提供的是经验性比较而非新的覆盖保证，并释放任务卡、结果溯源、允许的派生数组与重建说明。作者主张将包装器选择视为诊断性比较，而非普遍排名。

Abstract: Conformal prediction guarantees marginal coverage, but a single calibration threshold can still spread that coverage unevenly, over-covering easy regions and under-covering hard ones. SimplexUQ is, to our knowledge, the first benchmark and reproducible protocol for measuring this allocation problem on simplex-valued predictions; it compares existing conformal wrappers rather than proposing a new one. Its task suite, SimplexTasks-12, combines six controlled synthetic regimes with six frozen-predictor real tasks spanning class probabilities, topic mixtures, spectral abundances, cell-type fractions, age distributions, and emotion mixtures. Each comparison fixes the predictor, score, and response-free stratification map, varies only the wrapper, and reports marginal coverage, worst-stratum coverage, max disparity, and within-task radius and compute. Global calibration can look valid while failing badly: on CIFAR-10 it attains 0.900 marginal coverage but only 0.542 in the worst entropy stratum, and Mondrian calibration raises that stratum to 0.886 while reducing max disparity from 0.358 to 0.022. No wrapper dominates, however. Under smooth synthetic heterogeneity, several repairs are competitive; fixed-map analyses show that rankings depend on the evaluation groups and protocol; and in a 12-task comparison, Mondrian has lower disparity on its single target partition for all 12 tasks, whereas BatchMVP has lower disparity over overlapping groups on five. These are empirical comparisons, not new coverage guarantees. A controlled predictor-bias sweep shows that removing predictor bias only partly reduces global-threshold disparity. We release task cards, result provenance, permitted derived arrays, and rebuild instructions, and treat wrapper selection as a diagnostic comparison rather than a universal ranking.

</details>


### [244] [Random Recursive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00541)
*Jama Hussein Mohamud,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Random Recursive Model (RRM)，通过随机采样层池中的层进行递归计算，参数高效且在推理任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归模型大多重复应用单一学习变换或固定的变换序列，将计算限制在固定的层顺序中，缺乏层组合的灵活性。作者希望保留递归的参数效率优势，同时实现灵活的层重用。

Method: RRM维护一个包含L个已学习层的层池，对每个样本和每个步骤独立地有放回采样一层，执行T个递归步骤，从而在保持递归参数效率的同时实现灵活的层重用。

Result: 在具有挑战性的推理任务上，RRM达到或超过基线模型，且常常减少50-75%的参数。RRM可在推理时改变深度（甚至超过训练时的深度），无需重新训练或增加参数；并支持蒙特卡洛推理和概率性测试时扩展，均能在不重新训练的情况下提升性能。

Conclusion: RRM为神经网络架构设计开辟了新方向，展示了随机递归在参数效率、推理深度灵活性和测试时扩展方面的潜力。

Abstract: Recursive models create computational depth through parameter reuse, offering a parameter-efficient alternative to increasing model size. However, most recursive models repeatedly apply one learned transformation or a prescribed sequence of transformations, restricting computation to a fixed layer order. We introduce the Random Recursive Model (RRM), which maintains a pool of $L$ learned layers and performs $T$ recursive steps by sampling one layer independently with replacement for each example and step. This enables flexible layer reuse while retaining the parameter efficiency of recurrence. We evaluate RRM on challenging reasoning tasks, where it matches or exceeds the baselines, often with 50-75 % fewer parameters. RRM can vary its depth at inference, including beyond that seen during training, without retraining or adding parameters, improving tasks that benefit from deeper iterative computation. RRM also supports Monte Carlo inference and probabilistic test-time scaling, both of which improve performance without retraining. These insights may open new directions in neural network architecture design.

</details>


### [245] [Geometry-Dependent Bounds for Online Non-Monotone DR-Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00545)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非负、非单调DR-子模函数在紧凸下闭集上的对抗在线最大化问题，给出新的比较器一致一阶不等式与系数4/9，并刻画了最优系数的上下界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线子模最大化通常关注0.401等已有基准系数，作者希望提升该基准，并在给定几何参数下精确刻画可达到的最优系数，同时给出下界（oracle上界）以说明紧性。

Method: 采用有序坐标论证构造与目标无关的有理动作，证明比较器一致一阶不等式；通过三组对称间隙构造得到离线oracle上界；并给出参数化扩展与有限实例精确界，构造覆盖所有ζ的上界函数。

Result: 在线系数提升至4/9，超过0.401基准；在ζ1∈K⊆[0,1]^d时系数为1/2−(1−2ζ)_+^2/[2(3−2ζ)^2]；离线oracle上界β*=0.470438681380894…；在ζ≥1/2时上下界匹配于1/2，且最优亏损为Θ((1/2−ζ)^2)。多项式情形：二次为1/2，三次从8/17起且ζ=1/5时为0.49。常数目标序列可得多项式次一阶查询下(4/9−ε)离线近似。另给自适应对手与值反馈保证，含O(T^{3/4})遗憾。

Conclusion: 论文改进了在线DR-子模最大化的近似系数并给出匹配的上下界刻画，揭示了系数随几何参数ζ的变化规律，并扩展到多项式、离线近似及噪声值反馈等设定。

Abstract: We study adversarial online maximization of nonnegative, non-monotone DR-submodular functions over compact convex down-closed sets. A learner commits each action before observing its objective and competes with the best fixed action in hindsight. We prove a comparator-uniform first-order inequality that gives coefficient $4/9$, improving the online $0.401$ benchmark, with one gradient query and one projection per round and $O(\sqrt T)$ expected approximate regret. If $ζ{\bf 1} \in K\subseteq[0,1]^d$, the coefficient improves to $\underlineα(ζ)=\tfrac12-(1-2ζ)_+^2/[2(3-2ζ)^2]$. The proof is a direct ordered-coordinate argument with an objective-independent rational action. Conversely, a three-group symmetry-gap construction yields an offline oracle upper bound $β_*=0.470438681380894\ldots$ at $ζ=0$, even with exact value and full-gradient responses. A parameterized extension and exact finite-instance bounds define an upper function for every $ζ$. The lower and upper bounds match at $1/2$ for $ζ\ge1/2$, and show that the optimal deficit from $1/2$ is $Θ((1/2-ζ)^2)$ as $ζ\uparrow1/2$. For coefficient-revealed polynomials we obtain $1/2$ for quadratics and a geometry-dependent cubic coefficient starting at $8/17$, including $0.49$ at $ζ=1/5$. A constant objective sequence yields an offline $(4/9-\varepsilon)$ approximation with polynomially many first-order queries on the cube and projections, without requiring a supplied positive lower bound on the optimum. We also give nonanticipating adaptive-adversary and value-feedback guarantees, including $O(T^{3/4})$ regret with one noisy value per round.

</details>


### [246] [Evaluating Hybrid Quantum-Classical Models for Reduced-Order Brain Deformation Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00554)
*Tao Liu,Ge He,Dongyu Liang,Wujie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究混合量子-经典机器学习在脑变形时空场降阶预测中的表现，结论是经典网络（POD-MLP、POD-LSTM）仍优于量子及混合量子变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时空位移场的直接预测计算代价过高，需要降阶处理；同时作者希望为近期量子机器学习（QML）寻找一个严谨的测试平台，检验混合量子-经典架构在物理场学习任务中的实际竞争力。

Method: 采用本征正交分解（POD）将高维脑变形位移场投影到低维潜空间；设计两类学习目标：静态时间到潜变量回归与自回归潜状态预测；将紧凑经典基线（POD-MLP、POD-LSTM）与最小及增强型混合量子架构（VQC、QLSTM）进行系统基准对比。

Result: 静态回归中，经典POD-MLP优于所有量子变体，但增强型VQC显著优于最小VQC基线；时序预测中，经典POD-LSTM在不同历史窗口和随机初始化下，其预测精度与统计稳健性均优于增强型QLSTM。

Conclusion: 降阶物理场学习可作为近期量子机器学习的严谨测试平台；混合增强能有效恢复弱量子电路的表达能力，但经典架构在保真度和稳定性上仍保持明确优势。

Abstract: We evaluate hybrid quantum-classical machine learning for the reduced-order prediction of spatiotemporal brain deformation fields. To mitigate the computational intractability of high-dimensional displacement fields, we employ Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) to project the data into a compact latent space. Within this framework, we formulate two distinct learning objectives: static temporal-to-latent regression and autoregressive latent state forecasting. We systematically benchmark compact classical baselines against both minimal and enhanced hybrid quantum architectures. Our results demonstrate that classical networks provide the strongest baselines in the present setting. For static regression, a classical POD-MLP outperforms all evaluated quantum variants, although an enhanced Variational Quantum Circuit (VQC) substantially improves upon a minimal VQC baseline. For temporal forecasting, a classical POD-LSTM delivers superior predictive accuracy and statistical robustness compared to an enhanced Quantum LSTM (QLSTM) across varying history windows and random initializations. Overall, this study establishes reduced-order physical field learning as a rigorous testbed for near-term QML, highlighting that while hybrid enhancements successfully recover expressivity in weak quantum circuits, classical architectures retain a definitive advantage in both fidelity and stability.

</details>


### [247] [Redundancy Meets Synergy: Dependency-aware Expert Selection for MoE via Submodular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00558)
*Zheng Lin,Shaoke Fang,Yuxin Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Zihan Fang,Zhe Chen,Wei Ni,Jun Luo,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DS-MoE框架，将专家子集选择重新定义为差次模（DS）优化问题，通过解耦冗余削减与协同增益，结合MM算法高效选出最优专家子集，优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型通过稀疏激活扩展容量，但显存需求成为部署瓶颈，提取紧凑专家子集是可行方案；然而现有启发式方法多依赖Top-k排序，孤立评估单个专家，忽略了门控网络带来的专家间复杂依赖关系。

Method: 提出DS-MoE，基于损失退化的二阶泰勒展开揭示专家组合中的功能二元性：冗余（表示重叠）与协同（互补误差抵消）；数学上将冗余削减与协同最大化解耦，把选择目标建模为差次模函数；设计带单调性保证的Majorization-Minimization（MM）算法以高效求解最优专家子集。

Result: 大量实验表明DS-MoE能有效保留不可或缺的专家组合，性能优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: DS-MoE从理论上重新定义了专家选择问题，兼顾专家间冗余与协同关系，为MoE模型压缩部署提供了有理论保证的有效方案。

Abstract: While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models effectively scale model capacity through sparse activation, their deployment is often bottlenecked by prohibitive memory requirements. Extracting a compact subset of experts presents a promising solution. However, existing expert selection heuristics predominantly rely on Top-k ranking, which isolates the evaluation of individual experts and ignores the intricate inter-expert dependencies introduced by the MoE gating network. In this paper, we propose DS-MoE, a theoretically grounded framework that redefines expert selection via difference-of-submodular (DS) optimization. By analyzing the second-order Taylor expansion of the loss degradation, we reveal functional duality within expert combinations: redundancy (where experts encode overlapping representations) and synergy (where experts provide complementary error cancellation). To navigate this duality, we mathematically decouple redundancy reduction from synergy maximization by formulating the selection objective as a DS function. Furthermore, we devise a tailored majorization-minimization (MM) algorithm with provable monotonicity guarantees to efficiently identify the optimal expert subset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DS-MoE effectively preserves indispensable expert combinations, achieving superior performance compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond Affine Transformations: A Soft Dominance Layer for Coordinate-Wise Neural Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00563)
*Mariano Rivera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种替代仿射变换的"软支配层"（Soft Dominance Layer），通过可学习参考向量与sigmoid松弛实现可微阈值比较，在MNIST单次实验中精度达0.9061（退火后0.9173），低于MLP基线0.9827，属概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规神经网络层以仿射变换为核心，作者希望探索一种具有直接阈值解释性的替代原语，检验其可训练性与可解释性，而非宣称取代仿射层。

Method: Soft Dominance Layer中每个输出单元将输入坐标与可学习参考向量比较，聚合平滑的不等式响应；用sigmoid松弛使比较可微，锐度参数α控制其向硬阈值决策的过渡。在MNIST上做单次运行实验，并比较有无退火两种设置，与MLP基线对照。

Result: 最佳观测精度为无退火0.9061、退火0.9173，MLP基线为0.9827；作者指出这些描述性结果不足以确立可靠的配置排序或统计显著的退火收益。学习到的参考向量呈现空间结构，提供了结构化学习的定性证据。

Conclusion: 该工作为初步概念验证，尚需多随机种子重复实验与更广泛数据集来评估鲁棒性与实际价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary study of an alternative to the affine transformation underlying conventional neural-network layers. In the proposed Soft Dominance Layer, each output unit compares input coordinates with a learnable reference vector and aggregates smooth inequality responses. A sigmoid relaxation makes the comparisons differentiable, while a sharpness parameter $α$ controls their transition toward hard threshold decisions. The aim is to examine the trainability and direct threshold interpretation of this primitive, not to claim a replacement for affine layers. In single-run MNIST experiments, the highest observed Soft Dominance accuracy is $0.9061$ without annealing and $0.9173$ with annealing, compared with $0.9827$ for the MLP baseline. These descriptive results do not establish reliable configuration rankings or a statistically supported annealing benefit. Learned reference vectors exhibit spatial structure, providing qualitative evidence of structured learning. Repeated-seed experiments and broader datasets are required to assess robustness and practical relevance beyond this proof of concept.

</details>


### [249] [Attention Kernels for Learning Maps Between Heavy-Tailed Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00564)
*Kailen Hargenrader,Edoardo Calvello,Bohan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究重尾概率测度上的算子学习，指出softmax的指数加权会导致测度级注意力积分发散，提出使用增长更慢的注意力核，并通过两个基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer可用于概率测度上的算子学习。对于具有多项式尾部的测度，softmax中的指数加权会使测度级注意力积分发散，因此有必要用增长更慢的函数替代指数函数。

Method: 构建了两个具有闭式目标的测度算子学习基准，用其研究post-norm transformer中的注意力核增长与数据变换；对比softmax与三种增长更慢的核，并测试symlog预处理的作用；同时考察样本量对经验能量距离和Wasserstein距离对尾部差异敏感性的影响。

Result: 无数据变换时，softmax模型在两个重尾基准上都出现ensemble collapse，而三种慢增长核均避免崩溃。Symlog预处理使softmax在矩阵求逆任务上避免崩溃，但在sheared swap任务上无效。在高斯对照上，四种核表现相近。样本量会影响距离度量对尾部差异的敏感性。

Conclusion: 增长更慢的注意力核是post-norm transformer从重尾集成中学习的有效设计选择。

Abstract: Operator learning on probability measures can be accomplished with transformers. For measures with polynomial tails, the exponential weighting in softmax can make the corresponding measure-level attention integrals diverge. This motivates replacing the exponential with slower-growing functions. We construct two benchmarks for operator learning on measures with closed-form targets. We use these benchmarks to study attention kernel growth and data transformation in post-norm transformers. Without data transformation, the softmax models exhibit ensemble collapse on both heavy-tailed benchmarks, while the three slower-growing kernels avoid collapse. Symlog preprocessing allows softmax to avoid collapse on the matrix inverse task but not on the sheared swap task. On the Gaussian control, all four kernels perform similarly. We also examine how sample size affects the sensitivity of empirical energy and Wasserstein distances to tail differences. These results support slower-growing attention kernels as an effective design choice for post-norm transformers learning from heavy-tailed ensembles.

</details>


### [250] [Make Sparse Rewards Count: Density-Aware Reward Aggregation for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00574)
*Tong Zheng,Skylar Zhai,Zhan Cheng,TianMing Sha,Youling Huang,Shuo Zhou,Shaotong Qi,Jingcheng Liang,Xuwei Ding,Pengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多奖励强化学习中奖励学习进度不均衡问题，提出基于"优势能量"与"活跃组密度"关系的密度感知奖励聚合方法 DARA，在工具调用和数学推理任务上更快达到目标行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多奖励强化学习需同时优化多个行为目标。虽然 GDPO 中的奖励归一化保留了组内奖励的相对信息，但不同目标的学习进度仍不均衡，存在批级别的信号不平衡问题，需要一种能校准各奖励贡献的方法。

Method: 作者引入"优势能量"（一个奖励在批次上平方优势之和）进行分析，证明在理想化 GDPO 归一化下该能量正比于"活跃组密度"（该奖励产生非零相对优势的 rollout 组比例）。据此提出 DARA，采用反平方根密度修正，为活跃频率较低的奖励赋予更大权重；权重由每个 rollout 批次实时计算，随训练动态自适应，且不改变底层策略优化目标。

Result: 在工具调用任务上，DARA 最多减少 26% 的训练步数即可达到高格式合规；在数学推理任务上，最多减少 65% 步数达到接近饱和的长度合规，同时最终性能与 GDPO 相当。

Conclusion: DARA 通过密度感知的奖励权重校准缓解了多奖励训练中的信号不平衡，在不改变优化目标的前提下加速目标行为的学习，是一种有效的多奖励聚合策略。

Abstract: Multi-reward reinforcement learning trains large language models to satisfy multiple behavioral objectives simultaneously. Reward-wise normalization, as used in GDPO, preserves reward-specific relative information within rollout groups, but different objectives can still exhibit uneven learning progress. We study this behavior through advantage energy, the sum of a reward's squared advantages over a batch. Under idealized GDPO normalization, we show that this energy is proportional to active-group density: the fraction of rollout groups in which the reward provides nonzero relative advantages. This reveals a residual batch-level signal imbalance and provides a basis for calibrating reward contributions. Based on this relation, we propose Density-Aware Reward Aggregation (DARA). We derive an inverse-square-root density correction that gives greater weight to signals from less frequently active rewards. DARA computes its weights from each rollout batch, adapting to changes in reward activity throughout training without modifying the underlying policy optimization objective. Experiments on tool calling and mathematical reasoning show that DARA learns the targeted behaviors faster than GDPO, reaching high format compliance in up to 26% fewer training steps on tool calling and near-saturated length compliance in up to 65% fewer steps on mathematical reasoning, while remaining competitive in final performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhaihaotian/DARA.

</details>


### [251] [From Task Mixtures to Specialized Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00580)
*Hojat Allah Salehi,Mehrdad Mahdavi,Andrew Arash Mahyari,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究联邦基础模型微调中的"复合异质性"问题，提出FedSEE方法，通过输入路由到任务专家来避免负迁移，整体性能提升2.9个点，最差服务四分位提升3.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习只关注客户端之间的数据异质性，却忽视了每个客户端内部可能存在未知的多个任务/数据分布混合。这种"复合异质性"（跨客户端与客户端内均异质）会导致单一本地模型难以同时适配内部多任务，从而产生负迁移。因此需要一种能对客户端内部混合任务进行专门化处理的方法。

Method: 作者在共享冻结表征上进行适配分析，证明当任务共享相同特征几何时，客户端任务混合的最优模型是各底层任务最优模型的凸组合；当任务最优解构成单纯形时，任务对齐路由的风险低于任何单一适配模型。基于少量带任务标注的公共样本，作者推导出凸规划来恢复任务专家并将其与对应任务匹配，进而提出FedSEE，实现与客户端任务混合对齐的输入依赖专家选择。

Result: 实验表明，FedSEE避免了所评估基线方法中出现的负迁移现象，整体性能提升2.9个百分点，对服务最差的四分位客户端提升3.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的专门化需要与客户端任务混合对齐的输入依赖专家路由，FedSEE验证了该分析的有效性，在复合异质性联邦微调场景下优于现有基线。

Abstract: In collaborative foundation model fine-tuning, client data is rarely homogeneous. Instead, clients typically possess unknown mixtures of distinct data distributions, or tasks. Conventional federated learning primarily addresses heterogeneity across clients without explicitly resolving latent task mixtures within each client. We study this setting as compound heterogeneity, where data is heterogeneous both across and within clients. We study adaptation over a common frozen representation and show that, when tasks share the same feature geometry, the optimal model for a client's task mixture under squared loss is a convex combination of the optimal models for its underlying tasks. Thus, a single locally trained model represents the client's overall task mixture, while individual inputs may be drawn from different underlying task distributions. This motivates routing inputs to specialized experts, and we show that, when the task optima form a simplex, task-aligned routing achieves lower risk than any single adapted model for genuinely mixed clients. With access to a small set of task-labeled public samples, we derive a convex program to recover task experts and match them to their corresponding tasks. Our routing analysis shows that effective specialization requires input-dependent expert selection aligned with each client's task mixture. Motivated by this analysis, we propose FedSEE. Across our experiments, FedSEE avoids the negative transfer observed in the evaluated baselines and improves performance by 2.9 points overall and 3.7 points for the worst-served quartile.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Heavy-Tailed Memory Traces in Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00010)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出核-尾世界模型（CTWM），通过基于秩的记忆控制器按单一指数τ分配提示预算并保留摘要化长尾，以更高效地利用智能体外部记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前长期语言智能体的外部记忆系统多以任务成功率或token成本为评价标准，忽略了记忆使用的"形状"：在有限上下文与重复检索下，记忆会集中在小型核心而将稀有状态留在长尾，造成预测误差累积。

Method: 作者首先进行保守的长尾审计，发现记忆集中现象可复现但因策略而异：随机游走智能体产生对数正态兼容的检索痕迹，语义LLM策略产生最强截断幂律兼容的核心-尾痕迹。基于此提出CTWM，一种基于秩的记忆控制器，用单一指数τ分配提示预算，同时保留摘要化的尾部。

Result: 在Synthetic Graph World上，相较于图记忆基线，CTWM保持完整状态与转移覆盖，提示token减少5.9%，下半尾预测误差降低13.6%。在ALFWorld上获得一致的token节省，在LongMemEval上token减少24.48%且总体准确率持平。

Conclusion: 重尾记忆痕迹不仅是有限检索的诊断指标，也可作为token高效智能体世界模型的实用控制信号。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents increasingly rely on external memory as a frozen world model, yet current memory systems are usually judged only by task success or token cost. We argue that the missing object is the shape of memory use: under finite context and repeated retrieval, agent memory can concentrate on a small core while leaving rare states in a long tail where prediction errors accumulate. We study this effect through a conservative tail audit and find that concentration is reproducible but policy-dependent. Random-walk agents produce log-normal-compatible retrieval artifacts, whereas semantic LLM policies yield the strongest truncated-power-law-compatible core--tail traces. Motivated by this audit, we propose Core--Tail World Model (CTWM), a rank-based memory controller that allocates prompt budget with a single exponent $τ$ while retaining a summarized tail. On Synthetic Graph World, CTWM preserves full state and transition coverage, reduces prompt tokens by 5.9%, and lowers bottom-half tail prediction error by 13.6% relative to a graph-memory baseline. The same paired comparison gives consistent token savings on ALFWorld and a 24.48% token reduction on LongMemEval with aggregate accuracy parity. These results suggest that heavy-tailed memory traces are not only a diagnostic of finite retrieval, but also a practical control signal for token-efficient agent world models.

</details>


### [253] [When Do Causal World Models Help Modular LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00012)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FedCausalCompose 因果世界模型框架，用于模块化 LLM 智能体，论证并验证跨模块接口的因果结构在统计可识别且以可用形式呈现时最有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多地通过模块化系统（订单、支付、库存、物流等）行动，一个模块中的动作会改变另一个模块中的有效转移。标准世界模型通常拟合观测轨迹，但这并非干预时规划所需的信息：轨迹可能显示"支付先于发货"，却无法区分支付是否授权发货、库存是否起中介作用，或存在隐藏共同触发因素。因此需要研究观测世界模型在干预场景下的根本缺陷及其补救条件。

Method: 提出 FedCausalCompose，一种面向模块化 LLM 智能体的因果世界模型框架，利用局部动作提供针对跨模块接口的干预—响应证据。理论上先分析观测世界模型在未阻断后门路径下的不可约干预误差，考察接口恢复与干预—响应覆盖度的关系，并比较 oracle 因果组合与非因果下界。随后在诊断性智能体环境中测试该预测，涵盖结构化工具环境（API 签名暴露前置条件与下游效应）以及对话与叙事环境。

Result: 观测世界模型在存在未阻断后门路径时会产生不可约的干预误差；接口恢复随干预—响应覆盖度提升而改善；在覆盖度与局部机制误差受控时，oracle 因果组合可优于非因果下界。实验中，因果接口在结构化工具环境中帮助最大；对话与叙事环境常忽略原始边列表，除非加入简短的注意力锚点使因果信息与决策相关。

Conclusion: 因果世界模型在 LLM 智能体中的有效条件明确：当跨模块接口既统计可识别、又以智能体在行动时可使用的形式呈现时，因果结构才能带来收益。这为模块化 LLM 智能体的世界建模与规划提供了具体的设计准则。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act through modular systems, such as order, payment, inventory, and shipment services, where actions in one module change which transitions are valid in another. Standard world models usually fit observational traces, but this is not the quantity needed for intervention-time planning: a trace may show that payment precedes shipment without identifying whether payment authorizes shipment, inventory mediates the effect, or a hidden trigger explains both. We study this gap through FedCausalCompose, a causal world-model framework for modular LLM agents in which local actions provide intervention-response evidence for cross-module interfaces. We first show that observational world models incur an irreducible interventional error under unblocked back-door paths, that interface recovery improves with intervention-response coverage, and that an oracle causal composition can beat the non-causal lower bound when coverage and local mechanism errors are controlled. We then test the resulting prediction in diagnostic agent settings. Causal interfaces help most in structured tool environments, where API signatures expose preconditions and downstream effects. In contrast, dialogue and narrative environments often ignore raw edge lists unless a short attention anchor makes the causal information decision-relevant. These results identify a concrete condition for causal world models in LLM agents: causal structure helps when cross-module interfaces are both statistically identifiable and presented in a form the agent can use at action time.

</details>


### [254] [From Proposal to Verified Effect: Praxa, an Evidence-Bound Harness for Governed AI Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00015)
*Stefan G. Creadore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Praxa 是一个将"提议—授权—调度—已验证外部效果—服务上线"等状态显式区分的 LLM agent 运行框架，论文以四条证据链报告其架构与实证结果，主张的核心贡献是"证据约束下的可测试权威到效果转换架构"，而非性能优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM agent 能提出并执行动作，但"提议、授权、调度、已验证的外部效果、服务上线"是彼此不同的断言，常被混为一谈。作者希望把这些权威到效果的转换状态显式化、可测试化，避免将"提出动作"误当作"已产生并验证效果"。

Method: 提出 Praxa，一个 agent harness，通过确定性准入（deterministic admission）、代理执行（brokered execution）、外部回读（external read-back）、对账（reconciliation）和经审查的上线（reviewed promotion）显式表示上述状态。评估采用四条证据链：作者自跑的仓库本地审计（固定 revision）、provider 支持的 Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 试点（12 个任务）、调试后的两阶协调代理开发对比（180 次试验），以及已部署源码/配置证据审查。

Result: 其一，仓库本地审计在固定修订版通过 1,027/1,027 单元测试与 89/89 Workerd 测试，对全部 363 个预期源文件完成插桩并满足四项覆盖率下限，但原始逐测试记录与独立复现不可得。其二，Terminal-Bench 试点中基线与可靠性层各通过 17/36 严格试验，可靠性层多用 37.49% 输入与 50.73% 输出 token，故不支持优越性。其三，协调代理开发对比中基线与候选各完成 180/180 试验，实测准确率相等，均具备完整 hermetic 崩溃恢复与零受保护违规；候选少用 37.11% token、低 33.84% 估计端点成本、少 11.63% 步数，但未证明质量、延迟或生产行为改善。其四，部署证据显示有界反思、召回核算、记忆编译与工具健康路径，但无生产结果提升。

Conclusion: Praxa 的受支持贡献是提供一个证据约束架构，使权威到效果的转换显式且可测试。现有证据尚不能确立对抗安全性、生产安全、通用专家优越性、自主递归优化或用户收益。

Abstract: Large-language-model agents can propose and execute actions, but proposal, authority, dispatch, verified external effect, and serving promotion are different claims. We present Praxa, an agent harness that represents these states explicitly through deterministic admission, brokered execution, external read-back, reconciliation, and reviewed promotion. We report four evidence lanes. First, an author-run repository-local audit at a pinned revision passed 1,027/1,027 unit tests and 89/89 Workerd tests, instrumented all 363 expected source files, and met four coverage floors; raw per-test transcripts and independent reproduction are unavailable. Second, in a provider-backed Terminal-Bench Core 0.1.1 pilot across 12 curated tasks, baseline and reliability-layer arms each passed 17/36 strict trials. The reliability layer used 37.49% more input and 50.73% more output tokens, so the pilot does not support superiority. Third, in a post-debug, two-order coordination-proxy development comparison, baseline and a source-authored candidate each completed 180/180 trials with equal measured accuracy, full hermetic crash recovery, and zero protected violations. The candidate used 37.11% fewer tokens, 33.84% lower estimated endpoint cost, and 11.63% fewer steps; this does not establish improved quality, latency, or production behavior. Fourth, deployed source/configuration evidence shows bounded reflection, recall accounting, memory compilation, and tool-health paths, but no production outcome lift. Praxa's supported contribution is an evidence-bound architecture that makes authority-to-effect transitions explicit and testable. Current evidence does not establish adversarial security, production safety, general specialist superiority, autonomous recursive optimization, or user benefit.

</details>


### [255] [What Do Rationales Communicate? A Message-Intervention Study in Role-Specialized QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00018)
*Jiameng Zhang,Hongqiu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"消息干预诊断"方法，固定证据与候选答案、仅改变reasoner传给verifier的rationale，发现忠实rationale几乎不提升答案准确率，而损坏的rationale会大幅改变支持性判断，说明rationale共享应被视为一种验证消息机制而非单纯的提分途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色分工式QA流水线（reasoner→verifier）日益普及，但传递的rationale究竟带来什么价值并不清楚：是更好的答案、更强的支持性评估，还是新的失效面。作者希望厘清该消息通道的真实作用。

Method: 提出消息干预诊断：固定证据和候选答案，仅变化跨越reasoner-verifier边界的rationale。在MuSiQue、HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA各400例上，以DeepSeek作为生成器与验证器；设置盲验证器提示与显式rationale核查提示，并做跨模型与任务边界检验及人类审计。

Result: 忠实rationale相对无rationale几乎不增加答案准确率；损坏rationale显著改变支持判断。盲提示下无害改写仅使支持变化0–2.5%，损坏rationale变化10–22%；显式核查提示放大到34–55%。最终答案变化较小（2–30%），仅2.9–35.3%的支持翻转伴随答案变化。人类审计中16/42有效损坏属"过度信任损坏"，人类拒绝或标记不清9/10被模型接受的损坏rationale。

Conclusion: rationale共享应作为验证消息机制来评估，而不仅看作提升答案准确率的路径；该通道在跨模型与任务边界下可表现为激活、放大、惰性或被折叠进任务标签。

Abstract: Role-specialized QA pipelines increasingly pass rationales from a reasoner to a verifier, but it is unclear what this message actually buys: better answers, stronger support assessment, or a new failure surface. We introduce a message-intervention diagnostic that fixes the evidence and candidate answer while varying only the rationale passed across the reasoner-to-verifier boundary. On 400 MuSiQue, HotpotQA, and 2WikiMultiHopQA examples with DeepSeek as generator and verifier, faithful rationales add almost no answer accuracy over no rationale, while corrupted rationales strongly alter support judgments. Under a blind verifier prompt, harmless paraphrases shift support by only 0--2.5%, whereas corrupted rationales shift support by 10--22%; an explicit rationale-checking prompt amplifies the same pattern to 34--55%. Final answers move less (2--30%), and only 2.9--35.3% of corrupted support flips co-occur with answer changes. Human audits show why this matters: 16/42 valid corruptions are corruption-overtrust cases, and blind humans reject or mark unclear 9/10 audited corrupted rationales that the model accepts. Cross-model and task-boundary checks show when the channel is active, amplified, inert, or folded into the task label. Rationale sharing should be evaluated as a verification-message mechanism, not merely as a route to higher answer accuracy.

</details>


### [256] [Measuring the Microtask Eligibility Gap: When Is an Off-the-Shelf SLM Enough for an Agent Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00025)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了4个微任务基准，检验现成小语言模型（SLM）能否达到实践者设定的阈值，发现存在"资格缺口"：16种配置无一通过，量化无法改变结论，缺口主要随模型规模而非精度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent系统希望用SLM处理前沿LLM规划器周边的微任务（如自动批准shell命令、写记忆、选工具、排序历史轮次）。作者想探究：现成SLM能否满足实践者定义的阈值？失败原因是能力不足还是解码阈值问题？量化是否会改变结论？

Method: 构建含4个微任务的基准，使用固定提示和自动指标，每个任务预设阈值τ（锚定于廉价非LLM基线）和CI感知的资格规则；扫描Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B（FP16、贪心、单一冻结提示、无微调）；用logprob决策阈值诊断区分失效类型；对4-bit量化（RTN/GPTQ/AWQ）进行测试；并在Llama-3.x上复现，做τ扫描与提示改写鲁棒性检验。

Result: 出现"资格缺口"：16个配置（4任务×4模型）全部不通过。logprob诊断将失败分为能力缺陷与可通过调整解码阈值解决的失败（4种模式）。4-bit量化造成的损害取决于模型规模，未使任何配置达到资格；缺口更多随模型规模而非精度变化。在Llama-3.x上复现（12/12不合格），对锚点选择（τ扫描）和提示措辞（每格0/112合格）稳健。

Conclusion: 应将SLM置于满足CI支持阈值的基线之后，仅在基线未达标处使用SLM；例如在BM25短名单上用4B重排序器优于BM25（+0.047 [0.020, 0.073]），但自身并未获得资格认证。

Abstract: Agent harnesses increasingly want to run small language models (SLMs) on the microtasks around a frontier large language model (LLM) planner: auto-approving shell commands, writing memory, selecting tools, ranking past turns. We ask whether off-the-shelf SLMs meet practitioner-defined thresholds and, when they fail, why, and whether quantization changes the answer. We build a benchmark of 4 such microtasks with fixed prompts and automatic metrics, each with a pre-specified threshold $τ$ anchored to a cheap non-LLM baseline and a CI-aware eligibility rule (a configuration passes only if its confidence bound clears $τ$). Sweeping Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B at their best (FP16, greedy, one frozen prompt, no tuning), we find an eligibility gap: 0 of 16 (4 tasks $\times$ 4 models) configurations pass (verified by checking the raw outputs and parser behavior). A logprob decision-threshold diagnostic (T1/T3/T4; T2 via a context-length/cascade probe) separates the failures into capability deficits and failures that can be addressed by changing the decoding threshold (4 regimes). Quantization to 4-bit (RTN/GPTQ/AWQ) does damage that depends on model size and moves no configuration into eligibility (certified on the reconstructable hard-label tasks T1/T3, diagnostic/windowed robustness on T2/T4), so the gap tracks model size more than precision; it replicates on Llama-3.x (12/12 ineligible) and is robust to the anchor choice (a $τ$-sweep) and to prompt wording (0/112 eligible across the original plus 3 neutral paraphrases per cell). The practical implication: place SLMs behind a baseline that meets the CI-backed threshold, and use the SLM only where the baseline fails to meet the threshold; e.g. a 4B re-ranker over a BM25 shortlist beats BM25 ($+0.047$ [0.020, 0.073], without itself certifying eligibility).

</details>


### [257] [Before Agents Decide: Epistemic Action in LLM-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00511)
*Yizhi Liu,Balaji Padmanabhan,Siva Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知科学中的"认知行动"（epistemic actions）概念引入LLM智能体设计，区分三种模式——获取缺失证据、转换已有证据、探测系统以产生揭示性响应，并主张智能体设计须解决"决策就绪证据"如何生成的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的智能体虽具备搜索与探索能力，但设计重心多放在搜索与探索本身，而忽视了更前置的问题：现有证据是否已就绪可用于决策。证据可能缺失，可能形式不当而掩盖关键信息，也可能缺乏必要的对照。认知科学早已研究这类"为后续选择改善证据基础"的行动，但智能体设计尚未系统吸纳这一视角。

Method: 这是一篇立场/观点性论文（position paper），非实证研究。作者以认知科学中的"认知行动"理论为框架，将其迁移到LLM智能体情境，提出三种认知行动模式的分类：一是获取缺失证据（acquisition），二是转换已有证据的形式（transformation），三是探测系统以诱发揭示性响应（probing）。同时提出"认知脚手架"（epistemic scaffolding）概念，用以指代使这些行动得以实现且可审计的接口、工具与环境。

Result: 论文为概念性论证，未报告实验数据或定量结果。其核心产出是一套概念区分与分析框架：三种认知行动模式，以及"认知脚手架"这一统摄接口、工具与环境的术语，用于刻画智能体如何生产可审计的决策就绪证据。

Conclusion: 论文主张，智能体设计不能只关注搜索与完成任务，必须显式处理"决策就绪证据如何被生产"这一前置问题，并将认知行动与认知脚手架纳入智能体设计的核心考量。

Abstract: Before a difficult decision, people often act simply to understand the situation better. We turn an object to see another side, place alternatives next to each other, or change one condition and observe what happens. These actions may not complete the task, but they improve the evidence needed for the next choice. LLM-based agents can search and explore, yet agent design gives less attention to an earlier question: is the available evidence ready for the decision? Sometimes necessary evidence is missing. In other cases, the evidence is present but its form hides what matters, or the comparison needed to judge it does not yet exist. Cognitive science calls actions that improve the basis for a later choice epistemic actions. We bring this idea to LLM-based agents and distinguish three modes: acquiring missing evidence, transforming available evidence, and probing a system to create a revealing response. We use the term epistemic scaffolding for the interfaces, tools, and environments that make these actions possible and auditable. This paper argues that agent design must address how decision-ready evidence is produced.

</details>


### [258] [Characterizing a Configuration Where Inference-Time PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting Is Inert: Evidence from Three Reasoning LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00047)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统检验了"将PRM剪枝出的高PRM前缀作为上下文示范移植到仍在解码的平行链"这一机制（PPFG），发现其在测试的操作点上与独立平行思维链基线在统计上无差异，属于机制"零效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行思维链存在"多样性崩塌"，以往片段移植工作声称有增益，但往往依赖额外的补偿性设计。作者希望隔离并验证"跨轨迹步骤级迁移"这一最省成本的操作化方案（PPFG）本身是否真正有效。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct配合Math-Shepherd、完整MATH500（n=500，三个种子）上测试PPFG的停滞目标与随机目标两种变体；对322次注入事件做四桶分类；扫描复合门控；加入随机对照；跨三个基础LM、六个基准、第二个PRM及兼容门控扫描复现；用双单侧检验（TOST）做等价性分析；并进行事件级抽查与幸存兄弟反事实分析，最后用事后oracle给出上界。

Result: PPFG在测量各轴与独立平行CoT基线统计不可区分；四桶分类显示仅14%注入命中真正挣扎的链，其余落在已成功、接近完成或PRM平台期的链上；无复合门控能同时满足良好命中率与足够密度；随机对照以2.4倍触发率仍同样"惰性"；结论在三个LM、六个基准、第二个PRM与门控扫描中复现，TOST将等价性提升为十二个Qwen/LLaMA单元上的正向等价；注入链剪枝率为匹配步的2.75倍，但无反事实层面的群体补偿；事后oracle给出的每问题最大增益上界仅+0.13个百分点。

Conclusion: PPFG在所测操作点上是一个真正的推理期机制"零结果"，其惰性源于触发定位与链状态的不匹配而非启发式特定性。论文贡献了一个用于确立推理期机制零效应的等价性检验模板，所有结论均限定于其测试的操作点。

Abstract: Diversity collapse in parallel chain-of-thought has motivated inference-time interventions built on a natural design: when a process reward model (PRM) prunes a chain, its high-PRM prefix is extracted and grafted verbatim as an in-context demonstration into a still-decoding sibling. We isolate this mechanism, PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting (PPFG), as the most cost-minimal operationalization of cross-trajectory step-level transfer, and test it at the operating point where prior fragment-grafting work reports gains only under additional compensating ingredients. On Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with Math-Shepherd on full MATH500 (n=500, three seeds), PPFG in both stagnation- and random-targeting variants is statistically indistinguishable from an independent parallel-CoT baseline on every measured axis. We characterize why: a four-bucket classification of 322 stagnation-rule injection events shows only 14% targeted a genuinely struggling chain; the rest landed on chains that had already succeeded, were near completion, or sat on a flat PRM plateau, states a rescue graft cannot change. No compound-gate refinement jointly achieves well-targeted firing and adequate density, and a random control matches the same parity at 2.4x the firing rate, so the inertness is not heuristic-specific. The finding replicates across three base LMs, six benchmarks, a second PRM, and a compatibility-gate sweep; two-one-sided-tests analysis promotes the parity to positive equivalence on all twelve Qwen/LLaMA cells. A per-event spot-check finds injected chains prune at 2.75x the matched-step rate, but a surviving-sibling counterfactual finds no population-level compensation. A hindsight oracle bounds any per-problem gain from choosing PPFG over independent at +0.13 pp. We contribute an equivalence-testing template for establishing inference-time mechanism nulls, with every claim scoped to its tested operating point.

</details>


### [259] [Meta-Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Fast Adaptation of Interactive Policies with Applications to Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00705)
*Huiwen Yan,Kyriakos G. Vamvoudakis,Mushuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个元多智能体强化学习（meta-MARL）框架，将MARL问题建模为马尔可夫博弈，定义meta-NE解概念，实现跨博弈分布的快速交互策略适应，并在自动驾驶任务上验证其适应速度优于预训练MARL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元强化学习（meta-RL）多聚焦于单智能体系统，而将其扩展到多智能体系统面临额外挑战：任务不仅由环境刻画，还涉及智能体之间的策略交互。为让多智能体系统能快速适应新任务/环境中的交互策略，需要新的元学习框架与算法。

Method: 将多智能体强化学习问题建模为马尔可夫博弈（MGs），提出跨MG分布的meta-MARL框架用于快速交互策略适应；定义新概念meta-NE以刻画meta-MARL问题的期望解概念；建立meta-NE与基于梯度博弈（gradient-play）的meta-MARL算法驻点之间的等价性充分条件。

Result: 在自动驾驶任务上的评估表明，所提出的meta-MARL方法相比预训练的MARL基线实现了更快的适应速度，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将元学习引入多智能体交互策略适应，通过meta-NE解概念与理论等价性条件为算法提供支撑，并在自动驾驶任务中证明其适应效率优势。

Abstract: This paper develops a meta-multi-agent reinforcement learning (meta-MARL) framework to enable fast adaptation of interactive policies in a multi-agent system (MAS). Meta-reinforcement learning (meta-RL) enables agents to rapidly adapt to new tasks/environments using a bi-level optimization mechanism. However, existing meta-RL generally focuses on single-agent systems. Extending these frameworks and algorithms to multi-agent systems poses additional challenges, as tasks are characterized by not only the environment but also agents' strategic interactions. To address these challenges, we model multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) problems as Markov games (MGs) and develop a meta-MARL framework for rapid interactive policy adaptation across a distribution of MGs. A new concept, called meta-NE, is defined to describe the desired solution concept in a meta-MARL problem. Sufficient conditions for the equivalence between a meta-NE and a stationary point of the gradient-play-based meta-MARL algorithm are established. Our evaluation on autonomous-driving tasks demonstrates that the proposed meta-MARL method achieves faster adaptation than pretrained MARL baselines, validating the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [260] [Gradient-Aligned Pair Selection for Personalized Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00061)
*Ruoming Jin,Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Lei Xu,Haixun Wang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 GAP-DPO，将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐优化问题，通过效用感知、几何对齐的偏好对选择与逐轮重生成控制分布偏移，显著提升个性化文本生成的风格保真度与偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化 LLM 需使生成行为对齐用户特定偏好，而非仅追求整体质量。DPO 虽提供稳定的偏好学习框架，但在个性化场景下其效果关键取决于偏好对的选取方式。现有方法多依赖启发式准则（如基于似然的极端样本），使优化与显式用户效用脱节，可能损害个性化效果。

Method: 作者将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐的优化问题，分析期望用户效用梯度与 DPO 更新方向之间的一阶交互。分析表明，在离策略采样下，当偏好间隔与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO 更新会从纯误差纠正信号转变为类强化学习更新。据此提出 GAP-DPO：一种迭代算法，执行效用感知、几何对齐的偏好对选择，并通过逐轮（epoch-wise）重新生成控制分布偏移。

Result: 在个性化文本生成基准上，GAP-DPO 在风格保真度、偏好对齐和生成质量上均持续优于标准 DPO 变体。

Conclusion: 结果确立了梯度对齐作为个性化偏好优化的统一原则，并表明偏好对选择是优化几何的内在组成部分，而非启发式的预处理步骤。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) requires aligning generation behavior with user-specific preferences rather than aggregate quality. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) provides a stable framework for preference learning, its effectiveness in personalized settings critically depends on how preference pairs are selected. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristic criteria, such as likelihood-based extremes, which decouple optimization from explicit user utility and can lead to degraded personalization. We formalize personalized preference learning as a geometry-aligned optimization problem by analyzing the first-order interaction between gradients of expected user utility and DPO update directions. Our analysis reveals that, under off-policy sampling, the DPO update transitions from a purely error-corrective signal to a reinforcement-like update when preference margins are directionally aligned with utility gradients. This perspective exposes pair selection as a geometric decision that governs whether preference optimization advances or hinders personalization. Motivated by this insight, we propose GAP-DPO (Geometry-Aligned Preference DPO), an iterative algorithm that performs utility-aware, geometry-aligned pair selection while controlling distribution shift via epoch-wise regeneration. Experiments on personalized text generation benchmarks show that GAP-DPO consistently improves stylistic fidelity, preference alignment, and generation quality compared to standard DPO variants. Together, our results establish gradient alignment as a unifying principle for personalized preference optimization and demonstrate that pair selection is an intrinsic component of the optimization geometry rather than a heuristic preprocessing step.

</details>


### [261] [Kepler: Auditable World Models for ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00834)
*Wensen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kepler 是一个开源框架，将假设表示为可执行世界模型，在 ARC-AGI-3 交互环境中通过回溯式转移检查与条件预测检查进行验证，单次冻结配置下在全部 25 个公开游戏上取得 100.00 RHAE，并报告了成本、失败模式，指出公开集分数区分度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC-AGI-3 的规则与目标需从观察中推断，现有评测往往只报告最终分数，缺乏对假设验证机制、失败模式、成本与验证充分性的考量，容易产生误导性结论。

Method: 提出开源框架 Kepler，将假设形式化为可执行世界模型，并通过两类检查验证：回溯式转移检查与条件预测检查；在单一冻结的模型配置下运行，不进行逐游戏模型选择或基于分数的重跑；同时记录动作数、token 用量、缓存比例与成本，并设置对照条件与失败案例分析。

Result: 在 25 个公开游戏上获得经服务器验证的 100.00 RHAE；183 个已完成关卡中有 181 个的最终尝试动作数不超过对应人类中位基线；保留运行共 8,256 次环境动作（其中 7,292 次在计分关卡）；记录 8.58 亿 token、97.37% 缓存读取、$777.72 成本。另报告三类评测失败：源码泄漏导致无效满分运行、对照组中智能体重建被移除的框架、自主修复掩盖规划器故障；观察案例显示动画帧含任务相关信息。最终两个模型板共 50 个游戏-模型单元中有 48 个达到 100。

Conclusion: 仅凭公开集分数区分度有限，应推动首次尝试、成本约束与验证感知的评测报告方式。

Abstract: ARC-AGI-3 evaluates agents in interactive environments whose rules and objectives must be inferred from observation. We present Kepler, an open-source harness that represents hypotheses as executable world models and validates them through retrospective transition checks and conditional prediction checks. Under one frozen Claude Opus 5 configuration, Kepler obtained a server-verified 100.00 RHAE on all 25 public games, with no per-game model selection or score-conditioned reruns. On 181 of 183 completed levels, the final Opus attempt used no more actions than the corresponding median-human baseline. The retained board runs used 8,256 environment actions, of which 7,292 occurred in scored levels. Retained local provider-session records yield 858.0 million tokens, 97.37% cache reads, and a \$777.72 cost at September 1, 2026 API list-equivalent rates. We also report three evaluation failures: source-code leakage that produced an invalid perfect run, agents reconstructing a removed harness in a control condition, and autonomous repair masking a broken planner. A single-game observation case study showed that animation frames contained task-relevant information absent from settled text grids. Across the final Claude Opus 5 and GPT-5.6 Sol boards, 48 of 50 game-model cells reached 100. These results indicate that public-set score alone has limited discriminative value and motivate first-attempt, cost-conditioned, and verification-aware reporting.

</details>


### [262] [K-Dense BYOK: An Open-Source AI Research Assistant That Runs Locally and Keeps a Hash-Chained Lab Notebook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00074)
*Aubrey M. Brueckner,Darshil Patel,Yuhuan He,Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: K-Dense BYOK 是一个免费开源的本地 AI 科研助手，通过科学流程库、Living Lab Notebook 与不可篡改的观测日志提升科研可复现性，在二十个跨学科任务上的科学质量与研究执行均优于两个托管平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聊天助手或通用编程智能体缺乏科研专用脚手架、过程记录与可追溯性，且模型普遍存在"过度声称"（overclaiming）问题；作者希望让研究数据、代码与记录留在研究者自管的机器上，并使主张可被核查。

Method: 系统运行于研究者自有计算机，由研究者自带模型（本地或托管）。提供三层支撑：书面科学流程库、引导式工作流模板、科研数据源目录及审稿与写作角色；维护 Living Lab Notebook，条目相互链接、只增不删；通过观察智能体实际行为（而非采信其自述）记录日志，且智能体无写入该日志的工具。以二十个跨学科研究提示、预先固定的评分标准进行评估。

Result: 在科学质量与研究执行两项指标上均领先两个托管平台；其交付物是唯一记录了所运行软件、且通常附带可复现结果的命令的产物；其中一托管平台使用相同前沿模型却两者皆无。环境记录由智能体以文件形式写出，并非观测日志的一部分，日志尚未捕获软件环境本身。

Conclusion: 通过"使主张可核查而非直接阻止"的设计，K-Dense BYOK 有效应对模型过度声称这一最常见失败模式，并在可复现性与执行记录上优于托管方案；但软件环境的自动捕获仍有待完善。代码以 MIT 许可发布于指定仓库。

Abstract: K-Dense BYOK (bring your own keys) is a free, open-source AI research assistant for scientists in any field that runs on the researcher's own computer. The researcher supplies access to a model of their choice, hosted or running locally, and the application supplies everything else: a place for the work to run, a layer of scientific scaffolding, and a complete record. Each project is an ordinary folder, so the data, the code, the results, and the record stay on a machine the researcher administers and can be read years later without the application. Three things separate it from a chat assistant or a general-purpose coding agent. It ships a library of written scientific procedures, guided workflow templates, catalogs of where research data can be found, and reviewer and writer roles the agent can hand work to. It keeps a Living Lab Notebook whose entries link into an argument and are added to but never erased. And it records what happened by watching what the agent does rather than by taking the agent's word for it, in a log the agent has no tool that can write to. That choice targets the most common failure, model overclaiming, in our earlier benchmark of nine frontier models, by making claims checkable rather than preventing them. On twenty interdisciplinary research prompts, scored under a rubric fixed in advance, K-Dense BYOK led two managed platforms on both scientific quality and research execution. Its deliverables were the only ones that recorded the software they ran in, and the only ones that usually arrived with a command that regenerates the results. One of the managed platforms ran the same frontier model and supplied neither. Those environment records were files the agent wrote, not part of the observed log, which does not yet capture the software environment itself. The code is available under the MIT license at https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/k-dense-byok.

</details>


### [263] [ActiveSaddler: Automated Curriculum Learning for Agent Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00906)
*Sungho Park,Wonjoong Kim,Jue Zhang,Wook-Shin Han,Pengfei Gao,Chanyoung Park,Yongqiang Yao,Rao Fu,Elsie Nallipogu,Qingwei Lin,Victor Rühle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将 harness（智能体框架）优化中被忽视的"训练场景选择"维度形式化为自动化课程学习问题，提出 ActiveSaddler，用非平稳 bandit 动态调整课程，在 GAIA2 与 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上显著提升 Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 harness 优化方法只关注"如何更新"提示、工具接口与控制逻辑，而把"用哪些训练场景产生反馈"基本固定下来。随着 harness 演进，最有价值的训练场景会变化，因此课程本身也应当与 harness 共同演化，这一维度此前被忽略。

Method: 将 harness 优化中的课程选择建模为带动态实例化优化目标的非平稳 bandit 问题：把反复出现的失败抽象为可复用的"失败模式臂"，估计继续针对每种模式的潜在学习进展，并自适应平衡"重访已知弱点"与"探索新场景以发现新失败"；优化结果持续更新失败模式集合及其优先级，使课程与 harness 协同演化。

Result: 在 GAIA2 和 Terminal-Bench 2.0 上，ActiveSaddler 稳定发现更强的 harness，相比使用优化前固定场景顺序的同一 harness 优化器，测试 Pass@1 分别提升 4.4 和 7.5 个百分点。消融实验表明收益依赖于动态构建优化目标、估计其演化效用以及平衡持续优化与新失败发现。

Conclusion: 自动化课程学习是 harness 优化的一个新的关键维度，与 harness 协同演化的课程能带来一致性提升，该方法为 LLM 智能体框架优化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Automated harness optimization can substantially improve LLM agents by iteratively updating their prompts, tool interfaces, and control logic from execution feedback. However, existing methods primarily optimize how the harness is updated while largely fixing which training scenarios generate the feedback that drives those updates. As the harness evolves, the scenarios most useful for further optimization can change, suggesting that the training curriculum itself should adapt alongside the harness. We formulate this missing dimension of harness optimization as an automated curriculum learning problem and introduce ActiveSaddler. ActiveSaddler models the evolving curriculum as a non-stationary bandit with dynamically instantiated optimization targets. It abstracts recurring failures into reusable failure-pattern arms, estimates the potential learning progress from further targeting each pattern, and adaptively balances revisiting known weaknesses with exploring unseen scenarios for new ones. Optimization outcomes continually update both the set of discovered failure patterns and their priorities, allowing the curriculum to co-evolve with the harness. Experiments on GAIA2 and Terminal-Bench 2.0 show that ActiveSaddler consistently discovers stronger harnesses, improving test Pass@1 by 4.4 and 7.5 percentage points over the same harness optimizer using a scenario order fixed before optimization, respectively. Ablations further show that these gains depend on dynamically constructing optimization targets, estimating their evolving utility, and balancing continued optimization with new failure discovery. Together, these results establish automated curriculum learning as a new crucial optimization dimension for harness optimization.

</details>


### [264] [Scientific Agents: Evaluating Profession-Specific System Prompts on Scientific Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00084)
*Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对503个职业专属AGENTS.md科学智能体画像的大规模评测显示，使用完整职业画像提示词并未提升准确率，反而使输出token增1.5–2.3倍、单次调用成本增2.2–4.5倍；工具类任务上画像条件准确率反而低10个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界常默认认为详细的职业专属系统提示词能提升大模型在科学任务上的表现，但缺少对其准确性收益与token/成本代价之间权衡的系统性证据，因此作者希望验证这种默认做法是否真的划算。

Method: 作者构建并评估了Scientific Agents——一个包含503个职业专属AGENTS.md画像的开源语料库，使用Gemini 3.8 Flash经OpenRouter在Pi智能体框架中运行。采用匹配画像与四种对照进行比较：最小基线（"你是一个有用的助手"）、画像开头的角色句、通用科学严谨性指南、以及来自无关领域的画像。评测覆盖9个文本科学基准（4,531个抽样问题、100个匹配画像），在API错误重试后共4,488个条目完成全部五种条件，使用自动化规则评分；另在60个需调用工具的BioMysteryBench生物信息学问题上各运行三次（基线 vs 画像）。

Result: 画像与基线的平均准确率差异为-0.6个百分点（95%自助法区间[-1.5, +0.2]），没有任何基准显示统计上明确的提升。匹配画像输出token数为基线的1.5–2.3倍，每次成功调用成本为2.2–4.5倍。在BioMysteryBench上，画像平均求解率为46.7%，基线为56.7%，差异-10.0个百分点（95%区间[-16.7, -3.3]），原因是画像条件下更频繁地触发token与时间上限。唯一意外优势出现在操作性层面：在SuperGPQA上，频繁的API中断使短基线仅在54.0%的条目上获得正确的首轮答案，而画像条件为71.6%；但通用与错配提示词的可靠性与之相近，说明这一增益源于提示词长度或格式而非领域专业知识。

Conclusion: 在测试所用模型与任务上，默认加载完整职业画像既不提升准确率，还显著增加成本，因此不建议无条件使用；至于选择性检索画像片段或在开放式科学任务上是否会改变结论，仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Detailed profession-specific system prompts raise token use and estimated cost per response without a consistent accuracy gain. We evaluate Scientific Agents, an open-source corpus of 503 profession-specific AGENTS.md profiles, with Gemini 3.8 Flash via OpenRouter in the Pi agent harness. We compare matched profiles with four controls: a minimal baseline ("You are a helpful assistant"), the profile's opening role sentence, a generic scientific rigor guide, and a profile from an unrelated domain. Across nine text-based science benchmarks (4,531 sampled questions, 100 matched profiles), 4,488 items completed all five conditions after API-error retries, scored with automated, rule-based grading. The average profile-baseline accuracy difference is -0.6 percentage points (95% bootstrap interval [-1.5, +0.2] across fixed tasks), and no benchmark shows a statistically clear improvement. Matched profiles produced 1.5-2.3 times as many output tokens and cost 2.2-4.5 times more per successful call. On 60 tool-using BioMysteryBench bioinformatics problems (three runs each for baseline and profile), mean solve rates were 46.7% with the profile and 56.7% at baseline, a difference of -10.0 percentage points (95% interval [-16.7, -3.3]) driven by more frequent token- and time-limit stops under the profile. Longer prompts had one unexpected operational advantage: on SuperGPQA, frequent provider API drops left the short baseline with a correct first-pass answer on only 54.0% of items, against 71.6% with the profile. Generic and mismatched prompts were about as reliable, so this gain comes from prompt length or formatting rather than domain expertise. For the tested model and tasks, loading full profession profiles by default does not improve accuracy and costs considerably more; whether selective retrieval of profile sections or open-ended scientific tasks would change this remains to be tested.

</details>


### [265] [VeriHarness: Scaling Agentic Verification for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00972)
*Caiqi Zhang,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Zoey CuiZhu,Nigel Collier,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriHarness 将生成用的大模型改造成具备工作区、证据工具和可复用验证技能的智能体验证器，通过分歧解析与共识挑战提升长程任务的验证与选择能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 智能体承担日益复杂、长时程任务，验证其输出变得愈发困难；本文研究在固定基座模型、测试时无参考答案与评分标准条件下，如何强化验证能力。

Method: 作者发现分歧常暴露正确替代方案、共识可能掩盖错误，据此提出 VeriHarness：把底层 LLM 变为智能体验证器，配备工作区、证据工具和可复用验证技能；其中分歧解析器针对环境证据核验竞争性主张，共识挑战者检验共同主张并搜寻遗漏需求，其发现指导最终产物的选择与修订。

Result: 在五个长时程工作区基准与两个前沿模型上，VeriHarness 取得所评估基线中最高的选择分数；证据支持的修订进一步提升平均表现，相较单次 rollout 分别提升 6.2 分（Gemini 3.5 Flash）与 6.4 分（Claude Opus 4.8）。验证技能还能从失败反馈中自我改进。作者公开约 26,000 条 rollout 数据，成本超 10 万美元。

Conclusion: VeriHarness 是一种新颖且关键的智能体验证扩展方法，可显著提升长时程智能体的验证与最终产物选择，并为后续智能体验证研究提供大规模数据支持。

Abstract: As LLM agents undertake increasingly complex, long-horizon tasks, verifying their outputs becomes increasingly challenging. We study how verification capability can be strengthened with a fixed base model, without access to reference answers or grading rubrics at test time. Repeated sampling yields multiple rollouts that can contain complementary correct claims, but we need a reliable verification mechanism to determine which claims to trust. We first find that disagreement often exposes correct alternatives, while consensus can conceal errors. These observations motivate VeriHarness, which turns the underlying LLM a generator uses into an agentic verifier by giving it a workspace, evidence tools, and reusable verification skills. A disagreement resolver checks competing claims against environmental evidence, while a consensus challenger tests shared claims and searches for omitted requirements. Their findings guide the selection and revision of the final artifact. Across five long-horizon workspace benchmarks and two frontier models, VeriHarness achieves the highest selection scores among the evaluated baselines. Evidence-backed revision further improves average performance, bringing gains over a single rollout to 6.2 points with Gemini 3.5 Flash and 6.4 points with Claude Opus 4.8. We further show that verification skills can self-improve from failure feedback, demonstrating VeriHarness as a novel and critical approach for scaling long-horizon agentic verification. We release the full pool of approximately 26,000 rollouts across all five benchmarks and both models, produced at a cost of over $100,000, to support future research on agentic verification.

</details>


### [266] [Comedic Fool's Gold: Reward Exploits and Countermeasures in Conversational Humor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00197)
*Sam Larson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究对话幽默训练中的自动化奖励设计，揭示奖励投机漏洞及对策的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语言模型的对话幽默能力设计自动化奖励时，奖励容易被模型利用（reward exploit），因此需要研究这些漏洞并寻找有效对策。

Method: 探索两种奖励途径：基于嵌入的"意外感"奖励与预测观众娱乐度的奖励。通过受控测试检验漏洞，并设计对策（流畅性过滤器、跨说话人归一化笑声线索），并进行三次强化学习训练迭代评估奖励修订效果。

Result: 嵌入式意外奖励会同等接受打乱词序的回复；流畅性过滤器能识别打乱文本，但组合奖励会误拒部分妙语。观众模型的预测笑声易受说话人消息中的笑声线索影响；跨说话人归一化可阻断已知攻击，但未匹配表达仍可被利用。最终训练使综合评分提升0.0903，零分会话减少40%，但幽默专项提升未达预定目标。

Conclusion: 自动化奖励设计的核心挑战在于：对策必须阻断可利用的捷径，同时保留奖励本应鼓励的目标行为。

Abstract: We investigate automated rewards for training language models in conversational humor, focusing on reward exploits and countermeasures. Two approaches aim to capture understandable surprise and predicted audience amusement. Controlled tests show that an embedding-based surprise reward accepts word-shuffled replies as readily as witty ones. A fluency filter detects the shuffles, but the combined reward also rejects some witty replies and fails further validation. An audience model's predicted laughter is instead vulnerable to laughter cues in either speaker's messages. Normalizing these cues across speakers blocks the covered attacks, although unmatched expressions remain exploitable. Three reinforcement-learning runs evaluate training with successive reward revisions. The final run improves the combined evaluation score by 0.0903 and reduces zero-score sessions by 40%, but its humor-specific improvement remains below our preregistered target. These findings illustrate a broader challenge for automated reward design: countermeasures must block exploitable shortcuts while preserving the behavior the reward was intended to encourage.

</details>


### [267] [EviGraph: Proof-Carrying Selective Recommendation over Temporal Public-Service Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00212)
*Yixi Zhou,Sikun Wang,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EviGraph，通过区分"关键决策需求"与"可保留未决信息"，减少公共服务推荐中不必要的弃答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务推荐需要与所请求服务、范围及日期相匹配的证据，但若把每个缺失细节都视为决定性因素，会导致系统过度弃答、拒绝给出有用建议。因此需要明确哪些信息是决策必须确立的，而非一味增加验证。

Method: EviGraph 使用语言智能体将关键决策需求链接到时序知识图谱中的证据，并由确定性检查器判定推荐是否获得支持；核心在于区分关键决策需求与可暂未解决的信息。

Result: 在一个带可执行政策引用的双语香港公共服务基准上评估表明，这种区分可减少不必要的弃答；但额外的验证反而会撤回有支持的推荐，且未提升决策质量。

Conclusion: 可靠的证据驱动导航取决于明确"为做出决策必须确立什么"，而非单纯叠加更多验证步骤。

Abstract: Public-service recommendations require evidence that matches the requested service, scope, and date. Yet treating every missing detail as decisive can withhold useful recommendations. We introduce EviGraph, which distinguishes critical decision requirements from information that can remain unresolved. A language agent links these requirements to evidence in a temporal knowledge graph, while a deterministic checker establishes whether a recommendation is supported. Evaluation on a bilingual Hong Kong public-service benchmark with executable policy references shows that this distinction reduces unnecessary abstention. Additional verification, however, can withdraw supported recommendations without improving decision quality. These findings suggest that reliable evidence-based navigation depends on specifying what must be established for a decision, rather than simply adding more verification.

</details>


### [268] [A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Personalized Health Checkup Interpretation and Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01451)
*HyungJun Kim,Taehan Lee,Soojin Cheon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体LLM系统用于个性化体检结果解读，在120条韩语复合查询上优于单智能体，但延迟与成本增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化体检结果解读需要同时综合纵向病历、医学知识、生活方式建议与就医导航等多方面信息，单一智能体难以完整处理这种复合需求，因此需要多意图识别与任务分工的机制。

Method: 构建多智能体LLM系统：识别多个意图，将每个意图映射到任务专用智能体，并行执行后综合其输出。使用合成体检记录，构造120条包含2至4项需求的韩语复合查询，对比单智能体与多智能体设置下的回答，并采用加权LLM评审分数、另外三个LLM评审、两名人工评估者以及延迟与成本指标进行评估。

Result: 多智能体将加权LLM评审分数从1.695提升至1.797（p=0.027），另外三个LLM评审也显示一致提升（Δ=+0.111至+0.186，均p<0.05）。增益主要来自有用性、一致性以及对复合查询中每项需求的覆盖；数值准确性与依据性仅在四个评审中的一个显著改善，医学安全性无差异，关键失败率相近（单智能体15.0% vs 多智能体13.3%）。两名人工评估者在66.7%和68.3%的成对比较中更偏好多智能体。多智能体使延迟和成本分别增加1.31倍和2.02倍。探索性亚组分析显示改进集中在涉及个人记录查询的查询上。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统能提升个性化体检解读的覆盖度与一致性并获人工偏好，但未改善医学安全性与关键失败率，且带来明显的延迟和成本开销；收益主要体现在需要个人记录查询的复合查询中。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of health checkup results requires reasoning across longitudinal records, medical knowledge, lifestyle guidance, and healthcare navigation. We present a multi-agent large language model (LLM) system that identifies multiple intents, maps each to a task-specific agent, executes them in parallel, and synthesizes their outputs. We compared answers generated in Single Agent and Multi Agent settings on 120 Korean compound queries combining two to four requirements, using synthetic health checkup records. The Multi Agent improved the weighted LLM-judge score from 1.695 to 1.797 (p = 0.027), and three additional LLM judges showed consistent improvements ($Δ$ = +0.111 to +0.186, all p < 0.05). The gains came from usefulness, consistency, and the handling of every requirement in compound queries, whereas numerical accuracy and grounding improved significantly under only one of the four judges and medical safety did not differ, and critical failures occurred at similar rates (Single Agent 15.0% vs. Multi Agent 13.3%). Two human evaluators preferred Multi Agent in 66.7% and 68.3% of pairwise comparisons. Multi Agent execution increased latency and cost by 1.31$\times$ and 2.02$\times$, respectively. In exploratory subgroup analyses, the improvement was concentrated in queries involving personal-record lookup.

</details>


### [269] [Build2SPARQL: A Large-Scale Text-to-SPARQL Benchmark Dataset for Building Knowledge Graph Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00224)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Build2SPARQL，一个面向建筑知识图谱的大规模 text-to-SPARQL 基准，通过图谱遍历代码生成并验证查询、LLM 仅生成自然语言问题，覆盖 201 个建筑 KG，产出 6,136 条可执行 SPARQL 查询与 30,680 个问题，检索增强后可显著提升小模型准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统日益以语义知识图谱（如 Brick、ASHRAE 223P 本体）表示，为 AI 应用提供机器可读基础。将自然语言问题翻译为 SPARQL 可让建筑运维人员用语言智能体查询图谱，但该方向进展受限于自然语言/SPARQL 基准数据稀缺，缺乏大规模、高质量的训练与评测资源。

Method: 提出 Build2SPARQL 基准，采用"KG 接地"的生成管线：SPARQL 查询完全由图遍历代码生成并验证，保证查询正确性不依赖模型行为；大语言模型仅负责生成对应的自然语言问题。管线挖掘六类查询模式（线性链、分支、UNION、聚合、OPTIONAL、属性过滤），并将每个查询改写为五种词汇语体。基于 201 个建筑 KG（180 个 Brick、21 个 ASHRAE 223P）构建数据集。

Result: 共生成 6,136 条可执行 SPARQL 查询和 30,680 个自然语言问题。对 300 个问题进行双人人工验证，得到 98.8% 语义保真度、98.8% 自然度和 84.0% 操作合理性。在三个开放权重语言模型上的检索增强评估显示，精确匹配准确率从零样本的 0.2%–20% 提升到三样本检索的 56%–65%。

Conclusion: Build2SPARQL 通过图谱接地的方式规避了 LLM 生成查询带来的正确性风险，为建筑知识图谱的 text-to-SPARQL 研究提供了大规模、可执行且人工验证质量高的基准；检索增强能显著改善开放权重模型在该任务上的表现，表明该基准可作为语言智能体查询建筑运维数据的有效评测与训练资源。

Abstract: Building automation systems are increasingly represented as semantic knowledge graphs (KGs) using ontologies such as Brick and ASHRAE 223P, creating a machine-readable substrate for artificial-intelligence applications. One promising application is translating natural-language questions into SPARQL (text-to-SPARQL), which would let building operators query these graphs through language agents, but progress is limited by the scarcity of large natural-language/SPARQL benchmarks. This paper presents Build2SPARQL, a large-scale benchmark for building KGs generated by a KG-grounded pipeline: SPARQL queries are produced and validated entirely by graph-traversal code, while large language models generate only the natural-language questions, keeping query correctness independent of model behavior. The pipeline mines six query-pattern families -- linear chains, branching, UNION, aggregation, OPTIONAL, and attribute-filtered -- and phrases each query across five vocabulary registers. Applied to 201 building KGs (180 Brick, 21 ASHRAE 223P), it yields 6,136 executable SPARQL queries and 30,680 questions. A two-rater human validation of 300 questions found 98.8% semantic fidelity, 98.8% naturalness, and 84.0% operational plausibility. A retrieval-augmented evaluation across three open-weight language models raised exact-match accuracy from 0.2-20% (zero-shot) to 56-65% (three-shot retrieved).

</details>


### [270] [Robust Is Salient: An Informed Adversary Moves the Optimal Signal onto the Salience Pole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00233)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在带对抗者的受限信号博弈中，对抗鲁棒最优信号与既有研究中的"显著性极点"完全重合，二者仅在显著性—贝叶斯坐标未定义处存在差异，这构成对经验评估的结构性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察：当知情的对抗者与听众共享同一受限信号通道时，什么样的信号最能保护真相。作者希望检验对抗鲁棒最优与既有"显著性"模型（salience pole）之间的关系，并揭示这一重合对经验评估方法论的影响。

Method: 作者将对抗者引入一个强制选择任务（抽象自桌游《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》）。对抗者知道目标、观测信号，并以说服预算 β 为最强错误答案辩护。分析信号在 β 增大时的最优转移，并在 108 个确证条目与 200,000 条目池上，比较对抗鲁棒最优与此前工作中的显著性极点；同时用两种对抗设定测试七个语言模型在 108 个条目上的选择变化。

Result: 在 108 个确证条目上，对抗鲁棒最优与显著性极点完全一致；在 200,000 条目池中二者仅在 2,748 个条目上不同，且差异恰出现在显著性—贝叶斯坐标未定义处。随 β 增大，最优信号从后验最大化选项转向边际最大化选项；β=0 时复现原有 oracle 模型（听众温度 τ=1）。18.2% 的条目池存在有限预算下最优转移，且每条目的临界预算可精确求解。两种对抗设定使七个语言模型在 108 个条目中的 30 至 77 个上改变所选选项，超过精确的零效应率；但无法判定这种移动是朝向对抗感知最优还是朝向显著性，因为两者完全相同。

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒最优与显著性启发式目标在评估条目上高度重合，这是一种结构性限制而非零结果，使得无法通过测量区分模型是否具备对抗感知。作者建议在评估对抗感知之前，先廉价地检查鲁棒目标是否与启发式目标在评估条目上重合。

Abstract: When an informed adversary shares the audience of a constrained signalling channel, the signal that best protects the truth is the signal that best describes it. On 108 confirmatory items, the adversary-robust optimum aligns exactly with the salience pole from prior work. Across a 200,000-item pool, the two differ on only 2,748 items --- lying exactly where the prior salience-to-Bayes coordinate is undefined. Where defined, robustness is achieved by moving from Bayesian discrimination entirely to salience. We show this by introducing an adversary to a forced-choice task (abstracted from Deception: Murder in Hong Kong). The adversary knows the target, observes the signal, and argues for the strongest wrong answer using a persuasion budget, $β$. As $β$ grows, the optimal signal shifts from the posterior-maximizing option to the margin-maximizing one; at $β= 0$, the game reproduces the original oracle model with a listener temperature of $τ= 1$. This effect is real: 18.2 percent of the pool has an optimum that shifts under a finite budget, and each item's critical budget is exact. This coincidence structurally limits empirical evaluation. Two adversary framings change the chosen option of seven language models on 30 to 77 of 108 items against an exact no-effect rate. Yet, no measurement can determine whether this movement is toward the adversary-aware optimum or toward salience, because the two options are identical. This is a structural limit, not a null result. The diagnostic check is cheap: before evaluating adversary-awareness, verify whether the robust target coincides with a heuristic target on the evaluation items.

</details>


### [271] [Conflicting Supervision Moves Commitment, Not Capability: A 12.29σ arrangement effect that is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00234)
*Wenhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 学习率调度是决定数据排序效应大小的"平均算子"，而非背景条件：常数调度下排序效应显著，衰减调度下被压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究报告了衰减式学习率调度能缓和数据排序效应，但缺乏机理解释、因素分离和受控测量，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明一个界，将"数据安排"与"调度"作为两个相乘的独立因子（安排只作块周期，调度只作端点对任一时刻的最大权重）；用同一语料、同一预算、仅改变lr_scheduler_type的十种排序进行两次对照实验。

Result: 常数调度下排序效应在分配上跨度达0.2221、对比地板11.63，且随块结构单调；余弦调度下十组排序仅落在两个可区分状态。十二组实验中 acc_A+acc_B 在9.7%内恒定，而分配份额从0.04到0.87，说明12.29-sigma的排序切换在惯例无关指标下恰好为零。

Conclusion: "排序重要"与"排序不重要"是同一旋钮的两端；路径写入的是模型承诺采用哪种惯例，而任何精确匹配基准都无法察觉。在提示中标明惯例可消除该切换，达到并集上限的87.5%。

Abstract: "Train a model on the same problems written under two incompatible conventions, both correct, and ask what the ordering of that data writes into the parameters. The learning-rate schedule is not a background condition for that question. It is the averaging operator, and it decides the answer. We prove a bound in which the arrangement and the schedule enter the ordering effect as separate multiplied factors: the arrangement only as a block period, the schedule only as how much weight the endpoint can place on any one moment of the run. A decaying schedule cannot put a large step size and an uncontracted remainder at the same moment; a constant one does exactly that at the last step. That decay moderates ordering effects has been reported in pretraining; the mechanism, the separation, and a controlled measurement of both halves are ours. Ten orderings of one corpus, one budget, everything but the path held fixed, run twice under families differing in lr_scheduler_type and nothing else: at a constant rate the interior spans 0.2221 in allocation, 11.63 contrast floors, monotone in how blocked the arrangement is. Under the single cosine every published arm uses, the same ten arms occupy two distinguishable states where their own resolution would allow about ten. "Order matters" and "order does not matter" are the two ends of one knob. What the path writes is which convention the model commits to, and no exact-match benchmark can see it. Across twelve arms acc_A+acc_B is constant to within 9.7% while the allocation share runs 0.04 to 0.87, so the 12.29-sigma arrangement switch this paper measures is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic metric. That conservation is quoted from the decayed family throughout, the constant-rate one being a noisier place to read it. Marking the convention in the prompt collapses the switch and reaches 87.5% of the union ceiling."

</details>


### [272] [Knowing When to Yield: Grounded Arbitration of User Corrections in Text-Based Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00282)
*Yezhou Cheng,Runjia Du,Zeming Liu,Hang Lyu,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将具身智能体面对可能错误的人类纠正时的"接不接受"问题形式化为接仲裁任务，提出GAVA方法，通过观察受限证据、合法探针与一步期望损失规则，在成本约束下实现选择性信息获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当人的纠正可能是错误时，具身智能体应如何回应？论文把该问题形式化为在"接受、拒绝、检查世界、询问说话者"之间做选择的地面化纠正仲裁，并关心在声明成本下如何平衡纠正准确性与交互成本。

Method: GAVA方法包含观察受限证据、合法探针，以及一步期望损失规则。在纯文本ALFWorld中，用162个检查点生成972对真/假干预进行评测；引入仅在训练期使用的探索性物体位置先验（语义GAVA）；实验设计包含冻结策略、成本、基线与多重性计划，并采用检查点自助法置信区间。

Result: 完整局部检查使GAVA与"总是验证"达到100%纠正准确率，确立证据契约而非相对优势。同回合执行中GAVA比"总是验证"降低交互成本，但在完美说话者下与某成本阈值持平。训练期先验在340个未见场景上把交互成本与声明联合成本相对均匀先验GAVA分别降低0.490和0.420；冻结后在77个不重叠已见检查点（308场景）复现为0.595和0.517，95%检查点自助置信区间均不含零。语义GAVA两个队列各出现4个事实错误，准确率98.8%与98.7%，所有方法均完成任务。

Conclusion: 结果支持在声明成本下结合语义先验进行选择性信息获取，但未确立环境信息价值相对澄清提问的普遍优势。研究限于规范化声明、完整符号观测与受控说话者，未评估人类参与者、视觉输入或物理机器人。

Abstract: How should an embodied agent respond when a person's correction may be wrong? We formulate grounded correction arbitration as a choice among accepting, rejecting, inspecting the world, and asking the speaker. GAVA implements this interface with observation-bounded evidence, legal probes, and a one-step expected-loss rule. In text-only ALFWorld, 162 checkpoints produce 972 paired true and false interventions. Complete local inspections give GAVA and always verify 100 percent correction accuracy, establishing the evidence contract rather than a comparative advantage. In same-episode execution, GAVA reduces interaction cost against always verify but ties a cost threshold under a perfect speaker. An exploratory training-only object-location prior lowers interaction and declared joint cost on 340 unseen scenarios by 0.490 and 0.420 relative to uniform GAVA. After freezing the policy, costs, baselines, and multiplicity plan, the gains replicate on 77 non-overlapping seen checkpoints, covering 308 scenarios: 0.595 and 0.517, with both 95 percent checkpoint-bootstrap confidence intervals excluding zero. Joint cost also improves over an identical-prior fixed policy, while the matched calibrated no-VOI comparison remains inconclusive. Semantic GAVA makes four factual errors in each cohort, corresponding to 98.8 percent and 98.7 percent accuracy, and all methods complete every task. Results support selective information gathering with semantic priors under declared costs, but do not establish a general advantage of environmental value of information over clarification. The study uses normalized claims, complete symbolic observations, and controlled speakers; it evaluates neither human participants, visual input, nor physical robots.

</details>


### [273] [Rules to Tools: Executable Checks for LLM Agents in Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00313)
*Jingjie Ning,Guojiang Zhao,Chen Xu,Shanshan Zhong,Xiaochuan Li,Ji Zeng,Guolin Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Rules to Tools (R2T)：将公开科学需求转化为可调用执行检查，用于科学编码智能体的程序修复。匹配对照实验显示，在有准备的检查下修复完成度略优于纯文本（任务簇置信区间跨零），但成本与收益呈任务依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学编码智能体收到以文字形式给出的方程、边界条件与输出要求，还必须评估其修改后的程序。纯文本需求容易产生歧义或遗漏，作者希望探究：把公开科学需求预先编译成可调用的可执行检查（工具），能否改善智能体的程序修复表现，并考察其对智能体侧输出开销与公共 CPU 消耗的影响。

Method: 提出 Rules to Tools (R2T)：为公开科学需求提供预先准备的可执行检查，并以可调用实现的形式交给智能体。实验采用匹配的 SciCode 修复组设计，控制书面检查、起始程序、模型与预算一致，比较"文本组"与"工具组"。设置两个 task-ID 队列（8 个 ID 与更大队列）、共享定义的 SciCode 队列、以及五个开发期暴露且更换起始程序的任务；另设 source-through-Python 新臂与匹配 PDE 对比。统计上采用任务簇 bootstrap 估计差异区间。

Result: 两个队列中完全修复为文本 26/30、工具 29/30；三个任务 ID 偏好工具，一个偏好文本，十一个持平。8-ID 队列为 13/16 对 15/16，差异的任务簇 bootstrap 95% 区间为 [-12.5, 43.75] 个百分点。更大共享定义队列两组均 13/24 持平。五个开发期暴露且更换起始程序的任务为 3/10 对 7/10。工具组在任务 17、77、11 上占优；初始检查标记任务 17，对 77 与 11 未报告违规；任务 37 偏好文本且无初始违规报告。新的 source-through-Python 臂同样达到 15/16，与专用命令总体持平。匹配 PDE 比较中，详细文本 23/24、检查 24/24，且检查组报告模型输出低 31.2%。

Conclusion: 预先准备的可执行检查可在部分科学修复任务上带来小幅、任务依赖的收益，并降低智能体侧的报告输出量，但会提高公共 CPU 使用；整体差异在统计上不显著（置信区间跨零）。作者据此刻画了准备式检查下的修复结果与智能体成本权衡。

Abstract: Scientific coding agents receive equations, boundary conditions, and output requirements in writing, then must assess the programs they revise. Rules to Tools (R2T) supplies prepared executable checks of public scientific requirements. Matched SciCode repair groups share written checks, starting programs, model, and budgets; the tool group receives a callable implementation. Across two task-ID cohorts, complete repair is 26/30 with text and 29/30 with the prepared checks. Three task IDs favor tools, one favors text, and eleven tie. The eight-ID cohort scores 13/16 versus 15/16, with a task-cluster bootstrap 95% interval of [-12.5, 43.75] percentage points for the difference. The larger shared-definition SciCode cohort ties at 13/24 per group. Five development-exposed tasks with alternate starting programs score 3/10 versus 7/10. The tool group favors tasks 17, 77, and 11; initial checks flag task 17 and report no violation for tasks 77 and 11. Task 37 favors text and has no initial reported violation. A fresh source-through-Python arm also reaches 15/16, matching the dedicated command's aggregate. In a matched PDE comparison, detailed text scores 23/24 and checks score 24/24, with 31.2% lower reported model output for checks. Agent-side output savings vary by cohort, while public CPU use rises in both task-ID cohorts. These results measure task-dependent repair outcomes and agent-side costs with prepared checks.

</details>


### [274] [Predictive Credit: Measuring What Scientific Explanations Add to Experimental Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00314)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出用配对预测协议来测量"研究智能体"对计划实验的解释是否具有预测性信用（predictive credit），在336个前瞻状态、12个Tox21端点与24个OpenML任务上检验解释、捐赠者上下文与描述之间的差异，但主要信用判定与点精度增益均未获确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究智能体会解释其计划实验，但这些解释是否真正具有预测价值（即为预测结果带来可归因的增益）尚不明确。作者希望建立一个可检验的协议，把"解释质量"与预测信用区分开，避免仅凭解释表面合理性来判断其价值。

Method: 采用配对预测设计：在共享同一干预、预测者与结果的条件下，变化实验描述、匹配解释与捐赠者上下文。设置五项检查（承诺、交付、预测增益、对齐、已知信号采纳）。评测覆盖336个前瞻状态、12个Tox21端点、24个OpenML任务，并使用DeepSeek V4 Pro、DeepSeek V4 Flash及直接文本Flash进行复现，同时加入研究者手工机制阳性对照。

Result: v5冻结信用判定不结论；Tox21预注册ROC AUC区间伤害检验未通过（D−M = −.0026，95%区间[−.0174, .0104]），OpenML联合形成、点等价与可重复性规则也未通过。描述之外的匹配点精度增益未获确认，Tox21/OpenML种子-捐赠者区间跨零。在DeepSeek V4 Pro下，匹配卡与捐赠者卡分别将次要Tox21漂移降低64.5%与59.1%；Flash重放使匹配点MAE从.01823升至.02020且未达区间等价。OpenML全卡分配使名义80%区间扩大21%，覆盖率49.3%（描述与内容为51.4%，66/144张卡）。直接文本Flash交付全部144条注记但无可检测的匹配点增益。阳性对照将点MAE相对描述降低2.60个百分点。

Conclusion: 该协议为研究智能体基准与科学预测提供了一种测量预测信用的方法，但在所测试的捐赠者分辨率下，自然解释的信用仍未得到确认；同时提示名义区间可能存在覆盖不足与膨胀问题。

Abstract: Research agents explain planned experiments. We measure predictive credit with paired forecasts sharing an intervention, forecaster, and outcome while varying description, matched explanation, and donor context. Five checks track commitment, delivery, predictive gain, alignment, and known-signal uptake. Across 336 prospective states in controlled learning, 12 Tox21 endpoints, and 24 OpenML tasks, v5's frozen credit decision was inconclusive. Tox21's preregistered ROC AUC interval-score harm test was unmet ($D-M=-.0026$, 95 percent interval [$-.0174$, .0104]); OpenML's joint formation, point-equivalence, and repeatability rule was unmet. Matched point-accuracy gains over description remained unconfirmed, and Tox21/OpenML seed-donor intervals spanned zero. Under requested DeepSeek V4 Pro, matched and donor cards reduced secondary Tox21 drift by 64.5 and 59.1 percent. A DeepSeek V4 Flash replay raised matched point MAE from .01823 to .02020 and missed matched-donor interval-score equivalence. OpenML full-card assignment widened nominal 80 percent intervals by 21 percent, with 49.3 percent coverage versus 51.4 percent for description and content in 66/144 cards. Direct-text Flash delivered all 144 notes without detectable matched point-accuracy gain. A researcher-authored mechanism positive control lowered point MAE by 2.60 percentage points versus description. The protocol measures predictive credit for research-agent benchmarks and scientific forecasting; natural-explanation credit remained unconfirmed at the tested donor resolutions.

</details>


### [275] [ContractRL: Shielded Group-Relative Policy Optimization for Auditable Tool-Call Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00328)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Yina Sa,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ContractRL，将验证器引导的JSON修复建模为有界决策过程，通过契约约束的序贯修复显著提升语义成功率并降低生成token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化工具调用在少量字段违反schema或执行契约时即失败，而完全重新生成整个对象会扩大动作空间并难以审计反复修复过程。

Method: 将修复建模为有界决策过程：每步策略观察候选、类型化验证器反馈、JSON Pointer、不可变修复历史与剩余预算；用契约导出的动作掩码在确定性验证器执行转换前过滤非法RFC-6902操作；设计契约约束的组相对目标以处理patch、retry与abstention决策，并将规范目标与语义标签排除在在线状态之外直至轨迹冻结。

Result: 在相同验证器信息下，ContractRL语义成功率达0.9362、生成34.4 token；Patch-SFT为0.9076/44.9 token，完全重生成仅0.9148/137.2 token。策略优化将语义成功从0.9186提升至0.9375；三seed配对评估相对Patch-SFT提升+0.0396（95% CI [+0.0137,+0.0662], p=0.0039）。

Conclusion: 反馈、动作掩码、预算与schema漂移分析表明增益来自局部化修正；对抗与多轮评估刻画了残余失效模式。该工作为结构化工具调用修复提供了高效且可审计的方案。

Abstract: Structured tool calls often fail after only a small number of fields violate a schema or an execution contract. Regenerating the complete object enlarges the action surface and makes repeated repair difficult to audit. We introduce ContractRL, a contract-constrained sequential repair protocol that models verifier-guided JSON repair as a bounded decision process. At each step the policy observes the candidate, typed verifier feedback, JSON Pointer, immutable repair history, and remaining budget; a contract-derived action mask filters malformed or prohibited RFC-6902 operations before a deterministic validator performs the transition. We specify a contract-constrained group-relative objective for patch, retry, and abstention decisions while keeping canonical targets and semantic labels outside the online state until trace freeze.
