Under identical verifier information, ContractRL attains 0.9362 semantic success with 34.4 generated tokens, compared with 0.9076 and 44.9 tokens for Patch-SFT and 0.9148 and 137.2 tokens for full regeneration over 192 cases per seed and five seeds. Policy optimization improves semantic success from 0.9186 for supervised ContractRL to 0.9375. A separate three-seed paired evaluation against Patch-SFT yields a semantic difference of +0.0396 (95% CI $[+0.0137,+0.0662], p=0.0039$). Feedback, action-mask, budget, and schema-shift analyses connect these gains to localized correction, while adversarial and multi-turn evaluations characterize the remaining failure modes.

</details>


### [276] [Mathematical Transfer in LLMs Follows Reasoning Approach More Than Topic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00331)
*Sajad Goudarzi,Samaneh Zamanifard,Seyed Amin Seyed Haeri,Moloud Nasiri,Hamed Rahimian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在数学训练数据选择中，按推理方法匹配（而非按主题匹配）能带来更强的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择数学训练数据时，通常按主题组织（如给概率目标配概率例题），但作者想探究是否按推理方法匹配（即使数学领域不同）能带来更好的迁移。

Method: 采用两个平衡的 2×2 设计：概率与组合数学 × 不变推理与双重计数（2000题）；数论与几何 × 补集与鸽巢推理（800题）。每个格子轮流作为留出目标，同方法（SA）来源共享目标方法但换主题，同主题（ST）来源共享主题但换方法。每个来源在每种角色中出现一次，从而消除加性来源质量效应。在五个基础模型、每个设计三个训练种子上评估。

Result: 在所有40个种子汇总的模型—目标对比中，SA 均优于 ST。主设计中模型级优势为8.2至16.2个百分点（均值10.8），第二设计中为12.0至16.0（均值14.3）；全部十个模型级95%置信区间均不含零。虽 ST 来源在嵌入与词汇相似度上更接近目标，但 SA 优势与其相反。

Conclusion: 推理方法比主题是更有效的数学迁移匹配标准。

Abstract: When selecting mathematical training data for LLMs, a natural organizing principle is topic: probability examples for probability targets. An alternative is reasoning approach: worked solutions that share a solution method with the target, even when the mathematical domain differs. We ask which relation produces greater transfer after fine-tuning. We evaluate two counterbalanced $2\times2$ designs: probability and combinatorics crossed with invariant reasoning and double counting (2,000 problems), and number theory and geometry crossed with complement and pigeonhole reasoning (800 problems). In each design, every cell serves as the held-out target in turn: same-approach (SA) sources share the target's method but change the topic, while same-topic (ST) sources share the topic but change the method. Every source appears once in each role, so additive source-quality effects cancel from the equally weighted aggregate contrast. Across five base models and three training seeds per design, SA outperforms ST in all 40 seed-pooled model--target comparisons. Model-level advantages range from 8.2 to 16.2 percentage points in the primary design (mean: 10.8) and from 12.0 to 16.0 in the second design (mean: 14.3); all ten model-level 95% confidence intervals exclude zero. In both designs, ST sources are more similar to targets under embedding and lexical measures, so the SA advantage runs opposite to the measured ordering of statement-level resemblance. These findings identify reasoning approach as a more effective matching criterion than topic for mathematical transfer across the evaluated topic--approach combinations.

</details>


### [277] [Fault-Tolerant Budget Conservation in Distributed Multi-Agent Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00349)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文形式化了分布式多智能体委派中的容错预算守恒问题，提出基于独占托管信用的委派DAG机制，并通过形式化证明、TLA+检查与崩溃注入实验验证其安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源限制正成为AI智能体委派的授权边界，但父子分配约束、仿射对象与分布式托管本身无法防止在消息丢失、超时后完成、消息重复、分支分区或DAG汇合别名同一谱系时出现超支。

Method: 将预算量化为资源向量，由独占托管信用表示并在委派DAG中流转；分派前将信用转换为绑定谱系、纪元、归一化效果、最大费用、接收方与幂等键的操作预留，并持久化带隔离的签名分派许可，由网关在首次接受前校验。不确定效果在认证结算、经验证的无效果证明或永久退役前持续计费。

Result: 证明了所有权划分、账本与效果守恒、后代非放大、至多一次结算、延迟完成安全性与分区约束等性质，并给出分区本地可用性需要独占预分配的不可能性结果；TLA+有界检查、独立JavaScript探索器与崩溃注入的双进程SQLite实验覆盖了声明范围并检测出超时退款与历史证书校验变异体。

Conclusion: 该机制在评估的崩溃、重试、重复、分区、汇合与延迟完成调度下保持了已发放预算的上界。

Abstract: Resource limits are becoming an authorization boundary for AI agents that delegate work across concurrent and failure-prone workers. Parent-child allocation constraints, affine objects, and distributed escrow do not by themselves prevent overspend when replies are lost, effects complete after timeout, messages repeat, branches partition, or DAG joins alias one lineage. We formalize fault-tolerant budget conservation for distributed multi-agent delegation. Budgets are quantized resource vectors represented by exclusive escrow credits that move through a delegation DAG. Before dispatch, a branch converts credit into an operation reservation bound to lineage, epoch, normalized effect, maximum charge, receiver, and idempotency key. It persists a signed dispatch permit with quarantine; the gateway verifies that permit before first acceptance. Uncertain effects remain charged until authenticated settlement, a fenced authoritative no-effect proof, or permanent retirement. We prove ownership partition, ledger and effect conservation, descendant non-amplification, at-most-once settlement, late-completion safety, and partition confinement under explicit mediation, durability, authentication, normalization, and gateway assumptions. An indistinguishability result shows that partition-local availability requires exclusive preallocation. Bounded TLA+ checking, an independent JavaScript explorer, and crash-injected two-process SQLite experiments exercise the declared scope and detect timeout-refund and historical-certificate-validation mutants. The mechanism preserves the issued budget bound across the evaluated crash, retry, duplicate, partition, join, and late-completion schedules.

</details>


### [278] [JusticeAxis: Benchmarking Legal Judgment between Rigid Rule Application and Ungrounded Discretion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00353)
*Zhengkai Tu,Mingda Zhang,Zijia Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Jimmy Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将法律判决形式化为"参照锚定"任务，提出JusticeAxis基准（18国256个真实刑事案件，含音视频与文本证据及律师撰写的三种判决）与JusticeAgent框架（要素智能体确立事实、法官智能体在技能指导下适用法律，技能经贝叶斯可信界筛选），并发现失败方向随模型规模反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律判决方法在僵化的法条匹配与无根据的自由裁量之间摇摆，基准仅评分标签或评分表，且能提供平衡的经验性判断未被验证，缺乏同时锚定法条与具体情境的评价与建模方式。

Method: 将法律判决形式化为参照锚定任务，产出同时依赖法条与情境的单一决策；构建JusticeAxis基准，包含来自18个国家的256个真实刑事案件，含音频、图像、文本证据，并为每案提供三份律师撰写的判决（一份实际判决与两类失败判决）；提出JusticeAgent：要素智能体确立事实，法官智能体在携带情境经验的技能下适用法律，技能从执行轨迹中蒸馏并通过贝叶斯可信界准入。

Result: 实验显示失败方向随规模反转：开源权重骨干偏向无依据的理由，前沿模型偏向法条默认答案；JusticeAgent作为简单有效的插件，可将冻结的开源权重骨干提升至商业模型水平。

Conclusion: 法律判决需同时锚定法条与情境，参照锚定任务与JusticeAxis基准提供了可验证的评测方式，JusticeAgent以技能蒸馏与贝叶斯准入机制有效弥补开源模型能力差距。

Abstract: A sound judgment applies the law to established facts and weighs the circumstances in which they arose. However, existing methods swing between rigid statute matching and ungrounded discretion, benchmarks score a label or a rubric, and the experience that would supply the balance stays unverified. We formalize legal judgment as a reference-anchored task, whose object is a single decision that stays tied to the statute and to the circumstances at once. We introduce JusticeAxis, 256 real-world criminal cases from 18 countries with audio, image, and text evidence, and three lawyer-written judgments for every case: the recorded one and one for each failure. We further propose JusticeAgent, a harness whose element agents establish the facts and whose judge agent applies the law under skills carrying experience of the circumstances. Skills are distilled from execution trajectories and admitted only under Bayesian credible bounds. Experiments show that failure turns direction with scale: open-weight backbones drift to unsupported grounds, frontier models to the statutory default. We further verify that JusticeAgent, as a simple yet effective plugin, carries a frozen open-weight backbone to commercial level. Project resources are available at https://github.com/beita6969/JusticeAxis.

</details>


### [279] [What Should an Agent Remember? Disentangling Retention from Retrieval in Bounded-Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00366)
*Juli Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个流式召回基准，通过交叉保留规则与选择规则，分离并量化了记忆保留与查询感知选择对召回性能的独立贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评估常将"保留"与"选择"两个决策混淆，比较的方法在保留与选择上均存在差异，导致无法辨别性能提升究竟来自何处，因此需要一个能解耦二者的评测框架。

Method: 构建流式召回基准，交叉组合不同保留规则与选择规则，并在相同的300个带种子episode上评估所有条件；在固定访问（access）的条件下比较查询感知选择的影响，并在有界保留条件下分析失败原因；另在SQuAD上重复评估以验证结论。

Result: 固定访问时，查询感知选择将必需事实召回提升15.5个百分点（95% CI: 12.8–18.2）；而同时改变历史访问的混合比较报告68.7个百分点优势，其中53.2个百分点归因于访问。有界保留下，查询感知、稠密与oracle选择均达到保留上限；有界近期条件下319次失败全部源于驱逐而非排序错误；目标过于久远时召回降至0%。SQuAD上保留上限依然成立，且稠密检索在自然文本上可超越词法检索。

Conclusion: 有界记忆评测应固定访问条件，并分别报告保留与选择两方面的结果，以避免混淆归因。

Abstract: A persistent agent must decide both what to retain as information arrives and what to surface once a query appears, yet memory evaluations can confound these decisions by comparing methods that differ in both retention and selection. We build a streaming-recall benchmark crossing retention and selection rules and evaluate every condition on the same 300 seeded episodes. Holding access fixed, query-aware selection improves required-fact recall by 15.5 percentage points (95% CI: 12.8 to 18.2), whereas a mixed comparison that also changes history access reports a 68.7-point advantage, of which 53.2 points are attributable to access. Under bounded retention, query-aware, dense, and oracle selection reach the retention ceiling, and all 319 observed failures in the bounded recency condition are caused by eviction rather than ranking errors. Recall falls to 0% as targets recede sufficiently far into the past. Repeating the evaluation on SQuAD preserves the retention ceiling while showing that dense retrieval can outperform lexical retrieval on natural text. These results show that bounded-memory evaluations should hold access fixed and report retention and selection separately.

</details>


### [280] [When Harnesses Lose the Signal: Causal Evaluation of Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00372)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Xuan Chen,Ke Chao,Ming Cui,Feifei Qian,Chaoyang Mei,Fanlin Meng,Ziming Yu,Junxi Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将LLM智能体的"恢复"行为建模为因果决策问题，提出因果干预路由器CIR，选择性触发恢复，在ALFWorld上提升成功率3个百分点且不损害健康轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖外部工具（harness）在模型与环境间传递信息并从执行错误中恢复，但恢复效果通常仅用平均任务成功率衡量。这掩盖了一个核心矛盾：同一恢复操作可能挽救失败轨迹，也可能破坏本可成功的轨迹。因此需要区分"挽救"与"伤害"，并判断何时介入才值得。

Method: 作者把恢复问题表述为因果决策问题：从相同执行状态出发，对比"有恢复"与"无恢复"两种情形，将挽救与损害分离，并研究恢复价值随时间的变化。在此基础上提出因果干预路由器（CIR）——一个轻量策略，仅利用恢复前可获得的信息来判断是否值得介入。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B驱动的长时程ALFWorld任务上，CIR将成功率从70.33%提升至73.33%，提高3.00个百分点；且对所有观测正确的评估轨迹均不做干预（保持其原状）。额外对照实验表明，恢复带来的收益不能仅由环境返回的新观测解释。

Conclusion: 该工作为恢复机制提供了更细粒度的因果评估方式，并给出了一种有选择性地应用恢复的实用方法，避免了对健康轨迹的干扰。

Abstract: Large language model agents rely on external harnesses to pass information between the model and its environment and to recover from execution errors. Yet recovery is usually judged only by average task success. This hides an important tension. The same operation can rescue a failing trajectory or disrupt one that would otherwise succeed. We frame recovery as a causal decision problem. Starting from the same execution state, we compare what happens with and without recovery, separate rescue from harm, and study how the value of recovery changes over time. We then introduce the Causal Intervention Router (CIR), a lightweight policy that uses information available before recovery to decide when intervention is worthwhile. On long-horizon ALFWorld tasks with Qwen3-14B, CIR raises success from 70.33% to 73.33%, a gain of 3.00 percentage points. It leaves all evaluated trajectories with correct observations untouched. Additional controls show that the benefit of recovery cannot be explained solely by the new observation returned by the environment. These results provide a practical way to evaluate recovery and apply it selectively.

</details>


### [281] [Benchmarking Prompt Optimization of Large Language Models With Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00416)
*Timothée Lesort,Alejandra López de Aberasturi Gómez,Tristan Karch,Tom Veniat,Philippe Modard,Karl Tuyls,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于118个国际象棋谜题（1,118 Lichess puzzles）的基准，用于研究冻结LLM的自动提示优化（APO），评测6种APO算法在8个模型上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力提升，评测愈发困难：基准易饱和、公开测试集有污染风险、困难任务评测成本高。这些问题在APO中因反复评测而被放大，因此需要一个廉价、确定、够难且可更新的基准。

Method: 构建国际象棋基准（1,118 Lichess谜题），在冻结LLM上优化提示而不更新权重。利用精确匹配打分、引擎评估替代着法、可调节难度与可更新题目供给；并将解题收益与同领域短局博弈rollout关联。在8个目标模型上评测6种APO算法，测量基线强度、优化响应度及提示迁移性。

Result: 基准具有挑战性（最强模型Gemini 3.5 Flash作为元模型也仅解出约55%谜题）；具备区分度，可揭示不同方法与模型下的提升、不变与退化；可再生，新谜题降低污染风险并保持提升空间；成本低，完整研究约800美元。

Conclusion: 国际象棋是适合APO的基准，兼具挑战性、区分度、可再生性与低成本，并已公开谜题、优化与评测代码及数据集更新脚本。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models becomes increasingly challenging as their capabilities advance: benchmarks can saturate, public test sets risk contamination, and assessing harder tasks can require expensive grading or execution infrastructure. These challenges are amplified in automatic prompt optimization (APO), where evaluation is repeated throughout the search for better prompts. Studying APO therefore requires a benchmark that is cheap and deterministic to score, hard enough to leave room for improvement, and renewable as models evolve. We introduce a chess benchmark built from 1,118 Lichess puzzles to study APO for frozen LLMs: we optimize their prompts without updating their model weights. Chess combines inexpensive exact-match scoring, engine-based evaluation of alternative moves, and a renewable supply of problems with adjustable difficulty. Unlike evaluations that report only success on isolated test items, the benchmark also connects puzzle-solving gains to short game-play rollouts within the same domain. We use it to evaluate six APO algorithms on eight target models, measuring not only baseline strength but also how much each model responds to optimization and whether optimized prompts transfer across models and to game play. Chess is thus a well-suited benchmark for APO: it is (i) challenging, as even the strongest evaluated model, Gemini 3.5 Flash (used as the meta-model), solves only about 55\% of puzzles; (ii) discriminative, revealing gains, unchanged performance, and regressions across methods and models; (iii) renewable, with fresh puzzles to reduce contamination risk and adjustable difficulty to maintain headroom as models improve; and (iv) affordable, as the complete study runs for around \$800. We release the puzzles, optimization and evaluation code, and dataset-renewal scripts (https://github.com/imec-ailabs/Automatic-Prompt-Optimization-with-Chess).

</details>


### [282] [JevSpawn: Adaptive Agentic Inference through Compositional Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00437)
*Haoyang Su,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 JevSpawn,一种将自然语言任务规范与有限概率探索相连接的组合式策略,通过并行动作生成与反馈驱动的分支选择,在不额外训练的情况下提升 LLM 智能体的任务表现与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体逐 token 生成推理与动作,长程交互缓慢且计算昂贵;Jev 类模型可快速进行有限域上的概率预测,但要求预先指定动作域,难以适应需从自然语言指令中推导并动态调整动作的自主任务求解场景。

Method: JevSpawn 是一种组合式策略:将自然语言任务规范映射到有限概率探索空间;采用并行动作孵化(parallel action spawning)配合反馈驱动的分支选择;支持表示修订以及从保留备选中恢复。通过共享动作结构与模型前缀,减少重复生成与上下文计算,且无需额外训练。

Result: 在八项基准任务上,与七种智能体基线及一个 TypeSafe Jev 变体对比,实验表明 JevSpawn 在任务性能上有所提升,且导航速度更快。

Conclusion: JevSpawn 是将自然语言任务规范与有限概率探索结合的可行方案,为结构化智能体推理提供了一条有前景的路径,兼顾性能提升与效率优化。

Abstract: LLM agents generate intermediate reasoning and actions token by token, making extended interactions slow and computationally expensive. Jev-style models offer fast probabilistic predictions over finite fields, but require those fields to be specified in advance. This requirement limits autonomous task solving, where the available actions must be derived from natural language instructions and adapted through interaction. We introduce JevSpawn, a compositional policy that connects natural language task specifications to finite probabilistic exploration. Parallel action spawning is coupled with feedback driven branch selection, representation revision, and recovery from retained alternatives. Shared action structure and model prefixes reduce repeated generation and context computation without additional training. Evaluations on eight benchmark tasks against seven agent baselines and a TypeSafe Jev variant establish JevSpawn as a promising approach to structured agentic inference, with improved task performance and faster navigation.

</details>


### [283] [Frozen Scenes, Shifting Winners: Configuration Fragility in Text-to-3D Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00447)
*Anson Y. Lam,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计文本到3D渲染图像评测中协议因素（相机设置、字幕措辞）对排行榜的影响：在300个冻结场景、六种生成器上系统变化渲染与字幕因素，发现配置方差常超过生成器间方差，排名虽较稳定但多数评测器的最优点估计赢家会改变；经同时推断后无反转成立，赢家变化仅是描述性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到3D评测依赖渲染图像，而相机设置与字幕措辞本身成为测量协议的一部分。若这些非生成器因素未被控制，排行榜可能反映协议而非生成器优劣，因此需要审计评测结论的稳定性、决策不确定性与敏感性。

Method: 在六个生成器的300个冻结场景上，变化八种渲染与字幕因素，评测19个对齐评测器加一个感知质量对照，并施加四种针对性场景退化。通过配置方差与生成器间方差比较、prompt-bootstrap下界、排名点估计赢家变化、成对协议边际包络及同时推断来量化影响。

Result: 17/19对齐评测器的峰值配置方差超过生成器间方差，11/19的prompt-bootstrap下界大于1。排名比分数更稳定，但18/19评测器在某配置下最优点估计赢家发生变化；成对协议边际包络给出方向保持的比较。部分配对点估计区间相反，但在全搜索同时推断下无反转成立。敏感性方面，无评测器（含无提示对照）在布局打乱上超过67%的平局调整方向判别，属诊断性而非人工验证真值。

Conclusion: 该审计区分了分数稳定性、决策不确定性与针对性敏感性，建议同时报告（生成器、分数、卡片ID），并采用协议依赖的比较方式与考虑选择效应的不确定性。观察到的赢家变化是描述性的，而非确证生成器优越性的改变。

Abstract: Can a text-to-3D leaderboard change when every generated scene stays fixed? We audit this question for rendered-image evaluation, where camera settings and caption wording become part of the measurement protocol. Across 300 frozen scenes from six generators, we vary eight render and caption factors for 19 alignment evaluators plus one perceptual-quality control, then test four targeted scene degradations. Peak configuration variance exceeds between-generator variance for 17/19 alignment evaluators, with prompt-bootstrap lower bounds above 1 for 11/19. Rankings are more stable than scores, yet 18/19 evaluators change their point-estimate winner under some configuration. Pairwise protocol margin envelopes show which comparisons keep their direction across the tested settings. Selected pairs have opposite pointwise intervals, but no reversal survives simultaneous inference over the full search. Thus the observed winner changes are descriptive, not confirmed changes in generator superiority. Sensitivity remains separate: no evaluator, even the prompt-free control, exceeds 67% tie-adjusted directional discrimination on layout scrambling, which is diagnostic rather than human-validated ground truth. The audit separates score stability, decision uncertainty, and targeted sensitivity, and recommends reporting (generator, score, card ID) with protocol-dependent comparisons and selection-aware uncertainty.

</details>


### [284] [Ontology-Based Contextual AI Evaluations (OB-CAIE) Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00529)
*Julie Krugler Hollek,Michael Zargham,Mala Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OB-CAIE 方法论，用领域本体与评估过程本体清晰界定 AI 评估的测试范围，提升科学性、可复现性并合理平衡人工与自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 评估存在测试覆盖不明确、缺乏科学严谨性、人工专业知识与自动化难以平衡以及测试环境可复现性差等问题。OB-CAIE 旨在通过科学方法的第一步——明确界定"要测试什么"来弥补这些不足。

Method: OB-CAIE 方法论采用两个本体刻画可处理的问题空间：领域特定本体（DSO）回答"测什么"（what），评估过程本体（EPO）回答"怎么测"（how）。一个 OB-CAIE 问题空间可支持一项或多项 AI 评估，并在特定节点引入人类判断，以科学方式处理人类反馈不可替代的复杂领域。

Result: 该方法使评估覆盖范围得到清晰定义，实现了人工与自动化的科学平衡；其关键优势在于失败点可在规范的 OB-CAIE 问题空间内被追踪、可视化与分析。

Conclusion: OB-CAIE 通过本体驱动的测试范围界定，增强了 AI 评估的科学严谨性与可复现性，为复杂领域中人类判断的合理介入提供了结构化框架。

Abstract: The ontology-based contextual AI evaluation (OB-CAIE) methodology was developed to address a lack of scientific rigor that arises from unclear testing coverage, to balance human expertise and automations, and to address a lack of reproducibility of AI evaluation testing environments. OB-CAIE strengthens the current state of AI evaluations by addressing the first step in the scientific method by clearly defining what will be tested. Two ontologies represent the tractable problem space in the OB-CAIE methodology: the Domain-Specific Ontology (DSO) and the Evaluation Process Ontology (EPO). The DSO is the what; the EPO is the how. An OB-CAIE problem space can be used for one or multiple AI evaluations. The OB-CAIE methodology allows for human judgment at specific points, in scientifically grounded ways, and in complex subject areas where human feedback is genuinely irreducible or machine irreplaceable. A key advantage of the OB-CAIE methodology is that failure points can be traced, visualized and analyzed within the canonical OB-CAIE methodology problem space.

</details>


### [285] [Science or Slop?: Benchmarking and Mitigating Scientific Slop in AI-Generated Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00531)
*Yerim Oh,Young-Jun Lee,Jaewoo Ahn,Gunhee Kim,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"科学灌水(scientific slop)"概念，构建SciSlopBench基准与六项度量，并提出SciSlopHarness框架以减少AI生成论文的推理断层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成内容（AI slop）在学术界日益普遍，但科学论文中的灌水具有更复杂模式，现有基于词元的AI检测器难以识别——文章各局部看似合理，但连接各部分的科学推理断裂，从而误导读者判断。作者因此希望系统度量并缓解这一问题。

Method: 作者将此类失败定义为跨越Structure、Argument、Artifacts三个维度的六项度量，并构建SciSlopBench：包含390篇AI生成论文（以计算机科学为主，覆盖生命、社会与自然科学），每篇配以研究问题与贡献类型匹配的人类撰写论文。此外提出SciSlopHarness这一harness-level框架，引导固定LLM仅在实验记录支持之处进行修改。

Result: 六项度量以85.9%准确率区分每对中的AI论文，优于Binoculars的68.7%；更高的科学灌水伴随更低的ICLR评分，并在2017至2025年每一年都能以高于随机的水平区分被拒与接收论文。标准修订仍残留灌水，直接灌水感知提示会触发reward hacking，而SciSlopHarness在无需人类参考目标的情况下，较最强修订基线将AI-人类差距缩小63%。

Conclusion: AI生成科学论文在其全局推理中留下根本性痕迹，负责任的缓解需要严格的证据落地，而非仅仅润色行文。

Abstract: AI-generated content, often called AI slop, is increasingly common everywhere, particularly in academia. Slop in AI-generated scientific papers, however, has more complex patterns that cannot be easily detected by existing token-based AI detectors. Each part of such a paper looks plausible while the scientific reasoning that connects the parts breaks down, which can mislead how readers assess the work. We benchmark these failures as scientific slop through six measures across Structure, Argument, and Artifacts. We construct SciSlopBench with 390 AI-generated papers, mostly in computer science but spanning the life, social, and natural sciences, each paired with a human-written paper matched by research problem and contribution type. Our measures identify the AI paper in each pair with 85.9% accuracy, compared with 68.7% for Binoculars. Higher scientific slop accompanies lower ICLR ratings and distinguishes rejected from accepted papers above chance in every year from 2017 to 2025. Reducing these patterns, however, is not as simple as directly optimizing the measures. We therefore propose SciSlopHarness, a harness-level framework that guides a fixed LLM to revise slop only where the experiment records support the change. While standard revisions leave residual slop and direct slop-aware prompting triggers reward hacking, SciSlopHarness reduces the remaining AI-human gap by 63% over the strongest revision baseline without requiring human reference targets. Overall, we demonstrate that AI-generated scientific papers leave fundamental traces in their global reasoning, and that responsible mitigation demands strict evidentiary grounding rather than mere prose refinement.

</details>


### [286] [Worse Together: How Performance Breaks Down in Multi-User Multi-Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00583)
*Sahan Paliskara,Nattaput Namchittai,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究多用户多AI代理在共享资源下的协调问题，发现团队协作在五个前沿模型、四个环境、77个场景中普遍劣于单一协调者代理，并提出环境特定的缓解措施及MAMUBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人们越来越多地将任务委托给AI代理，且不同用户的代理需要在代码库、日历、预算等共享资源上交互，此时各代理目标不同，协调常常失败，导致群体结果甚至不如由单一代理服务所有人。因此需要系统研究多用户、多代理的协调问题及其失效行为。

Method: 论文在五个前沿模型和77个场景上研究了多用户多代理设定，覆盖四个环境：共享计算预算的API密钥环境、共享日历的诊所、共享团购或预订的个人助理环境，以及共享发布截止时间的合并队列。每个场景中，将服务所有用户的单一协调者代理与每个代理服务一个用户的团队进行对比，并分别在有、无代理间通信渠道两种条件下评估。同时识别失败行为并提出缓解措施。

Result: 在所有环境中，团队产生的群体结果都劣于协调者。无通信渠道时，团队在两个环境中完全崩溃；即便有渠道，协调开销仍造成显著差距，例如在个人助理环境中，协调者满足目标用户请求的频率约为团队的两倍。团队表现差的行为包括：随团队规模增大而停滞、互相覆盖彼此操作、以及捏造声明。缓解措施有效但依赖具体环境，例如设置团队负责人、明确程序性指令，以及平台检查要求代理在提交前阅读同伴消息。

Conclusion: 多用户、多代理协调在共享资源场景下仍存在严重困难，团队协作普遍不如单一协调者代理，且需要针对性缓解手段。作者将发布API密钥、诊所和个人助理环境作为MAMUBench，共含74个场景，用于评估多用户、多代理协调能力。

Abstract: People are increasingly delegating tasks to AI agents, and those agents are increasingly encountering other people's agents over shared resources such as a codebase, a calendar, or a budget. When each agent acts for a different user with different goals, coordination often fails, and the group ends up worse off than if a single agent had acted for everyone. We study this multi-user, multi-agent setting across five frontier models and 77 scenarios in four environments: an API key environment in which agents share a compute budget, a clinic in which they share a calendar, a personal assistant environment in which they share a group order or booking, and a merge queue in which they share a release cutoff. In each scenario, we compare a single agent that serves every user (a coordinator) to a team in which each agent serves one user, with and without a communication channel between the agents. Teams deliver worse group outcomes than the coordinator in every environment: without a channel, they completely collapse in two environments, and even with one, coordination overhead creates substantial gaps. For example, in the personal assistant environment, the coordinator fulfills a targeted user request about twice as often as teams. We identify distinct behaviors associated with this poor group-level performance, including stalling as teams grow, overriding each other's actions, and fabricating claims. We find effective but environment-specific mitigations, such as a team lead, explicit procedural instructions, and a platform check that makes an agent read its peers' messages before committing. We will release the API key, clinic, and personal assistant environments as MAMUBench, comprising 74 scenarios for evaluating multi-user, multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [287] [Legal Research Bench: Measuring End-to-End Reliability in Long-Horizon Legal Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00609)
*Katrina Drozdov,Oliver Chen,Langston Nashold,Rayan Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Legal Research Bench (LRB)，一个由专家撰写的 413 道美国开放式法律研究问题基准，配备金标准答案、支持性权威依据与二元评分量表，并用带检索工具的评测框架对 13 个前沿模型进行评估，发现最强模型完全正确率仅 42.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律研究是核心且耗时的法律工作流程，需要识别有效权威、验证时效性、协调法条与判例并综合出有依据的答案。语言模型智能体天然适合这种检索密集型任务，但其价值取决于可靠性——遗漏权威、引用过时或结论错误都会使答案不可用。然而目前缺乏能衡量这种可靠性的严格基准。

Method: 构建 LRB 基准：413 道由专家撰写的开放式美国法律研究问题，每题配对金标准答案、支持性权威依据和二元评分量表。在配备网页搜索、判例检索、页面解析与检索工具的评测框架中评估 13 个前沿模型。采用"全通过"评分加来源验证：仅当所有必需标准均满足且引用权威可验证时才算正确。同时用专家律师验证 LLM 评判器，确保基准分数与律师判断一致。

Result: 智能体远未达到可靠水平：测试中最强模型 Claude Opus 4.8 仅在 42.9% 的问题上完全正确。性能随任务场景差异显著，不同法律领域的全通过率不同，在需要协调相互冲突权威的问题上表现更低。跨模型来看，更多轮次、工具调用和推理成本并不能预测更高准确率。

Conclusion: LRB 揭示了当前前沿模型在法律研究这一高可靠性要求任务上的明显不足，并强调准确率不能简单通过增加交互轮次、工具使用或计算成本来提升，为该领域的智能体可靠性评估提供了严格基准。

Abstract: Legal research is a core and time-consuming legal workflow. Lawyers must identify controlling authority, verify that it remains valid, reconcile statutes and cases, and synthesize a grounded answer. Language model agents are a natural fit for this retrieval-intensive workflow, and automating even part of it would be valuable. But that value depends on reliability: a single missing authority, stale citation, or wrong legal conclusion can make an otherwise plausible answer unusable. We introduce \textbf{Legal Research Bench} (LRB), a benchmark of 413 open-ended U.S. legal research questions written by experts, each paired with a gold answer, supporting authorities, and a binary grading rubric. We evaluate thirteen frontier models in a harness with web search, case-law search, page parsing, and retrieval tools. We score agent responses through all-pass grading with source verification, where a response is correct only if every required criterion is satisfied and its cited authorities verify. We also validate the LLM judge against expert attorneys ensuring that benchmark scores track attorney judgment. Agents remain far from reliable: among the models we tested, the strongest, Claude Opus 4.8, is fully correct on 42.9\% of questions. Performance also varies substantially by task setting: all-pass rates differ across areas of law and are lower on questions requiring reconciliation of conflicting authorities. Across models, more turns, tool calls, and inference cost do not predict higher accuracy.

</details>


### [288] [Spatial Strategies, Not Actions: Vector-Quantized Geodesics as Tools for LLM-Driven Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00613)
*Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过将几何工具与LLM结合，探索LLM智能体的空间理解能力，在网格世界环境中实现高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体常被批评缺乏空间理解能力，主要依赖统计文本模式。作者希望探究并提升LLM在空间任务中的理解与决策能力。

Method: 提出一种分层架构：LLM作为高层协调器，几何工具作为低层执行器。离线阶段，收集测地轨迹并进行矢量量化，提取代表性轨迹子集，LLM为每条轨迹关联自然语言描述，形成工具库。在线阶段，LLM根据当前状态和目标选择合适的工具，底层通过执行对应轨迹的原语动作完成控制。

Result: 在部分可观测的动态2D网格环境中，使用开源视觉语言模型（Qwen3.6-35B-A3B）进行测试。将几何工具库与智能体中心缩放工具和碰撞检测工具结合后，快速非推理配置达到了与高成本思维链版本相同的目标达成率，同时将决策成本从分钟级降至秒级。

Conclusion: 该方法有效分离了工具发现（无监督量化）与推理决策（LLM），证明LLM智能体可借助几何工具提升空间理解能力，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based agents are often criticized for lacking spatial understanding and mainly exploiting statistical text patterns. We investigate their spatial comprehension through an architecture combining geometrical tools with a LLM serving as a high-level orchestrator in grid-world environments. The agent first collects geodesic trajectories, which are then vector-quantized to extract a representative subset. Offline, the LLM associates a natural language description of the underlying behavioral patterns to each selected trajectory, making it a tool. Online, the LLM chooses the appropriate tool conditioned on the current state and goal. Low-level control is handled by primitive actions that execute the trajectory associated with the tool. From an agentic AI perspective, this approach separates learning into two levels: tool discovery is handled through unsupervised quantization of trajectories, while reasoning and decision-making are handled by the LLM. We test the approach in a partially observable dynamic 2D grid environment with an open vision-language model (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B). Pairing the geometry-derived tool library with an agent-centered zoom tool and a collision detection tool lets a fast, non-reasoning configuration match the goal-reaching rate of a much more costly chain-of-thought version, while cutting the cost of a decision from minutes to seconds.

</details>


### [289] [CompMat-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents for Computational Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00636)
*Chenmu Zhang,Levi Felix,Jun-Jie Zhang,Xingfu Li,Xuelian Jiang,Tao Jiang,Subhendu Mishra,Xixi Qin,Boris Yakobson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CompMat-Bench，一个包含 94 项计算材料学研究任务的基准，通过预先复现实验步骤来避免昂贵模拟，从而评估 AI 智能体在真实科研步骤上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学任务上评估 AI 智能体受限于实验或计算所需的时间与资源。在计算材料研究中，跨智能体和多次试验重复相同的高成本模拟会让评估变得不可行，因此需要一种既能反映真实科研流程又无需重复昂贵模拟的评估方式。

Method: 构建 CompMat-Bench，94 项任务源自近期发表的计算材料研究，每项任务要求智能体完成通向该研究科学目标的一个步骤。研究者提前复现这些研究步骤，让智能体只负责准备输入和分析输出，从而在评估时跳过昂贵模拟。复现的输入与结果作为真值，用固定规则而非 LLM 评判进行打分。基准支持四种评估条件：单任务与由相关任务组成的工作流，各自配以完整或精简的方法论指导。

Result: 在单任务且提供完整指导的情况下，基于三种 LLM 的智能体能够完成单个材料研究步骤，94 项任务的通过率为 66.0%–90.4%。更长的工作流和精简指导都会限制智能体表现，但不同智能体受影响方式不同：较弱智能体的通过率下降，而最强智能体仅在长工作流与精简指导同时出现时才下降。失败分析显示，多数失败源于科学错误而非软件使用错误。

Conclusion: CompMat-Bench 为在真实材料研究的各步骤上比较智能体、并分析其失败模式提供了基础，说明当前 LLM 智能体已能胜任单步材料研究任务，但在长流程与指导不足的条件下仍存在明显局限。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents on scientific research tasks is constrained by the time and resources required for the underlying experiments or calculations. In computational materials research, repeating the same expensive simulations across agents and trials can make evaluation impractical. We introduce CompMat-Bench, a benchmark of 94 tasks derived from recently published computational materials studies, each asking agents to complete a step toward achieving the study's scientific goal. We reproduce the research steps in advance and assess agents on preparing inputs and analyzing outputs for expensive simulations, so expensive simulations can be avoided during evaluation. The reproduced inputs and results serve as ground truth for grading agents with fixed rules, without an LLM judge. The benchmark supports four evaluation conditions: single tasks and workflows composed of related tasks, each with full or reduced methodological guidance. With full guidance on single tasks, agents based on three LLMs demonstrate the ability to complete individual materials research steps, with pass rates of 66.0-90.4% across 94 tasks. Both longer workflows and reduced guidance can limit agent performance, but in different ways for different agents: they lower the pass rates of the weaker agents, whereas the strongest agent falls only when a long workflow is combined with reduced guidance. Failure analysis attributes most failures to scientific errors rather than to errors in software usage. CompMat-Bench provides a basis for comparing agents on the steps of real materials research and for analyzing agent failure modes.

</details>


### [290] [Incident-Arena: Getting agents to the last nine of reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00648)
*Andre Fu,Malik Drabla,Leon Liu,Meji Abidoye,Marek Suppa,Lata Mishra,Adnan El Assadi,Yiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Incident-Arena，一个面向 agentic SRE（智能体站点可靠性工程）的真实生产级基准，包含 20 个基于真实开源软件部署的任务，在临时 Kubernetes 集群中注入故障并施加持续负载，采用功能化验证器替代静态检查，前沿模型得分低于 64.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码智能体已广泛用于工程流程，但其在生产事故响应（即 agentic SRE）上的能力关注不足。现有基准存在三大局限：环境不真实（多为玩具仓库）、框架实现非标准、验证器为简单的静态检查，无法真实衡量智能体在长周期事故排查与修复中的表现。

Method: 构建 Incident-Arena 基准：人工精心设计 20 个任务，基于真实部署的开源软件；每个任务将生产应用部署到临时 Kubernetes 集群，从配置层到底层镜像注入故障，并按任务需求施加持续负载曲线。同时提出新型验证方法——超越静态检查的功能化验证器，在保持系统级指标稳定的同时确保修复的安全性。智能体试验平均消耗 2.81M tokens、41 轮交互。

Result: 在 20 个任务、3 种应用底座上，前沿模型得分均低于 64.3%。失败模式涵盖诊断/定位错误、修复不完整以及不安全回归。任务平均交互轮次与 token 消耗显著高于现有基准，体现出智能体长周期推理的需求。

Conclusion: Incident-Arena 揭示了当前前沿模型在生产事故响应这一新兴 agentic SRE 领域的能力缺口，表明其在长周期诊断、修复完整性与安全性方面仍远未达标，为后续研究提供了更真实、更严格的评测平台。

Abstract: AI coding agents are ubiquitous in engineering workflows amongst industry and academia. Yet, despite their use in app coding, relatively less attention has been paid to their ability to execute on production incident response. This emerging field, termed agentic site-reliability-engineering (SRE) contains benchmarks limited by (1) unrealistic environments, typically toy repositories (2) non-standard framework implementations and (3) simple static verifiers. We introduce Incident-Arena, a human-built benchmark of 20 carefully selected tasks grounded in real-world deployed open source software. Each task deploys a production application to an ephemeral Kubernetes cluster, injecting a fault from the config layer through underlying images, and a sustained load profile given the task requirements. We also present a novel verification method, going beyond static checks to functional verifiers, holding systems level metrics stable, while ensuring repairs are done safely. Agent trials run an average of 2.81M tokens and 41 turns, going beyond existing benchmarks, demonstrating agentic long horizon reasoning. Across 20 tasks and 3 application substrates, frontier models score below 64.3%, with failures extending from diagnosis/localization errors, through incomplete repairs and unsafe regressions.

</details>


### [291] [Agent Evaluation Reliability: More Tasks Won't (Always) Fix An Agent Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00651)
*Michael Hardy,Ruhana Azam,Anka Reuel,Mykel Kochenderfer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯方差分解框架，分析稀疏不平衡的智能体排行榜，发现智能体评估的可靠性取决于具体的结论主张（claim）：固定"模型-脚手架"系统可靠度高（0.935-0.994），但底层模型排名可靠性显著较低（0.148-0.841）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估被越来越多用于比较LLM并指导部署决策，但排名不仅反映模型本身，也受评估条件（如脚手架、任务）影响。因此可靠性是"依赖主张的"：一个能可靠排名部署系统的评估，未必能可靠排名底层模型。作者旨在厘清当前智能体评估究竟能可靠支撑哪些结论，以及需要哪些额外评估来改进。

Method: 开发贝叶斯方差分解框架，适用于稀疏、不平衡的智能体排行榜；将其应用于Holistic Agent Leaderboard和Harbor Index的22个基准，将"信号"（与目标主张相关的性能差异）与"噪声"（仍会改变排名但无关的变异）分离。

Result: (1) 可靠性取决于测量目标：固定模型-脚手架系统排名可靠（0.935-0.994），底层模型可靠性较低（0.148-0.841）；(2) 脚手架选择可改变结论，跨脚手架可靠性显示脚手架效应在各评估间差异很大；(3) 增加任务无法解决所有不确定性，当不确定性主要来自有限脚手架覆盖时，即使任务无限多，模型排名可靠性提升最多0.097；(4) 池化多样基准可更低成本改善跨任务排名，在相同任务预算下将预测可靠性从0.44提升至0.75，并最多降低83%成本。

Conclusion: 评估设计应遵循预期主张：明确分数或排名应代表什么，诊断限制其可靠性的因素，并将评估预算花在真正重要的不确定性来源上。

Abstract: Agent evaluations are increasingly used to compare LLMs and inform deployment decisions, yet ranks can reflect not only the model but also the effects of the evaluation conditions such as the scaffolds or tasks. This makes reliability claim-dependent: an evaluation that reliably ranks deployed systems may not reliably rank underlying models. We ask which conclusions current agent evaluations reliably support and what additional evaluation would improve them. We develop a Bayesian variance-decomposition framework for sparse, imbalanced agent leaderboards and apply it to 22 benchmarks from the Holistic Agent Leaderboard and Harbor Index. The framework separates signal, performance differences relevant to the intended claim, from noise, irrelevant variation that can still change rankings. We find: (1) Reliability depends on the measurement goal. Fixed model-scaffold systems are ranked reliably (0.935-0.994), while underlying-model reliability is substantially lower (0.148-0.841). (2) Scaffold choice can change conclusions. Inter-scaffold reliability measures whether scaffolds preserve model rankings, showing that scaffold effects vary substantially across evaluations. (3) More tasks cannot resolve all uncertainty. Even infinitely many similarly constructed tasks improve model-ranking reliability of a benchmark by at most 0.097 when uncertainty is dominated by limited scaffold coverage. (4) Pooling diverse benchmarks can improve cross-task rankings at lower cost. For rankings across diverse agentic tasks, pooling benchmarks raises projected reliability from 0.44 to 0.75 at the same task budget and can reduce projected cost by up to 83\%. Evaluation design should follow the intended claim: identify what a score or ranking should mean, diagnose what limits its reliability, and spend evaluation budget on the sources of uncertainty that matter.

</details>


### [292] [When More Data Is Not Enough: The Context-Sufficiency Frontier in Generative AI Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00654)
*Merieme Askour,Ayoub Merimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"上下文充分性"理论，指出生成式AI个性化中，上下文的相关性比数量更重要，并通过实验验证了相关上下文提升适宜性、无关上下文降低适宜性并破坏检索稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化长期依赖客户历史行为与偏好（客户证据）。生成式AI使提供方能够在生成响应时实时提供情境信息，无需预先编码所有条件。但这种便利带来新问题：上下文变得易得后，数量增多未必更好，需要理论来解释何时上下文是"足够的"。

Method: 作者提出上下文充分性理论，区分四种状态：不足、充分、饱和与干扰，并提出"上下文充分性前沿"以定位最小相关上下文集合。随后在一家大型家居用品零售商的生成式推荐系统上开展全因子实验进行验证。

Result: 实验显示，相关上下文提升了推荐适宜性，而无关上下文降低了适宜性并导致检索不稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架将个性化从"提供更多上下文"转向"识别当前交互真正需要什么"，并在整个服务流程中施加约束。

Abstract: Personalization has long relied on customer data to infer what an individual is likely to value. We call this customer evidence: the customer's historical behavior and preferences. Generative AI extends personalization by allowing providers to supply changing situational information at the moment a response is produced, without encoding every condition in advance. We define this provider-side context as information about what is possible, permitted, or advisable now. This flexibility creates a new problem: once context becomes easy to supply, more is not necessarily better. We develop a theory of context sufficiency in which the relevance of context to the customer's current intent matters more than its volume. The theory identifies four states, insufficiency, sufficiency, saturation, and interference, and introduces the Context-Sufficiency Frontier to locate the minimal relevant set. In a full-factorial experiment with a generative recommender at a large home-furnishing retailer, relevant context improved appropriateness, while irrelevant context reduced it and destabilized retrieval. The framework shifts personalization from supplying more context toward identifying what the current interaction actually requires and enforcing constraints throughout the service process.

</details>


### [293] [Backdoor Containment via Expert Quarantine and Shutdown in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00663)
*Jianwei Li,Min-Seon Kim,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"学习但隔离"（learn, but channel）新范式，用QES方法将后门行为隔离到可关停的专家模块，实现高效后门遏制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门防御分为四阶段，但底层策略只有两种：抑制后门学习，或先学习后净化。作者希望探索第三条路径——允许后门在训练中形成，但将其引流到隔离组件中，部署时可随时禁用。

Method: 提出Quarantined Expert Shutdown (QES)，在正则化引导的类MoE框架下实现。给定中毒数据集，为Transformer语言模型附加路由专家专属LoRA分支与轻量路由器，通过辅助路由目标将触发条件行为吸引到指定专家，同时保留其他部分的良性能力。部署时只需将隔离专家的路由权重置零，为常数时间操作，无需触发筛查或权重更新。

Result: 在两项任务、三种攻击、四个模型族上，攻击成功率(ASR)从100%降至0-10%，下游效用常被保持或仅受轻微影响。

Conclusion: 结果确立了"learn, but channel"作为生成式LLM后门遏制的一个全新且未被探索的范式，兼顾了高效防御与效用保持。

Abstract: Backdoored large language models (LLMs) can behave normally on benign inputs while producing attacker-specified outputs under hidden triggers. Existing defenses span four stages--prior-training, in-training, post-training, and inference-time--and share one of two underlying strategies: either suppress backdoor learning (by filtering poisoned data or interrupting its acquisition during optimization) or learn, then purify (by repairing model weights or gating inputs after a fully backdoored model has formed). We propose a third strategy, learn, but channel: allow backdoor formation during training but route it into a designated, quarantined component that can be disabled at deployment. To this end, we propose Quarantined Expert Shutdown QES, a computationally efficient containment strategy built in a regularization-steered MoE-like setting. Specifically, given a poisoned dataset, QES augments a Transformer-based language model with routed expert-specific LoRA branches and lightweight routers, and uses auxiliary routing objectives to attract trigger-conditioned behavior into a designated expert while preserving benign capability elsewhere. At deployment, mitigation reduces to a single constant-time operation: zeroing the quarantined expert's routing weight, without trigger screening or further updating model weights. Empirically, our methods reduce the attack success rate ASR from 100% to 0-10% on most settings across two tasks, three attacks, and four model families, while downstream utility is often preserved or only modestly affected. These results establish learn, but channel as a previously unexplored regime for backdoor containment in generative LLMs.

</details>


### [294] [A Simple Doxastic Deontic Logic for Norm-Guided Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00668)
*Thorsten Engesser,Agata Ciabattoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单的信念道义逻辑（doxastic deontic logic），用于在规范冲突与信息不完备场景下的自主决策，并定义了信念规范合规优化问题及其可归约为加权部分MaxSAT的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在决策时常面临规范冲突、信息不完整或不可靠的困境，需要一个能刻画"规范依赖智能体对事实与规范的信念"的形式化框架来支持此类决策。

Method: 构造一种经典可归约的Chellas极小道义逻辑片段，引入显式条件规范，并与多智能体KD45信念逻辑结合；在此基础上定义"信念规范合规优化问题"（DNCOP），区分基于智能体信念的主观优化与基于实际事实的客观优化，并给出二者一致以及优化可多项式时间归约为加权部分MaxSAT的条件。

Result: 给出了主观优化与客观优化相重合的条件，以及最优决策可多项式时间归约为加权部分MaxSAT的充分条件，从而为规范化自主决策提供了可计算的求解途径。

Conclusion: 该逻辑框架为在规范冲突和信息不可靠条件下的自主决策提供了形式化基础，并将规范合规优化问题与成熟的MaxSAT求解技术相连接。

Abstract: Making decisions despite conflicting norms and incomplete or unreliable information is a fundamental challenge for autonomous systems. We introduce a simple doxastic deontic logic for this setting: a classically reducible fragment of Chellas' Minimal Deontic Logic, extended with explicit conditional norms and combined with multi-agent KD45, so that norms can depend on agents' beliefs about both facts and norms. On this logic we define the Doxastic Norm Compliance Optimization Problem, where an agent chooses a decision minimizing weighted norm violations. We distinguish subjective optimization (relative to the agent's beliefs) from objective optimization (relative to the actual facts). We give conditions under which (i) the two coincide and (ii) optimal decision-making can be reduced to weighted partial MaxSAT in polynomial time.

</details>


### [295] [Ontology-Grounded, Reasoner-Verified Benchmarks for Evaluating LLM Reasoning in Scientific AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00682)
*Nishtha N. Vaidya,Stephan Grimm,Thomas Hubauer,Thomas A. Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从OWL 2本体自动生成多选问答基准的流水线，通过本体蕴含检查保证答案正确性与干扰项错误性，并在三个本体上生成多尺度MCQ数据集评测LLM逻辑推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在科学AI应用中常做结构化知识推理，但逻辑推理能力不足，易产生事实错误；且现有评测基准构建困难：人工构建难以规模化，LLM生成又可能引入其要测量的缺陷。因此需要能被标准推理器形式化验证、且正负样本都有明确知识背景的高质量基准。

Method: 提出自动生成本体驱动的多选问答（MCQ）基准流水线：从任何公理化充分的OWL 2本体生成题目，正确答案由本体设计保证；干扰项通过扰动类定义公理的右侧类表达式生成，并利用OWL推理器进行蕴含检查以形式化验证其错误性。干扰项覆盖四类语义类型（从类不可满足到弱化包含关系），支持诊断性评测特定推理失败。

Result: 在三个本体上评测：Pizza（小型学术）、PMDco（复杂材料科学）、DOID（大型生物医学），分别生成112、2,491、15,216道MCQ。自然语言质量方面，LLM评判平均分为4.02、4.36、3.36（满分5），正确答案与干扰项相似度高于0.8，说明错误选项无法仅凭表层形式排除。六个LLM零样本准确率为41.1%–76.8%，远高于25%随机基线，表明基准具有挑战性和区分度。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估科学AI中的逻辑推理提供了更可靠的基准构建方法，是迈向可靠评测的一步。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly underpin scientific AI applications that reason over structured knowledge, from biomedical question answering to materials informatics. However, their logical reasoning often falls short, producing factual inaccuracies unacceptable in these settings. Reliable evaluation remains challenging: manual dataset construction scales poorly, and LLM-based generation risks embedding the very flaws it aims to measure. High-quality benchmarks must ground both correct and incorrect labelled examples in explicit background knowledge, formally verifiable by a standard reasoner. We propose a pipeline that automatically generates ontology-grounded multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks from any sufficiently axiomatised OWL 2 ontology, with correct answers grounded in the ontology by design. Distractors are generated by perturbing the right-hand-side class expressions of class definition axioms, and their incorrectness is formally verified by an OWL reasoner via entailment checks. We evaluate the pipeline on three ontologies: Pizza (small, academic), PMDco (complex, materials science), and DOID (large, biomedical), generating 112, 2,491, and 15,216 MCQs respectively. Distractors span four semantic categories from class unsatisfiability to weakened subsumptions, enabling diagnostic evaluation of specific reasoning failures. Items meet natural language quality standards: mean LLM judge scores of 4.02, 4.36, and 3.36 out of 5 confirm fluency, and correct-answer-to-distractor similarity above 0.8 shows that wrong options cannot be dismissed on surface form alone. Six LLMs evaluated zero-shot achieve 41.1-76.8% accuracy, well above the 25% random-guessing baseline, indicating the benchmarks are challenging and discriminative. This work is a step towards more reliable benchmarks for assessing logical reasoning in scientific AI.

</details>


### [296] [Backdoor Purification for LoRA-Tuned LLMs via Null-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00685)
*Jianwei Li,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需任何强假设、无需事后重训练的后门净化方法，通过零空间投影将LoRA微调LLM的攻击成功率从近100%降至10%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM与PEFT（如LoRA）的广泛应用，后门攻击风险加剧。现有后门净化方法普遍依赖至少一种强假设，例如已知触发器先验、可访问干净参考数据或进行激进的重训练，且缺乏全面评估，严重限制其实用性。

Method: 在不依赖上述假设、也不对可疑参数进行事后重训练的前提下净化LoRA微调后的LLM。通过数据筛选与特征近似提取高保真后门方向，对每一层或每个注意力头在输入与输出通道上构造正交零空间，将LoRA更新投影到该零空间上。并通过一系列消融实验，从文本分类单层设置逐步扩展到生成任务的全参数LLM。

Result: 零空间投影方法将攻击成功率从接近100%降至10%以下，同时保持基座模型的通用能力与适配器学到的下游任务能力。

Conclusion: 该工作在不依赖触发器先验、干净参考和重训练等强假设的条件下实现了LoRA微调LLM的后门净化，兼顾安全性与性能保留，具备更强的实际适用性。

Abstract: With the rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, the risk of backdoor attacks has become more severe. Existing backdoor purification methods typically rely on at least one of the strong assumptions, such as prior knowledge of triggers, access to clean references, or aggressive retraining, and they often lack comprehensive evaluations. These constraints substantially limit their practical applicability. To overcome these challenges, our work proposes purifying LoRA-tuned LLMs without these assumptions and even without post-hoc retraining of the suspect parameters. Our objective is to significantly reduce the attack success rates (ASR) while preserving both (i) the base model's general capabilities and (ii) the new downstream skills learned through the adapter. Through a series of ablation studies, we progressively scale our approach from a single layer in a text classification setting to a full-parameter LLM in the generative task. Through careful data curation and feature approximation, we extract high-fidelity backdoor directions and, for each layer or head, construct orthogonal null spaces in both the input and output channels, onto which the LoRA updates are projected. Empirically, our null-space projection method reduces the ASR from nearly 100% to less than 10%, while preserving the base model's benign performance and the adapter's learned abilities during downstream task adaptation.

</details>


### [297] [R-GroundBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for R-Group Groundingin Markush Molecular Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00700)
*Xin Wang,Zichuan Ying,Xinna Lin,Junqi Zhang,Hanyi Xiong,Tianyu Gao,Hairong Zhang,Qixiang Hua,Botian Shi,Zhenhailong Wang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出R-GroundBench，一个基于真实专利Markush结构的诊断基准，用于评估模型在含R基团变量（R1、R2、X等）的不完整化学表示上的分子-语言接地能力，发现现有AI系统在识别与分子接地之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Markush结构通过可变R基团占位符编码分子家族，广泛存在于制药专利中，需要跨分子、文本和化学信息进行接地。然而现有分子-语言基准主要关注完全指定的分子，R基团接地能力基本未被评估，导致AI在科学发现中的推理能力存在盲区。

Method: 构建诊断基准R-GroundBench，数据来源于真实专利Markush结构。基准包含两个模块：一个多选题（VQA）赛道，设有可控的难度和模态划分；以及一个开放式生成（Generation）赛道，用于评估模型的执行与接地能力。

Result: 模型在Easy VQA上准确率超过90%，但在控制捷径的Hard VQA上降至56-66%。化学领域VLM尽管经过领域预训练，在Hard VQA上仅达25.7-46.2%。生成任务上多数模型精确匹配低于20%，当需要视觉输入时低于8%。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在Markush编辑任务上缺乏可靠的接地与执行能力，识别能力与实际分子接地之间存在显著鸿沟，这为AI驱动的科学发现提出了重要挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for scientific discovery enable molecular understandingand design, yet reasoning over incomplete chemical representations remainsunclear.Markush structures, which encode molecular families through variable R-groupplaceholders (\textit{R\textsubscript{1}}, \textit{R\textsubscript{2}}, \textit{X}, etc.), are ubiquitous in pharmaceutical patents and requiregrounding across molecular, textual, and chemical information.However, existing molecule-language benchmarks focus on fully specifiedmolecules, leaving R-group grounding largely unevaluated.We introduce R-GroundBench:, a diagnostic benchmark built from real patent Markushstructures, featuring a Multiple-Choice (VQA) track with controlled difficultyand modality splits, and an open-ended Generation track.Our results reveal a substantial gap between recognition andmolecular grounding.While models achieve over 90\% accuracy on Easy VQA, performance drops to56--66\% on Hard VQA when shortcuts are controlled.Chemical-domain VLMs also remain unreliable, achieving only 25.7--46.2\% on HardVQA despite domain-specific pretraining.Moreover, Generation Exact Match remains below 20\% for most models and below8\% when visual input is required.These findings reveal that current AI systems lack reliable grounding andexecution for Markush editing, highlighting challenges for AI-drivenscientific discovery.

</details>


### [298] [ReLiveGym: Evaluating Long-Lived Agents over Weeks of Replayed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00710)
*Xisen Jin,Jingheng Li,Zhenglun Chen,Junyi Du,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReLiveGym诊断评估环境，考察长时期任务中LLM智能体的行动时机、框架设计与反馈学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体被广泛用于需要持续监控或周期性执行的任务，需数天至数周无人值守运行，并动态适应环境；但现有长时程智能体工作多假设静态环境，未充分覆盖时间动态变化带来的挑战。

Method: 构建ReLiveGym，一个长时期任务的诊断评估环境：智能体在按时间顺序重放的数周真实世界新闻、市场与社交媒体流中稀疏行动；任务覆盖不同时间敏感性、推理强度与重复性。在八个基座语言模型上，考察模型选择与框架（harness）设计对性能的影响，并评估基于事后反馈的持续学习效果。

Result: 如何决定"何时行动"成为长时期任务中关键的框架设计维度；最优设计随任务变化，有时也随模型选择变化；持续学习能影响性能并缓解所观察到的部分失败模式。

Conclusion: 模型选择、行动时机机制与反馈利用是设计长时期智能体时的重要考量因素。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents become widely adopted, they are increasingly deployed for tasks that require persistent monitoring or recurring actions (e.g., market analysis). These agents are expected to operate unattended for days or weeks, act at the right timing, and adapt to the dynamic environment over time. These challenges are not fully captured in the existing long-horizon agent work, as they often consider a static environment that is not temporally changing. We introduce ReLiveGym, a diagnostic evaluation environment of long-lived tasks in which agents act sparsely over simulated weeks of chronologically replayed real-world news, market, and social-media streams. The tasks span diverse levels of time sensitivity, reasoning intensity, and recurrence. Across eight base language models, we investigate how model choice and harness design affect agent performance on such long-lived tasks. Our results show that how agents determine when to act arises as an important harness-design axis for long-lived tasks; and that the optimal design varies across tasks and sometimes model choices as well. We also evaluate how continuous learning from hindsight feedback affects performance and addresses failure modes observed in these long-lived tasks. These findings indicate model choice, action timing mechanism, and use of feedback as important considerations in the design of long-lived agents. Code: https://github.com/SaharaLabsAI/ReLiveGym

</details>


### [299] [Robust Nash Alignment under Preference Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00715)
*Shihab Ahmed,Debamita Ghosh,David Tang,Yudan Wang,Alvaro Velasquez,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Robust Nash Alignment 博弈论框架，用四玩家原始-对偶代理博弈和单环乐观镜像下降-上升算法，解决不确定成对偏好下的对齐鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏好的对齐方法通常只针对单一偏好模型优化，当成对偏好存在噪声、异质性或部署后发生偏移时表现脆弱。因此需要一个能应对不确定偏好的鲁棒对齐框架，并给出最坏情况性能的理论保证。

Method: 将问题建模为博弈：主学习者寻找策略，使其在对抗竞争者及位于名义偏好周围模糊集内的任意偏好核面前都具有较大的最坏情况胜率，该鲁棒目标可直接给出最坏情况性能的认证下界。由于原问题计算困难，引入包含领导者策略、跟随者策略、对抗核与对偶变量的四玩家原始-对偶代理博弈，并设计单环乐观镜像下降-上升算法求解。

Result: 证明了代理问题始终下界截断的硬约束目标，量化了代理与硬约束目标之间的差距，并给出代理可恢复鲁棒目标的精确性条件；证明代理博弈对偶间隙具有 O(1/√T) 的平均迭代收敛率，从而隐含得到原鲁棒目标的近优策略。在受控表格博弈和不确定偏好下的 LLM 对齐实验中，验证了收敛理论并优于名义基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为不确定成对偏好下的对齐提供了有理论保证的博弈论鲁棒框架，并通过算法与实验验证了其有效性和对名义基线的改进。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment methods typically optimize against a single preference model, and can therefore be brittle when pairwise preferences are uncertain: noisy, heterogeneous, or shift after deployment. To address these issues, we propose Robust Nash Alignment, a game-theoretic framework for alignment to uncertain pairwise preferences. Our formulation has a major learner seeking a policy with a large worst-case win rate against both an adversarial competitor and any preference kernel lying in an ambiguity set around a nominal preference. When the ambiguity set captures the uncertainty in preferences, the resulting robust objective of the game directly yields a certified lower bound on worst-case performance. However, we note this problem is computationally challenging to optimize, and to address this, we introduce a four-player primal-dual proxy game involving the leader policy, follower policy, adversarial kernel, and dual variable, and develop a single-loop optimistic mirror descent-ascent algorithm for it. We show that the proxy always lower-bounds the truncated hard-constrained objective, quantify the proxy-to-hard gap, and characterize an exactness condition under which the proxy recovers the robust objective. We then prove an \(\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})\) average-iteration convergence for the proxy-game duality gap, which implies a near-optimal robust policy for the original robust objective. Experiments on controlled tabular games and LLM alignment with uncertain preference further validate the convergence theory and show improved performance over nominal baselines.

</details>


### [300] [Enterprise Representation Simplification (ERS): Reducing Representational Complexity for Enterprise AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00791)
*Terry Dorsey,Kevin Huggins*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出企业表示简化（ERS）概念与表示中立的复杂度模型（ERC），从对象、交互、行为、支撑来源四维度刻画表示复杂度，并给出生命周期义务与经济成本模型，指出任务级复杂度降低有助于提升AI推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业信息由应用、项目、技术、组织边界与局部需求等长期累积塑造，形成不断增长的表示复杂度，企业需维护、信息消费者与AI系统需解读，因此需要一套可比较、可度量的复杂度框架来界定简化。

Method: 提出ERS（在既定范围内减少非必要表示复杂度并保留必要信息）与ERC（表示中立的复杂度模型）。ERC通过Representation Objects、Interactions、Behaviors、Supporting Sources四个维度刻画表示范围，前三个为依赖类别，后者刻画表示暴露程度，并在表示层与任务层定义以支持比较，区分架构简化与检索优化。同时构建经济模型，区分全局表示成本、任务级成本与一次性转换成本。

Result: 给出ERS的两点推论：一是表示结构带来维护、治理、依赖、变更、增强与运行的周期性生命周期义务，可通过经济模型在特定时间范围内评估；二是任务级ERC降低可减少AI系统需识别、关联与解读的表示范围，Text-to-SQL研究提供证据表明schema与推理复杂度降低可提升推理准确率。

Conclusion: ERC并非通用复杂度、性能或成本指标，而是提供可度量的架构变量，用于比较表示方案、转换效果、经济结果与AI推理表现。

Abstract: Enterprise information is represented through artifacts shaped by applications, projects, technologies, organizational boundaries, and local requirements. These structures accumulate over time, creating representational complexity that must be maintained by the enterprise and interpreted by information consumers and AI systems. This paper introduces Enterprise Representation Simplification (ERS) as reducing unnecessary representational complexity while preserving required information within a defined scope, and Enterprise Representation Complexity (ERC), a representation-neutral model for comparing complexity across representation states.
  ERC characterizes representational extent through four dimensions: Representation Objects, Interactions, Behaviors, and Supporting Sources. Objects, Interactions, and Behaviors form dependent categories, while Supporting Sources characterize representation exposure. ERC is defined at representation and task levels, enabling comparison and distinguishing architectural simplification from retrieval optimization.
  The paper develops two consequences of ERS. First, representational structures create lifecycle obligations for maintenance, governance, dependencies, change, enhancement, and operation. An economic model distinguishes recurring global representation cost, recurring task-level cost, and one-time transformation cost, enabling evaluation over a defined time horizon. Second, reductions in task-level ERC reduce the representational extent an AI system must identify, relate, and interpret. Text-to-SQL research provides evidence that reduced schema and reasoning complexity can improve reasoning accuracy.
  ERC is not a universal complexity, performance, or cost metric. It provides measurable architectural variables for comparing representational alternatives, transformation effects, economic outcomes, and AI reasoning performance.

</details>


### [301] [Sapien: A Stateful Policy Engine for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00797)
*Corinn Tiffany,Wen Zhang,Eugene Bagdasarian,Lillian Tsai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sapien 是一个用于执行有状态上下文策略的策略引擎，通过带状态谓词的正则表达式、延迟策略生成和局部语义检查约束 AI 智能体的工具调用序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文安全防御通过合成任务特定策略并在智能体工具调用上执行来阻止违规行为，但在多步任务中，哪些动作有效往往取决于智能体已执行和已获知的内容，静态策略难以应对。

Method: 提出 Sapien 策略引擎，用扩展了状态化谓词、延迟策略生成和局部语义检查的正则表达式，来规定允许的工具调用序列，从而执行有状态的上下文策略。

Result: Sapien 的效用与无约束智能体仅相差几个百分点；即使智能体被完全劫持，其策略也能排除 AgentDojo 上 93-95%、Toolathlon 上 62-85% 的攻击，在长时程任务中排除的攻击数量是工具允许列表的两倍。

Conclusion: Sapien 能在保持接近无约束智能体效用的同时，有效防御多步任务中的攻击，尤其在长时程任务上显著优于工具允许列表方案。

Abstract: Contextual security defenses prevent AI agents from taking rogue actions by synthesizing a task-specific policy and enforcing it on the agent's tool calls. In multi-step tasks, however, which actions are valid often depends on what the agent has already done and learned. We present Sapien, a policy engine for enforcing stateful contextual policies. A Sapien policy specifies permitted tool-call sequences using a regular expression extended with stateful predicates, deferred policy generation, and scoped semantic checks. We show that Sapien stays within a few percent of an unconstrained agent's utility. Even if the agent is fully hijacked, Sapien's policies rule out 93-95% of attacks on AgentDojo and 62-85% on Toolathlon (twice as many as tool allowlists on long-horizon tasks).

</details>


### [302] [Learning Multiple Timescales for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00849)
*Pedro Robles Dutenhefner,Dikshant Shehmar,Wagner Meira,Marlos C. Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GITA，一种将价值函数条件化于时间抽象步长 k 的离线目标条件强化学习方法，在 OGBench 上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）在长时程任务上表现不佳：折扣会使远距离状态之间的价值差异缩小到低于函数逼近误差，导致智能体无法对状态进行排序。时间抽象（将 k 个环境步视为一次转移）可以恢复远距离信号，但固定单一 k 无法适配所有状态-目标距离——大 k 保留长时程价值差异却抹平邻近状态的区别，小 k 则相反。

Method: 作者显式刻画这一权衡，提出 Generalized Implicit Temporal Abstraction（GITA），将单一价值函数条件化于 k。GITA 通过聚合多个 k 值下的优势加权监督来训练一个策略，使得对某状态-目标对赋予更大正优势的尺度对该更新的贡献更强，从而无需在局部分辨率与长程信号之间做取舍，也不必固定单一 k。

Result: 在 OGBench 上，GITA 超越多种离线 GCRL 基线：相比 HIQL，所有任务平均成功率提升 25 个百分点（相对提升 73%）；相比最强的固定 k 方法 OTA，提升 7 个百分点（相对提升 14%）。

Conclusion: GITA 通过将价值函数条件化于时间抽象步长 k 并聚合多尺度优势加权监督，同时保留局部分辨率与长程信号，在离线 GCRL 长时程任务上取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Existing approaches to offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) struggle with long-horizon tasks. Discounting shrinks value differences between distant states until they fall below the function approximation error, leaving the agent with no signal for ranking states. Temporal abstraction, which treats k environment steps as a single transition, restores this signal at long range, but no single fixed k suits all state-goal distances: large k preserves value differences across long temporal distances while collapsing distinctions between nearby states, and small k does the reverse. We make this trade-off explicit and introduce Generalized Implicit Temporal Abstraction (GITA), which conditions a single value function on k. GITA trains one policy by aggregating advantage-weighted supervision across multiple k values, so scales assigning larger positive advantages to a state-goal pair contribute more strongly to its update. GITA does not need to choose between local resolution and long-range signal; it retains both without committing to a single k. On OGBench, GITA outperforms a broad range of offline GCRL baselines, raising average success rate across all tasks by 25 percentage points (73% relative improvement) over HIQL. It also improves over the strongest fixed-k method, OTA, by 7 percentage points (14% relative).

</details>


### [303] [An Educator-Guided LLM Pedagogical Agent for Scaffolded Feedback in Conceptual Database Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00870)
*Sara Riazi,Pedram Rooshenas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由教育者引导的LLM教学智能体，用于概念数据库设计中的脚手架式反馈，并在ERD编辑器中部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念数据库设计（ERD）学习中，学生需要针对性的脚手架式反馈，但现有反馈难以同时结合学生作品、作业要求、教育者评分标准和教学资源，也难以控制反馈的披露程度与学生自主性。

Method: 系统集成于ERD编辑器，反馈基于学生ERD作品、作业要求、教育者编写的评分标准和教学资源。架构将“隐藏的、基于作品的诊断”与“控制学生可见支持形式及披露程度的工作流”分离。实例化为四阶段工作流：概念检查、引导应用、低细节反馈、局部澄清。每次反馈请求生成与版本化ERD状态关联的有状态episode。

Result: 在三个ERD环境、383个反馈episode的部署中，71.1%的观察到的目标级修改完全或部分采纳了隐藏诊断目标，包括许多发生在阶段1-2之后的修改。定性分析显示，分阶段披露有时隐藏了不准确细节、支持选择性采纳或允许后期修正，但部分错误仍影响了修改。自选样本的问卷偏好延迟披露与学生自主性，但指出反馈存在间接性和重复性。

Conclusion: 教育者引导的分阶段披露LLM反馈架构在概念数据库设计中具有可行性，能有效促进学生对诊断目标的采纳并支持学生自主性，但需改进反馈的间接性与重复性问题。

Abstract: We present an educator-guided LLM pedagogical agent for scaffolded feedback in conceptual database design. Integrated into an entity--relationship diagram (ERD) editor, the system grounds feedback in the student artifact, assignment requirements, educator-authored rubrics, and instructional resources. Its architecture separates hidden, artifact-grounded diagnosis from the workflow that controls the form and disclosure level of student-facing support.
  We instantiate the architecture as a four-stage workflow progressing from concept checks and guided application to low-detail feedback and localized clarification. Each feedback request creates a stateful episode linked to versioned ERD states. In a deployment spanning three ERD environments and 383 feedback episodes, 71.1\% of observed target-level changes fully or partially incorporated the hidden diagnostic target, including many after Stages~1--2. Qualitative analysis showed that staged disclosure sometimes withheld inaccurate details, supported selective uptake, or allowed later recovery, though some errors still shaped revisions. Survey responses from a self-selected sample favored delayed disclosure and student agency but noted indirectness and repetition.

</details>


### [304] [MemFit: Efficient Long-Term Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00872)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemFit 是一种面向对话智能体的长期记忆系统，通过免 LLM 的追加式存储与多路径检索，在大幅降低记忆构建成本与延迟的同时，于 LoCoMo、MemGallery、LongMemEval-S 三个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 长期记忆系统依赖 LLM 智能体来组织和整合记忆，导致写入操作成本高、效率低；而基于压缩的方法又丢失了表层细节。因此需要一种既低成本又能保留原始信息的记忆方案。

Method: MemFit 将每一轮对话逐字存入仅追加（append-only）的存储中，实现近实时的、无需 LLM 的插入；使用片段摘要对轮次进行索引而非替换原文；并采用免 LLM 的多路径检索策略，结合词汇与语义信号，并在文本与多模态场景下对字幕增强片段进行交叉编码器重排。

Result: 在 LoCoMo、MemGallery 和 LongMemEval-S 三个广泛使用的基准上，MemFit 达到当前最优性能，同时将记忆构建时间与成本降低数倍。

Conclusion: MemFit 为持久化的智能体记忆提供了一种可扩展且高效的解决方案，在保证性能领先的同时显著降低了记忆操作的开销。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems for large language models (LLMs) have gained popularity for extending reasoning capabilities across applications. Current memory systems rely on LLM agents to organize and consolidate memory, resulting in costly, inefficient write operations. To address this limitation, we propose MemFit, a long-term memory system for conversational agents that reduces the cost and latency of memory operations. Unlike existing systems that rely on expensive LLM calls for memory construction or discard surface-level details through compression, MemFit stores each turn verbatim in an append-only store with near-instantaneous, LLM-free insertion, indexing turns with segment summaries rather than replacing them. Additionally, MemFit uses an LLM-free, multi-path retrieval strategy that combines lexical and semantic signals with cross-encoder reranking over caption- augmented episodes in both textual and multimodal settings. Empirical results on three widely used benchmarks, LoCoMo, MemGallery, and LongMemEval-S, show that MemFit achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing memory construction time and cost several-fold, providing a scalable and efficient solution for persistent agentic memory.

</details>


### [305] [OR for AI That Does OR: Routing LLMs up the Escalator inside the OSCAR Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00912)
*Jinzhi Bu,Haixin Tang,Huanan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSCAR 提出一种由仿真器、编码器和审查器组成的优化建模框架，通过验证改进并按成本分配多个LLM调用，在五个基准问题上以两个小型开源模型达到95%–100%准确率，且成本显著低于Codex与Claude Code。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可将业务描述转为优化模型，但生成的代码可能错误表达约束或目标，导致求解器对错误问题给出最优解；即使解满足运行规则，也可能存在更优方案。企业需要低成本的LLM框架，能生成准确建模并尽可能本地运行。

Method: 提出OSCAR，包含离线Simulator（用标注决策样本认证）、Coder与Reviewer；用Simulator比较候选并持续搜索直至获得认证改进。将“寻找下一次认证改进”建模为难度未知下的序贯决策：选择调用哪些LLM及何时停止。已知先验下给出成本递增升级最优的条件；一般菜单下给出无先验的竞争保证。

Result: 在五个基准问题上，使用两个小型开源LLM（各可单GPU本地部署），OSCAR在报告设置下达到95%–100%准确率，而单次尝试平均准确率仅29%和48%。每问题五次运行中，Codex与Claude Code的平均token成本分别是OSCAR的3.1倍和5.8倍。

Conclusion: OSCAR支持开源权重模型本地或云端部署，兼顾预算与保密需求。企业应维护可行/不可行决策的标注样本以明确规则，当LLM解释与标注冲突时以标注为准。随着LLM能力与价格变化，其简单规则与可调设置有助于企业调整模型选择并受益于技术进步。

Abstract: Large language models can translate business descriptions into optimization models, but executable code may misrepresent constraints or objectives. A solver can then return an optimal solution to the wrong problem. Even when the solution satisfies the intended operating rules, a better plan may exist. For organizations that repeatedly use optimization modeling, an LLM-based framework should produce accurate formulations at low cost and, ideally, run locally. We study how to verify improvements and allocate attempts across LLMs that differ in price and capability. We develop OSCAR (Optimization modeling by Simulator, Coder, And Reviewer), which uses an offline Simulator certified against labeled decision examples to compare candidates and continues searching beyond feasibility. We model the search for the next certified improvement as sequential decisions under unobserved difficulty: which LLMs to call and when to stop. In a simplified known-prior setting, we give conditions under which cost-ordered escalation is optimal. For general menus, we derive a prior-free competitive guarantee. On five benchmark problems, OSCAR achieves 95% to 100% accuracy at the reported settings using two small open-weight LLMs, each deployable locally on a single GPU. Their single-attempt accuracies average 29% and 48%. In five runs per problem, Codex and Claude Code incur average token costs 3.1 and 5.8 times OSCAR's, respectively. OSCAR supports open-weight models locally or in the cloud, depending on budget and confidentiality requirements. Firms should maintain labeled decision examples of feasible and infeasible decisions to clarify plain-language operating rules. OSCAR follows these labels when an LLM's interpretation conflicts with them. As LLM capabilities and prices change, OSCAR's simple operating rules and adjustable settings help firms adapt their model choices and benefit from these advances.

</details>


### [306] [Finding the Right Fit: Model-Harness Interactions across Agent Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00917)
*Yixuan Li,Yiyun Zhou,Yao Long Teng,Fuchao Yang,Yanchen Deng,Zhiyi Lyu,Xuyu Dong,Feng Chen,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过66种"模型×脚手架"组合的评测，论证LLM智能体能力取决于模型、脚手架与任务的匹配，而非单一因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择智能体系统实际上是同时选择语言模型与承载其行动的脚手架（harness），但现有实践常默认"强模型"或"强脚手架"在更换场景后依然强。作者希望验证这种稳健性假设是否成立，即模型排名、脚手架优劣是否随评测基准变化而改变。

Method: 构建66种配置：4种可配置脚手架（OpenHands、DeepSeek Harness、PI、openJiuwen）与5个模型两两配对，外加原生Codex-GPT与Claude Code-Claude配对；在TUA-Bench、ALE-CLI、Terminal-Bench 4三个基准上评测，并对轨迹进行匹配分析以解释差异来源。

Result: 模型排名随脚手架发生反转：Terminal-Bench 4上Claude在OpenHands中领先GPT 7.94分，但在PI中落后30.16分。五个模型中有四个的最优脚手架随基准变化，但存在稳定配对（openJiuwen让Kimi在三个基准上均取得最高分，提升5.61至11.11分）。厂商自带脚手架并不可靠地最优，成本更高也不保证得分更高：GPT在PI下得分高于DSH，单任务成本却不足其四分之一。轨迹显示模型几乎都自行发起修复，效果取决于脚手架是否以模型可用的形式反馈失败；GPT适配PI的轻量结构，而常产生畸形工具调用的Kimi更适合openJiuwen。

Conclusion: 模型、脚手架与任务应被联合评估，而非孤立比较；作者开源了脚手架适配器、评测代码与全部6,204条带分轨迹，呼吁以配对视角看待智能体能力。

Abstract: Choosing an agent system means choosing both a language model and the harness through which it acts. We ask whether a strong model, harness, or pairing stays strong when the setting changes. We evaluate 66 configurations: four configurable harnesses (OpenHands, DeepSeek Harness, PI, and openJiuwen) paired with five models on TUA-Bench, ALE-CLI, and Terminal-Bench 4, plus the native Codex-GPT and Claude Code-Claude pairings. Model rankings reverse across harnesses. On Terminal-Bench 4, Claude leads GPT by 7.94 points in OpenHands but trails it by 30.16 points in PI. For four of the five models, the best harness changes from one benchmark to another, yet some pairings hold: openJiuwen gives Kimi its highest score on all three benchmarks, by 5.61 to 11.11 points. A model's own vendor harness is not reliably its best, and higher cost does not reliably buy a higher score. On Terminal-Bench 4, GPT scores higher under PI than under DSH at less than a quarter of the cost per task. Matched trajectories suggest why fit varies. Models start almost all repairs themselves, so much depends on whether the harness hands failures back in a form the model can use. GPT does best with PI's lean scaffold, while Kimi, which often issues malformed tool calls, does best in openJiuwen. We argue that the model, the harness, and the task should be evaluated together, and we release the harness adapters, evaluation code, and all 6,204 scored trajectories at https://github.com/liyix/finding-the-right-fit and https://huggingface.co/datasets/yixuanli97/finding-the-right-fit.

</details>


### [307] [ABDA-NL: A Natural-Language Scenario Explorer for Argument-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00947)
*Shawn Bowers,Martin Caminada,Haoyang Liu,Bertram Ludäscher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ABDA-NL 为基于 ASPIC- 知识库的论证讨论系统 ABDA 增加了自然语言接口，由大语言模型负责语言与形式化之间的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原系统 ABDA 基于形式化知识库，用户需要理解形式化语法才能使用，门槛较高。本文希望让非专业用户也能通过自然语言探索论证、提问和编辑知识库。

Method: 在 ABDA（基于 ASPIC- 知识库、grounded 语义的论证系统）之上构建自然语言界面。用户可查看结论的接受/拒绝/未定状态、打开交互式论证博弈可视化、通过暂停假设与规则或修改偏好来探索 what-if 方案、基于场景参考文档提问，并用自然英语编写新事实、假设和规则。大语言模型充当语言与形式化之间的桥梁，负责基于文档和当前场景回答问题并将自然语言编辑翻译为候选形式化语句；确定性的 ABDA 引擎仍是论证、攻击和接受标签的唯一来源，模型的每个建议都需经系统验证并由用户确认后才生效。

Result: 系统实现了自然语言问答、what-if 探索与自然语言知识库编辑功能，同时通过“确定性引擎为准 + 用户确认”的机制保证了输出结论的可靠性。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型作为语言接口、确定性论证引擎作为语义核心的组合，可在保持形式化论证严谨性的同时显著降低用户使用门槛。

Abstract: ABDA-NL adds a natural-language interface to ABDA, a system for argument-based discussion using ASPIC- knowledge bases under grounded semantics. Users see which conclusions are accepted, rejected, or undecided, open an interactive rendering of the grounded discussion game to learn why, explore what-if alternatives by suspending assumptions and rules or changing preferences, ask questions that are answered from a scenario's reference documents, and author new facts, assumptions, and rules in plain English. A large language model provides the bridge between language and formalism: it answers questions from the documents and the current state of the scenario, and it translates plain-English edits into candidate formal statements. The deterministic ABDA engine remains the sole source of arguments, attacks, and acceptance labels, and every proposal of the model is validated and confirmed by the user before it takes effect.

</details>


### [308] [PG-SFT: Balancing Capability Acquisition and Retention in Offline Agent Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00949)
*Ronghua Li,Zi Liang,Zhishan Li,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Privilege-Guided SFT（PG-SFT），利用 agent 轨迹的轮次级信息增益来调节监督强度，从而在获得新能力与保留原有能力之间取得更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线 agent 轨迹上做监督微调（SFT）是训练专用工具使用型 agent 的标准做法，但逐 token 模仿推理和动作会损害基座模型的其他能力（如通用推理、工具调用、代码生成）。作者希望研究如何在 agent 轨迹 SFT 中更好地平衡"获得新能力"与"保留已有能力"之间的权衡。

Method: 作者先比较了多个基线：标准 SFT 提升目标基准但降低非目标基准；用 KL 惩罚约束分布漂移或限制更新幅度都无法避免这一退化。受近期 token 级自适应学习目标启发，提出 PG-SFT，以 agent 轨迹的 turn-level 信息增益作为指示器，自适应调整监督强度。

Result: PG-SFT 在所评估基准上取得了更有利的权衡：显著减少了分布漂移和广泛的能力退化，代价是目标任务性能略有下降。

Conclusion: 平衡"能力获取—保留"不仅取决于模型是否被锚定到基座行为，还取决于监督应在何处、以多强的程度偏离基座行为。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on offline agent trajectories is the standard approach for training specialized tool-using agents, but forcing models to imitate reasoning and actions token by token may harm other capabilities (e.g., general reasoning, tool calling, code generation) of the base model. In this work, we focus on studying \emph{how to better balance the trade-off between acquiring new capabilities and preserving existing ones during agent trace SFT}. By comparing several baselines in our setup, standard SFT improves the target benchmark while lowering several non-target benchmark scores; meanwhile, simply constraining distributional drift using KL penalty or limiting the update magnitude did not avoid this regression trend. Motivated by recent token-wise adaptive learning objectives, this work proposes \textbf{Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)} to leverage turn-level information gain of agent trajectories as an indicator to adjust supervision strength. PG-SFT yields a more favorable observed trade-off on the evaluated benchmarks, substantially reducing distributional drift and broad capability degradation at the cost of slight degradation in target-task performance. Our findings suggest that balancing the acquisition--retention trade-off depends not only on whether the model is anchored to its base behavior, but also on where and how strongly supervision should depart from that behavior.}

</details>


### [309] [Cybernetic and Epistemic: A Missing Vocabulary for Trustworthy Agentic Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00961)
*Jérémie Lumbroso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 随着代码生成被委托给AI系统，监督问题从"写代码"转向"监督写代码的系统"。论文指出"人类监督"缺乏区分语言两种功能（协调行动与协调理解）的词汇，提出以"可检索、可核查的推理"为核心的治理准则及操作化形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于：代码生成任务越来越多地交由AI系统完成，瓶颈从编写代码转向监督这些系统，这暴露了一个词汇空白——领域要求"人类监督"，却未区分语言在委托通道中的两种作用：协调行动（控制论语义，词语在现实与之匹配时成功）与协调理解（认识论语义，词语在回应世界且听者能核查时成功）。所命名的失败并非控制论语言，而是"认识论形式的语言在做控制论的工作"——为获得认可而非真理性校准的、解释外形的输出。仅检查输出是否获批的监督可被橡皮图章式通过满足，而真正问责需要智能体工作背后的推理可被检索与核查。

Method: 论文提出一个面向智能体系统治理的准则：每一个有后果的选择都应携带"在什么条件下它本会不同"的条件，并以第三方可测试的形式呈现。作者给出该准则的操作化形式——一个两部分的重构测试，通过第二位读者能否预测智能体在扰动下的行为来给委托记录评分——以及一套审议记录约定ORRCF，使该条件成为每个被记录选择的必需组成部分。研究以三个委托片段（作为示例而非受控证据）展示认识论参与既可实践又可审计，另含一份按其自身陈述条件撤回建议的公开记录，以及一个体现"自由裁量选择无需理由"的监督失败案例。

Result: 三个委托片段表明认识论参与在实践中可行且保持可审计；一份公开记录显示建议按自身陈述的条件被撤回；一个失败案例展示了不要求理由的监督如何运作。论文未提供受控实验证据，所述案例为示例性说明。

Conclusion: 论文主张在既有技术信任属性之外，为智能体系统治理增设一项准则：每个有后果的选择都应携带使其可能不同的条件，且以第三方可测试的形式呈现；否则第三方无法区分决策与橡皮图章。作者将该准则操作化为两部分重构测试，并提出ORRCF审议记录约定，使该条件成为每个记录选择的必需组件。

Abstract: As code generation is increasingly delegated to AI systems, the bottleneck is shifting from writing code to supervising the systems that write it --- a shift CS-education researchers have begun to name. This shift exposes a vocabulary gap: the field asks for "human oversight" without a working distinction between the two things language does in a delegation channel --- coordinate action (cybernetic: words succeed when the world comes to match them) and coordinate understanding (epistemic: they succeed when they answer to the world and a hearer can check that they do). The failure this names is not cybernetic language but epistemic-form language doing cybernetic work: explanation-shaped output calibrated for approval rather than truth. Oversight that checks only whether an output was approved is satisfiable by rubber-stamping; oversight that holds an agent accountable requires the reasoning behind its work be retrievable and checkable. We present three delegation episodes --- illustrations, not controlled evidence --- in which epistemic engagement proved practicable while remaining auditable, one public record where a recommendation was withdrawn on its own stated terms, and one failure case illustrating oversight that requires no reasons for its discretionary choices. We propose a criterion for agentic-system governance, alongside existing technical trust properties: every consequential choice should carry the condition under which it would have gone otherwise, in a form a third party can test. Without such a condition, a third party cannot distinguish a decision from a rubber stamp. We give the criterion an operational form --- a two-part reconstruction test scoring a delegation record by whether a second reader can predict what the agent does under a perturbation --- and a deliberation-recording convention, ORRCF, that makes the condition a required component of every recorded choice.

</details>


### [310] [RISED: RubrIcs for agentic multi-environment Selection and sElf-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00979)
*Jingtan Wang,Sirajul Salekin,Young mok Jung,Javier Movellan,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RISED 利用规则文本（rubric）同时指导在线数据选择与策略监督，提升多环境 LLM 智能体训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有课程与数据选择策略多停留在环境级分配、依赖局部标量奖励，忽视跨环境 rollout 之间的关系；且不同环境学习速率不同，批次内会同时出现全失败与全成功组，导致组内相对奖励缺失。仅靠标量奖励难以提供跨环境关系信息和组内对比信号，因此需要更丰富的文本反馈（如描述行为规则的 rubric）来引导学习。

Method: 提出 RISED 框架：设置跨环境共享的 rubric 词表，由 LLM judge 对每条 rollout 打标签生成行为轮廓；据此选择与混合环境批次整体行为构成一致、且与已选数据重叠最小的样本；将正向 rubric（期望行为）作为特权上下文提供给 on-policy 自蒸馏教师，产生额外 token 级监督；负向 rubric（不期望行为）则引导后续 rollout 生成远离重复失败模式。三者共同构成 RISED。
